### [1] [Dancing Tokens on the Vocabulary Network from LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18248)
*Yuan-Chao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将六个开源大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）的输入嵌入（input embeddings）构建为相似性网络（similarity network），并将生成过程映射到该网络之上，以图学习（graph learning）视角考察生成与预训练所学几何结构的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 逐词元（token）生成文本，但特定生成与预训练所学几何结构之间的关系尚不清晰，其根本可解释性（explainability）难以把握，需要微观层面的新视角。

Method: 基于六个开源 LLM 的输入嵌入构建相似性网络，并将生成映射到该网络上；同时以协方差匹配的随机嵌入作对照，分析网络拓扑（topology），并结合逐层的 logit lens 观察词元在深度上的收敛情况。

Result: 词元嵌入网络呈无标度（scale-free）且重尾（heavy-tailed），与协方差匹配的随机嵌入相似但更为碎片化；蒸馏（distillation）几乎不改变该拓扑，因输入嵌入多为继承而来。连续生成的词元极少为图邻居，多数模型中其频率不高于随机打乱顺序，说明生成并非在预定义网络上进行游走（walk），但所访问的词元集中于网络内部。logit lens 显示多数模型中词元在较深层才稳定，重复性模型局限于小的领域特定子图（sub-graph）。

Conclusion: 知识空间在很大程度上由预训练决定；上述可观测量可作为推理时（inference-time）退化（degeneration）的监测指标，并提供了从节点或链路预测（node or link prediction）角度理解 LLM 生成的微观新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text one token at a time, yet how a particular generation relates to the geometry learned in pre-training remains unclear. The fundamental explainability of LLMs is elusive. Here, we build a similarity network from the input embeddings of six open LLMs and map generation onto it. The token-embedding network is scale-free while heavy-tailed, as are random embeddings with matched covariance, but far more fragmented. Distillation barely changes this topology because input embeddings are largely inherited. The knowledge space is thus deterministic from pre-training. Consecutive generated tokens are rarely graph neighbours and, in most models, no more often than in shuffled order, so generation is not a walk on the predefined network, although the tokens it visits are concentrated within it. Through the logit lens in a layer- wise manner, tokens settle late in depth in most models, and repetitive models remain confined to small domain-specific sub-graphs. These observables could serve as inference-time monitors of degeneration. We thus provide a fresh perspective on understanding LLM generation in the microscopic way from node or link prediction by graph learning.

</details>

### [2] [Mean-field theory of neural networks: from spikes to firing rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17512)
*Tawan T. A. Carvalho,Matheus Haas,Denis D. Caprioti,Luis A. R. Delgado,Artyom Kuznetsov,Henrique L. Schweitzer,Constantino F. Vasconcelos,Rodrigo P. Rocha,Mauricio Girardi-Schappo*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理放电率模型（firing-rate models）的基础框架：通过时间、群体与试次平均形式化定义放电率，并借助平均场近似（mean-field approximation）把单神经元脉冲与兴奋-抑制循环群体的集体动力学联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算神经科学（computational neuroscience）的核心挑战在于将微观神经元脉冲（neuronal spiking）与宏观脑功能相连，而放电率模型提供了介于单脉冲与大尺度群体动力学之间的中间层描述框架，因此有必要系统阐明其定义基础、实验证据与建模逻辑。

Method: 以时间平均、群体平均与试次平均形式化定义放电率；综述从单神经元输入-输出传递函数（input-output transfer functions）到群体调谐曲线（tuning curves）的实验证据；并利用平均场近似建立脉冲与放电率在单神经元及循环连接的兴奋-抑制群体中的对应关系。

Result: 展示集体动力学如何从神经元相互作用中涌现，包括群体刺激-响应曲线（stimulus-response curves）、由循环耦合（recurrent coupling）诱导的双稳态（bistability），以及由突触抑制（synaptic depression）产生的脑节律（brain rhythms）。

Conclusion: 放电率模型可作为神经活动的有效描述，将个体神经元动力学与跨尺度的集体计算（collective computations）相连接；其扩展已应用于学习、癫痫样振荡活动（seizure-like oscillatory activity）、视觉选择性（visual selectivity）、神经振荡中的相位-幅度耦合（phase-amplitude coupling）及全脑建模（whole-brain modeling）。

Abstract: Connecting microscopic neuronal spiking to macroscopic brain function is a central challenge in computational neuroscience. Firing-rate models provide a framework for addressing this problem by describing neuronal activity at an intermediate level between individual spikes and large-scale population dynamics. Here, we introduce the foundations of firing-rate descriptions, formalizing their definitions through temporal, population, and trial averaging. We discuss experimental evidence for firing rates, from the input-output transfer functions of individual neurons to the tuning curves of different neuronal populations. We then establish the connection between spikes and firing rates in single neurons and recurrently connected excitatory-inhibitory populations using mean-field approximation. We illustrate how collective dynamics emerge from neuronal interactions, including population stimulus-response curves and bistability induced by recurrent coupling, and brain rhythms arising from synaptic depression. We briefly review extensions and applications of firing-rate models to learning, seizure-like oscillatory activity, visual selectivity, phase-amplitude coupling in neural oscillations, and whole-brain modeling. Together, these examples illustrate how firing-rate models provide effective descriptions of neural activity, linking the dynamics of individual neurons to collective computations across scales.

</details>

### [3] [Narrow-Band Magnetism Allowed by Quantum Geometry: Altermagnetism and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17414)
*Minxuan Wang,Pei-Han Lin,Yang Ge,Jiabin Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过对比量子几何强弱而色散相同的两个极限，证明量子几何是窄带中交错磁（altermagnetism）从相互作用中涌现的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何（quantum geometry）在窄带关联相中作用重要，但其对不同磁序竞争、尤其交错磁（altermagnetism）涌现的影响尚不清楚；且在该体系中晶格对称性本身不足以约束磁序。

Method: 研究带有严格 onsite 广义 Hubbard-Kanamori 相互作用（generalized Hubbard-Kanamori interactions）的四极绝缘体模型（quadrupole insulator model），其中 Néel 反铁磁与交错磁在对称性上均被允许；比较最低窄带分别为 obstructed atomic（强量子几何）与 trivial atomic（弱量子几何）而色散相同的情形，并对部分填充的最低带做 Hartree-Fock 计算。

Result: 弱量子几何情形下仅出现常规铁磁相；强量子几何情形下则出现两个共线交错磁相（collinear altermagnetic phases）以及向列相（nematic phases）。差异源于投影相互作用（projected interaction）的不同性质：强量子几何情形获得可观的长程通道，弱量子几何情形则由 onsite Hubbard 通道主导。

Conclusion: 量子几何对交错磁从相互作用窄带中涌现起决定性作用，尤其在晶格对称性无法充分约束磁序的体系中。

Abstract: Quantum geometry plays a central role in shaping correlated phases in narrow bands, yet its influence on the competition between distinct magnetic orders, especially the emergence of altermagnetism, remains largely unexplored. To address this question, we study the quadrupole insulator model with strictly onsite generalized Hubbard-Kanamori interactions, in which both Néel antiferromagnetism and altermagnetism are symmetry-permitted. Depending on the parameters, the isolated narrow lowest band of the model can either be obstructed atomic with strong quantum geometry or trivial atomic with weak quantum geometry, while the dispersion is identical in the two cases. By partially filling the lowest band, our Hartree-Fock calculations show only conventional ferromagnetic phases for the weak quantum geometric case, while we find two collinear altermagnetic phases, as well as nematic phases, for the strong quantum geometric case. Such a dramatic difference can be understood by the distinct characters of the projected interaction: the projected interactions acquire sizable longer-range channels only for the strong quantum geometric case, while they are dominated by the on-site Hubbard channel in the weak quantum geometric case. These results establish the essential role of quantum geometry for altermagnetism to emerge from interacting narrow bands where crystalline symmetries cannot sufficiently constrain the magnetic order.

</details>


### [4] [Invertible parafermionic topological phases in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17429)
*André L. G. Mudry,Ömer M. Aksoy*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文将一维可逆拓扑相（invertible topological phases）的分类范式从玻色子/费米子推广到具有对称性的仲费米子（parafermion）自由度，通过上同调不变量（cohomological invariants）刻画边界投影表示（projective representations）并计算堆叠（stacking）规则，发现仲费米子可逆相在堆叠下可构成非阿贝尔群（non-Abelian group）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子与费米子可逆拓扑相的分类已较为成熟，尤其在一维可通过边界投影表示理解；作者希望将该范式推广至具有对称性的仲费米子自由度，以刻画其边界投影表示与堆叠结构。

Method: 定义刻画不同边界投影表示的上同调不变量并计算其堆叠规则；对阶为素数幂 N 的仲费米子，构造实现具 ℤ_N^3 对称性可逆拓扑相的代表性哈密顿量（representative Hamiltonians）。

Result: 给出利用上同调不变量计算受保护边界简并度（protected boundary degeneracy）的方法；表明与玻色子或费米子不同，仲费米子可逆相在堆叠下可形成非阿贝尔群；对阶为素数幂 N 的情形，这些相在堆叠下生成阶为 2N^3 的非阿贝尔群，当 N 为奇素数时该群为 Heis(ℤ_N)⋊ℤ_2，其中 Heis(ℤ_N) 为阶 N^3 的海森堡群（Heisenberg group）。

Conclusion: 一维仲费米子可逆拓扑相可通过上同调不变量与边界投影表示加以分类，其堆叠结构呈现非阿贝尔特性，区别于玻色子与费米子的阿贝尔情形。

Abstract: Invertible topological phases of matter are described by gapped ground states of local Hamiltonians that are non-degenerate on any closed spatial manifold. Classification of invertible topological phases realized by bosonic or fermionic degrees of freedom is well-understood, particularly in low dimensions. In one-dimensional space, the classification can be understood through the boundary projective representations. We extend this paradigm to one-dimensional invertible topological phases of parafermionic degrees of freedom with symmetries. We define cohomological invariants that characterize distinct boundary projective representations and compute their stacking rules. Among other results, we show (i) how these cohomological invariants can be used to compute protected boundary degeneracy and (ii) how, in contrast to bosons or fermions, invertible phases of parafermions can form a non-Abelian group under stacking. For order-$N$ parafermions with $N$ a prime power, we define representative Hamiltonians realizing invertible topological phases with $\mathbb{Z}^{3}_{N}$ symmetry. We show that under stacking they generate a non-Abelian group of order $2N^3$. When $N$ is an odd prime, this group is $\mathrm{Heis}(\mathbb{Z}_N)\rtimes \mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}$, where $\mathrm{Heis}(\mathbb{Z}_N)$ is the Heisenberg group of order $N^{3}$.

</details>


### [5] [Anyon Proliferation at Self-Triality: From a Lattice Model to Adjoint QCD$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17724)
*Yuxuan Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究自对偶（self-trial）$\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论中三类任意子（anyons）的竞争性增殖如何驱动对称性破缺并保留部分拓扑序（topological order），并提出晶格模型与两种对偶连续场论描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性局域序参量通常导致一阶的三态 Potts 转变；本文探索任意子增殖机制是否能产生连续的 $S_3$ 对称性破缺转变，同时保留残余拓扑序，从而区别于传统图像。

Method: 构造蜂窝颜色码（honeycomb color code）的形变自旋模型，具有置换竞争任意子的 $S_3$ 对称性、时间反演（time reversal）与费米型 $\mathbb{Z}_2^{(1)}$ 一形式对称性（one-form symmetry）；并给出玻色与费米两种对偶连续描述：$\mathrm{PSU}(3)_3\times\mathrm{Spin}(8)_{-1}$ Chern-Simons-Higgs 理论（实 Higgs 场处于 $(\mathbf{8}_{\mathrm{adj}},\mathbf{8}_v)$ 表示），以及经费米-玻色对偶（fermion-boson duality）得到的 $\mathrm{PSU}(3)_{3/2}$ QCD$_3$（单个伴随 Majorana 费米子、费米子宇称规范化）。

Result: 两种连续描述与晶格模型的幺正对称性及所提相图（phase diagram）一致，将双环面码序（double-toric-code order）连接到具有残余环面码序（toric-code order）的对称破缺相；转变分别由 Higgs 凝聚（Higgs condensation）与 Majorana 质量变号描述，并在临界点恢复晶格时间反演。

Conclusion: 该与伴随 QCD$_3$ 的联系提示：竞争任意子间可能存在连续的 $S_3$ 破缺转变，与竞争局域序参量所导致的传统一阶三态 Potts 转变形成对比。

Abstract: We study how competitive proliferation among three types of anyons in a self-trial $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory can drive symmetry breaking while preserving part of the topological order. We construct a deformation of the honeycomb color code, a spin model rooted in quantum error correction, with an $S_3$ symmetry that permutes the competing anyons, together with time-reversal and fermionic $\mathbb{Z}_2^{(1)}$ one-form symmetries. We propose two dual continuum descriptions of this transition. The bosonic description is a $\mathrm{PSU}(3)_3\times\mathrm{Spin}(8)_{-1}$ Chern-Simons-Higgs theory with a real Higgs field in the $(\mathbf{8}_{\mathrm{adj}},\mathbf{8}_v)$ representation. Fermion-boson duality gives a particularly simple fermionic dual: $\mathrm{PSU}(3)_{3/2}$ QCD$_3$ with a single adjoint Majorana fermion and gauged fermion parity. Both descriptions match the lattice model's unitary symmetries and proposed phase diagram, connecting double-toric-code order to a symmetry-broken phase with residual toric-code order. The transition is described by Higgs condensation and by a Majorana mass changing sign, respectively. The continuum theories are proposed to recover lattice time reversal at criticality. This connection to adjoint QCD$_3$ points to a possible continuous $S_3$-breaking transition from competing anyons, in contrast to the conventional first-order three-state Potts transition from competing local order parameters.

</details>


### [6] [Evaluate Laughlin spectral gap lower bound in vortexable bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17871)
*Yuchen Liu,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文为涡旋化陈带（vortexable Chern bands）中的 Laughlin 母体哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonians）导出了一个可计算且与粒子数无关的谱隙（spectral gap）下界，证明在一定空间调制下仍可严格为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋化陈带虽存在精确的 Laughlin 型零模（Laughlin-type zero modes），但零模存在并不保证有限的激发能隙，因此需要严格的能隙判据来评估分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators）的稳定性。

Method: 研究针对一类由最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level）波函数乘以空间变化包络（envelope）得到的涡旋化带，构造辅助的费米子损失（fermion-loss）方案以控制轨道正交化误差，从而导出仅依赖两个量的下界：投影轨道归一化（projected orbital normalizations）的展宽，以及加权局域配对排斥（weighted local pair repulsion）的下界。

Result: 误差相对于相互作用能一致有界且不随粒子数增长；朗道能级平均（Landau-level averaging）可使能隙下界在显著空间调制乃至含节线（nodal lines）的包络下仍严格为正；分析进一步推广至多分量轨道纹理（multicomponent orbital textures）与涡旋坐标形变（deformations of the vortex coordinate）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 Laughlin 母体哈密顿量的严格能隙判据，为理解涡旋化带中分数陈绝缘体的稳定性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Vortexable Chern bands admit exact Laughlin-type zero modes of local parent interactions, but their existence alone does not guarantee a finite excitation gap. Here, we derive a computable, particle-number-independent lower bound on the spectral gap of Laughlin parent Hamiltonians in a class of vortexable bands obtained by multiplying lowest-Landau-level wavefunctions by a spatially varying envelope. The bound depends only on two quantities: the spread of projected orbital normalizations and a lower bound on the weighted local pair repulsion, both determined by one- and two-particle operators. The proof employs an auxiliary fermion-loss construction to control orbital orthogonalization, with an error bounded relative to the interaction energy uniformly in particle number. We demonstrate that Landau-level averaging permits strictly positive gap bounds even under substantial spatial modulation, including envelopes with nodal lines. Beyond the scalar-envelope construction, we extend the analysis to multicomponent orbital textures and deformations of the vortex coordinate. These results establish rigorous gap criteria for Laughlin parent Hamiltonians, providing a foundation for understanding the stability of fractional Chern insulators in vortexable bands.

</details>


### [7] [Classical weak antilocalization in electron hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17927)
*Alberto Cortijo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在电子流体动力学（electron hydrodynamics）中弱局域化（weak localization）被强烈抑制，但动量弛豫率（momentum-relaxation rate）的空间涨落仍可通过横向动量的扩散回返产生经典记忆核（memory kernel），导致对流体动力学电阻率的对数修正，且符号为反局域化（antilocalizing）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子流体中频繁的电子-电子碰撞会快速退相位（dephase）Cooperon，使弱局域化被抑制；但无序是否仍能产生对数修正、其机制与符号如何，尚需澄清。

Method: 指出动量弛豫率的空间涨落通过横向动量的扩散回返生成经典记忆核；二维下该回返呈对数形式，只涉及单个剪切传播子（shear propagator）而非量子干涉；并借助守恒律与耗散正定性确定其符号。

Result: 该回返减小动量弛豫从而降低体电阻率，呈反局域化符号；与量子弱局域化不同，对数由动量弛豫截断且振幅依赖黏度（viscosity）；对温度无关的杂质摩擦，修正在费米液体（Fermi liquid）中为 $-T^2\ln(T_ξ/T)$，在普朗克金属（Planckian metal）中为 $-T\ln(T_ξ/T)$。

Conclusion: 该经典回返机制构成流体动力学区中区别于量子弱局域化的对数电阻率修正；在通道（channel）几何中它与黏度重整化（viscosity renormalization）相互竞争，可通过宽度扫描分离两种效应。

Abstract: Weak localization is strongly suppressed in electron hydrodynamics, where frequent electron--electron collisions rapidly dephase the Cooperon. Nevertheless, disorder can still produce a logarithmic correction to the bare hydrodynamic resistivity. We show that spatial fluctuations of the momentum-relaxation rate generate a classical memory kernel through the diffusive return of transverse momentum. In two dimensions, this return is logarithmic, as in weak localization, but involves a single shear propagator rather than quantum interference. Conservation laws and positivity of dissipation fix its antilocalizing sign: the return reduces momentum relaxation and hence the bulk resistivity. Unlike quantum weak localization, the logarithm is cut off by momentum relaxation and has a viscosity-dependent amplitude. For temperature-independent impurity friction, the resistivity correction scales as $-T^2\ln(T_ξ/T)$ in a Fermi liquid and $-T\ln(T_ξ/T)$ in a Planckian metal. In channels, the return competes with viscosity renormalization, and a width scan separates the two effects.

</details>


### [8] [Charge and spin orders of the generalized Wigner crystal phases at fractional fillings in twisted MoTe$_2$: unrestricted Hartree-Fock and variational Monte Carlo studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18029)
*Arindam Pramanik,Andrzej Biborski,Michał Zegrodnik*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在扭转双层 MoTe$_2$ 的多个分数填充处观测到相互作用驱动的绝缘态，其中多数被认为是三角莫尔晶格（triangular moiré lattice）背景下的广义 Wigner 晶体，但其电荷与自旋序的具体性质尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示 4/3 与 3/2 填充绝缘态的微观序，并考察扭转角（twist angle）与位移场（displacement field）的调控作用。

Method: 采用无限制 Hartree-Fock（HF）与变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）两种方法，在多种扭转角与外加强度位移场下计算 4/3 和 3/2 空穴填充态；通过比较 VP 与层间/谷间相干（intervalley-coherent, IVC）相的关联能，并结合 Jastrow 因子与 Gutzwiller 因子分解分析非局域与局域关联的贡献。

Result: 4/3 填充在低扭转角下基态为谷极化（VP）相，电荷离域较弱、接近经典极限，位移场诱导层极化并进一步抑制电荷涨落。3/2 填充的 VP 相表现出双组分 GWC 特征，且在有限位移场下尤为稳健；增大扭转角则驱动系统进入以涨落为主导的 IVC 相。VMC 分析表明 VP 相关能可忽略（与其经典性质一致），而 IVC 相获得显著关联能增益，主要由 Jastrow 因子刻画的长程非局域关联贡献，Gutzwiller 局域因子作用较小。

Conclusion: 4/3 与 3/2 填充绝缘态的序参量强烈依赖于填充、扭转角与位移场：低扭转角下呈现近经典的 VP 广义 Wigner 晶体，位移场增强其层极化与稳健性，而增大扭转角促发涨落主导的 IVC 相。关联能分析进一步区分数类相的物理机制，为非局域关联在 IVC 相中的决定性作用提供依据。

Abstract: Recent experiments have revealed interaction-driven insulating states at various fractional fillings of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$. Many of these insulating states are believed to be generalized Wigner crystals (GWC) condensed in the background of triangular moir$\acute{e}$ lattice. In this work, we focus on revealing the charge and spin order of the insulating states at 4/3 and 3/2 hole fillings over a range of twist angles and applied displacement fields using unrestricted Hartree-Fock (HF) and variational Monte Carlo (VMC) methods. We find that, at 4/3 filling for low twist angles the charge ordered ground state is valley-polarized (VP), characterized by weak charge delocalization approaching the classical limit. Application of the displacement field induces layer-polarization and suppresses the charge fluctuations even further. In contrast, at 3/2 filling, the VP phase exhibits signatures of a two-component GWC phase, which is particularly robust at finite displacement field. Additionally, by increasing the twist angle one pushes the system into an intervalley-coherent (IVC) phase, which is found to be fluctuation-dominated. Our VMC-based analysis shows that the correlation energy is negligible in the VP phase, which is consistent with its classical nature. However, the IVC phase shows significant gain in correlation energy, which is dominated by the nonlocal correlations captured by the Jastrow factors, while the local Gutzwiller factors play a minor role.

Weakly coupled networks of both chain types therefore provide an ideal platform for exploring spinon-magnon coupling and the hybridization of sharp magnon modes with spinon continua. Here we investigate the spin-excitation spectrum of the frustrated quantum magnet rouaite, Cu$_2$(NO$_3$)(OH)$_3$ --- a model realization of weakly coupled ferro- and antiferromagnetic Cu$^{2+}$ spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ chains. Neutron spectroscopy reveals a spectrum of well-defined spin waves associated with both types of chains, with clear signatures of hybridization but no observable spinon continuum. Certain magnon branches resulting from this hybridization exhibit substantial broadening near the zone center. We attribute this to vestiges of spinon fractionalization persisting partway through the dimensional crossover from isolated quantum spin chains to a 2D network of frustrated spins.

Abstract: In altermagnets, the exchange symmetry of two mutually compensating magnetic sublattices permits spin-polarized electronic bands and chirality-split magnon bands. Abstract: The nonlinear Hall effect (NLHE) is a novel transport phenomena and shows great potential applications of future electronics.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 第一种基于单粒子轨道的方案仅适用于非相互作用态；第二种基于量子流体涌现性质的方案同样适用于相互作用态，因而成为椭圆情形下边界自旋的合适定义。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对具有非平凡形状的 Laughlin 流体亦可以定义边界自旋，支持了边界自旋由拓扑而非几何对称性决定的观点。

Yet, the value of the edge spin is not dictated by symmetry, but by topology: it is expected that the specific shape of the edge should not matter. In this work, we therefore generalize the edge spin to elliptically shaped droplets. We consider two different generalizations of the edge spin and test them on a family of elliptic Laughlin states introduced in the present work. The first, most straightforward, generalization is based on the properties of the single-particle orbitals for the elliptic confining potential. We show that this proposal applies to non-interacting states. The second proposal is based on the emergent properties of the quantum fluid. We show that this proposal also applies to interacting states, making it the suitable definition of the edge spin in the elliptic case. Our work demonstrates that it is possible to define an edge spin for Laughlin fluids with a non-trivial shape.

</details>

Motivation: 量子模拟所用寄存器（register）远小于其背后的电子问题，二者之间的映射直接决定了测量所得关联量的物理含义；因此需要量化不同编码方案在该映射中的误差表现，并检验有限量子硬件能否可靠解析激子转移中的布居与相干信息。

Conclusion: 编码映射的选择显著决定可测关联量的可信度，极化修饰编码在误差上优于裸乘积态编码；有限规模、含噪的量子线路可分辨激子转移的布居与相干特征，且自适应谱编码能以可量化的维度成本达到既定精度。

Abstract: A quantum simulation of a material runs on a register much smaller than the electronic problem behind it, and the map between the two decides what a measured correlation means. Abstract: Matching an excitation centroid does not establish the resolved structure of a correlated spin--orbit multiplet. Independent BAGEL calculations isolate contraction dependence in a three-state spin-free model: on the same reference, replacing SS--SR with MS--MR reduces the symmetry-defined E-block splitting from 0.448~meV to $4.67\times10^{-5}$~meV. Native reconstruction, reference symmetry, representation invariance, sensitivity controls and comparison with fitted spectral features or magnetic response provide an ordered validation procedure whose tests constrain different properties of the Hamiltonian.

Abstract: Long-range interactions, from the Coulomb repulsion between electrons to the power-law interactions of trapped ions and Rydberg atoms, are widespread in quantum many-body physics. In TMC, an interaction is written as a function of the transfer matrix, and one set of contraction rules then yields the effective Hamiltonians of DMRG and VUMPS, the tangent-space excitation action, and the energy variance, without an MPO. Long-range interactions thus enter with their prescribed distance dependence. For the half-filled lattice Coulomb chain with a $1/d$ interaction of strength $V$, theory predicts CDW order for all $V>0$, but at weak coupling this order is too weak for earlier numerics to resolve. Using TMC, we obtain infinite-MPS ground states at $V=2$ directly in the thermodynamic limit, with an energy variance per site of $2\times10^{-10}$. Our data, extrapolated to zero energy variance, indicate a small nonzero CDW amplitude, $m_0=0.002213(14)$, consistent with this prediction. The complete $V=2$ optimization workflow across the full series of bond dimensions takes less than a day on a single AMD MI250X GPU die. For an exactly solvable long-range hopping model, TMC reproduces the nonanalytic $|k|$ cusp of the one-particle dispersion that any fixed finite sum of decaying exponentials rounds off. We also use TMC to optimize first-quantized MPS directly in the thermodynamic limit. For half-filled free fermions, our entanglement entropy data show scaling consistent with central charge $c=1$ over the studied bond dimensions, as for second-quantized MPS.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce an approach to solving locally ordered quantum spin systems using neural network quantum states. First, our approach rests on choosing a spin basis constructed from the magnetic texture of a classical or quantum reference state. Second, the method improves convergence and suppresses parameter-space drift through a regularized Riemannian gradient descent adapted to the quantum state geometry of the variational state manifold. Combining these techniques enables stable ground-state calculations for two- and three-dimensional chiral spin systems with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions. The resulting variational ground-state energies are competitive with those of high-fidelity density matrix renormalization group calculations and, for periodic and layered geometries, provide significantly tighter upper bounds on the ground-state energy.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: gPOZ 方法可给出2-团簇 FQH 波函数的结构化分类，并表明无需3-团簇结构即可实现 Fibonacci 统计；所构造的4-体理想哈密顿量基态为 $(\tfrac14,\tfrac32,\tfrac{7}{10})$ 态，为相关拓扑有序态提供了可行的模型实现途径。

Abstract: Fibonacci anyons support universal topological quantum computation. They have been proposed to emerge in fractional quantum Hall (FQH) systems, such as the filling-fraction $ν=\tfrac{2}{3}$ state described by an effective $SU(3)_2$ Chern-Simons theory, and the $ν=\tfrac{2}{5}$ state with a $3$-cluster structure (an $n$-cluster structure means that $n$-electron bound states form a Laughlin-type wave function of the form $(z^{(n)}_i-z^{(n)}_j)^m$). In this work, we systematically classify FQH wave functions $P(z_1,\ldots,z_N)$ in the $N\rightarrow\infty$ limit, through a generalized pattern of zeros (gPOZ) approach, where the wave function is determined by the leading order of zero along with the accompanying leading polynomial that develops as successive number of $z_i$'s are brought together. Within this approach, we obtain a $(ν,h,c)$-classification of wave functions with a $2$-cluster (pair) structure. Among these wave functions, those with $(h,c) = (\tfrac12,\tfrac12)$, $(\tfrac32,\tfrac{7}{10})$, $(3,\tfrac45)$ correspond to gapped FQH states with filling fraction $ν$ and edge-state chiral central charge $c+1$, while others with $c<0$ may be gapless. We find that even a $2$-cluster structure can give rise to Fibonacci statistics, for $(ν,h,c) = (\tfrac14,\tfrac32,\tfrac{7}{10})$, corresponding to the $(5,4)$ minimal model, and for $(ν,h,c) = (\tfrac17,3,\tfrac45)$, corresponding to the $(6,5)$ minimal model. We further construct an ideal $4$-body Hamiltonian whose ground state is the $(\tfrac14,\tfrac32,\tfrac{7}{10})$ state.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅考虑支撑一致离散（uniformly discrete）且原子等质量的晶体测度，排除了许多重要情形；本文旨在推广到更一般的平移有界晶体测度框架。

Method: 将扰动建模为随机测度（random measure）而非随机集，从而同时涵盖支撑扰动与原子质量扰动；并借助傅里叶分析（Fourier analysis）的基本技巧处理衍射。

Conclusion: 研究将恢复性结果从等质量、一致离散支撑的情形推广至一般平移有界晶体测度，随机测度框架统一处理支撑与质量扰动，并给出衍射测度的显式表达。

Abstract: In this paper, we study random perturbations and the diffraction properties of translation-bounded crystalline measures. Under a non-vanishing assumption on the known perturbation factor, we prove that, almost surely, any realization of the perturbed measure preserves sufficient structural information to allow the full recovery of the original measure. Previous works have only considered crystalline measures with uniformly discrete supports and equal-mass atoms. Our general setting includes many important crystalline measures that were excluded until now. To treat this broader setting, we adopt a different framework. In this work, we model the perturbation as a random measure rather than as a random set. In particular, this leads us to consider not only perturbations of the support, but also perturbations of the atoms' masses. On the other hand, using elementary techniques of Fourier analysis, we prove that any translation-bounded crystalline measure has a diffraction measure, and we explicitly compute its diffraction.

Abstract: Motivated by power-law dependences on the expansion scale in holographic dark-energy constructions, we study the Hubble-running equation of state $w_{\rm DE}=w_0(H/H_0)^q$ (HoloEoS), where $H$ is the Hubble expansion rate, $H_0$ and $w_0$ are the present values of $H$ and $w_{\rm DE}$, and $q$ is a constant dimensionless exponent controlling the running. For regular expanding histories with standard matter, we reconstruct the potential $V(φ)$ of its nondynamical auxiliary scalar field, providing a Lagrangian description of both the homogeneous background and cosmological perturbations. We confront this VCDM realization with SPT, Planck, and ACT CMB data, updated DESI DR2 distances, and optionally DESY5 supernovae with Dovekie recalibration, comparing it with $Λ$CDM and the Chevallier--Polarski--Linder parameterization embedded in the same gravitational framework (CPL-VCDM). For the full data combination, the posterior medians and central $68\%$ credible intervals are $w_0=-0.881^{+0.040}_{-0.038}$ and $q=0.453^{+0.143}_{-0.133}$, with a crossing redshift $z_\times=0.476^{+0.106}_{-0.081}$ conditional on $0<z_\times<6$. For this data combination, physical-likelihood optimization by Metropolis--Hastings (MH) simulated annealing gives Akaike information criterion differences of $Δ\mathrm{AIC}\simeq-3.40$ for HoloEoS and $-4.10$ for CPL-VCDM relative to $Λ$CDM. Thus, a Hubble-scale-motivated dark-energy history realized within a specified gravitational theory achieves fitting performance comparable to CPL-VCDM while naturally allowing phantom-divide crossing.

Local likelihood optimization gives an Akaike information criterion difference $Δ{\rm AIC}\simeq-10.87$ for the zero-vacuum model relative to flat $Λ$CDM. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶关联函数（higher order correlation functions）的树图贡献随外腿数增加而迅速复杂化，缺乏系统分类与振幅间关系的统一框架，且其在超慢滚（ultra-slow-roll）等非平庸背景下的符号性质尚待澄清。

Result: 任意树图振幅可因子化为原初振幅（primary amplitudes）之积，从而给出包括 Suyama-Yamaguchi 等式在内的高阶关联函数关系的通用框架；树拓扑数目随外腿数指数增长，而代数上不同的振幅乘积数目增长显著更慢。无势阶跃转变下得到全阶顶点振幅的显式表达并证明其正性及对转变锐度的依赖；含势阶跃时，向上阶跃下所有非线性顶点振幅保持为正，向下阶跃则在特定参数值处出现抵消。

Abstract: We study higher order correlation functions in single field inflation using the $δN$ formalism and methods from graph theory. By representing connected tree level contributions as graphs, we systematically classify their independent topologies, momentum shapes and amplitudes. We show that the amplitudes of arbitrary trees factorize into products of primary amplitudes, providing a general framework for relations among higher order correlators, including the Suyama-Yamaguchi equality. As the number of external legs increases, the number of distinct tree topologies grows exponentially, whereas the number of algebraically distinct amplitude products grows considerably more slowly. As an application, we consider ultra-slow-roll inflation followed by a transition to a slow-roll attractor. For transitions without a potential step, we derive an explicit all-order expression for the vertex amplitudes and establish their positivity and dependence on the sharpness of the transition. When a potential step is included, we prove that all nonlinear vertex amplitudes remain positive for upward steps, whereas downward steps allow cancellations at specific parameter values. These cancellations can eliminate entire families of tree contributions, including configurations in which the leading local bispectrum and trispectrum vanish simultaneously while higher order correlations remain nonzero.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期通过星系四点相关函数（4PCF）观测宇宙学宇称破缺（PV）的研究，需要从理论上仔细检验可能的系统性来源，以判断探测信号是否可靠。

Method: 在标准欧拉微扰理论（EPT）三阶框架下结合偏袒示踪剂展开与暗物质引力动力学，分析相对论性星系数密度；并将已有三谱宇称奇模式结果推广至局部视线定义（宽角效应）、潮汐偏袒及任意宇宙学背景，再用球谐函数展开4PCF。

Result: 由Wigner 3-j符号编码的角动量选择定则使得在平行近似与完整宽角情形下，经全部视线方向积分后仅宇称偶贡献存活，宇称奇项消失。

Conclusion: 结果支持将可能的PV探测解释为并非源于RSD系统性伪迹，对使用迷向估计量的大尺度统计分析具有重要意义；文中亦讨论了结论的注意事项并指出PV的可能来源。

Abstract: Recent observational investigation of cosmological Parity Violation (PV) through the galaxy Four-point Correlation Function (4PCF) has motivated a careful theoretical examination of potential systematic contributions. In this work, we demonstrate that parity-odd contributions to the isotropic 4PCF estimator induced by Redshift Space Distortion (RSD), up to the leading order $\mathcal{H}/k$ of general relativistic corrections, vanish upon averaging over all the Line-of-Sight (LOS). We analyse the perturbative relativistic galaxy number density, combining the biased tracer expansion and the dark matter gravitational dynamics within the framework of Standard Eulerian Perturbation Theory (EPT) to third order in the density perturbation. Building on prior studies of the parity-odd mode of the trispectrum (the Fourier-space counterpart of the 4PCF), we extend existing results in three directions: incorporating the wide-angle effect by adopting a local LOS definition for each galaxy in the 4PCF, accounting for tidal bias in the galaxy population, and generalising the assessment to an arbitrary cosmological background. Expanding the 4PCF in spherical harmonics, we show that angular momentum selection rules, encoded in the Wigner 3-j symbols, enforce that only parity-even contributions survive the integration over all LOS orientations, both in the plane-parallel approximation and in the full wide-angle case. This result is crucial for large-scale statistical analyses using isotropic estimators. It supports interpreting possible PV detections as a signal not due to a systematic RSD artefact. We conclude with a discussion of the caveats of our results and identify potential sources of PV.

</details>


### [23] [Does Dark Energy Evolve $\textit{and}$ Cluster?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18557)
*Maria Luisa Gallavotti,Alessandra Silvestri*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙学观测重新激发了对动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）的兴趣，由此自然引出问题：偏离标准膨胀历史是否伴随引力动力学（gravitational dynamics）的修改。

Method: 在不施加模型特定部门间关系的前提下，对膨胀中的有效暗能量贡献与主导聚类和透镜（clustering and lensing）的引力响应进行联合非参数重构（non-parametric reconstruction），并辅以组合协方差模（covariance-mode）分析；数据使用 CMB、DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+ 超新星，随后加入 DES Year 3 6×2pt 及巡天等大尺度结构观测。

Result: 恢复出 z≈0.6–0.7 附近有效暗能量密度的宽极大值；引力响应相对广义相对论（GR）值呈现暗示性低红移增强，且与背景特征大致重合。加入大尺度结构信息后，背景特征得以保留，但更倾向于略减弱的透镜响应。协方差模分析识别出当前观测约束最紧的模组合，其领先模决定性由背景主导，引力部门约束精度较低。

Conclusion: 现有数据尚不能判定两个部门中暗示性低红移特征是否具有共同物理起源，该问题有待正在进行的 Stage-IV 巡天（Stage-IV surveys）加以解答。

Abstract: Recent cosmological observations have renewed interest in dynamical dark energy, and naturally raise the question of whether departures from the standard expansion history are accompanied by modifications of gravitational dynamics. We address this question through a joint, non-parametric reconstruction of the effective dark-energy contribution to the expansion and the gravitational responses governing clustering and lensing, complemented by a combined covariance-mode analysis, without imposing model-specific relations between the sectors. Combining CMB, DESI DR2 BAO, and Pantheon+ supernova observations, and subsequently adding large scale structure observations including DES Year 3 $6\times2$pt data and other surveys, we recover a broad maximum in the effective dark-energy density near $z\simeq0.6$--$0.7$. The gravitational responses exhibit suggestive low-redshift enhancements over their GR values, approximately coincident with the background feature. Adding large scale structure information preserves the background feature but favours a mildly reduced lensing response. The combined covariance-mode analysis identifies the combinations of background and gravitational responses most tightly constrained by current observations and quantifies their departures from $Λ$CDM. The leading modes are decisively background dominated, while the gravitational sector remains less precisely constrained. Thus, present data cannot yet determine whether the suggestive low-redshift features in the two sectors share a common physical origin, a question that ongoing Stage-IV surveys will be able to address

</details>


### [24] [Reconstruction of Dynamical Dark Energy and Gravity from latest cosmological data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18558)
*Maria Luisa Gallavotti,Alessandra Silvestri*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文对有效暗能量密度（effective dark energy density）Ω_X(a) 与引力响应函数（gravitational response functions）μ(a)、Σ(a) 进行联合非参数重建（non-parametric reconstruction），发现 Ω_X 在 z≈0.5–0.7 存在持续极大值及有效幻影分界（phantom divide）穿越，而引力动力学偏离显著性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙学观测重新激起对非标准晚期演化（non-standard late-time evolution）的兴趣，本文考察偏离标准膨胀历史是否伴随引力动力学（gravitational dynamics）的修改。

Method: 联合 CMB、DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+ 与大尺度结构（large scale structure, LSS）观测（含 DES Year 3 6×2pt 数据），在有/无源自稳定 Horndeski 模型的理论相关先验（correlation prior）两种情形下，对 Ω_X(a)、μ(a)、Σ(a) 做非参数重建。

Result: Ω_X 在 z≈0.5–0.7 存在持续极大值，对应有效幻影分界的穿越，该特征在 CMB+BAO 组合中已出现，且在允许成团与透镜修改时仍稳健；μ 与 Σ 在相近红移范围呈提示性增强但显著性较低；纳入 LSS 信息后反而倾向于减弱的透镜响应 Σ<1，而背景特征基本不变。

Conclusion: 当前对 ΛCDM 的偏离在膨胀历史中最为显著；在广义 Brans-Dicke 理论（Generalized Brans-Dicke theories）框架下的解释表明有效引力耦合修改与标量媒介第五力（fifth force）可能贡献于上述趋势，引力动力学的相关修改仍有待未来 Stage IV 巡天检验。

Abstract: Recent cosmological observations have renewed interest in non-standard late-time evolution. We investigate whether departures from the standard expansion history are accompanied by modifications of gravitational dynamics through a joint, non-parametric reconstruction of the effective dark energy density, $Ω_{\rm X}(a)$, and the gravitational response functions $μ(a)$ and $Σ(a)$. Combining CMB, DESI DR2 BAO, Pantheon+, and large scale structure observations, including DES Year 3 $6\times2$pt data, we perform reconstructions with and without a theory motivated correlation prior derived from stable Horndeski models. We find a persistent maximum in $Ω_{\rm X}$ at $z\simeq0.5$--$0.7$, corresponding to a crossing of the effective phantom divide. This feature is already present in the CMB+BAO combination and remains robust when modifications to clustering and lensing are allowed. The reconstructed gravitational response exhibits suggestive enhancements in $μ$ and $Σ$ over approximately the same redshift range, although these departures are less significant. Including LSS information instead favours a reduced lensing response, $Σ<1$, while leaving the background feature largely unchanged. An interpretation within Generalized Brans-Dicke theories illustrates how modifications to the effective gravitational coupling and a scalar-mediated fifth force may contribute to these trends. Our results indicate that current departures from $Λ$CDM are most significant in the expansion history, while leaving open the possibility of correlated modifications to gravitational dynamics that future Stage IV surveys can test.

</details>


### [25] [Full-shape analysis of the Lyman-alpha forest power spectrum and bispectrum: validation on simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17405)
*Roger de Belsunce,James M. Sullivan,Mikhail M. Ivanov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一套联合全形状（full-shape）的有效场论（EFT）建模框架，将莱曼-α森林（LyA forest）自功率谱、其与暗物质晕（作为高红移类星体代理）的互相关谱以及森林双谱结合，用于从DESI高红移光谱数据中提取宇宙学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性氢的莱曼-α森林（LyA forest）可通过高红移光学光谱提供大体积、高分辨率的宇宙学涨落图，为充分利用正在运行的暗能量光谱仪（DESI）数据，需要一套可靠的多统计量联合建模理论框架。

Method: 采用有效场论（EFT）方法，联合建模莱曼-α森林的一圈功率谱（FF）、森林与暗物质晕互相关的一圈功率谱（FQ）以及森林的树级双谱（FFF），并在红移z=2.5的AbacusSummit N体模拟基础上生成的理想化大体积森林模拟数据中进行验证。功率谱拟合上限取k_max^FF(FQ)=0.6(0.3) h Mpc^-1，双谱取k_max^FFF=0.1 h Mpc^-1；双谱的关键作用在于打破平均通量与功率谱振幅之间的莱曼-α特定简并。

Result: 在V=8 h^-3 Gpc^3模拟体积、通量场完全采样条件下，全形状分析给出无偏的宇宙学约束：哈勃常数H_0精度0.6%、物质密度Ω_m精度1.6%、物质成团振幅σ_8精度3.5%；加入双谱使σ_8约束改善28%。进一步纳入类星体一圈功率谱（QQ）与树级双谱（QQQ）（k_max=0.2、0.08 h Mpc^-1）可使宇宙学约束再改善约35%。

Conclusion: 该理论框架可支持对DESI及其后继项目DESI-II高红移数据的全形状分析，从而加深对宇宙物质主导时期结构增长的理解。

Abstract: The Lyman-$α$ (LyA) forest of neutral hydrogen provides a large-volume, high-resolution map of cosmological fluctuations from optical spectra at high redshift. To extract more information from the currently observing Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), we derive an effective field theory (EFT) model for joint full-shape analyses of the LyA forest data. It combines the one-loop power spectrum of the LyA forest (FF), and of its cross-correlation with dark-matter halos, used as proxies for high-redshift quasars (FQ), with the tree-level bispectrum of the LyA forest (FFF). Importantly, the bispectrum breaks the LyA-specific degeneracy between the mean flux and the amplitude of the power spectrum. We validate our theory framework on idealized large-volume LyA forest mocks painted onto the AbacusSummit N-body simulation suite at redshift z=2.5. Fitting scales up to $k_\mathrm{max}^{FF\,(FQ)}=0.6\,(0.3)\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ in the power spectrum and $k_\mathrm{max}^{FFF}=0.1\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ in the bispectrum, our full-shape analysis yields unbiased constraints on the cosmological parameters in simulations. For a fully sampled flux field in a simulated volume of $V=8\,h^{-3}\,{\rm Gpc}^3$, we measure the Hubble constant, $H_0$, to $0.6\%$, the matter density, $Ω_{\rm m}$, to $1.6\%$ and the amplitude of matter clustering, $σ_8$, to $3.5\%$. Adding the bispectrum improves the constraints on $σ_8$ by $28\%$. We also demonstrate that the inclusion of the quasar one-loop power spectrum (QQ) and tree-level bispectrum (QQQ) further improves cosmological constraints by $\approx 35\%$ using $k_\mathrm{max}^{QQ,\,(QQQ)}=0.2,\,(0.08)\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$. Our theory framework enables full-shape analyses of high-redshift data from DESI and its successor DESI-II, enhancing knowledge of the growth of structure of the Universe during matter-domination.

</details>

*Georges Obied,Cumrun Vafa,David H. Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文检验“渐隐暗区”（Fading Dark Sector, FDS）模型，将DESI DR1全形状星系功率谱（full-shape galaxy power spectrum）纳入拟合，检验其对宇宙结构增长的预言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDS模型源自弦论（string theory），暗区渐隐由标量场驱动，此前已能较好拟合DESI BAO、CMB与SN数据；本文旨在用含结构演化信息的全形状数据作独立一致性检验。

Method: 模型由两个O(1)参数(c, c')刻画暗能量与暗物质密度对场（以普朗克单位计）的对数导数；在CMB+DESI DR2 BAO+DES-Dovekie基础上联合DESI DR1全形状星系功率谱进行参数拟合与模型比较。

Result: 联合数据下，FDS在相同参数数目下优于CPL w0-wa参数化，Δχ²_MAP=-4；相对ΛCDM偏好度为4.1σ；且(c, c')的后验值与先前分析一致。

Conclusion: (c, c')不变而拟合改善，表明FDS对结构增长的预言比w0-wa参数化更好地捕捉了观测到的增长历史。

Abstract: We analyze the Fading Dark Sector (FDS) model, which is motivated by string theory and in which the fading of the dark sector is driven by a scalar field. The model is specified by two $O(1)$ numbers $(c,c')$ that parametrize the logarithmic derivatives of the dark energy and dark matter densities with respect to the field in Planck units. In previous work, we showed that this model provides a good fit to the DESI BAO, CMB, and SN datasets. Here, we extend that analysis to include the DESI DR1 full-shape galaxy power spectrum, which adds information about the time evolution of cosmic structure and provides an independent consistency check of the FDS model. We find that, when the DESI DR1 full-shape data are combined with CMB+DESI DR2 BAO+DES-Dovekie, the FDS model outperforms the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) $w_0$-$w_a$ parameterization by $Δχ^2_{\rm MAP}=-4$ at equal parameter count and is preferred over $Λ$CDM at $4.1σ$. The inferred values of $(c,c')$ with the full-shape data are unchanged from our previous analysis, so this improvement indicates that FDS makes a prediction for the growth of structure that captures the observed growth history better than the $w_0$-$w_a$ parameterization.

</details>

### [27] [Diffusion Tree Sampling for Inference Time Adaptation of Crystal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17462)
*Daniel T. Levy,Vineet Jain,Tara Akhound-Sadegh,Sékou-Oumar Kaba,Siamak Ravanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在推理阶段采用扩散树采样（Diffusion Tree Sampling, DTS）对扩散生成模型进行适配，通过将条件生成模型作为引导提议并扩展至多目标采样，在晶体从头生成任务上实现无需微调的多性质调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将扩散模型的结构先验转化为有前景的候选材料，需同时朝向多个相互冲突的性质引导；微调（fine-tuning）成本高且每个新目标都需重训模型，可微引导（differentiable guidance）又难以适用于许多真实任务。

Method: 在去噪轨迹（denoising trajectory）上执行蒙特卡洛树搜索（Monte Carlo tree search），即DTS，并作两项扩展：一是将已有的性质条件生成模型（如经无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）微调的模型）作为DTS内部的引导提议（guided proposals），且不扭曲目标分布；二是将DTS扩展为多目标采样（multi-objective sampling），使单次搜索即可覆盖整个帕累托前沿（Pareto front）。

Result: 在晶体从头生成（de novo crystal generation）上实例化两项扩展：提升不同模型生成晶体的稳定性，利用预训练条件模型生成属于稀有空间群（rare space groups）或具有高体积模量（bulk modulus）的晶体，并生成沿高介电常数（dielectric constant）与高带隙（bandgap）帕累托前沿的晶体。

Conclusion: 基于DTS的推理阶段适配在这些任务中成功改善了模型输出，且无需修改模型本身，为AI-for-science场景中多目标、多约束的材料生成提供了灵活且低成本的替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have become strong priors over plausible crystal structures, but turning that prior into promising candidates requires steering toward several conflicting properties at once. Fine-tuning is expensive and produces a new model for every new objective, while differentiable guidance is not applicable to many real-world tasks. We instead adapt these generative models at inference time using Diffusion Tree Sampling (DTS), a Monte Carlo tree search procedure over the denoising trajectory. We make two technical contributions on top of DTS that are particularly relevant for AI-for-science applications. First, we show how to use existing property-conditional generative models (e.g., models fine-tuned for classifier-free guidance) as guided proposals inside DTS, without distorting the target distribution. Second, we extend DTS to multi-objective sampling, so that a single search produces samples spanning an entire Pareto front. We instantiate both adaptations on de novo crystal generation: improving the stability of crystals generated by different models, using pretrained conditional models to generate crystals belonging to rare space groups or with high bulk modulus, and generating crystals along a Pareto front of high dielectric constant and high bandgap materials. Across these tasks, DTS-based inference-time adaptation successfully improves model outputs without modifying the models themselves.

</details>


### [28] [A mesh-free deep energy method for phase-field modeling of thermally driven brittle fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17630)
*Han Zhang,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari,Babak Shahbodagh,Mohammad Vahab,Cosmin Anitescu,Timon Rabczuk,Elena Atroshchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多分辨率神经表示（multiresolution neural representations）的无网格深度能量方法（deep energy method），在瞬态热载荷下自发预测裂纹形核、间距及选择性扩展与止裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场热致断裂模拟需解析路径未知的局部裂纹带与厚度随瞬态演化的热边界层；已有神经能量方法须预先布置缺陷，无法预测自发形核。

Method: 用两个各自具备多分辨率编码的网络分别表示位移—相场与瞬态温度，在每次迭代重抽的积分点上估计机械能与后向欧拉（backward Euler）热方程增量泛函；将不可逆性限制于裂纹集、跨载荷步传递优化器状态并在步内衰减学习率以抑制虚假损伤累积。

Result: 两缺口板起裂峰值载荷与独立有限元参考吻合良好；淬火玻璃带裂纹路径由直变振荡再分叉，振荡阈值接近线性稳定性预测；淬火陶瓷板形成与实验类似的裂纹阵列，间距接近已发表基准，且板与环形体上阵列比同材料场有限元结果更密。

Conclusion: 全程无需预设裂纹路径、形核位置或裂纹自适应网格，即可再现热冲击下的自发裂纹形核、间距选择与扩展—止裂行为。

Abstract: Phase-field simulation of thermally driven fracture requires resolving localized crack bands whose paths are unknown in advance and thermal boundary layers whose thickness evolves during the transient. Neural energy-based computations of thermal fracture have so far started from defects placed in advance. This paper proposes a mesh-free deep energy method based on multiresolution neural representations that predicts spontaneous crack nucleation, crack spacing, and selective crack propagation and arrest under transient thermal loading. Two networks, each with its own multiresolution encoding, represent the displacement and phase fields and the transient temperature, and the mechanical energy and the incremental functional of the backward Euler heat equation are estimated on integration points redrawn at every iteration. Restricting irreversibility to the crack set, carrying the optimizer state across load steps and decaying the learning rates within each step help prevent spurious damage accumulation. On two notched plates the initiation peak loads agree closely with an independent finite element reference. On a quenched glass strip under an analytical temperature field the crack path changes from straight to oscillatory and then to branched, with an oscillation threshold close to the linear stability prediction. Under a prescribed boundary temperature a quenched ceramic slab forms a crack array of the type observed in experiment, with a spacing close to that of a published benchmark. On the slab and a quenched annulus the arrays are denser than those of a finite element computation on the same material field. No crack paths, nucleation sites or crack-adapted meshes are prescribed.

</details>

### [29] [Theoretical exploration of ambient-pressure superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate: A strategy of atomic substitution for tuning chemical pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17709)
*Tomo-Hiko Hamada,Kensei Ushio,Shu Kamiyama,Kazuhiko Kuroki,Hirofumi Sakakibara*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过第一性原理与多体计算，提出在 La₃Ni₂O₇ 镧位进行 Ba 化学替代（chemical substitution）施加化学压力，以抑制削弱超导的八面体畸变，并在 x ~ 0.15 时预测出现 s± 波超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索 La₃Ni₂O₇ 在常压（ambient pressure）下实现超导的可能性，并解决八面体畸变（octahedral distortions）对超导性的抑制问题。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）与多体计算（many-body calculations），以 Ba 替代镧系位点施加化学压力；通过声子计算（phonon calculations）检验结构动力学稳定性，并对由稳定结构导出的有效模型哈密顿量（effective model Hamiltonian）应用涨落交换近似（fluctuation exchange approximation）计算 Eliashberg 本征值 λ。

Result: 声子计算表明，在小掺杂比 x ~ 0.15 时 La₃₋ₓBaₓNi₂O₇ 的高对称四方相（high-symmetry tetragonal phase）动力学稳定；基于 λ 评估的相对超导转变温度（T_c）显示 s± 波超导（s±-wave superconductivity）很可能出现。

Abstract: We investigate constraints imposed by four-dimensional unitarity (recently formalised as graded unitarity) on possible simply-laced affine Kac--Moody vertex algebras arising from four dimensions via the SCFT/VOA correspondence. General ideas in the AdS/CFT correspondence suggest that the entanglement between the two copies should lead to some form of connectivity between the two geometries---a kind of wormhole. We use von Neumann algebra methods in the SYM theory to diagnose the possible existence of such wormholes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观因果性在任意态（包括自发破坏洛伦兹不变性的态）中都成立，要求推迟格林函数（retarded Green's function）的极点在整个复平面上满足 $\mathrm{Im}\,\omega \le |\mathrm{Im}\,k|$；本文旨在沿Heller、Serantes、Spaliński与Withers的工作，系统研究该条件对低 $k$ 线性色散关系的后果。

Abstract: Microcausality holds in any state, including those that spontaneously break Lorentz invariance, and requires the poles (and other singularities) of the retarded Green's function to satisfy $\mathrm{Im}\,ω\le |\mathrm{Im}\,k|$ throughout the complex plane. The optimal constraints are characterised by an infinite hierarchy of Toeplitz determinants. We derive analogous bounds on $ω^2(k)$, whose radius of convergence $\tilde R$ marks the scale at which new states must appear. The bounds shrink to zero as the sound speed $c_s$ approaches 1: for a fixed higher-derivative correction, both $R$ and $\tilde R$ become parametrically smaller than naive dimensional analysis suggests. In particular, in the EFT of Inflation all higher-derivative quadratic operators must vanish as $c_s \to 1$.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知该散射的出射态往往不能在有限激发空间内构造，在二维共形场论（2d CFT）语言中位于希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）的扭曲扇区（twisted sector）；本文旨在从 d=3+1 观察者视角阐明其物理含义。

Abstract: We describe what happens when chiral fermions scatter off magnetic monopoles. It has long been known that, in many situations, the outgoing state cannot be constructed from any finite number of excitations. In the language of 2d CFT, it lives in a twisted sector of the Hilbert space. Here we explain what this means from the perspective of a d=3+1 observer. We show that an ingoing one-particle excitation is scattered into an outgoing state surrounded by a soft cloud which consists of an infinite number of low energy particle-anti-particle pairs. We compute energy and charge correlators, showing that this cloud fragments in any measurement. A detector will register one or more hard particles which leave tracks and carry standard quantum numbers. The remaining exotic quantum numbers live in the soft cloud, and are characterised by large charge fluctuations in the detector

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量型调节器（mass-type regulator）$R_k=k^2$ 具有保持对称性、局域性（locality）与幺正性（unitarity，欧氏域中即 Osterwalder–Schrader 反射正性）的优点，但与动量依赖调节器不同，它不能在四维中压制有效势（effective potential）流方程的对数发散。受 Callan 微扰重整化方案中高阶 $\theta$-插入（$\theta$-insertions）改善紫外收敛的启发，作者希望发展高阶泛函流方法以解决该缺陷。

Result: 精确二阶流方程对纳入两圈效应与检验截断方案（truncation schemes）有用，但一般不解决紫外收敛问题。次级流方程在取足够多阶导数后变为紫外有限（ultraviolet finite），且无需额外紫外调节器即可求值；回积到标准流所需的积分常数编码微观紫外物理，类似于相关耦合常数（relevant coupling constants）。

Abstract: The functional renormalization group provides a powerful non-perturbative framework for quantum field theory through the exact flow equation for the scale-dependent effective action. A mass-type regulator $R_k = k^2$ is particularly attractive because it preserves symmetries, locality, and unitarity (or Osterwalder--Schrader reflection positivity in the Euclidean domain). However, unlike momentum-dependent regulators, it does not tame all ultraviolet divergences: in four dimensions, flow equations for the effective potential exhibit logarithmic divergences. The resulting secondary flow equations become ultraviolet finite once sufficiently many derivatives have been taken, and can be evaluated without an additional ultraviolet regulator. Motivation: 高能对撞机中的粒子径迹重建（particle track reconstruction）是高计算量的组合问题，传统卡尔曼滤波（Kalman filter）随探测器占用率上升而扩展性差；GNN 虽为有潜力的替代方案，但低动量粒子径迹紧密卷曲，易与组合噪声混淆。目标是面向高等级触发（HLT）的实时软粒子径迹重建。

Abstract: Particle track reconstruction in high-energy collider experiments is a computationally demanding combinatorial problem, traditionally solved by Kalman filters that scale poorly with increasing detector occupancy. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative. Consequently, the network can distinguish true physical tracks from combinatorial noise with good precision. Crucially, the manifold embedding yields a fake track rate of 0.0037. This represents a 13-fold improvement over the baseline Helical-IN (0.0508). Furthermore, the model maintains good track coherence, as quantified by our proposed Mean Hit Recovery Fraction (MHRF) of 0.858, and achieves a highly efficient inference latency of approximately 150 ms per event on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU. These results represent a critical step toward real-time soft particle tracking for the High-Level Trigger (HLT). By operating within practical HLT latency budgets, this geometry-aware approach provides a pathway to recover soft, anomalous Beyond Standard Model signatures such as unclustered energy patterns (SUEPs), that routinely evade standard hardware filters.

Abstract: A full-tensor superconducting gravity gradiometer whose four test masses carry two compositions in a $2+2$ pattern acquires a composition-dependent channel for dilaton-coupled ultralight dark matter. For copper and lead, the sensitivity reaches $d_{\hat m}-d_g\simeq3.4\times10^{-8}$, and $4.9\times10^{-10}$ with upgraded accelerometers.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Energy reconstruction from electromagnetic calorimeter data underpins particle identification, event reconstruction and precision measurements in high-energy physics. Machine learning already performs well on this task, yet two questions remain open: how much the choice of data representation matters, and whether hybrid quantum-classical models add value in practice. We compare classical and quantum-enhanced models for incident positron energy reconstruction on the CaloGAN dataset, a public benchmark of 100,000 simulated electromagnetic shower events spanning 1 to 100 GeV. Models that follow the longitudinal development of the shower reach the highest accuracy, which points to the value of physically informed representations. The hybrid QLSTM gives the lowest reconstruction error, with an RMSE of 0.294 GeV. Under matched-data conditions with only 1,500 training events, the QLSTM improves on the classical LSTM by 52.7% and also surpasses an LSTM trained on the full set. These observations indicate that longitudinal representations are central to calorimeter energy reconstruction and give preliminary evidence that hybrid quantum-classical sequence models can be more sample-efficient for regression in high-energy physics.

In this work, an overview of the PTOLEMY project is given together with the design, simulation, construction and commissioning of the RF Tracker prototype: the Electron Trap. Abstract: Active-target gas time projection chambers (TPCs) could provide the ultimate measurements of neutrino interactions needed for future neutrino oscillation measurements. Their development requires optimisation of the gas mixture, pressure, electric fields, and readout configuration. Warwick TPC (WarTPC) is an R\&D platform for investigating in these directions for neutrino physics and beyond. It can operate at pressures up to approximately 10 bar and features a combination of thick gas electron multipliers and a Timepix3 camera for optical readout. In this paper, we present the construction and commissioning of the WarTPC platform, describing the design and integration of its subsystems, and report the first measurements of its optical response under different operating conditions, demonstrating a stable and functioning R&D platform.

Abstract: Accurate and precise density measurements are important for characterizing solid materials and for evaluating physical properties that are strongly affected by porosity. While several methods are available for determining density, measurements of small or irregularly shaped samples can be limited by sample geometry, balance precision, liquid density, and other experimental sources of uncertainty. In this study, an accessible Archimedes density measurement system was developed using 3D printed components and an analytical balance for the measurement of solid and liquid densities. The measurement of solid densities requires knowing the exact density of the liquid. Therefore, copper, vanadium, and indium standards were used to measure the densities of ethanol (0.8116 +/- 0.0005 g/cm3), distilled water (0.9982 +/- 0.0013 g/cm3), and hexanes (0.6748 +/- 0.0016 g/cm3), all of them showing strong agreement with literature values. Uncertainty analysis indicates that the relative uncertainty in the measured solid density decreases with increasing sample mass and balance precision, while higher density liquids can further reduce the required sample mass. The uncertainty of the immersed mass measurement was found to have a greater influence on the calculated density than the uncertainty of the air mass measurement. An open-source Archimedes LS software was developed to analyse liquid and solid densities including the corresponding uncertainties. These findings demonstrate that an accessible 3D printed Archimedes system combined with an open-source software provides a practical approach for improving the consistency and comparability of density measurements between laboratories.

</details>


### [67] [Axis-selective AC magnetic-field imaging in the tens-of-megahertz regime using RF-dressed continuous-wave scanning NV magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17366)
*Shuntaro Komuro,Ryusei Okaniwa,Kazuma Okura,Kunitaka Hayashi,Toshu An,Junko Ishi-Hayase*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出一种射频缀饰连续波（RF-dressed CW）传感方案，用于扫描氮-空位（NV）磁强计，在33.5 MHz实现交流磁场成像，并通过偏置场调控将RF耦合跃迁频率连续调谐至89.3 MHz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脉冲式扫描氮-空位磁强计（SNVM）在探测数十至数百兆赫兹射频磁场时，对微波（MW）脉冲控制与射频同步的要求日益苛刻，需要一种规避短时高功率脉冲的替代方案。

Method: 利用NV自旋-1三能级系统的电子自旋双共振：目标RF磁场相干耦合两个自旋本征态形成RF缀饰态，在连续激光、MW与RF激发下由CW-ESDR谱读出能级劈裂；结合原子力显微镜（AFM）与NV系综金刚石探针，通过静偏置场依次选择三条晶向NV轴。

Result: 成功对RF驱动的L形微电路奥斯特场成像，获得三个NV轴的RF磁场投影强度分布图，与毕奥-萨伐尔（Biot-Savart）计算结果定性吻合；横向偏置场将RF耦合自旋态跃迁频率从14.4 MHz调谐至89.3 MHz，并在89.3 MHz直接观测到RF缀饰共振。

Conclusion: 该RF缀饰CW原理（此前已在共聚焦NV装置中实现至632 MHz的RF磁场探测）支持将SNVM交流磁场成像扩展至数百兆赫兹频段，并有望在补充相位信息后实现矢量磁场重构。

Abstract: Spatially resolved magnetic-field imaging reveals material properties and local currents in microelectronic devices. Conventional pulsed scanning nitrogen-vacancy (NV) magnetometry (SNVM) faces increasingly stringent microwave (MW) pulse-control requirements for detecting radio-frequency (RF) magnetic fields at frequencies ranging from tens to hundreds of megahertz. To address this challenge, we implement an RF-dressed continuous-wave (CW) sensing scheme for SNVM and demonstrate AC magnetic-field imaging at 33.5 MHz. The method exploits electron-spin double resonance in the NV spin-1 three-level system: the target RF magnetic field coherently couples two spin eigenstates, forming RF-dressed states whose splittings are detected in continuous-wave electron-spin double resonance (CW-ESDR) spectra under continuous laser, MW, and RF excitation. This approach avoids short, high-power MW pulses and synchronization between the RF field and a pulsed sensing sequence. Using an NV-ensemble diamond probe integrated with an atomic force microscope (AFM), we image the Oersted field of an RF-driven L-shaped microcircuit. Successively selecting three crystallographic NV axes by static bias-field control yields maps of RF magnetic-field projection magnitudes, with distinct distributions qualitatively reproduced by Biot-Savart calculations. Transverse bias-field control also tunes the RF-coupled spin-state transition frequency from 14.4 to 89.3 MHz, as characterized by spectroscopy and Hamiltonian analysis, with RF-dressed resonances directly observed in CW-ESDR spectra at 89.3 MHz. Together with previous RF magnetic-field detection up to 632 MHz using the same RF-dressed CW sensing principle in a confocal NV setup, these results support extending AC magnetic-field imaging with SNVM toward the hundreds-of-megahertz regime and, with additional phase information, vector magnetic-field reconstruction.

</details>

### [68] [Linear monitoring-feedback Model for Linearized gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17642)
*Daisuke Miki,Alfred Li,Yiqiu Ma,Yubao Liu,Yanbei Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出引力量子监控-反馈（monitoring-feedback）框架，用辅助系统（ancilla）监测大质量物体位置并经引力响应核反馈，在线性输入-输出理论层面刻画确定性响应与力噪声。按辅助系统关联分为三个体制，分别给出经典引力噪声下界、行列式界以及可复现线性化量子引力噪声与对易子的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力是否具有量子自由度（quantum degrees of freedom）是根本性问题；需要构造统一框架，将半经典引力的局域经典记录模型（classical-record models）与线性化量子引力动力学纳入同一描述并加以区分。

Method: 构建线性监控-反馈框架：辅助系统监测大质量物体位置，记录通过选定的引力响应核（gravitational response kernel）反馈；在线性输入-输出理论（linear input-output theory）下显式给出确定性响应，并揭示辅助扇区导致的力噪声（force noise）及关联，按辅助系统关联性划分为三个体制（Regime-I/II/III）。

Result: Regime-I（独立、未压缩未关联的探测与反作用正交分量）中，单物体经典引力噪声（CGN）存在下界 S_F^min = 2ħMΩ_SN^2，Ω_SN为由微观质量分布决定的薛定谔-牛顿频率（Schrödinger-Newton frequency）。Regime-II（允许任意局域探测-反作用关联，但不同物体辅助系统独立）中，单物体CGN不受限，而联合力噪声矩阵满足等价于局域经典信道/无纠缠界（local classical-channel/no-entanglement bound）的行列式界。Regime-III（允许跨物体关联与共同非局域媒介）中，可联合压缩对易的力噪声、使质量间产生纠缠，并在合适延迟响应与输入态下复现线性化量子引力的噪声、对易子及非马尔可夫量子涨落（non-Markovian quantum fluctuations）。

Conclusion: 该框架以统一语言表明：引力是否具有量子自由度可映射为辅助系统关联体制的选择问题；经典记录半经典引力对应Regime-I/II的约束，而线性化量子引力动力学可由Regime-III的监控-反馈系统再现。

Abstract: Whether gravity has quantum degrees of freedom is a question of fundamental importance. We formulate a linear monitoring-feedback framework for gravitational interactions that includes local classical-record models of semiclassical gravity and, when quantum resources are supplied in correlated ancilla inputs, can reproduce linearized quantum-gravity dynamics. Auxiliary systems monitor the positions of massive bodies, and their records are fed back through a chosen gravitational response kernel. At the level of linear input-output theory, this construction makes the deterministic response explicit while exposing the force noise and correlations associated with the auxiliary sector. We organize the allowed ancilla correlations into three regimes. In Regime-I, independent ancillas have unsqueezed, uncorrelated sensing and back-action quadratures. A single object then has a minimum classical-gravity noise (CGN), S_F^min = 2 hbar M Omega_SN^2, where Omega_SN is the Schrodinger-Newton frequency set by the microscopic mass distribution. In Regime-II, arbitrary local sensing--back-action correlations are allowed, but the ancillas associated with different objects remain independent. The single-object CGN is then unrestricted, while the joint force-noise matrix obeys a determinant bound that is equivalent to the local classical-channel/no-entanglement bound. In Regime-III, cross-object correlations and a common nonlocal mediator are allowed. The commuting force noises can then be jointly squeezed, entanglement between the masses can occur, and--with a suitable retarded response and input state--the monitoring-feedback system can reproduce the noise and commutators of linearized quantum gravity, including non-Markovian quantum fluctuations.

</details>

Abstract: We show that a strong form of correlation decay in quantum Gibbs states has far-reaching dynamical consequences. For a smooth path of geometrically local commuting Hamiltonians whose Gibbs states satisfy strong decay of correlations, we construct $\mathrm{O}(1)$-time quasi-local Lindbladian evolutions exactly connecting these states in both directions, together with $\mathrm{O}(1)$-time quasi-local Hamiltonian evolutions exactly connecting their canonical purifications. These quasi-local connections strengthen previously known quantum-channel circuit constructions based on the decay of connected correlation functions [R. Ma, V. Khemani, and S. Sang, PRX Quantum 7, 033052 (2026)], which approximately connect states within the same phase but have depths that grow logarithmically with system size. Furthermore, if the Gibbs-state path contains a product state, we prove that the single-site Glauber dynamics has a $Ω(1)$ spectral gap independent of the system size throughout the path. This establishes Glauber-dynamics fast mixing for models that may violate the strong spatial mixing condition used in prior proofs, including (1) the $q$-state Potts model throughout the high-temperature phase for any $q$ and any finite dimension; (2) the Ising model with a longitudinal field at low temperature, with frustrating perturbations that destroy monotonicity of the dynamics; and (3) the three-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_2$ lattice gauge theory at low temperatures comparing to a field perturbation, even though the model supports classical memories at intermediate temperatures. Intriguingly, we establish fast mixing of these classical Markov chains using tools from quantum physics like Lieb-Robinson bounds.

Conclusion: 结果为能级统计与Krylov动力学之间提供了精确的解析联系，并给出向d能级系统的推广：系综平均扩展复杂度可表示为对相邻间距比联合分布的(d−2)维积分。

Abstract: The spread complexity characterizes the spreading of a quantum state over the Krylov basis and has been proposed to capture the correlations between energy levels. How such correlations manifest in the dynamics has been studied mainly numerically for large matrices. Here we consider three-level systems, the smallest setting with spacing correlations, and derive exact results for the spread complexity, the Krylov variance, and the Krylov entropy. At infinite temperature, the Krylov basis depends on the spectrum only through the ratio of consecutive level spacings, and the spectral variance enters only as an overall time scale. For Gaussian ensembles, these variables are statistically independent, and the averaged spread complexity and variance reduce to one-dimensional integrals over the spacing-ratio distribution encoding the level statistics. We establish universal late-time values of the complexity and variance, and an algebraic relaxation with exponents set by the Dyson index. We also outline the extension to $d$ levels, with the ensemble-averaged spread complexity expressed as a $(d-2)$-dimensional integral over the joint distribution of consecutive spacing ratios.

</details>

Abstract: How precisely can an observer estimate a parameter related to a quantum field? However, local algebras in relativistic quantum field theory are of type III: they carry no trace and normal states have no density matrices, so the SLD quantum Fisher information and the quantum Cramér--Rao bound cannot be defined in the usual way. We define the SLD (Bures) quantum Fisher information on arbitrary von Neumann algebras through Uhlmann's transition probability and prove a quantum Cramér--Rao bound for collective measurements. We also show that the bound is sharp, and equals the largest classical Fisher information obtainable by measurements in the region.

### [72] [The cone of states over time: exact tests and convergent two-sided hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18001)
*Matt Hoogsteder-Riera*

TL;DR: 本文研究由“时间态（state over time, stote）”(ρ⊗1)⋆J_E 生成的凸锥 Q̂，给出收敛的外层半定规划（SDP）层级及其迹范数 Hausdorff 误差界，并构造互补的内层层级，用于判定该锥的隶属关系与支撑值界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间态算子是量子信息中描述输入—信道关联（input–channel representation）的自然对象，但其生成的凸锥缺乏可计算的隶属判定与显式精度保证；本文旨在建立带显式误差界的分层半定松弛框架，并厘清该锥与其他算子锥的关系。

Method: 将约束对称扩展（constrained symmetric-extension）理论专门化到输入—信道表示：外层采用 Bose 扩展舍入（Bose-extension rounding），内层在每个选定纯输入方向上保留全部信道，形成嵌套方向集层级；并对二值经典输出情形给出精确 SDP 检验，另外计算各向同性边界。

Result: 外层层级收敛，且在迹一截面上有显式迹范数 Hausdorff 界；Bose 舍入保持信道边缘约束，第 k 层误差至多 2d_A²(d_A−1)/(k+d_A−1)。内层误差至多 2(d_A−1)sin²δ（δ 为 Fubini–Study 覆盖半径），N 个方向时至多 2π²(d_A−1)N^{−1/(d_A−1)}，与 d_B 无关。二值经典输出下得到精确隶属检验，在忠实边缘（faithful marginal）处化为标量不等式；迹一成员可由至多 rank ρ 个纯输入时间态构造实现。此外给出精确各向同性边界，并证明等局部维度 d≥2 时 PPT ⊊ Q̂∩PSD ⊊ PSD。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了具有显式尺寸—精度保证的锥隶属与支撑值认证方法，但不对精确边界隶属保证有限终止；结果揭示了时间态锥与 PPT、PSD 锥之间的严格包含关系，并为二值输出情形给出可实现的结构性刻画。

Abstract: A state over time (stote) pairs a state $ρ$ with a channel $\mathcal{E}$ as the Hermitian, generally non-positive operator $(ρ\otimes\mathbf{1})\star J_\mathcal{E}$. We study the convex cone $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ generated by these operators. Our main result is a convergent outer semidefinite hierarchy with an explicit trace-norm Hausdorff bound on its trace-one slices. Specializing constrained symmetric-extension theory to the input--channel representation, we show that Bose-extension rounding preserves the channel marginal constraint and gives error at most $2d_A^2(d_A-1)/(k+d_A-1)$ at level $k$. A complementary inner hierarchy retains all channels at each chosen pure-input direction. For $d_A\ge2$, its error is at most $2(d_A-1)\sin^2δ$, where $δ$ is the Fubini--Study covering radius; nested sets of $N$ directions give error at most $2π^2(d_A-1)N^{-1/(d_A-1)}$, independently of $d_B$. These bounds provide explicit size--accuracy guarantees and certified support-value bounds, without guaranteeing finite termination for exact boundary membership. For binary classical outputs, we obtain an exact SDP membership test, including indefinite output blocks and mixtures that are not single stotes. At faithful marginal the test reduces to a scalar inequality; every trace-one member of this sector with input marginal $ρ$ has a constructive realization using at most $\operatorname{rank}ρ$ pure-input stotes with binary measurement channels. Finally, we compute the exact isotropic boundary and prove $\mathrm{PPT}\subsetneq\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}\cap\mathrm{PSD}\subsetneq\mathrm{PSD}$ for equal local dimensions at least two.

</details>


### [73] [Hidden Quantum Markov Processes: Mealy Formulation and Observable Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18040)
*Soueidi El Gheteb,Abdessatar Souissi*

TL;DR: 本文在 Mealy 架构下形式化隐量子马尔可夫过程（hidden quantum Markov process），用单一完全正（completely positive）且保单位的转移期望耦合隐系统、输出与下一隐系统。文章建立因子化（Moore 实现）的秩障碍（rank obstruction），并证明操作等价（operational equivalence）不能决定输出的纠缠结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Mealy 机与 Moore 机是隐马尔可夫模型的两种经典架构，而量子情形下二者的关系、可实现条件及输出态性质尚缺乏统一刻画。作者希望以单一转移期望统一描述隐量子马尔可夫过程，并厘清 Moore 型因子化的代数条件及其对输出纠缠的影响。

Method: 以单一完全正保单位转移期望（transition expectation）加边界态（boundary state）生成准局域、有限关联的隐—输出态；将 Moore 实现刻画为在指定隐代数上因子化的转移期望，并用 Kraus 秩（Kraus rank）分析因子化条件；再通过投影读出（projective readout）与 Monràs–Beige–Wiesner 操作模型建立对应，并构造团簇态仪器（cluster-state instrument）上的两个等距提升作对比。

Result: 证明了因子化的秩障碍，并在等距生成元（isometric generator）情形下证明其充分性：因子化成立当且仅当诱导的隐记忆信道 Kraus 秩不超过隐 Hilbert 空间维数，该秩亦给出最小中间 Hilbert 空间维数。当隐维与输出维均不小于 2 时，可因子化的等距生成元构成测度为零的闭子集。任一投影读出下 Mealy 生成元诱导操作隐量子马尔可夫模型，反之任一有限维操作模型都可在同一记忆空间上以秩一读出实现 Mealy 提升。团簇态仪器上两个等距、Moore 可因子化的提升给出相同测量输出统计但不同量子输出态：其一在 n≥3 时边际纠缠于 1|2⋯n 分割且三输出负性为 1/2，另一则边际完全可分。

Conclusion: Mealy 架构为隐量子马尔可夫过程提供了统一且具操作意义的表述，Moore 因子化受 Kraus 秩约束且在高维情形下是稀疏（测度零）的特例。操作等价不蕴含输出量子态的纠缠等价，说明仅凭测量统计无法确定输出的纠缠性质。

Abstract: We formulate hidden quantum Markov processes in a Mealy architecture using a single completely positive unital transition expectation coupling the present hidden system, the emitted output, and the next hidden system. Together with a boundary state, it generates a quasi-local, finitely correlated hidden--output state; for the commutative generator associated with a classical Mealy kernel, this state is the full joint law of the classical Mealy model.
  Moore realizations correspond to transition expectations that factor on the prescribed hidden algebra. We establish a rank obstruction to such factorization and prove its sufficiency for isometric generators: factorization holds exactly when the induced hidden-memory channel has Kraus rank at most the hidden Hilbert-space dimension. The Kraus rank also gives the minimal intermediate Hilbert-space dimension. When the hidden and output dimensions are at least two, the factorizable isometric generators form a closed subset of measure zero.
  For any fixed projective readout, a Mealy generator induces an operational hidden quantum Markov model in the sense of Monràs, Beige and Wiesner; conversely, every finite-dimensional operational model admits a Mealy lift on the same memory space with a rank-one readout. For their cluster-state instrument, two isometric, Moore-factorizable lifts with the same instrument and initial memory state yield identical measured output statistics but different quantum output states: one has marginals entangled across $1|2\cdots n$ for $n\geq3$, with three-output negativity $\tfrac12$, whereas the other has fully separable marginals. Thus operational equivalence does not determine output entanglement.

</details>


### [74] [Quantum Preparation without Disturbance: Information Costs and Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18135)

TL;DR: 该研究确定：在保留设备记忆的前提下用同一设备制备两种量子态之一所需的最小信息代价，等于目标态的 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵（Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy）；若允许消耗记忆，则最小代价退化为 Umegaki 相对熵（Umegaki relative entropy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注量子态制备中的信息代价问题，特别是设备记忆（memory）的保留或消耗如何影响最小代价，并试图为 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵寻找操作层面的刻画。

Method: 以两种可能记忆态之间的 Umegaki 相对熵度量信息代价，分析保留记忆与消耗记忆两种情形下的最小代价；并将结论推广到满足数据处理（data processing）不等式的任意散度（divergence），同时研究输出次数有限及独立重复下的假设检验（hypothesis testing）情形。

Result: 保留记忆时最小代价为 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵，消耗记忆时为 Umegaki 相对熵；对满足数据处理的任意散度，记忆保留对应其经典限制的最大扩展（maximal extension）。有限输出情形下代价随输出数从 Umegaki 增至 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵；每轮最优 Stein 指数（Stein exponent）在每轮输出数发散时收敛到 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵。

Conclusion: Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵获得了量子统计（quantum statistics）中的操作刻画，即作为记忆受限制备的最小信息代价及极限 Stein 指数。

Abstract: We determine the minimum information cost of preparing either of two quantum states with one common device while preserving its memory. Measured by the Umegaki relative entropy between the two possible memory states, this minimum is the Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy of the target states. If the memory may instead be consumed, the minimum is their Umegaki relative entropy. More generally, for any divergence satisfying data processing, memory preservation selects the maximal extension of its classical restriction. We also study the cost of requiring only finitely many outputs, with the costs increasing from Umegaki to Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy as the number of outputs grows. Finally, we consider hypothesis testing of an unknown device over independent runs. The optimal Stein exponent per run converges to the Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy whenever the number of outputs per run diverges. This gives an operational characterization of the Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy in quantum statistics.

</details>


### [75] [Entropic quantum error correction for independent and correlated amplitude damping errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18192)
*Gang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用量子条件互信息（quantum conditional mutual information）的结构，将基于量子熵（quantum entropy）的量子纠错（QEC）恢复映射设计从需要 256×256 矩阵的高复杂度计算，简化为仅用 2×2 酉矩阵的手工精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以量子熵为工具的量子纠错思路长期受计算复杂度制约：即便 $[[4,1]]$ 码在标准独立振幅阻尼（amplitude damping）误差下也涉及 $256\times 256$ 厄米矩阵，码字长度增加时扩展性差；相关误差（correlated errors）情形更糟。

Method: 借助作者前作中的洞见并利用量子条件互信息的特定结构，通过仅 $2\times 2$ 酉矩阵（unitary matrices）的初等线性代数与微积分手工求解；先由熵分析精确识别泄漏信道（leakage channels）与泄漏量，再据此定义恢复映射（recovery map）——独立误差下基于 16 个显式给出的正交归一向量与 6 条指令，相关误差下基于 6 个正交归一向量与 6 条指令。

Result: 在常用的纠缠保真度（entanglement fidelity）上，该简单恢复映射取得紧致（sharp）结果；在最坏情况保真度（worst-case fidelity）上，最优结果仅优于该方法约 $9\%$；相关误差下短码字无可比结果。该恢复映射是已知技术中首个非全局（non-global）映射。

Conclusion: 基于熵泄漏分析的规则简单、直观且计算代价低，可在性能损失很小的前提下精确解决原有高复杂度问题，并为相关误差下的短码字纠错提供可行方案。

Abstract: The idea of using quantum entropy for quantum error correction (QEC) has existed for a long time. However, its computational complexity has limited its practical use: even a $[[4,1]]$ code for standard independent amplitude damping errors involves a $256\times 256$ Hermitian matrix, so the approach scales poorly as the codeword length increases. For correlated errors, the situation is even worse. In this paper, we show that, by using the insights developed in our previous paper and exploiting certain structures of quantum conditional mutual information, we can solve these problems exactly by hand, using only $2\times 2$ unitary matrices. By examining the entropy, we identify the leakage channels and the amount of leakage exactly, then define the recovery map accordingly. The rules defining our recovery map are simple, intuitive, and computationally inexpensive: for independent errors, it is based on 16 explicitly given orthonormal vectors and 6 instructions; for correlated errors, it is based on 6 explicitly given orthonormal vectors and 6 instructions. Our method sacrifices little in terms of performance: for the widely used entanglement fidelity, our simple QEC recovery map achieves a sharp result; for the worst-case fidelity, the best result is about $9\%$ better than ours; for correlated errors, there are no comparable results for short codewords. Among the known techniques, our recovery map is the first non-global one. The calculations use elementary linear algebra and calculus, and is presented in great detail.

</details>


### [76] [Fer expansion converges almost twice as far as Magnus for qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18249)
*Lorenzo Bagnasacco,Vittorio Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文给出量子比特情形下 Fer 展开（Fer expansion）的保证收敛阈值约为 6.2106（约 1.98π），接近通用 Magnus 展开（Magnus expansion）最优阈值 π 的两倍，并证明单个 Fer 步骤的界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fer 展开的误差随因子数呈双指数下降，但此前仅保证在约 2.6058 的半径内收敛，明显小于 Magnus 展开的收敛半径 π，限制了其理论可用性与与 Magnus 展开的对比。

Conclusion: Fer 展开在量子比特下的理论收敛保证被显著扩展，缩小了与 Magnus 展开阈值之间的差距；但精确收敛半径（exact convergence radius）仍未确定，留待后续研究。

Abstract: The Fer expansion writes the propagator of a time-dependent Hamiltonian as a product of unitary exponentials whose error decreases doubly exponentially with the number of factors, while the Magnus expansion improves by one order at a time. This is almost twice the optimal universal Magnus threshold $π$; the bound on a single Fer step is sharp, while the exact convergence radius remains open.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
