Result: 对无能隙量子自旋链，信号显示相互作用相关的代数标度（algebraic scaling）与弹道传播（ballistic propagation）；Luttinger 液体理论将扫频时量子比特频率处的符号变化与探针间传播时间联系起来，并预言关联前沿之前与晚期（传感窗口分辨量子比特频率时）贝尔信号具有不同的空间幂律。矩阵乘积态计算在零磁化与有限磁化下检验了前沿前标度，并从零磁化下的贝尔振荡中提取传播速度。探针耦合几何决定哪些自旋与动量扇区对测量信号有贡献。

Conclusion: 双量子比特互补协议可将弛豫测量从单频局域率拓展为对有限时间关联的有符号、频率可调探测，结合 Luttinger 液体理论与矩阵乘积态计算可定量刻画无能隙自旋链中的关联传播，且信号内容受探针耦合几何调控。

Abstract: Quantum impurities and spin qubits are powerful local probes of magnetic fluctuations, but conventional relaxometry reduces their information to a local relaxation rate at a single frequency. Building on advances in two-qubit sensing of spatial correlations, we show how two complementary protocols probe finite-time correlations in interacting quantum matter. Phase-cycled Bell-state relaxometry isolates the signed contribution of correlated relaxation to the joint ground-state population. To second order in the probe-environment coupling, the resulting population contrast measures a finite-time integral of the energy-ordered cross-correlator. In the longitudinal regime, XY8 covariance probes the complementary symmetrized cross-correlator within a tunable frequency window. For a gapless quantum spin chain, the signals reveal interaction-dependent algebraic scaling and ballistic propagation. Luttinger-liquid theory links sign changes as the qubit frequency is swept to the propagation time between the probes. It also predicts distinct spatial power laws for the Bell signal before the correlation front and at late times, when the sensing window resolves the qubit frequency. Matrix-product-state calculations for the spin chain test the pre-front scaling at zero and finite magnetization and extract the propagation velocity from Bell oscillations at zero magnetization. The probe coupling geometry determines which spin and momentum sectors contribute to the measured signals.

</details>


### [78] [Magic of Dicke states: density, cost and yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17623)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明Dicke态在除半填充外的任意填充下，其magic（以稳定子Rényi熵SRE度量）是广延的，并解析给出magic密度及次领项；同时揭示半填充处SRE仅对数增长，但制备成本与相对熵仍广延，说明小SRE不必然意味着弱资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke态是多体量子态的基本家族，但在容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computation）语境下其作为magic资源的定量刻画尚不清晰，尤其是magic是否广延、可提取的CCZ/T态产额如何随填充变化，需要系统研究。

Method: 以稳定子Rényi熵（stabilizer Rényi entropy, SRE）度量magic，对$L$个量子比特、$n$个激发的Dicke态在填充$f=n/L$下进行解析计算；并结合资源理论视角分析其制备成本、平均产额与magic相对熵。核心思路是将Dicke态视为倾斜自旋（tilted spins）乘积态的固定磁化投影（fixed-magnetization projection）。

Result: SRE在除半填充外的每个填充$f$处均为广延，magic密度及其次领项可解析得到。Dicke态在任意$0<f<1$下平均可产生广延数量的CCZ态（或带催化剂的$T$态），但该产额非自平均（not self-averaging），逐次运行间以$O(L)$波动，类似于由所有量子比特共享的隐藏相位决定。半填充处SRE仅对数增长，而制备成本、平均产额与magic相对熵仍为广延。

Conclusion: 小SRE不一定意味着弱资源：半填充处SRE虽弱但资源仍广延。倾斜自旋乘积态的固定磁化投影视角可统一理解上述现象，表明Dicke态是研究magic广延性与非自平均产额的有力平台。

Abstract: Dicke states are an elementary family of many-body quantum states, consisting of equal superpositions of all configurations of $L$ qubits with $n$ excitations. We show that their magic, as measured by the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE), is extensive at every filling $f=n/L$ except half-filling, and we obtain the magic density analytically, together with the subleading terms. In the context of fault-tolerant quantum computation, we also investigate the yield of Dicke states, and find that they can produce on average an extensive number of CCZ states (or $T$ states with a catalyst) at any $0<f<1$. This yield is not self-averaging, but fluctuates from run to run by $O(L)$, as if it were set by a hidden phase shared by all qubits. At half-filling, the SRE grows only logarithmically, while the preparation cost, average yield and relative entropy of magic remain extensive, so that a small SRE does not necessarily signal a weak resource. These results can be understood by viewing the Dicke state as a fixed-magnetization projection of a product of tilted spins.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画格子在Klein瓶拓扑下经反射粘合构造的拓扑码（topological code）与哈密顿量，特别是区分无阻挫码（unfrustrated code）与可交换但受阻挫哈密顿量，并厘清"受阻的字面缝合"这一此前未解的关键情形。

Method: 方法上采用单项远角拟设枚举交换粘合，按 \(\mathbb{Z}_N\) 单位 \(u\) 与缝合长度奇偶性（偶长度任意 \(N\) 存在；奇长度需 \(-1\) 为模 \(N\) 的平方）给出存在条件；进而计算任意子输运（anyon transport）与交换缝（含 \(P^2=C\)，\(C\) 为电荷共轭），并将非交换方片构成的 \(2L_x\) 个自对偶时钟环（self-dual clock rings）扇区由体（bulk）锁定从而精确约化谱；同时讨论扭曲系数同调（twisted-coefficient homology）与逻辑模（logical modules），以及有限网格上的装扮等距门（dressed-isometry gates）与单值性（monodromy）。

Result: 结果为：得到粘合分类与相位敏感的相容性判据；给出谱到时钟环谱的精确约化、\(N=2,4\) 时环基态能量的闭式表达以及基态简并度的闭式公式；在所述曲面区域上确定扭曲系数同调，并与注册格子网格上的逻辑模比较，发现复合维数不必是循环群的平方；此外借助 qudit Gottesman–Knill 定理界定计算解释的界限：无外源魔态（magic state）时 Clifford 形变与 Weyl 测量不能提供非稳定子资源。

Conclusion: 结论强调新结果属于显式的缝合分类、相位敏感的相容性与体锁定谱，而非新的普适 no-go 定理或通用计算架构；提供非稳定子态的电荷共轭测量超出该操作模型范围。

Abstract: We classify local reflection gluings of the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ plaquette model on lattice Klein bottles and solve the obstructed literal seam. Within the monomial far-corner ansatz, all commuting gluings form a family labelled by a unit $u$ of $\mathbb{Z}_N$. They exist for every $N$ at even seam length and, at odd length, exactly when $-1$ is a square modulo $N$. We determine the anyon transport, including exchange seams with $P^2=C$ for charge conjugation $C$, and the relation phases that distinguish an unfrustrated code from a commuting but frustrated Hamiltonian. For the literal gluing, the $2L_x$ noncommuting plaquettes form self-dual clock rings whose sectors are locked by the bulk. This gives an exact reduction of the spectrum to clock-ring spectra, closed-form ring ground energies for $N=2,4$, and a closed-form ground-state degeneracy. We further determine twisted-coefficient homology on the stated surface domains and compare it with logical modules on registered lattice grids; composite dimensions need not give squares of cyclic groups. Dressed-isometry gates and monodromy are reported with their finite-grid scope. Finally, the qudit Gottesman--Knill theorem bounds the computational interpretation: Clifford deformations and Weyl measurements with stabilizer inputs and no external magic states cannot supply a non-stabilizer resource. Charge-conjugation measurements that do supply such states lie outside this operation model. The new results concern explicit seam classification, phase-sensitive consistency and bulk-locked spectra, not a new general no-go theorem or a universal computing architecture.

</details>

*Junhao,Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 电荷共轭不变输入下记录概率与所支持的参考态无关；任意输入下舍弃参考态得到共轭扇区映射的布居加权混合。每个 CSS 分量的 243 个经典代表元可纠正支撑权重不超过 2 的全部 3,609 个 Pauli 错误；全综合征接受率区别于单轮 strange-state Golay 蒸馏（Golay distillation）已知的 1/1728 理想接受率。

Abstract: We formulate a charge-$4e$ parafermionic-vortex route to qutrit processing as a conditional theory with explicit operational assumptions. Sector preservation and same-label charge-conjugation covariance imply closure for every adaptive visible record. For charge-conjugation-invariant inputs, record probabilities are independent of the supported reference state; for arbitrary inputs, discarding the reference gives a population-weighted mixture of conjugate sector maps. We give a finite-depth stability bound for complete labelled instruments, including postselection and initial zero-sector contamination. A self-contained calculation reproduces the known dense single-qutrit closure of the registered Clifford-plus-Flip gate set. We also specify an abstract ternary CSS $[[11,1,5]]_3$ syndrome/recovery decoder: 243 classical representatives per CSS component correct all 3,609 Pauli errors of support weight at most two, and an exact integer dynamic program yields the full independent-Pauli logical enumerator. Its all-syndrome acceptance is distinct from the known $1/1728$ ideal acceptance of one-round strange-state Golay distillation. An accepted-run target-infidelity budget and a synthetic parameter protocol expose the remaining noise assumptions. Native charge-$4e$ syndrome extraction, material feasibility, a device threshold, and unconditional fault tolerance are not established.

</details>


### [81] [Quantum kinetic uncertainty relations: Role of coherence in current fluctuation bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18037)
*Varinder Singh,Euijoon Kwon,Jae Sung Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较三种含相干性修正的量子动力学不确定关系（kinetic uncertainty relation, KUR）版本，以三能级微波激射热机（maser heat engine）为范例确定各版本给出最紧约束的工作区间，并借助基态简并（ground-state degeneracy）引入噪声诱导相干（noise-induced coherence）作为可调资源来调控标准KUR违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典KUR界未考虑相干性，已有多种量子修正版本，但缺乏对它们在不同工作区间约束强弱的系统比较，也缺乏对相干性如何调控KUR违反的机制性理解。

Method: 以三能级微波激射热机作为典型量子热机（quantum thermal machine），对比三种量子KUR表述对电流涨落（current fluctuations）的约束；对其中两种推导更紧的模型相关界（model-specific bounds），分别给出数值支持与其中之一的解析证明；进一步引入基态简并以产生噪声诱导相干。

Result: 识别出每种KUR表述给出最紧约束的工作区间；获得并验证两种更紧的模型相关界（一数值、一解析）；发现噪声诱导相干可在无需额外相干驱动（coherent drive）的条件下迁移、增强或抑制标准KUR的违反，甚至在三能级热机满足经典界的区间内实现违反。

Conclusion: 不同量子KUR对相干性的刻画方式各异，其在约束量子热机精度（precision）上的强弱依赖于工作区间；噪声诱导相干构成一种可调资源，可用于灵活调控涨落界及其违反。

Abstract: We present a comparative analysis of three quantum versions of the kinetic uncertainty relation (KUR), which incorporate coherence-dependent corrections to the classical bound. Using a three-level maser heat engine as a paradigmatic quantum thermal machine, we identify the operating regimes in which each formulation provides the tightest constraint on current fluctuations. We further identify tighter, model-specific bounds for two of these formulations, provide numerical support for both, and establish one of them analytically. Introducing ground-state degeneracy, we then demonstrate that noise-induced coherence provides a tunable resource for relocating, enhancing, or suppressing violations of the standard KUR without requiring an additional coherent drive. In particular, it enables violations in regimes where the three-level engine satisfies the classical bound. Our results clarify how distinct quantum KURs account for coherence and constrain the precision of quantum thermal machines.

</details>


### [82] [Observing Enhanced Entanglement in an Ising Quantum Register Beyond Weak Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18278)
*K. G. S. H. Gunawardana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出周期性测量采样（PMS）协议，在均匀矩阵乘积态框架下识别测量后态中纠缠增强的峰值，并通过量子计算实验与有限尺寸重标度假设加以验证与推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量通常给出最可能的解纠缠态，而纠缠增强的测量结果概率可能指数级降低或缺失；若能使这类结果概率更高，则有利于在含噪声量子器件中以更少资源通过后选择或玻恩规则测量观测纠缠增强。

Method: 在均匀矩阵乘积态（uniform matrix product states）框架下构建周期性测量采样（periodic measurement sampling, PMS）协议，并引入由测量密度、外场强度及量子寄存器与辅助量子比特（ancilla qubits）间交换相互作用定义的“测量超胞（measurement super-cell）”进行优化。

Result: 利用PMS识别出均匀测量后态中的纠缠增强峰值，其概率结果可通过优化测量超胞而被最大化；该基于多体量子态经典描述的预测在具有伊辛相互作用（Ising interactions）与横向磁场的量子寄存器量子比特回路实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: PMS协议的预测可从热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）推广至有限尺寸系统，并提出一个重标度假设（rescaling ansatz），用以预测有限尺寸系统中观测到纠缠增强的可能性。

Abstract: Measurements often yield a disentangled quantum state which is the most probable measurement outcome while the measurement outcomes with enhanced entanglement could be exponentially less probable or absent. Higher probability in measurement outcomes with enhanced entanglement is virtue in experimental observation of enhance entanglement in post-selected or Born-rule measurements with less resources in noisy quantum devices. We develop a periodic measurement sampling(PMS) protocol in the framework of uniform matrix product states. Using PMS, we identify a peak enhancement of entanglement in a uniform post-measurement state whose probabilistic outcome can be maximized by optimizing a measurement super-cell. A measurement super-cell is the smallest repeating building block of qubits defined by the measurement density, external field strength and exchange interactions between quantum register and ancilla qubits. The above prediction based on a classical description of the many-body quantum state is demonstrated in a quantum computing experiment in a loop of quantum register qubits with Ising interactions in the presence of transverse magnetic field. Further, we show the scalability of predictions of the periodic measurement sampling protocol in the thermodynamic limit to finite size systems. Especially, we present a rescaling ansatz which would enable predicting the possibility of observing enhanced entanglement in finite size systems.

</details>


### [83] [Dissipation as a Resource for Entanglement via High-Spin Mediator Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18489)
*Ellen Martins Fernandes,Fabricio Macedo de Souza*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以Lindblad主方程比较相干驱动与耗散通道对高自旋S=5/2磁杂质中介体的纯化能力，发现只有耗散（光泵浦）能提高中介体纯度，且其带来的纠缠对泵浦速率非单调，在实验泵浦率下可实现稳健纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单个热混合高自旋磁杂质作为中介时，如何有效产生两个自旋量子比特（spin qubits）之间的纠缠，并厘清相干控制与耗散控制在纯化中介体上的本质差别。

Method: 采用Lindblad主方程（Lindblad master equation）框架，对比相干驱动与耗散通道两种中介体控制方式；并以单个Mn自旋的实测光泵浦（optical pumping）速率固定耗散通道参数进行建模。

Result: 任何相干驱动均无法将中介体纯化至超过其热值（酉演化保持纯度）；耗散通道可将纯度提升至光泵浦循环上限Tr[ρ_M^2]≈0.8。纠缠随泵浦强度非单调，在有限速率处达峰后下降；实验泵浦率下单个80ns脉冲即可纯化中介体，极化持续时间比纠缠动力学长三个数量级，12K时首次复现负度（first-recurrence negativity）由0.18升至0.64，30K以下仍高于0.6。

Conclusion: 耗散不应被视为需抑制的噪声，而是实现稳健纠缠产生（robust entanglement generation）的核心资源；中介体纯化与纠缠增强可由光泵浦循环同时提供。

Abstract: We study entanglement generation between two spin qubits mediated by a single, thermally mixed, high-spin ($S=5/2$) magnetic impurity, using the Lindblad master equation to compare coherent and dissipative control of the mediator. We show that no coherent drive, however strong or resonant, can purify the mediator beyond its thermal value --- a direct consequence of unitary evolution exactly preserving state purity --- while a dissipative channel purifies it up to the limit set by the optical pumping cycle itself, $\mathrm{Tr}[ρ_M^2]\approx0.8$. Modeling this channel with rates fixed by the measured optical pumping of a single Mn spin, we find that the entanglement it enables is not monotonic in the pump strength: it peaks at a finite rate and then decreases, since the same channel leaks decoherence to the qubits through the coupling that mediates their entanglement. At the experimental pumping rate, a single $80$ns pulse purifies the mediator, the resulting polarization persists for a timescale three orders of magnitude longer than the entangling dynamics, and the first-recurrence negativity rises from $0.18$ to $0.64$ at $12$K, remaining above $0.6$ up to $30$K. These results establish dissipation, not as noise to be suppressed, but as the very resource that makes robust entanglement generation possible.

</details>


### [84] [Solution of the Qubit Marginal Problem from Fermionic Representability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18503)
*David A. Mazziotti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用费米子（fermion）可表示性理论求解量子比特（qubit）的集合边际问题（ensemble marginal problem），证明可表示的比特边际构成费米子约化密度矩阵集的一个暴露面（exposed face），并由此获得精确迁移的可表示性层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域约化态（local reduced states）是否与某个全局量子态（global quantum state）相容，即量子边际问题（quantum marginal problem），是量子信息与量子模拟的核心问题；此前缺乏对多比特集合边际问题的完整刻画。

Method: 将每个量子比特编码为两个轨道上的一个费米子（one fermion in two orbitals），把比特边际映射为N-可表示费米子约化密度矩阵（N-representable fermionic reduced density matrices）的子集，该子集由各格点占据数方差（site-occupation variance）为零所选出，从而将费米子可表示性条件层级精确迁移至比特情形。

Result: 在受挫海森堡环（frustrated Heisenberg ring）与横场伊辛环（transverse-field Ising ring）上的应用显示，(2,3)-正性条件（(2,3)-positivity conditions）在Majumdar–Ghosh点精确成立，在临界点（criticality）精度约为0.5%。

Conclusion: 由于所导出的条件对任意量子态均成立，该框架亦可作为抑制基于比特的量子器件上测量误差（measurement errors）的方法；费米子可表示性层级因而为比特边际问题提供精确且可计算的条件体系。

Abstract: Whether local reduced states are compatible with a global quantum state---the quantum marginal problem---is central to quantum information and simulation. Here we present a solution to the ensemble marginal problem for qubits based on the representability theory of fermions. Encoding each qubit as one fermion in two orbitals, we prove that the representable qubit marginals form an exposed face of the set of $N$-representable fermionic reduced density matrices, selected by the vanishing of every site-occupation variance. Complete hierarchies of fermionic representability conditions therefore transfer exactly to qubits. Applications to frustrated Heisenberg and transverse-field Ising rings show that the $(2,3)$-positivity conditions are exact at the Majumdar--Ghosh point and accurate to about 0.5% at criticality. Because the conditions hold for any quantum state, they also provide a framework for mitigating measurement errors on qubit-based quantum devices.

</details>

### [85] [ADfiRe: An empirical- and CFD-informed advection-diffusion-reaction thermal model for wildfire spread](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18012)
*Konstantinos Vogiatzoglou,Nikolaos Sdiros,Costas Papadimitriou,Konstantinos Ampountolas,Petros Koumoutsakos,Vasilis Bontozoglou*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: ADfiRe 是一种混合野火实时预报框架，将经验关联式与 CFD（计算流体力学）参数化嵌入物理基对流-扩散-反应（advection-diffusion-reaction, ADR）模型，并在五个地中海灌木林地野外火烧实验中验证了蔓延速率（ROS）与火线强度等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时野火预报需在快速但物理相关的模型与高保真但计算昂贵的方法之间权衡，尤其针对非均质、多相固体燃料上的火蔓延。研究旨在兼顾计算效率与物理保真度。

Method: ADfiRe 在物理基 ADR 框架中集成经验关联式与 CFD 参数化，采用既有燃料数据库，支持多燃料与任意地形效应，并引入风致火焰倾斜以区分浮力羽流主导与风主导的蔓延；风场基于适当定义的 Byram 数（Byram number）建模，并显式求解耦合热化学降解与热质输运，采用基于 Arrhenius 的两阶段非均质反应格式耦合固体燃料热解与限氧焦炭/挥发分燃烧。

Result: 模型针对地中海灌木林地五个有充分记录的野外尺度火烧实验完成验证，在蔓延速率（rate of spread, ROS）与火线强度（fireline intensity）等关键指标上呈定量一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明该混合建模策略能够在保持物理机理刻画（多燃料、地形、风致火焰倾斜、耦合热化学与输运）的同时满足实时预报需求，为野火蔓延预测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Real-time wildfire forecasting requires a trade-off between rapid yet physically relevant models and high fidelity but computationally costly models of flame spread over heterogeneous, multiphase solid fuel. The present study introduces ADfiRe, a hybrid framework that integrates empirical correlations and CFD-informed parameterizations into a physics-based advection-diffusion-reaction (ADR) formulation. The key features of ADfiRe are the use of established fuel databases, the capacity to handle multiple fuels, and a methodology to capture the effect of arbitrary topography. Furthermore, it includes the effect of flame tilting due to wind, thus accounting for the differences between plume- and wind-dominated fire propagation. ADfiRe resolves explicitly the coupled thermochemical degradation as well as heat and mass transport mechanisms of the advancing fire front. The wind field is modeled based on an appropriately defined Byram number. The model is validated against five well-documented experimental field-scale fires in Mediterranean shrublands, showing quantitative agreement with key metrics such as the rate of spread (ROS) and the fireline intensity, through a two-stage, Arrhenius-based heterogeneous reaction scheme coupling solid-fuel pyrolysis and oxygen-limited char/volatile combustion.

</details>


### [86] [CGTransformer: A Topology-Aware Autoregressive Framework for Crystal Generation and Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18303)
*Zhennan Zhang,Shaobo Yu,Haoting Zhang,Yu Han,Zhiyue Guo,Junjie Wang,Hao Gao,Jian Sun*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出 CGTransformer（Crystal Graph Transformer）自回归框架，用图嵌入排序策略（graph-embedded sorting strategy）将周期晶体结构序列化，在保持模型容量不变的前提下提升晶体生成、结构预测与性质导向逆向设计的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型可实现统一的晶体生成与逆向设计，但其性能依赖于周期结构的序列化方式；基于坐标的序列化（coordinate-based serialization）会使对称等价或结构相近的晶体产生不同的原子排序，引入与结构连接性无关的变异性。

Method: 提出 Element Symmetry Graph（ESG），利用商图拓扑（quotient-graph topology）与重心嵌入（barycentric embeddings）将周期晶体网络转换为拓扑引导的一维序列，保留关键的局部与对称相关结构信息，并作为自回归生成框架的序列化基础。

Result: CGTransformer 取得高生成有效率，并在 MP-20 基线对比中获得最高单次晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP）匹配率（63.24%）；相同主干网络（backbone）对比显示结构回收率优于基于坐标的排序。在宽带隙半导体（wide-bandgap semiconductors）性质条件逆向设计中，ESG 的稳定、独特、新颖（stable, unique and novel, S.U.N.）率更高，目标性质对齐更强。

Conclusion: 拓扑引导的序列构建（topology-guided sequence construction）是在固定模型容量下改进晶体生成与性质导向设计的有效途径。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models offer a unified route to crystal generation and inverse design, but their performance depends on how periodic structures are serialized. Symmetry equivalent and closely related crystals can receive different atomic orders under coordinate-based serialization, introducing variability unrelated to their structural connectivity. Here, we present CGTransformer (Crystal Graph Transformer), an autoregressive generative framework that addresses this challenge via a graph-embedded sorting strategy. Using quotientgraph topology and barycentric embeddings, Element Symmetry Graph (ESG) converts periodic crystal networks into topology-guided one-dimensional sequences that retain key local and symmetry-related structural information. CGTransformer achieves high generation validity and the highest single shot crystal structure prediction (CSP) match rate among the compared MP-20 baselines (63.24%), while same backbone comparisons show improved structure recovery over coordinate-based ordering. Furthermore, we demonstrate property-conditioned inverse design for wide-bandgap semiconductors, where ESG achieves a much higher stable, unique and novel (S.U.N.) rate and stronger target-property alignment than the coordinate-based baseline. These results establish topology-guided sequence construction as an effective route to improving crystal generation and property-directed design at fixed model capacity.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

### [87] [The Triangular Abrikosov Constant and the Thomas--Fermi $Γ$-Limit of Rotating Bose--Einstein Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17543)
*Giovanni Di Fratta,Valeriy V. Slastikov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在 Γ-收敛（Γ-convergence）变分框架下解决了快速旋转二维玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（two-dimensional Bose–Einstein condensates）中 Abrikosov 系数猜想：有效相互作用系数 β_* 等于三角涡旋晶格（triangular vortex lattice）对应的 Abrikosov 比值 b_△。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对快速旋转二维玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚中 Abrikosov 系数猜想这一悬而未决的问题，寻求在变分框架下严格确定有效相互作用系数的取值及其对应的极限泛函刻画。

Method: 在 Γ-收敛框架下，借助重标度密度的弱 L^2 收敛（weak L^2 convergence）建立重标度 Gross–Pitaevskii 能量泛函的 Γ-收敛；并利用近期 OpenAI 预印本中的尖锐高斯优函数定理（sharp Gaussian-minorant theorem），结合四次能量的傅里叶表示（Fourier representation）与加权 Poisson 恒等式，对任意磁周期最低朗道能级（lowest-Landau-level）态导出下界，再经截断与磁投影推广至无边界条件、无周期性假设的局部全纯态。

Result: 确认 β_* = b_△，且极限 Thomas–Fermi 泛函给出最小能量的首个修正与近极小化子的宏观密度；对辅助重标度模型及其最低朗道能级限制，极小化密度与 Thomas–Fermi 剖面之间存在严格正的极限 L^2 距离；自由元胞系数以 O(L^{-1/5}) 的误差收敛到 b_△（边长 L 的方格）。

Conclusion: Abrikosov 系数猜想在 Γ-收敛变分框架下获得解决，有效相互作用系数被严格识别为三角晶格的 Abrikosov 比值 b_△，并给出了极限泛函与收敛速率方面的定量刻画。

Abstract: We resolve the Abrikosov coefficient conjecture for rapidly rotating two-dimensional Bose--Einstein condensates within the variational framework of $Γ$-convergence. For a harmonic trap, we establish the $Γ$-convergence of the rescaled Gross--Pitaevskii energy functionals in the fast-rotation regime, through weak $L^2$ convergence of the rescaled densities, and identify the effective interaction coefficient $β_*$ with $b_\triangle$, the Abrikosov ratio associated with the triangular vortex lattice. The limiting Thomas--Fermi functional determines the first correction to the minimum energy and the macroscopic density of almost minimizers. For the auxiliary rescaled model and its lowest-Landau-level restriction, we also compute a strictly positive limiting $L^2$ distance between minimizing densities and the Thomas--Fermi profile.
  The identification of the effective interaction coefficient $β_*$ with the triangular Abrikosov ratio $b_\triangle$ relies on the sharp Gaussian-minorant theorem established in a recent OpenAI preprint~\cite[Theorem~1.2]{Atomic}. A Fourier representation of the quartic energy and a weighted Poisson identity yield a lower bound for arbitrary magnetic-periodic lowest-Landau-level states. The sign conditions on the minorant and its Fourier transform give the bound, while the lattice contact conditions identify its coefficient with $b_\triangle$. Cutoff and magnetic projection transfer this estimate to local holomorphic states without boundary conditions or periodicity assumptions. Consequently, the free-cell coefficients converge to $b_\triangle$ with error $O(L^{-1/5})$ on squares of side length $L$.

</details>


### [88] [Inverse energy transfer for 2D Navier--Stokes near a self-similar vortex collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18269)
*Trinh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在二维纳维–斯托克斯（Navier–Stokes）方程的自相似三涡坍缩（three-vortex collapse）附近证明了逆向能量转移（inverse energy transfer），证实了 Novikov 与 Sedov 1979 年的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是检验并证明 Novikov 和 Sedov 于 1979 年关于自相似涡坍缩过程中能量由高频向低频转移的理论预测。

Method: 方法为对二维 Navier–Stokes 方程在自相似三涡坍缩邻域内作解析研究，考察黏性趋于零时收缩至坍缩时刻的公共时间区间内的非线性相互作用能量传输。

Result: 结果是：该区间内净非线性能量转移（net nonlinear transfer）在不断扩大范围的频率截断（frequency cutoff）上收敛到同一正常数，且误差界关于截断一致。

Conclusion: 结论为二维 Navier–Stokes 方程在自相似三涡坍缩附近存在逆向能量级串（inverse energy cascade），从而在数学上确认了 Novikov–Sedov 预测。

Abstract: We prove inverse energy transfer for the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations near a self-similar three-vortex collapse, confirming the 1979 prediction of Novikov and Sedov. Nonlinear interactions transfer kinetic energy from high to low frequencies over a common time interval that shrinks to the collapse time as the viscosity tends to zero. We prove that the net nonlinear transfer over this interval converges to the same positive constant across an expanding range of frequency cutoffs, with quantitative error bounds uniform in the cutoff.

</details>


### [89] [On the dynamics of N classical particles interacting with a complex environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18292)
*Thierry Goudon,Bruno Marcos,Thomas Oudeville,Simona Rota Nodari*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该研究刻画了多粒子系统在与环境相互作用并形成共同势井时的平衡解（静止粒子团簇）及其谱稳定性（spectral stability），给出线性化层面平均位置与速度呈指数衰减，并在简化情形下揭示非线性耗散还包含更慢的速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粒子与环境交换能量导致的运动阻尼（damping）机制已较清楚，但多粒子情形下粒子通过形成共同势井（common well）持续相互作用时，能量耗散机制尚不明确。

Method: 识别系统的平衡解，即处于静止状态的粒子团簇（clusters of particles at rest），并建立其谱稳定性；随后在线性化（linearized）层面分析平均位置与速度的衰减行为，并回到非线性问题在若干简化情形下讨论耗散速率。

Result: 线性化层面显示平均位置/速度对（mean position/velocity pair）以指数快速衰减；非线性情形下，耗散机制还涉及慢得多的速率（far slower rates），这解释了在个体轨迹（individual trajectories）上难以观察到弛豫（relaxation）行为的原因。

Conclusion: 多粒子系统的耗散不能仅由快速的线性指数衰减刻画，非线性效应引入的慢速率使得个体轨迹上的弛豫表现不明显；结果为理解共同势井中多体能量耗散提供了结构性认识。

Abstract: The interactions of a particle with its environment eventually lead to the damping of its motion. This phenomenom is well understood when considering a single particle exchanging energy with the environment. The situation is definitely more complex when many particles are considered: the energy dissipation mechanisms are not clear whenever particles create a common well in which they keep interacting.  In this framework, we identify the equilibrium solutions, that consist in clusters of particles at rest, and we establish their spectral stability. We show that the mean position/velocity pair is damped exponentially fast at the linearized level.  Coming back to the non linear problem, we can show, at least in some simplified cases, that the dissipation mechanisms also involve far slower rates, that explain the difficulty in observing the relaxation behavior on the individual trajectories.

</details>


### [90] [The BCC Lattice Maximizes the First Positive Neumann Eigenvalue among 3D Lattice Voronoi Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18368)
*Kaixin Deng,Senping Luo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在固定余体积（covolume）的三维格（lattice）中，体心立方格（body-centered cubic lattice, BCC）唯一地（至多差正交变换）使其 Voronoi 胞腔的第一正 Neumann 特征值（first positive Neumann eigenvalue）达到最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机；仅可看出问题属于固定余体积格中 Voronoi 胞腔 Neumann 特征值的极值优化（extremal optimization）问题。

Method: 摘要仅给出结论性陈述，未涉及具体证明方法或技术手段。

Result: 在三维固定余体积格中，体心立方格（BCC）唯一地最大化其 Voronoi 胞腔的第一正 Neumann 特征值，唯一性至多相差正交变换（orthogonal transformations）。

Conclusion: 体心立方格是三维固定余体积下该 Neumann 特征值优化问题的唯一极值格（至多差正交变换）。

Abstract: It is shown that, among three-dimensional lattices of fixed covolume, the body-centered cubic lattice maximizes the first positive Neumann eigenvalue of its Voronoi cell uniquely, up to orthogonal transformations.

</details>


### [91] [Maxwell's equations with thin wires in critical scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18446)
*Ben Schweizer,David Wiedemann*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文针对电磁波入射细导线阵列（wire array）的情形，推导了两种情况（理想导体导线与高导电导线）在临界标度下的有效方程（effective equations），揭示导线在临界标度下表现为振荡器并满足含通量二阶导数的界面关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细导线阵列间距 η 很小时，直接求解时谐麦克斯韦方程组（time-harmonic Maxwell equations）代价高昂，需通过渐近/均匀化（homogenization）手段获得可用的有效模型，并厘清不同参数标度下导线对极限场的影响。

Method: 以导线间距 η>0 为小参数，对满足时谐麦克斯韦方程组的电场 E^η 与磁场 H^η 作渐近分析，分别考察理想导体（perfectly conducting）与高导电（highly conducting）导线，并区分非临界标度（non-critical scaling）与临界标度（critical scaling）两种情形。

Result: 非临界标度下，导线或者不产生任何效应，或者使极限电场沿导线方向的切向分量（tangential component）在有效界面（effective interface）上消失；临界标度下出现有趣效应——导线充当振荡器（oscillators），纵向电场与横向磁场跳跃量之间存在关系，且该关系涉及某个通量（flux quantity）的二阶导数。

Conclusion: 细导线阵列的宏观行为强烈依赖于参数标度：仅临界标度能产生非平凡的有效界面条件，该条件以二阶导数形式耦合纵向电场与横向磁场跳跃，可用于替代对原始细尺度结构的直接数值求解。

Abstract: We derive effective equations for a situation in which electromagnetic waves hit an array of very thin wires. We study two cases, perfectly conducting wires and highly conducting wires, and investigate in both the critical scaling of the parameters. The electric field $E^η$ and the magnetic field $H^η$ satisfy the time-harmonic Maxwell equations, and the small parameter $η>0$ is the spacing of the wires. A non-critical scaling of the wires implies either that the wires have no effect or that the tangential component of the limiting electric field in the direction of the wires vanishes along the effective interface. In the critical scaling, we observe an interesting effect: The wires act as oscillators and there is a relation between the electric field in longitudinal direction and the jump of the magnetic field in transversal direction involving second derivatives of a flux quantity.

</details>

### [92] [Many-body Response to Spin-Independent and Spin-Dependent Dark Matter Scattering in a Superfluid Helium-3 Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17432)
*A. Ting,N. Darvishi,S. Autti,L. Bloomfield,A. Casey,N. Eng,P. Franchini,R. P. Haley,P. J. Heikkinen,A. Jennings,A. Kemp,E. Leason,J. March-Russell,J. Monroe,D. Münstermann,M. T. Noble,J. R. Prance,X. Rojas,T. Salmon,J. Saunders,J. Smirnov,R. Smith,M. D. Thompson,A. Thomson,V. Tsepelin,S. M. West,L. Whitehead,D. E. Zmeev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作对超流氦-3（superfluid $^3$He）靶中亚-GeV暗物质（dark matter, DM）散射率进行了第一性原理多体计算，发现费米-狄拉克统计（Fermi-Dirac statistics）导致运动学增强，可改善QUEST-DMC实验对低质量暗物质的探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUEST-DMC实验采用极低温超流$^3$He靶探测亚-GeV暗物质，而轻暗物质的低动量散射对介质的多体结构敏感，因此必须考虑介质的集体行为，单原子近似可能不足。

Method: 针对自旋无关（spin-independent）与自旋相关（spin-dependent）相互作用，在本质上属于量子多体介质的超流$^3$He中开展暗物质散射率的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculation）。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验低能有效理论中四光子算符$(F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu})^2$ 的正定性界（positivity bound）在单圈修正下是否被Yukawa相互作用破坏，并考察该负跑动（negative running）对弱引力猜想（Weak Gravity Conjecture）及黑洞极端性条件的影响。

Abstract: We study causality and unitarity constraints at one loop on a low-energy effective theory containing a photon, a massless scalar coupled to it through dilaton-like interactions, and a light neutral Dirac fermion with a Yukawa coupling to the scalar. In this setup, non-gravitational loop effects induced by the Yukawa interaction can drive the coefficient of the four-photon $(F_{μν}F^{μν})^2$ operator negative at low energies, apparently violating the tree-level positivity bound. Nevertheless, we find that these negative values are consistent with one-loop constraints from causality and unitarity, thanks to the low-energy contribution from the massless scalar to the relevant dispersion relation. We also examine the implications of this negative running for the Weak Gravity Conjecture in the magnetic limit of Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton black holes, since the RG-evolved Wilson coefficients can a priori shift the extremality condition towards the subextremal region. We find, however, that this shift is obstructed by the fermion mass induced by the same Yukawa coupling, which prevents the fermion from contributing to the running of the relevant operators down to the low-energy scales required to make the black holes subextremal. Finally, we discuss the relevance of negative running to loop-level positivity bounds in the SMEFT and to light-by-light scattering in the presence of a light scalar.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 在非超对称杂化弦紧致化中，E/N=8/3 源于耦合向量成比例与规范等级固定的情形，而直接通量破缺可使向量独立并使E/N偏离该值，偏离幅度受Hermitian Yang-Mills方程与Bianchi恒等式约束；无QCD耦合轴子的光子耦合存在几何上界。

Abstract: We study the photon couplings of QCD axions and axion-like particles in non-supersymmetric heterotic string theory on Calabi-Yau threefolds with line bundles. After the Stückelberg mechanism and anomaly matching are taken into account, the QCD axion direction is determined by the QCD axion-coupling coefficients together with the axion kinetic matrix. When the physical electromagnetic and QCD axion-coupling vectors are proportional with the ratio fixed by the gauge levels, the QCD axion has $E/N=8/3$ for $k_3=k_2=1$ and $k_Y=5/3$, independently of the axion kinetic matrix. It is found that direct flux breaking of the visible $SO(16)_{\rm v}$ can produce independent QCD and electromagnetic axion-coupling vectors for the same level assignment. The Hermitian Yang-Mills equations and the heterotic Bianchi identity then restrict the departure from $8/3$. We derive analytic bounds for backgrounds with and without five-brane charge under assumptions about the axion kinetic terms and low-energy matching. An $SU(5)$-like model and a direct-flux Standard-Model-like model illustrate proportional and independent Green-Schwarz coupling vectors, respectively. For axions with no QCD coupling, we derive a geometric upper bound on their photon couplings and evaluate it in an explicit compactification.

</details>


### [98] [Metastable vortex string decay in a brane picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18019)
*Akifumi Chitose,Hajime Fukuda,Kantaro Ohmori*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 Type IIA 型膜（brane）构型中将亚稳弦（metastable string）的衰变描述为一个 bounce 解，并在特定的 N=1 超对称 U(2) 规范理论中与场论结果进行对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋弦（vortex string）可通过产生't Hooft-Polyakov 单极子—反单极子对而断裂，其衰变率需要非微扰的 bounce 描述；本文旨在用弦论/膜构型给出该衰变的几何图像并与场论结果对照。

Abstract: We describe the decay of a metastable string as a bounce in a brane configuration. We consider a particular $\mathcal N=1$ supersymmetric $\mathrm{U}(2)$ gauge theory with cascade Higgsing $\mathrm{U}(2)\to \mathrm{U}(1)^2 \to \mathrm{U}(1)$, where the vortex string can break by creating a pair of 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole and anti-monopole. The theory is engineered in a Type IIA brane setup, where the metastable string is realized as a D2-brane stretched between NS5- and D4-branes. The string breaks when the endpoints of the D2-brane, moving along the NS5- and D4-branes, meet so that the D2-brane shrinks to nothing. We check that the tension of the D2-brane reproduces that of the vortex string obtained numerically in the field theory, with the ratio approaching one in the limit where the vortex string becomes BPS. Within the thin-wall approximation, we derive the bounce action from the Dirac-Born-Infeld action of the D2-brane and find that it agrees with that of the field theory, with the monopole mass given by the area swept by the D2-brane. As a consistency check, we numerically construct a bounce solution of the D2-brane equation of motion under simplifying assumptions, whose action agrees with the thin-wall result in the regime where our description is reliable.

</details>


### [99] [On the origin and geometry of anomaly-related poles in Double Deeply Virtual Compton Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18386)
*Shohini Bhattacharya,Alexander Sang Yi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将双深度虚康普顿散射（DDVCS）胶子通道单圈箱图（box amplitude）中与反常（anomaly）相关的极点，追溯到费曼参数域边界上的次领头朗道奇点（subleading Landau singularity），并给出其红外散度、维数正规化（DR）、有限夸克质量与动量转移的统一几何解释。
