  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已在DDVCS胶子-initiated通道的单圈箱振幅中观测到反常相关极点，其留数携带手征反常（chiral anomaly）与迹反常（trace anomaly）对应的胶子算符结构，但这些极点在箱振幅中的来源长期不明，因此需要厘清其物理与几何起源。

Method: 作者定位该极点为箱图费曼参数域边界上的次领头朗道奇点：一个参数趋于零，其余三个传播子构成三角子图（triangle subdiagram）；随后在维数正规化、非零动量转移t与有限夸克质量m框架下系统考察该边界，并分析横向胶子极化投影算符（transverse gluon-polarization projector）的作用，最后给出三种红外方案下极化的与非极化的DDVCS完整单圈结果。

Result: 在t=m=0时，边界贡献在四维下呈幂次红外发散（power infrared divergent），而DR的解析延拓将其隐藏，在d≈4处不产生1/ε极点；在临界维数d=6处同一端点呈对数发散并给出极点。非零t与有限m分别正则化同一边界区域，给出已知的反常相关1/t极点与文献未报道的1/m²行为；横向极化投影算符可消除显式极化矢量下仍残留的反常相关贡献。各图求和后，同一演化核（evolution kernel）同时支配DR中的红外极点以及各极化通道内的ln(Q²/(−t))与ln(Q²/m²)对数。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了单圈箱振幅中这些反常相关极点的共同几何起源，即费曼参数域边界上的次领头朗道奇点，并统一了不同红外正则化方案下的极点与对数结构。

Abstract: Anomaly-related poles have been reported in one-loop box amplitudes for the gluon-initiated channel of double deeply virtual Compton scattering (DDVCS). The residues of these poles carry the gluonic operator structures associated with the chiral and trace anomalies. The origin of the poles within the box amplitude has remained unclear. In this work, we trace both poles to a subleading Landau singularity on the boundary of the box's Feynman-parameter domain, where one parameter vanishes and the remaining three propagators form a triangle subdiagram. We examine the same boundary using dimensional regularization (DR), nonzero momentum transfer $t$, and a finite quark mass $m$. At $t=m=0$, the boundary contribution is power infrared divergent in four dimensions. Analytic continuation in DR conceals this divergence, which gives no $1/ε$ pole about $d=4$. At the critical dimension $d=6$, the same endpoint diverges logarithmically and gives a pole in the continued integral. Nonzero $t$ and finite $m$ regulate the same boundary region, yielding the known anomaly-related $1/t$ pole and a previously unreported $1/m^2$ behavior, respectively. We also show that a transverse gluon-polarization projector can eliminate an anomaly-related contribution that survives with explicit gluon polarization vectors. Finally, we present complete one-loop results for polarized and unpolarized DDVCS with all three infrared prescriptions. After summing the diagrams, the same evolution kernel governs the infrared pole in DR and the logarithms $\ln(Q^2/(-t))$ and $\ln(Q^2/m^2)$ within each polarization channel. This work establishes the common geometric origin of these anomaly-related poles within one-loop box amplitudes.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Mechanical acclimation of bacterial growth and division across generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18273)
*Chenyu Jin,G. H. Philipp Nguyen,René Wittmann,Hartmut Löwen,Anupam Sengupta*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究追踪大肠杆菌（Escherichia coli）从液体培养转移至玻璃附着或玻璃-凝胶受限条件后的跨代生长与分裂，发现机械受限引发多代适应过程，生长与尺寸调控呈现不同弛豫时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细菌在自然与工程环境中常遭遇机械受限（mechanical confinement），但机械条件突变如何跨代重组生长与分裂仍缺乏理解。

Method: 采用单细胞延时显微成像（single-cell time-lapse microscopy）跨代追踪大肠杆菌，并结合随机细胞周期模型（stochastic cell-cycle model）解析不同尺寸控制策略的代际相关性特征。

Result: 受限立即降低伸长速率（elongation rate），随后出现依赖受限强度的多代机械适应（mechanical acclimation）；适应期分裂行为更异质，细胞暂时偏离经典加法式（adder-like）尺寸调控并表现定时式（timer-like）相关特征；仅改变伸长速率无法解释该转变，而出生尺寸分布保持有界且形状基本不变。

Conclusion: 机械受限下的细菌尺寸控制是动力学非平衡过程（dynamical nonequilibrium process）：生长与分裂可暂时重组，但整体尺寸稳态（size homeostasis）得以维持，且加法式调控先于伸长速率完全恢复而复原。

Abstract: Bacteria frequently encounter mechanical confinement in natural and engineered environments, yet how an abrupt change in mechanical conditions reorganizes growth and division across generations remains poorly understood. Using single-cell time-lapse microscopy, we tracked Escherichia coli across generations following transfer from liquid culture to glass-attached or glass-gel-confined conditions. This mechanical confinement causes an immediate reduction in elongation rate, followed by a multi-generational mechanical acclimation process whose dynamics depend systematically on confinement strength. During this relaxation, division behavior becomes more heterogeneous and cells temporarily depart from canonical adder-like size regulation, exhibiting timer-like correlation signatures. Using a stochastic cell-cycle model that resolves the generation-dependent correlation signatures of different size-control strategies, we show that changes in elongation rate alone cannot account for the observed transition in growth-division coordination. Despite this transient reorganization, the birth-size distribution remains bounded and largely preserves its shape throughout acclimation. Notably, adder-like regulation is restored before the elongation rate fully recovers, revealing distinct relaxation timescales for growth and size-control dynamics. These results establish bacterial size control under mechanical confinement as a dynamical nonequilibrium process in which growth and division can transiently reorganize while global size homeostasis is maintained.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Composition Driven Band Alignment Evolution in Grafted Si/GeSn Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17453)
*Pargam Vashishtha,Samuel A. Haessly,Yang Liu,Tsung-Han Tsai,Kushal Dahal,Shui-Qing Yu,Edward K. Huang,Nanditha M. Dissanayake,Zhenqiang Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用X射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, XPS）与Kraut芯能级法，首次实验确定了Sn含量8.8%–12.4%范围内半导体嫁接（semiconductor-grafted）Si/GeSn异质结的能带对齐（band alignment）随组分的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格失配（lattice-mismatched）IV族半导体异质结的能带对齐决定其电子结构与载流子输运，是能带结构工程（band-structure engineering）的基础；尽管Si/GeSn异质结受到关注，其组分依赖的能带对齐尚缺乏实验确证。

Method: 采用X射线光电子能谱结合Kraut芯能级法（Kraut's core-level method），对Sn含量从8.8%至12.4%的半导体嫁接Si/GeSn异质结进行实验测量，提取价带偏移（valence-band offset）与有效导带偏移（conduction-band offset）。

Result: 随Sn组分增加，价带偏移由约0.29 eV系统性增至约0.57 eV，而有效导带偏移由约0.30 eV降至约0.12 eV。

Conclusion: 能带对齐的演化归因于GeSn价带顶（valence-band maximum）上移，其来源包括增强的p-p轨道杂化（p-p orbital hybridization）、减弱的成键—反键劈裂（bonding-antibonding splitting）以及压应变引起的重空穴带抬升（heavy-hole band lifting）；该结果建立了合金组分与界面能带对齐的实验关系，为晶格失配IV族异质结构提供定量化能带工程框架。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling band alignment in lattice-mismatched group-IV semiconductor-based heterostructures is fundamental to rational band-structure engineering, as it governs the electronic structure and carrier transport in heterointegrated semiconductor systems. Despite growing interest in Si/GeSn heterostructures, the composition-dependent band alignment has not been experimentally established. Here, we experimentally establish the evolution of band alignment in semiconductor-grafted Si/GeSn heterostructures with contents ranging from 8.8% to 12.4% using X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy and Kraut's core-level method. The valence-band offset increases systematically from ~0.29 to ~0.57 eV with increasing Sn composition, whereas the corresponding effective conduction-band offset decreases from ~0.30 to ~0.12 eV. The observed band-alignment evolution is attributed to an upward shift of the GeSn valence-band maximum arising from enhanced p-p orbital hybridization, reduced bonding-antibonding splitting, and compressive-strain-induced heavy-hole band lifting. These findings establish the experimental relationship between alloy composition and interfacial band alignment in semiconductor-grafted Si/GeSn heterostructures, providing a quantitative framework for rational band-structure engineering in lattice-mismatched group-IV semiconductor heterostructures.

</details>


### [102] [Deformation Kinetics of Charged Nanoclusters via Surface Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17514)
*Efstratios M. Kritikos,William A. Goddard,Paul M. Bellan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文建立解析模型描述低温带电纳米团簇（charged nanoclusters）因表面静电应力与表面张力应力失衡而经表面扩散（surface diffusion）发生形变的动力学，并与冰纳米团簇分子动力学模拟定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温带电纳米团簇在表面静电应力（electrostatic stress）与表面张力应力（surface tension stress）不平衡时会发生形变，但其形变动力学缺乏可解析描述的模型。

Method: 基于团簇尺寸、净电荷、表面张力、局部曲率、温度与扩散率（diffusivity），推导出描述表面扩散主导形变动力学的解析模型（analytical model），并预测表面张力或静电应力占主导时的形变时间。

Result: 模型预测表面张力主导导致团簇塌缩（collapse）、静电应力主导导致团簇伸长（elongation）；与含溶剂化电子的冰纳米团簇分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟比较，仅用一个拟合参数即达到定量一致。

Conclusion: 该模型可预测大气气溶胶（atmospheric aerosols）与复杂等离子体（complex plasmas）中带电纳米团簇的形变动力学。

Abstract: Low-temperature charged nanoclusters deform, primarily via surface diffusion, when surface electrostatic stresses and surface tension stresses are not balanced. We derive an analytical model for the kinetics of this process, based on the cluster size, net charge, surface tension, local curvature, temperature, and diffusivity. The model predicts the deformation time when either surface tension or electrostatic stress dominates, leading respectively to the collapse or elongation of the cluster. Comparison with molecular dynamics simulations of ice nanoclusters with solvated electrons shows quantitative agreement with a single fitted parameter. The model can predict the deformation kinetics of charged nanoclusters in atmospheric aerosols and complex plasmas.

</details>


### [103] [High-harmonic generation along a structural phase transition in silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17516)
*D. N. Purschke,D. Vick,A. Cardenas,N. Haram,P. Bastani,S. Gholam-Mirzaei,S. Mokhtari,V. Jelic,J. Chen,J. Canlas,J. Tordiff,Md. W. Rahman,A. Yu. Naumov,D. M. Villeneuve,A. Staudte,M. Salomons,R. E. F. Silva,A. Jimenez-Galan,G. Vampa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了经不同剂量 Ga 聚焦离子束（Ga$^+$ FIB）处理的硅中高次谐波产生（high-harmonic generation, HHG），识别出四个不同剂量区间，发现晶态向非晶态相变伴随低阶谐波产率的巨大增强，并与表面膨胀强烈相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究硅从晶态到非晶态转变过程中强场动力学（strong-field dynamics）的变化，并区分化学改性与结构改性对 HHG 的相对贡献。

Method: 以可变剂量 Ga$^+$ FIB 处理硅样品，结合原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）测量表面膨胀、能量色散 X 射线光谱（energy dispersive x-ray spectroscopy）表征损伤成分，并采用强非共振脉冲进行激光退火（laser-annealing）。

Result: 识别出四个性质不同的剂量区间；临界剂量以下观察到兼具晶态与非晶态特征的混合相 HHG 谱；激光退火优先向非晶区沉积能量；极高剂量下样品对激光损伤敏感性增加，且与 FIB 诱导刻蚀相关，损伤处形成富 Ga 纳米颗粒（Ga-rich nanoparticles）与表面孔隙。

Conclusion: FIB 剂量调控可系统改变硅的相态与 HHG 响应；激光退火提供了分离化学与结构因素对强场动力学影响的手段；高剂量下的激光损伤与刻蚀及富 Ga 纳米结构形成相关联。

Abstract: We explore high-harmonic generation (HHG) in silicon treated by a variable dose from a Ga focused ion beam (Ga$^+$ FIB). We identify four qualitatively different dose regimes as the sample transitions from a crystalline to amorphous phase. The phase transition manifests as a giant enhancement in yield of low-order harmonics, which is strongly correlated with surface swelling as measured by atomic force microscopy. Below the critical dose, we observe a mixed phase regime where the HHG spectra display elements of both crystalline and amorphous silicon. We show how the samples can be laser-annealed with intense non-resonant pulses, where energy is deposited preferentially into amorphous regions, allowing us to disentangle the relative importance of chemical and structural modifications to the strong-field dynamics. At very high dose, we observe an increased sensitivity to laser-induced damage, which is correlated with FIB-induced etching. Energy dispersive x-ray spectroscopy reveals that laser damage leads to the formation of Ga-rich nanoparticles and porosity at the surface.

</details>


### [104] [Data-driven exploration of the strength-ductility synergy in refractory high-entropy alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17615)
*Feng Zhao,Jingya Zhang,Yin Zhang,Huiling Duan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出基于数据驱动位错基因组（dislocation genome）方法的多尺度框架，构建跨成分的刃位错（edge dislocation）运动能量景观，并据此建立连接宏观强度与原子尺度局部阻力的标度律（scaling law），发展出面向BCC合金的固溶强化（solid-solution strengthening）新模型并用于强度—塑性兼顾的高通量合金设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难熔高熵合金（refractory high-entropy alloys, RHEAs）高温强度优异，但现有研究集中于近等原子比成分，成分空间大部分尚未探索；同时缺乏能跨成分定量关联原子尺度阻力与宏观强度的理论工具。

Method: 采用基于数据驱动的位错基因组方法构建多尺度框架，计算不同成分下刃位错运动的能量景观（energy landscape），提取局部构型力（local configurational force）并耦合入位错动力学（dislocation dynamics）模拟。

Result: 揭示出由局部构型力涨落控制的宏观强度与原子尺度局部阻力之间的标度律，并据此建立BCC合金刃位错固溶强化新模型；结合强度与塑性开展难熔合金的数据驱动成分筛选。

Conclusion: 所提方法与理论具有可迁移性，可推广至其他BCC合金体系，并指导高通量合金设计（high-throughput alloy design）。

Abstract: Refractory high-entropy alloys (RHEAs) have attracted increasing attention for their exceptional ability to retain strength at high temperatures. However, current research mainly concentrates on compositions close to equimolar alloys, leaving a large part of the composition space unexplored. Here, we develop a multiscale framework based on a data-driven dislocation genome method that constructs energy landscapes for edge dislocation motion across various compositions. The local configurational force derived from the energy landscape is incorporated into dislocation dynamics simulations, revealing a scaling law connecting macroscale strength and atomic-scale local resistance governed by fluctuations in the local configurational force. Based on this scaling law, a new solid-solution strengthening model for edge dislocation in BCC alloys is developed, followed by a data-driven exploration of refractory alloys considering both strength and ductility. The developed method and theory are adaptable, allowing for extension to other BCC alloy systems and guiding high-throughput alloy design.

</details>


### [105] [Unified constitutive model of body-centered cubic metals informed by discrete dislocation dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17617)
*Jiarui Zhang,Yin Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作面向体心立方（body-centered cubic, BCC）金属，构建了含相对论效应（relativistic effects）的螺位错（screw dislocation）温度相关迁移率律，并经由离散位错动力学（discrete dislocation dynamics, DDD）自下而上模拟导出统一本构模型，实现从微观机制到连续介质响应（continuum response）的桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测BCC金属在宽广温度与应变率区间内的塑性流动（plastic flow），需要区分结构阻力（structural resistance）与动力学贡献（kinetic contributions）；既有唯象描述难以给出二者叠加的明确物理判据，并存在加和性困境（additivity dilemma）。

Method: 将热激活理论（thermal activation theory）与相对论效应结合，建立螺位错的温度相关迁移率律；在不同初始位错密度构型上进行单轴拉伸与压缩的DDD模拟，厘清塑性应变率与应力、温度、位错密度之间的关联，并据此推导含温度与应变率的统一本构模型（constitutive model）。

Result: 模拟定量刻画了声子拖曳（phonon drag）超过热激活的交叉点（crossover point），为反常热硬化（anomalous thermal hardening）提供了基于机制的解释；模型以显式物理判据解决了结构阻力与竞争性动力学机制的叠加问题。

Conclusion: 该框架可在宽温度与应变率区间内对强度与蠕变（creep）行为作物理一致的预测，使合金设计由唯象描述转向透明、基于机制的设计路径。

Abstract: Predicting plastic flow in body-centered cubic metals across temperature and strain-rate regimes requires distinguishing structural resistance from kinetic contributions. Here, we develop a temperature dependent mobility law for screw dislocation, integrating thermal activation theory and relativistic effects. Through discrete dislocation dynamics simulations, uniaxial tension and compression simulations were conducted on configurations with different initial dislocation densities, elucidating the correlation between plastic strain rate and key parameters such as stress, temperature, and dislocation density. Subsequently, a unified constitutive model incorporating temperature and strain rate is derived based on microscopic mechanisms. The model naturally bridges microscopic mechanisms to continuum response, resolving the additivity dilemma by establishing explicit physical criteria for how structural resistances and competing kinetic mechanisms superimpose. Through rigorous bottom-up simulations, we quantitatively capture the crossover point where phonon drag overtakes thermal activation, providing a mechanism-based explanation of anomalous thermal hardening. This work enables physically consistent predictions of strength and creep behavior across broad temperature and strain-rate regimes, advancing beyond phenomenological descriptions toward transparent, mechanism-based alloy design.

</details>


### [106] [Machine-learning charge density for electronic materials and devices simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17627)
*Qingyun Zhang,Yang Hu,Aixia Zhang,Zehua Sun,Jiawei Yan,Xiaohao Zhou,Wei Lu,Youqi Ke*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出DeltaChargeNet：一种机器学习电荷密度模型，与全势紧束缚muffin-tin轨道（FP-TB-MTO）方法结合，实现材料与器件的电子结构与量子输运模拟。模型以等变图卷积网络预测Δρ（DFT密度与原子叠加密度之差），并施加电中性约束及残差补偿，在HgCdTe合金及Au/HgCdTe/Au器件上验证，势重构误差约0.1 eV，并演示了11220原子红外探测器的量子输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自洽DFT自洽场迭代（self-consistent field loop）计算成本高，难以支撑含界面与无序的大尺度器件模拟；需要以低线性标度代价获得高保真电荷密度，同时保持第一性原理级精度。

Method: 构建等变图卷积网络（equivariant graph convolutional network）预测Δρ，即DFT密度与原子叠加密度之差，并施加非均匀体系所需的电中性约束（charge neutrality constraint）；另设次级ML残差电荷模型校正主模型误差。所得密度输入全相对论FP-TB-MTO框架并与非平衡格林函数（non-equilibrium Green's function）方法结合。

Result: 在HgCdTe无序合金（含空位缺陷）中最大能带能量误差低于10 meV；在Au/HgCdTe/Au器件中，电中性约束抑制了0.68–1.88 eV的长波电荷偏差引起的静电势畸变，残差补偿进一步修正剩余0.16–0.46 eV电势失配，层分辨电势（含界面台阶与体态平台）与全自洽DFT吻合在0.1 eV内。

Conclusion: DeltaChargeNet将第一性原理精度与线性标度推理相结合，可在不运行完整DFT自洽场循环的情况下实现大尺度器件（如11220原子红外探测器）的量子输运模拟。

Abstract: We present a machine-learning (ML) charge density model, DeltaChargeNet, integrated with the full-potential tight-binding muffin-tin orbital (FP-TB-MTO) method for electronic structure and quantum transport simulations of materials and devices. The ML model predicts electron density from atomic structures with low linear-scaling cost, while FP-TB-MTO provides an accurate tight-binding representation of the system Hamiltonian. To achieve high-fidelity ML density for complex devices with interfaces and disorder, we train an equivariant graph convolutional network to predict $Δρ$-the difference between DFT and atomic superposition densities-under a charge neutrality constraint required for inhomogeneous systems. A secondary ML residual charge model compensates for errors in the primary $Δρ$ model. The predicted density is then used within the full-relativistic FP-TB-MTO framework combined with the non-equilibrium Green's function method. We validate our method on bulk HgCdTe disordered alloys (including vacancy defects) and Au/HgCdTe/Au disordered devices. The maximum band energy error is below 10 meV for bulk HgCdTe. For devices, the charge neutrality constraint suppresses long-wavelength charge bias that would otherwise distort the electrostatic potential by 0.68-1.88 eV near the device center, while residual compensation corrects the remaining 0.16-0.46 eV potential mismatch, essential for reproducing the correct potential barrier profile. Together, these corrections reproduce the layer-resolved potential, including interface steps and bulk-like plateaus, within 0.1 eV of fully self-consistent DFT. Finally, we demonstrate quantum transport in a 11220-atom infrared detector. By combining first-principles accuracy with linear-scaling inference, DeltaChargeNet enables large-scale device simulations without the full DFT self-consistent field loop.

</details>


### [107] [Crystal Structure Prediction from Search to Learning: A 30-Year Expedition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17670)
*Jianjun Hu,Ying Feng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了1996年至2026年10月间的晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP）方法、软件与基准记录，按候选构造与探索策略分类，并指出尚无普遍占优的方法，深度生成模型亦缺乏实验先验验证的独立发现案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSP需从化学组成与物理条件确定周期性原子或分子排布，三十年来已由化学构造与全局能量优化转向融合对称性、学习势、自适应搜索与深度生成模型的混合路线，亟需系统整理与证据审计。

Method: 综述方法、软件、综述与基准记录，覆盖无机与分子晶体；按候选构造与探索方式分类（模板与离散构造、随机搜索、进化搜索、群智能与差分进化、能量景观探索、自适应机器学习引导搜索、学习式生成），并区分表示、弛豫、排序与物理合格性判定。

Result: 基准显示无普遍占优方法：46目标排行榜快照中最佳模板方法top-1恢复率高于领先生成模型，但配对检验显示与若干生成方法无统计显著差异。另审计68个经实验检验材料（52个结构经衍射或显微确认），其中23个预测先于实验，多来自第一性原理全局搜索。

Conclusion: 在综述证据范围内，未见由深度生成模型在实验前预测结构并被独立确认的发现；文末总结当前CSP算法的主要局限与未来研究优先方向。

Abstract: Crystal structure prediction (CSP) determines periodic atomic or molecular arrangements from chemical composition and physical conditions. Over three decades, CSP has evolved from chemical construction and global energy optimization to hybrid approaches combining symmetry, learned potentials, adaptive search, and deep generative models. This survey reviews method, software, review, and benchmark records from 1996 to October 2026, covering inorganic and molecular crystals, including formula-conditioned diffusion, flow, and autoregressive models that preserve composition. We classify methods by candidate construction and exploration: template and discrete construction, random search, evolutionary search, swarm and differential evolution, energy-landscape exploration, adaptive machine-learning-guided search, and learned generation. We distinguish representation, relaxation, ranking, and physical qualification. Benchmarks reveal no universally dominant approach. In a 46-target leaderboard snapshot, the best-performing template method achieves higher top-1 recovery than leading generative models, but paired tests show no statistically significant differences from several generative competitors. We also audit predictions for 68 experimentally examined materials, including 52 with structures confirmed by diffraction or microscopy. Predictions for 23 of the 68 preceded experiment, mostly through first-principles global search. Within the reviewed evidence, we find no independently confirmed discovery in which a deep generative model predicted the structure before experiment. We conclude with the principal limitations of current CSP algorithms and priorities for future research.

</details>


### [108] [Electric Fields Turn Vacancy-trapping Grain Boundaries into Li-Ion Pathways in Li2O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17679)
*Youjeong Choi,Po-yen Chem,Tasuku Sugiura,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以Li2O体相及晶界（grain boundaries, GBs）为对象，结合机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potential）与Bader电荷预测模型进行电场分子动力学模拟，表明电场驱动离子输运不能仅由平衡态热力学描述，晶界输运取决于电场方向与扩散路径之间的夹角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体中离子输运传统上以平衡扩散（equilibrium diffusion）理解，但电池中的离子实际由电场驱动，因此需要检验电场驱动输运是否遵循平衡行为，并厘清晶界这一关键微观结构在电场下的作用。

Method: 采用电场分子动力学（electric-field molecular dynamics），并将机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potential）与Bader电荷预测模型相结合，对体相Li2O及其晶界（grain boundaries, GBs）进行模拟。

Result: 立方Li2O中Li扩散在零场下为各向同性（isotropic），加电场后变为取向相关（orientation dependent），原因是Li沿有限的晶格路径跳跃且电场对各路径的偏置不等；在晶界处该各向异性表现为定性差异：强捕获Li空位但场响应强的晶界在电场下释放空位，而场响应弱的晶界尽管空位捕获较弱仍将其保留。

Conclusion: 电场驱动的晶界输运并非仅由静态热力学（static thermodynamics）决定，而由电场与构成晶粒扩散路径之间的夹角所主导；该结论对基于平衡假设理解电池离子输运提出修正。

Abstract: Ion transport in solids has conventionally been understood from equilibrium diffusion, yet ions in batteries are driven by electric fields. Does field-driven ion transport follow equilibrium behavior? We examined this question in bulk Li2O and at its grain boundaries (GBs) using electric-field molecular dynamics combining a machine learning interatomic potential with a Bader charge prediction model. Although Li diffusion in cubic Li2O is isotropic at zero field, an electric field renders it orientation dependent because Li hops along a limited set of crystallographic pathways that the field biases unequally. At GBs, this anisotropy becomes a qualitative difference: a GB that strongly traps Li vacancies but exhibits a strong field response releases them under a field, whereas a weakly responsive GB retains them despite weaker vacancy trapping. These results show that field-driven GB transport is governed not by static thermodynamics alone but by the angle between the electric field and the diffusion pathways of the constituent grains.

</details>


### [109] [Pressure-driven evolution of crystal and molecular structures reshapes excited-state competition in coronene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17698)
*Wenju Zhou,Shuhua Yuan,Dominique Laniel,Yoshifumi Hashikawa,Philip Dalladay-Simpson,Jun Deng,Jianbo Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Kejun Bu,Akun Liang,Simon Parsons,Dominik Daisenberger,Egor Koemets,Hirofumi Ishii,Weiwei Dong,Huiyang Gou,Qingyang Hu,Yang Ding,Martina Vrankić,Takeshi Nakagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过高压单晶X射线衍射、拉曼光谱、光谱学与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，揭示分子几何形变与晶体堆积如何共同调控晕苯（coronene）的光致发光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子纳米碳（molecular nanocarbons）在有机器件中具应用前景，但分子几何结构与晶体堆积如何共同决定π共轭分子材料（π-conjugated molecular materials）物理性质仍是基础难题。

Method: 结合高压单晶X射线衍射（high-pressure single-crystal X-ray diffraction）、拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）、光学光谱（optical spectroscopy）与密度泛函理论计算，建立结构变化与竞争性发光态演化之间的对应关系。

Result: 晶体多晶型（crystal polymorphism）改变自由激子（free-exciton, FE）与自陷激子（self-trapped-exciton, STE）发光的平衡；压力诱导的分子弯曲（molecular bending）导致STE发光非单调演化；分子间距缩短增强分子间电子相互作用，产生激基缔合物（excimer）相关发光通道。

Conclusion: 分子固体的光学响应并非单独由分子形变或晶体堆积决定，而是源于二者与分子间电子耦合（intermolecular electronic coupling）的相互作用；该工作直接解析分子弯曲与激发态发光变化，为理解不同结构自由度如何重塑π共轭分子固体的光物理行为提供依据。

Abstract: Molecular nanocarbons exhibit unique optoelectronic properties and considered as promising candidates for organic device applications, however, understanding how molecular geometry and crystal packing jointly govern physical properties remains a fundamental challenge in π-conjugated molecular materials. Here, we demonstrate how these structural factors modulate the photoluminescence of coronene under high pressure. Combining high-pressure single-crystal X-ray diffraction, Raman spectroscopy, optical spectroscopy, and density functional theory calculations, we establish a relationship between distinct structural changes and the evolution of competing emissive states. Our results reveal that crystal polymorphism modifies the balance between free-exciton (FE) and self-trapped-exciton (STE) emission, while pressure-induced molecular bending is associated with a nonmonotonic evolution of STE emission. As intermolecular distances shorten, enhanced intermolecular electronic interactions give rise to excimer-related emission, introducing an additional emissive pathway. Although pressure-dependent optical properties of coronene have been linked to changes in crystal packing, our findings highlight that the optical response of a molecular solid is not governed by molecular deformation or crystal packing alone, but emerges from their interplay with intermolecular electronic coupling. By directly resolving molecular bending alongside changes in excited-state emission, this work provides insight into how distinct structural degrees of freedom can reshape the photophysical behavior of π-conjugated molecular solids.

</details>


### [110] [Dopant-vacancy complexes as single-photon-emitter candidates in monolayer silicon carbide: radiative and nonradiative decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17729)
*Yue Zang,Jijun Huang,Wenhao Deng,Xueling Lei,Song Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）评估单层碳化硅（monolayer silicon carbide）中XSiVC掺杂-空位复合色心（color centers）的辐射与非辐射衰减，指出PSiVC是最具潜力的单光子源（SPS）候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态色心（solid-state color centers）被视为有前景的单光子源（single-photon sources, SPSs），二维材料中的色心兼具高光子亮度、可规模化制备与易集成优势；但实现高效SPS需同时具备强辐射发射与可忽略的非辐射衰减，故需系统筛选。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），针对单层碳化硅中XSiVC（X = N、O、P、S）掺杂-空位复合体，评估其辐射与非辐射衰减通道，并计算超精细张量（hyperfine tensors）作为自旋活性中心（spin-active centers）的识别特征。

Result: 四类缺陷均呈稳定中性电荷态（neutral charge states）并具有缺陷内光学跃迁（inter-defect optical transitions）。PSiVC 兼具快速辐射衰减、可忽略的非辐射衰减与在所建模通道内接近单位的量子产率（quantum yield），为最优候选；NSiVC 虽具高 Debye-Waller 因子，却受多声子衰减（multiphonon decay）限制而量子产率受限。

Conclusion: 研究不仅识别出有前景的色心候选，还建立了辐射-非辐射联合筛选策略（joint radiative-nonradiative screening strategy），为固态单光子源色心的理性设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: Solid-state color centers have emerged as promising single-photon sources (SPSs). In particular, color centers in two-dimensional (2D) materials combine high photon brightness with scalable fabrication and ease of integration, offering a versatile platform for quantum information technologies. Realizing high-efficiency SPSs, however, requires strong radiative emission and negligible nonradiative decay. Using first-principles calculations, we assess radiative and nonradiative decay in dopant-vacancy complexes XSiVC (X = N, O, P, and S) in monolayer silicon carbide. All four defects exhibit stable neutral charge states and inter-defect optical transitions. Among them, PSiVC emerges as the leading single-photon-emitter candidate, combining rapid radiative decay with negligible nonradiative decay and a near-unity quantum yield within the modeled channels. By contrast, multiphonon decay limits the quantum yield of NSiVC despite its high Debye-Waller factor. Hyperfine tensors provide characteristic signatures for identifying the spin-active centers. Beyond identifying promising candidates, these results establish a joint radiative-nonradiative screening strategy and provide theoretical guidance for the rational design of color centers as solid-state SPSs.

</details>


### [111] [Ultrafast Imaging Reveals Energy Transport Anisotropy Grows from Bulk to Monolayer Black Phosphorus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17731)
*Stephanie M. Hart,Siddhartha Sohoni,Amal Mathew,Aidan L. O'Beirne,Bowen Hou,Reef Sweeney Ethan J. Dunsworth,Diana Y. Qiu,Tony F. Heinz,Naomi S. Ginsberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用超快频闪光学散射显微镜（stroboSCAT）以皮秒分辨率成像黑磷中能量载流子的时空演化，测得块体热与电荷输运及单层激子输运的各向异性扩散系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑磷的可调带隙、高载流子迁移率与各向异性输运使其在电子与热电定向能量输运中具有吸引力，但缺乏对热、电荷与激子输运在层数依赖下的直接、同时测量。

Method: 采用超快频闪光学散射显微镜（stroboscopic optical scattering microscopy, stroboSCAT），以皮秒分辨率成像能量载流子的时空演化，同时分辨块体中的热与电荷输运以及单层中的激子（exciton）输运，并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）解释结果。

Result: 面内扶手椅:锯齿（armchair:zigzag）各向异性由块体的5.3:1升至单层的6.7:1，源于扶手椅方向激子扩散率（45.5 cm²/s）高于块体扶手椅方向电荷载流子扩散率（16.5 cm²/s）；单层各向异性小于仅由激子色散（exciton dispersion）预期的值。

Conclusion: 第一性原理计算将上述差异归因于激子-声子散射（exciton-phonon scattering）优先向锯齿方向的大动量转移；直接测得的层分辨扩散率表明扶手椅取向单层是定向输运的最优几何构型，且输运各向异性不能仅由能带色散（band dispersion）推断。

Abstract: Black phosphorus is an emerging low-dimensional semiconductor whose tunable band gap, high carrier mobility, and anisotropic transport make it attractive for directional energy transport in electronics and thermoelectrics. Using ultrafast stroboscopic optical scattering microscopy (stroboSCAT), we image spatiotemporal evolution of energy carriers with picosecond resolution, simultaneously resolving heat and charge transport in bulk black phosphorus and exciton transport in the monolayer. The in-plane armchair:zigzag anisotropy increases from 5.3:1 in the bulk to 6.7:1 in the monolayer, driven by enhanced armchair exciton diffusivity (45.5 cm$^2$/s) relative to bulk armchair charge carriers (16.5 cm$^2$/s). The monolayer anisotropy is smaller than the calculated exciton dispersion alone would suggest, which first-principles calculations attribute to preferential exciton-phonon scattering to large momenta along the zigzag direction. These directly measured, layer-resolved diffusivities identify armchair-aligned monolayers as the optimal geometry for directional transport and show that transport anisotropy cannot be inferred from band dispersion alone.

</details>


### [112] [Influence of interfacial roughness amplitude and periodicity on giant chiral asymmetry of domain wall mobility in epitaxial Cu/Pd/Co/Pd(111) films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17932)
*N. N. Chernousov,A. S. Pashenko,A. G. Kozlov,A. A. Turpak,A. S. Samardak,A. V. Davydenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过改变Cu缓冲层厚度调控外延Si(111)/Cu/Pd/Co/Pd异质结界面形貌，发现增大粗糙度振幅与横向周期可使蠕变区（creep regime）内手性相反畴壁（domain walls, DWs）的速度比升至约50000，远超仅调控底层Pd厚度所得的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明界面粗糙度可诱导手性依赖的畴壁移动不对称，但粗糙度的振幅与横向空间周期性（lateral periodicity）各自的作用尚未被区分，本文旨在厘清界面无序的空间组织方式对手性畴壁动力学的影响。

Method: 在保持Pd/Co/Pd三层膜成分与厚度不变的前提下，改变Cu缓冲层厚度（2–7 nm）以系统调节磁性界面形貌；用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）表征粗糙度振幅与横向周期，并测量蠕变区中相反手性畴壁的速度。

Result: 随Cu厚度增加，界面形成振幅与横向周期同步增大的分级形貌（hierarchical morphology）；7 nm Cu样品中相反手性畴壁速度比达约50000，比此前通过底层Pd厚度调控粗糙度的Pd/Co/Pd结构中观察到的有效饱和不对称性高出一个数量级以上，尽管两者的均方根（root-mean-square）界面粗糙度相当。

Conclusion: 不对称性增强源于界面无序空间组织的差异，其改变钉扎能景观（pinning energy landscape）并放大了手性依赖的热激活畴壁传播；因此，不仅粗糙度振幅，其横向空间关联（lateral spatial correlations）的工程化设计也是调控磁性异质结中手性畴壁动力学的有效途径。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of interfacial roughness amplitude and lateral periodicity on the chirality-dependent mobility of domain walls (DWs) in epitaxial Si(111)/Cu(2-7 nm)/Pd(3 nm)/Co(0.7 nm)/Pd(3 nm) heterostructures. By varying the Cu buffer layer thickness, we systematically modify the morphology of the magnetic interfaces while maintaining a constant composition and thickness of the Pd/Co/Pd trilayer. Scanning tunneling microscopy reveals that increasing the Cu thickness leads to the development of a hierarchical interfacial morphology characterized by increasing roughness amplitude and lateral periodicity. We observe a dramatic enhancement of the asymmetry between the velocities of DWs with opposite chirality in the creep regime, reaching a velocity ratio of approximately 50000 in the sample with a 7-nm-thick Cu buffer layer. This value exceeds by more than an order of magnitude the effective saturated asymmetry previously observed in Pd/Co/Pd structures with roughness controlled by the bottom Pd layer thickness. Despite comparable root-mean-square interface roughness in both series, the Cu-buffer-induced morphology exhibits a substantially larger characteristic lateral periodicity. We propose that the enhanced asymmetry originates from the different spatial organization of interfacial disorder, which modifies the pinning energy landscape and amplifies chirality-dependent thermally activated DW propagation. Our results suggest that engineering not only the amplitude but also the lateral spatial correlations of interfacial roughness provides an effective approach for controlling chiral DW dynamics in magnetic heterostructures.

</details>


### [113] [Correlating orbital order and photovoltaic effect in Layered Ruddlesden-Popper Pr0.5Ca1.5MnO4 and PrCa2Mn2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17985)
*Christopher Hausmann,Annika Dehning,Qian Ma,Ege Oezmen,Nick Luke Bennewitz,Joerg Hoffmann,Tobias Meyer,Christian Jooss,Lukas Allen-Rump,Peter Bloechl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究成功用离子束溅射（ion beam sputtering）生长外延（epitaxial）PrCa₂Mn₂O₇薄膜，并与Pr₀.₅Ca₁.₅MnO₄对比电输运、光吸收、能带结构与光伏响应。研究发现PrCa₂Mn₂O₇中Ruddlesden-Popper型堆垛层错密度增加，作为失配应变弛豫通道，降低了电荷/轨道有序的转变温度与空间相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状Ruddlesden-Popper钙钛矿氧化物（layered Ruddlesden-Popper perovskite oxides）在半掺杂时具有比非层状Pr₁₋ₓCaₓMnO₃（Tc=240 K）更高的电荷与轨道有序转变温度（Pr₀.₅Ca₁.₅MnO₄为320 K，PrCa₂Mn₂O₇可达370 K），为调控关联电子性质与制备新型关联电子结（如光伏器件）提供新途径，因而需要理解其生长与结构-性质关系。

Method: 采用离子束溅射（ion beam sputtering）在(Nb:)SrTiO₃ (110)衬底上外延生长PrCa₂Mn₂O₇薄膜，并与外延Pr₀.₅Ca₁.₅MnO₄进行对比，比较其电输运、光吸收（optical absorbance）、电子能带结构（electronic bandstructure）与光伏响应（photovoltaic response）。

Result: PrCa₂Mn₂O₇中Ruddlesden-Popper型堆垛层错（stacking faults）密度增强，被识别为失配应变（misfit strain）的弛豫通道；这似乎是其Tc降至320 K、且电荷与轨道有序空间相干性（spatial coherence）较Pr₀.₅Ca₁.₅MnO₄降低的起因。

Conclusion: 由于热极化子光伏效应（hot polaron photovoltaic effect）的起始与电荷和轨道有序的形成相关，温度依赖的电输运与光伏响应对比研究揭示了应变及应变弛豫相关缺陷（strain relaxation related defects）对光电性质的不同作用。

Abstract: The understanding of growth and structure-property relations of epitaxial thin films of layered Ruddlesden-Popper perovskite oxides open up new pathways to tune correlated electron properties and to fabricate new types of correlated electron junctions, such as e.g. photovoltaic devices. In particular, the layered Ruddlesden-Popper systems Pr$_0.5$Ca$_1.5$MnO$_4$ and PrCa$_2$Mn$_2$O$_7$ at half doping exhibit much higher transition temperatures for the formation of charge and orbital ordered phases of $T_c$ = 320 K and $T_c$ up to 370 K, respectively, compared to the half doped non-layered system Pr$_{1-x}$Ca$_{x}$MnO$_3$ (PCMO) with $T_c$ = 240 K. Here, we demonstrate the successful growth of epitaxial PrCa$_2$Mn$_2$O$_7$ thin films by ion beam sputtering and compare electric transport, optical absorbance, electronic bandstructure and photovoltaic response with epitaxial Pr$_0.5$Ca$_1.5$MnO$_4$, both deposited on (Nb:)SrTiO$_3$ (110) substrates. An enhanced density of Ruddlesden-Popper type of stacking faults is identified as a relaxation channel for misfit strain in PrCa$_2$Mn$_2$O$_7$ and seems to be the origin for the reduced $T_c$ = 320 K and the reduced spatial coherence of charge and orbital order in comparison to Pr$_0.5$Ca$_1.5$MnO$_4$. Since the onset of the hot polaron photovoltaic effect is correlated to the formation of charge and orbital ordering, the comparative study of temperature dependent electric transport and photovoltaic response gives insights into the different role of strain and strain relaxation related defects on photo-electric properties.

</details>


### [114] [Epitaxial integration of GaN $μ$-platelets and thin films on metallic TiN using a hybrid approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17986)
*Philipp M. John,Thomas Auzelle,Pierre-Marie Coulon,Blandine Alloing,Ileana Florea,Mikel Gómez Ruiz,Jonas Lähnemann,Lutz Geelhaar,Oliver Brandt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过在金属TiN薄膜上先生长自组装GaN纳米线作籽晶，再以金属有机气相外延实现外延自组织GaN微米片，并证明其N极性、与TiN衬底形成欧姆接触且形貌与发光可通过生长条件调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是实现外延、自组织的GaN微米片（μ-platelets）与金属TiN薄膜的直接结合，以获得与金属衬底形成欧姆接触的GaN结构，并通过两步生长（分子束外延成核+金属有机气相外延过生长）实现可控形貌。

Method: 先用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）在TiN上成核自组装GaN纳米线，数密度为0.01–300 μm⁻²；随后用金属有机气相外延（metal-organic vapor phase epitaxy）过生长，得到与种子密度对应的GaN微米片，并调节载气种类与NH₃/三甲基镓（trimethylgallium）前驱体比例控制形貌。

Result: GaN微米片呈N极性，并与金属TiN衬底形成欧姆接触；N₂载气、1:1 NH₃/三甲基镓条件下得到直径与高度分布较宽的微米片集合；N₂/H₂混合载气则得到更均匀、尺寸分布更窄、直径约2 μm的集合；H₂载气下富N条件促进横向生长，形成聚合的GaN层。

Conclusion: 在H₂载气、富N条件下生长的GaN展现出强近带边发射（near-band-edge emission），室温下仅伴随微弱深能级发光（deep-level luminescence），表明该条件下获得高质量GaN。

Abstract: We demonstrate the synthesis of epitaxial, self-organized GaN $μ$-platelets on metallic TiN thin films. To this end, we first nucleate self-assembled GaN nanowires with number densities between 0.01 to 300 $μm^{-2}$ by molecular beam epitaxy. Subsequent overgrowth of these seed nanowires by metal-organic vapor phase epitaxy yields GaN $μ$-platelets with corresponding number densities. The platelets exhibit N-polarity and form an ohmic contact to the metallic TiN substrate. By adjusting the growth conditions during the metal-organic vapor phase epitaxy, particularly the carrier gas and precursor ratio, the morphology of the GaN can be controlled across a broad range. Growth under N$_{2}$ carrier gas and with a 1:1 NH$_{3}$/trimethylgallium precursor flow ratio results in platelet ensembles with broad diameter and height distributions. In contrast, employing an N$_{2}$/H$_{2}$ carrier gas mixture promotes the formation of more homogeneous ensembles with narrower size distributions around a diameter of $\approx$2 $μm$. Finally, N-rich growth conditions under H$_{2}$ carrier gas enhance lateral growth, leading to the formation of coalesced GaN layers. GaN grown under these latter conditions exhibits strong near-band-edge emission, accompanied by only weak deep-level luminescence at room temperature.

</details>


### [115] [A Machine-learning-aided Workflow for Sampling Small Polaron Dynamics in Energy Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18041)
*Tavinder Singh,Harald Oberhofer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将机器学习（machine learning, ML）代理模型与动力学模型结合，用于高效定量估算含缺陷电池负极材料 Li₄Ti₅O₁₂（LTO）中小极化子（small polaron）跳跃导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数能源材料（如电池电极、光伏与光催化材料）的导电性由强局域化的移动小极化子主导；DFT 虽可较准确处理小极化子及其跃迁势垒，但真实材料中对称不等价极化子构型与跃迁势垒数量庞大，难以逐一显式计算。

Method: 采用自相互作用校正或杂化密度泛函理论（DFT）结合局域几何优化处理小极化子，并以 DFT 训练机器学习（ML）代理模型，将预测结果输入动力学模型，在真实缺陷条件下定量估算 LTO 负极材料的电导率。

Result: 在 3649 个对称不等价极化子跃迁势垒中仅计算 154 个即完成建模，计算效率提升逾 20 倍；估算的室温电导率为 6.7 × 10⁻³ S/cm，处于实验报道范围内。

Conclusion: DFT 训练的 ML 代理模型可定量且高效地采样能源材料中小极化子的动力学行为，为大规模极化子输运研究提供了可行途径。

Abstract: One of the defining characteristics of most energy materials, used as e.g.~battery electrodes, photovoltaics, or photocatalysts, is their ability conduct charges such as electrons and holes. The exact mechanism of this conductivity depends on the material, but is often dominated by the existence of mobile small polarons, i.e.~electrons or holes strongly localised through the polarisation of their environment. Computationally, small polarons can be treated with reasonably accuracy through the use of self-interaction corrected or hybrid density functional theory (DFT) together with local geometry optimisation. Similar approaches can be employed to determine the energy barriers to movement of the polarons through hopping. Unfortunately, realistic energy materials tend have very large numbers of symmetry inequivalent polaronic configurations and correspondingly large numbers of hopping barriers, making them hard to tackle explicitly.
  In this work we therefore extend the computational reach of DFT calculations with machine-learned (ML) surrogate models, which then feed into a kinetic model to quantitatively estimate the conductivity of a battery anode material (Li$_4$Ti$_5$O$_12$ or LTO) at realistic, defected conditions. We calculate the conductivity at room temperature to be 6.7 $\times$ 10$^{-3}$ S/cm which lies well in the experimental range. To this end, we only needed a total of 154 polaron barrier calculations, out of 3649 symmetry inequivalent ones, achieving an over 20-fold increase of computational efficiency. Overall, our work demonstrates how DFT-trained ML surrogates can be used to quantitatively and efficiently sample the dynamics of small polarons in energy materials.

</details>


### [116] [Pressure dependent DFT study of mechanical, thermal, electronic, magnetic, and optical properties of EuCd2As2: a magnetic semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18109)
*Salauddin Muhammad Anis,M. A. Afzal,R. S. Islam,S. H. Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用自旋极化密度泛函理论（DFT）系统研究EuCd2As2在0–8 GPa压力下的结构、力学、热物理、声学、电子、磁性与光学性质，揭示其半金属性、磁性及力学脆–韧转变等压力可调行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EuCd2As2兼具磁性与拓扑特性，在自旋电子学（spintronics）与光电子学（optoelectronics）领域具有潜在应用价值，但其压力依赖的物理性质尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 基于自旋极化密度泛函理论（spin-polarized DFT），在0、2、4、6、8 GPa压力下计算结构、弹性常数、热物理量（德拜温度、熔化温度、Grüneisen参数）、声子热导率、能带结构与态密度（DOS）、磁矩及光学性质（介电函数、折射率、吸收系数等）。

Result: 零压晶格参数与实验吻合良好，力学稳定性在考察压力范围内保持；弹性模量随压力可调，高压下发生脆性到延性转变（brittle-to-ductile transition）；德拜温度、熔化温度与Grüneisen参数强烈依赖压力；0 GPa铁磁（FM）相能带结构在费米能级处无带隙，证实半金属（semimetallic）特性；单胞净磁矩为6.96 μ_B；光学谱在可见光与紫外区显示强吸收。

Conclusion: EuCd2As2的力学、热物理、电子与光学性质均随压力显著可调，其半金属性与较大磁矩支持自旋电子学应用前景，可见–紫外强吸收则提示光电子学应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive spin-polarized density functional theory (DFT) investigation of the pressure-dependent physical properties of EuCd2As2, a magnetically and topologically rich compound with potential applications in spintronics and optoelectronics technologies. We have investigated the structural, mechanical, thermophysical, acoustic, electronic, magnetic, and optical properties of this material at pressures of 0, 2, 4, 6, and 8 GPa. The calculated lattice parameters at zero pressure are in good agreement with experimental values. The mechanical stability of EuCd2As2 is maintained over the investigated pressure range. The elastic constants, bulk modulus, shear modulus, and Young's modulus are found to be tunable with applied pressure. The compound undergoes a brittle to ductile transition at higher pressures. The Debye temperature, melting temperature, and Grüneisen parameter are found to be strongly pressure-dependent. Phonon thermal conductivity has been evaluated. The electronic properties of EuCd2As2 were studied through band structure and density of states (DOS). The effect of pressure up to 8 GPa was investigated for the ferromagnetic (FM) phase. At 0 GPa, the calculated FM band structure exhibits no band gap at the Fermi level, which confirms the semimetallic nature of EuCd2As2. The net magnetic moment of 6.96 μ_B per unit cell was obtained for this compound indicating its potential for spintronics application. The optical properties, including the dielectric function, refractive index, absorption coefficient, optical conductivity, reflectivity, and loss function, are calculated. The calculated optical spectra indicate strong absorption in the visible and ultraviolet regions, suggesting that EuCd2As2 may have potential for optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [117] [Making Plasticity Visible: A Case Study for High-Temperature Exposed Aluminum Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18120)
*Bakhtiyar Mammadli,Karol Frydrych,Casim Yazici,Azad Mammadli,Kazimierz Skrobas,Siddharth Suman,F. Javier Dominguez-Gutierrez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出结合数字图像相关（Digital Image Correlation, DIC）与主成分分析（Principal Component Analysis, PCA）的数据驱动框架，用于从单轴拉伸全场应变数据中识别塑性变形起始与早期应变局部化特征，并在不同热处理状态的AA6063-T5铝合金上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塑性变形起始与应变局部化的早期发展对表征材料行为、识别易失效区域至关重要，但常规全场应变测量往往难以清晰区分这些转变阶段，因此需要能够提取可解释变形特征的数据驱动方法。

Method: 对AA6063-T5铝合金（原始态及100、200、300 °C热暴露态）进行单轴拉伸试验，采用DIC获取序列应变场，并对其应用PCA，分析第二主成分投影的时间演化及高时间分辨率应变场对应的主成分图谱。

Result: 第二主成分投影在屈服转变附近出现特征峰，其结果与常规0.2%残余应变（0.2% offset）判据在所研究条件下一致；在转变前的高时间分辨率应变场中，主成分图谱可突出与后续失效区域相关的局部化变形特征，且比瞬时应变图中的竞争性应变集中更易辨识。

Conclusion: PCA导出的变形特征在热致力学响应变化下仍保持可识别性，表明无监督降维（unsupervised dimensionality reduction）方法具备从实验全场测量中提取塑性变形与早期应变局部化物理可解释信息的潜力。

Abstract: Understanding the onset of plastic deformation and the early development of strain localization is essential for characterizing material behavior and identifying failure-prone regions. However, these transitions are not always distinguishable from conventional full-field strain measurements. This study investigates a data- driven framework combining Digital Image Correlation (DIC) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to extract temporal and spatial deformation signatures during uniaxial tensile loading. The methodology is evaluated using AA6063-T5 aluminum alloy specimens in the as-received condition and following thermal exposure at 100~$^\circ$C, 200~$^\circ$C, and 300~$^\circ$C. PCA applied to sequential strain fields reveals a characteristic peak in the second principal component projection near the yielding transition, showing agreement with the conventional 0.2\% offset criterion across the investigated conditions. Furthermore, principal component maps extracted from high-temporal-resolution strain fields preceding this transition highlight localized deformation features associated with subsequent failure regions. These features are more clearly distinguishable than competing strain concentrations in the corresponding instantaneous strain maps. The results indicate that PCA-derived deformation signatures remain identifiable despite thermally induced variations in mechanical response. This approach demonstrates the potential of unsupervised dimensionality reduction for extracting physically interpretable information on plastic deformation and early strain localization from experimental full-field measurements.

</details>


### [118] [Mapping the Phase Space of Cubic Chiral Semiconductors: High-Throughput Search,Stability, and Prototype Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18161)
*Pierre-Paul De Breuck,Hai-Chen Wang,Reynaldo Mares-Gil,Miguel A. L. Marques,Aldo H. Romero*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过机器学习与密度泛函理论结合的高通量筛选，在立方空间群P4₃32中构建24227个含至多五元素的立方手性结构，识别出368个热力学稳定半导体及3048个能量近简并候选，显著扩展了立方手性半导体的已知相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性晶体（chiral crystals）在拓扑与自旋电子学应用中具有潜力，但已识别的候选非常有限，尤其缺乏立方对称性的手性半导体，制约了其技术应用。

Method: 将机器学习（machine learning）技术与密度泛函理论（density functional theory）相结合，对立方空间群P4₃32（No. 212）开展高通量计算搜索，生成含至多五种元素的立方手性结构，并依据凸包（convex hull）稳定性与带隙判据进行筛选。

Result: 共获得24227个立方手性结构；其中368个为位于凸包上的热力学稳定半导体，另有3048个处于50 meV/atom能量窗口内。结构基元、稳定性与能带结构分析揭示出可重复出现的原型家族（prototype families），部分表现出本征中间带（intermediate-band）特征。

Conclusion: 研究大幅拓展了立方手性半导体的已知相空间，可为手性材料的发现与设计提供指导。

Abstract: Chiral crystals show unique electronic, magnetic, and optical characteristics that make them attractive for topological and spintronic applications. Nevertheless, the limited set of identified chiral semiconductors, especially those with cubic symmetry, has so far constrained their technological application. In this work, we perform a high-throughput computational search for semiconducting chiral compounds in the cubic space group $P4_332$ (No. 212). By integrating machine-learning techniques with density functional theory, we obtain 24\,227 cubic chiral structures containing up to five elements, and identify 368 thermodynamically stable semiconductors on the convex hull and 3\,048 within 50~meV/atom. We analyze their structural motifs, stability, and electronic band structures, highlighting recurring prototype families, several of which show intrinsic intermediate-band features. Our results substantially expand the known phase space of cubic chiral semiconductors and may guide future efforts in the discovery and design of chiral materials.

</details>


### [119] [Tunable coupling between valley and magnetic excitons in WS2/CrSBr heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18187)
*Jianchen Dang,Liza Godziashvili,Kaichen Xie,Arun Ramanathan,Vadym Kulichenko,Alejandro Busto,Jia-Yi Ji,Robert Boyd,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Sina Najmaei,Daniel Rhodes,Luis Balicas,Xavier Roy,Ting Cao,Xiao-Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作研究了反铁磁CrSBr与单层WS2构成的二维范德华异质结的磁光性质，揭示了II型能带对齐、磁邻近效应（magnetic proximity effect）导致的谷塞曼劈裂增强（Zeeman valley splitting）、自旋选择性层间电荷转移，以及依赖扭转角（twist angle）的层间耦合调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华二维异质结中的界面耦合（interfacial coupling）是调控电子、光学与磁性的有效途径，且可通过电场、磁场、光激发及堆叠几何等外部参数进一步精确调控；因此有必要探索兼具激子（exciton）与磁性的体系中的磁光耦合机制。

Method: 制备由反铁磁CrSBr与单层WS2组成的二维半导体异质结，测量其磁光性质（magneto-optical properties），包括能带对齐、塞曼劈裂、圆偏振光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）及扭转角依赖的PL淬灭行为。

Result: 观察到两层间为II型能带对齐（type-II band alignment），CrSBr的磁邻近效应使WS2层中塞曼谷劈裂增强；层间电荷转移（interlayer charge transfer）具有强自旋选择性，受WS2光生载流子谷-自旋极化与CrSBr磁化相对取向支配，产生自旋敏感的圆偏振PL；CrSBr各向异性电子色散使层间相互作用随扭转角变化，表现为PL淬灭的扭转角依赖。

Conclusion: 该体系通过多重耦合通道整合了谷电子学（valleytronics）与磁激子（magnetic excitons），为全光调控自旋与磁振子（magnon）态及柔性自旋光电器件开发提供了可调平台。

Abstract: Interfacial coupling in van der Waals two-dimensional (2D) heterostructures offers an effective route to tailor the electronic, optical, and magnetic properties of materials. Versatile control and precise engineering of such interlayer interactions can be further achieved through external parameters, like electric field, magnetic field, optical excitation, as well as layer stacking geometries. Here, we examined the magneto-optical properties of 2D semiconductor heterostructures made of antiferromagnetic CrSBr and a monolayer WS2, both of which host intriguing excitonic properties. We identified a type-II band alignment between these two layers and the magnetic proximity effects from CrSBr, manifested by an enhanced Zeeman valley splitting in the WS2 layer. The interlayer charge transfer shows strong spin selectivity and is governed by the relative alignment between the valley-spin polarization of photoexcited carriers in WS2 and the magnetization in CrSBr, giving rise to spin-sensitive circularly polarized photoluminescence (PL) from WS2. Furthermore, the highly anisotropic electronic dispersion in CrSBr enables stacking-dependent control of interlayer interactions, as inferred from a twist-angle dependence of PL quenching. Our work establishes a highly tunable 2D system that integrates valleytronics and magnetic excitons through multiple coupling channels, opening up new opportunities in all-optical manipulation of spin and magnon states and for the development of flexible spin-optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [120] [Reconciling global and local spin models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18207)
*Bendegúz Nyári,András Lászlóffy,László Szunyogh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出投影方案，从局域相互作用矩阵中系统性剔除纵向虚假贡献，仅在横向自旋涨落（transverse spin fluctuations）范围内，将局域自旋模型与全局多自旋哈密顿量（global multispin Hamiltonian）严格对应，从而统一两种第一性原理磁性相互作用描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于第一性原理（first-principles）的磁相互作用既可表述为构型无关的全局自旋模型（global spin models），也可表述为参数显式依赖自旋构型的局域自旋模型（local spin models），两者常给出看似矛盾的结果，且非共线框架下Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）等有效相互作用的识别长期存在歧义。

Method: 发展了一套投影方案（projection scheme），将局域相互作用矩阵限制在横向自旋涨落子空间，系统移除纵向贡献，并据此建立投影后局域相互作用与底层全局多自旋哈密顿量之间的严格联系。

Result: 在原型体系 (i) Au(111) 上的 Cr 三聚体（Cr trimer）与 (ii) Cu(001) 上的 Fe、Cr 二聚体（dimers）中，投影后的局域相互作用参数定量重现了全局四自旋模型（four-spin model）所得参数。

Conclusion: 该研究为局域与全局自旋模型提供了可同等解释的统一框架（unified framework），并澄清了非共线体系中DMI等有效相互作用识别上的长期歧义。

Abstract: Magnetic interactions based on first-principles calculations can be casted either into global spin models, where the interactions are configuration-independent, or into local spin models, where the effective parameters depend explicitly on the underlying spin configuration. Although these two perspectives often yield seemingly inconsistent results, we demonstrate that they can be reconciled by restricting the analysis to transverse spin fluctuations. We develop a projection scheme that systematically removes spurious longitudinal contributions from local interaction matrices, thereby establishing a rigorous connection between the projected local interactions and the underlying global multispin Hamiltonian. Applying our approach to the prototype systems (i) a Cr trimer on Au(111) (ii) Fe and Cr dimers on Cu(001), we show that the projected local interaction parameters quantitatively reproduce those obtained from the global four-spin model. This work provides a unified framework to interpret local and global spin models on equal footing and resolves long-standing ambiguities in the identification of effective interactions such as the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) in a non-collinear framework.

</details>


### [121] [The Rise of Zirconium Metal-Organic Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18217)
*Joohan Nam,Seohyeon Kim,Eunjie Ju,Shinhyeok Moon,Wonyoung Choe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述依据构建单元的连接性（connectivity）与几何构型，将锆金属有机多面体（Zr-MOPs）分类为边导向与面导向四面体、立方体和茧状结构，并系统梳理了配体—结构对应关系及晶体中笼体的超分子组装规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由环戊二烯基（Cp）封端的Zr₃簇与羧酸配体组装形成的Zr-MOPs结构多样，其最终产物取决于Zr₃节点的连接性、配体几何与组装条件，但缺乏系统的结构分类与设计规律总结。

Method: 综述性归纳：按构建单元的连接性与几何对Zr-MOPs进行分类，基于已报道结构建立配体与MOP类型的映射，并比较Zr₃簇前驱体与反应条件在四面体与茧状结构之间的选择性作用，同时分析氢键与离子缔合驱动的超分子组装。

Result: 明确了哪类配体对应哪类MOP，揭示了同一配体可生成四面体或茧状结构时前驱体与反应条件的决定作用，并阐明外部相互作用位点的几何与分布如何决定网状网络（reticular nets）与多孔盐（porous salts）的晶体堆积。

Conclusion: 将笼体几何与节点—配体连接性、晶体堆积与外部相互作用位点相关联，为Zr-MOPs及其超分子组装体的设计提供了结构基础。

Abstract: Zirconium metal-organic polyhedra (Zr-MOPs) assembled from cyclopentadienyl (Cp)-capped Zr$_3$ clusters and carboxylate linkers form a structurally diverse family. Which MOP forms depends on the connectivity of the Zr$_3$ node, the geometry of the linker, and assembly conditions. This review classifies Zr-MOPs into edge- and face-directed tetrahedra, a cube, and cocoons according to the connectivity and geometry of their building blocks. From reported structures, we map which linkers give which MOPs and examine how the Zr$_3$ cluster precursor and the reaction conditions decide between the tetrahedron and the cocoon when one linker can give both. We describe how hydrogen bonding and ionic association organize the MOPs into reticular nets and porous salts in the crystal. Together, these comparisons relate cage geometry to node-linker connectivity and crystal packing to the geometry and distribution of external interaction sites, providing a structural basis for designing Zr-MOPs and their supramolecular assemblies.

</details>


### [122] [Uniaxial strain induces $d$-wave phonon chirality in ferroelectric BaTiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18402)
*Juan M. Florez,Eric Suárez Morell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 第一性原理计算表明，单轴应变（uniaxial strain）可将铁电 BaTiO$_3$ 自支撑膜中可电控的 $g$-波声子手性（phonon chirality）纹理降格为 $C_{2v}$ 对称性，并额外诱导出与正交应变 $η$ 和极化双线性耦合的 $d$-波螺旋度（$d$-wave helicity），其节线沿应力轴分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆二色共振非弹性 X 射线散射（circularly dichroic resonant inelastic x-ray scattering）已揭示 BaTiO$_3$ 膜中可由电场切换的 $g$-波声子手性，但除电场外是否存在其他调控手性对称性与符号的手段尚不明确，本文旨在考察单轴应变对这一手性纹理角向分类的作用。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first principles）计算，基于 PBEsol 与 PBE+D3 力常数（force constants），分析 $C_{4v}$ 降至 $C_{2v}$ 时手性纹理的角向结构，并借助双能带模型（two-band model）描述应变劈裂 $E$ 双重态（$E$ doublets）的响应。

Result: 在 $η=1\%$ 的 38~meV 支上，对称性本应使螺旋度消失的对角方向上应变诱导值达无应变最大值的约 60\%，而在镜面外实测波矢处则降低一半以上；$d$-波分量随 $η$ 线性增长，并在 $η^*\approx1.3\text{--}1.4\%$ 处与 $g$-波分量相等；按支分辨时，应变劈裂 $E$ 双重态的响应对 $η$ 呈非解析（non-analytic）行为。

Conclusion: 应变与电场并列为调控铁电膜（ferroelectric membranes）中声子手性对称性与符号的第二个调控旋钮（knob），为手性声子的应变工程提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Circularly dichroic resonant inelastic x-ray scattering has revealed electrically switchable $g$-wave phonon chirality in freestanding BaTiO$_3$ membranes. Here, we show from first principles that uniaxial strain changes the angular class of this texture. Lowering $C_{4v}$ to $C_{2v}$ adds a $d$-wave helicity that is bilinear in the orthorhombic strain $η$ and the polarization, with nodal lines along the stress axis. For the 38~meV branches at $η=1\%$, we find that (i) along the diagonal, where the helicity vanishes by symmetry without strain, it reaches about 60\% of the unstrained maximum, and (ii) at the measured wave vectors off the mirror it is reduced by more than half. With PBEsol and PBE+D3 force constants, the $d$-wave component grows linearly in $η$ and equals the $g$-wave one at $η^*\approx1.3\text{--}1.4\%$. Resolved by branch, the response of strain-split $E$ doublets is non-analytic in $η$, as captured by a two-band model. Strain therefore provides a second knob, besides the electric field, that controls the symmetry and the sign of phonon chirality in ferroelectric membranes.

</details>


### [123] [Graph diffusion transformer for interpretable generation and inverse design of disordered atomic structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18420)
*Jiani Hu,Chenyan Wang,Yongkun Yang,Guanyu Hou,Jihaochen Zhang,Lei Liu,Ji Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 Graph Amorphous Diffusion Transformer（GaDiT），一种结合动态周期图（dynamic periodic graphs）、掩码条件（masked conditioning）与区间加权训练噪声分布的生成模型，用于无序原子结构的可解释生成与逆向设计，并在二维非晶碳与三维非晶硅、二氧化硅上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子无序（atomic disorder）为调控材料性质提供自由度假，但巨大的构型空间（configuration space）使结构—性质关系难以厘清，并阻碍理性的逆向设计（inverse design）。

Method: 模型融合三项设计：动态周期图表示、掩码条件机制，以及区间加权训练噪声分布（interval-weighted training-noise distribution）；并针对结构与训练网络提出条件原子响应映射分析（conditioned atomic response mapping analysis）。

Result: 在二维非晶碳与三维非晶硅、二氧化硅上生成高质量无序结构，采样速度较现有基于校正器（corrector-based）的方法提升两个数量级；单一模型可支持单一或组合的结构特征、物理性质与制备条件的条件生成（conditional generation），逆向设计可拓展至训练分布之外的结构—性质范围。

Conclusion: 条件原子响应映射分析揭示了主导目标性质的全局结构描述符（global structural descriptors）与局部原子环境（local atomic environments），GaDiT 为无序原子结构的可解释生成、逆向设计与结构—性质发现提供了通用框架（general framework）。

Abstract: Atomic disorder offers a potent degree of freedom to tailor material properties, yet the resulting vast configuration space obscures structure-property relations and hinders rational inverse design. Here we introduce Graph Amorphous Diffusion Transformer (GaDiT), a generative model that combines dynamic periodic graphs, masked conditioning and an interval-weighted training-noise distribution. GaDiT generates high-quality disordered structures while accelerating sampling by two orders of magnitude compared with existing corrector-based approaches, as demonstrated across two-dimensional amorphous carbon and three-dimensional amorphous silicon and silica. Moreover, GaDiT supports conditional generation under single or combined structural features, physical properties and preparation conditions in a unified model, and enables inverse design that expands accessible structure-property ranges beyond the training distribution. To interpret these structures and the trained network, we propose a conditioned atomic response mapping analysis, revealing both the global structural descriptors and the local atomic environments that govern target properties. Overall, GaDiT establishes a general framework for interpretable generation, inverse design and structure-property discovery for disordered atomic structures.

</details>


### [124] [Validating Graph Neural Network Feature Attribution Through Local Perturbations: A Study on CoMoFeNiCu Decomposition Enthalpy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18430)
*Miguel Tenorio,James Chapman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过故意过拟合一个物理感知（physics-aware）图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN）集成模型，验证特征归因方法能否反映真实物理效应而非统计假象，并借助原子交换扰动确认特征排序编码的是邻域壳层效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在材料性质预测中表现良好，但可解释性（interpretability）不足：特征重要性归因究竟反映物理现实还是统计假象尚不清楚，缺乏可信的方法验证。

Method: 以CoMoFeNiCu高熵合金（high-entropy alloy）体系的三种化学计量比为对象，训练GNN从DFT计算结构预测分解焓（decomposition enthalpy），并将输出权重约束为编码特征重要性；数据集规模受控（约900条二元、4500条三元分解路径），并通过逐位点原子交换扰动（atomic swap perturbation）后重新排序特征进行验证。

Result: 扰动单个原子位点后重新排序特征，呈现与GNN相互作用截断半径（interaction cutoff）一致的空间衰减，表明权重编码的是邻域壳层（neighbor-shell）效应，而非仅由全局化学计量比造成的假象；说明在严格验证下，特征归因在过拟合条件下仍可保持物理解释性。

Conclusion: 该工作为材料GNN的可信可解释性（trustworthy interpretability）奠定基础，并为扩展到多化学体系及跨成分空间（compositional space）的泛化测试提供前提。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) show promise for predicting materials properties, but interpretability remains a challenge, particularly understanding whether attributed feature importance reflects physical reality or statistical artifacts. Here, we present a methods validation study that deliberately overfits a physics-aware GNN ensemble to probe the robustness of feature-ranking approaches. Using the CoMoFeNiCu high-entropy alloy system with three stoichiometries, we train GNNs to predict decomposition enthalpies from DFT-calculated structures while explicitly constraining output weights to encode feature importance. By design, our dataset is limited (approximately 900 binary and 4,500 ternary decomposition pathways) to control compositional complexity and enable precise interrogation of local chemistry effects. We validate that feature rankings capture meaningful local-environment information through a series of atomic swap perturbations: perturbing individual atomic sites and re-ranking features reveals spatial decay consistent with the GNN's interaction cutoff, confirming that weights encode neighbor-shell effects rather than global stoichiometric artifacts alone. Results demonstrate that feature attribution methods can remain physically interpretable even under overfitting, provided validation is rigorous. This work establishes a foundation for trustworthy interpretability in materials GNNs and sets the stage for scaling to multiple chemistries and testing generalization across compositional spaces.

</details>


### [125] [High Entropy Oxides as Room Temperature Anderson Insulators with Colossal Soft Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18433)
*Elena Salagre,Heather Hare,Eli Rotenberg,Jonathan D. Denlinger,Joshua D. Sugar,Yiyang Li,Elliot J. Fuller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作证明高熵氧化物（high-entropy oxide, HEO）中的多阳离子晶格可本征构建相互作用安德森绝缘体（interacting Anderson insulator），其局域化行为可延续至500 K，突破传统安德森效应仅见于低温的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安德森效应（Anderson effect）通常仅在低温下观测，且源于硅中磷掺杂或过渡金属氧化物中氧空位等外禀缺陷（extrinsic defects），因此需要寻找能在高温下稳定存在且不依赖外禀缺陷的安德森绝缘行为。

Method: 通过调控La0.7Sr0.3Mn1-x(Cr,Fe,Co,Ni)x/4O3（LS5B）中过渡金属阳离子的填充量，将体系从x=0.00的原型关联金属La0.7Sr0.3MnO3调至x=0.80的局域绝缘体，并结合角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）与输运测量进行表征。

Result: 在x=0.80时无序长度收缩至近晶胞尺度（3.85 Å），电子平均自由程达Ioffe-Regel极限；ARPES显示费米能级处巡游d准粒子态系统性局域化并伴随软库仑隙（soft Coulomb gap），而能带结构与对称性保持不变；输运呈现由巨大软隙主导的Efros-Shklovskii变程跳跃，特征温度TES>50,000 K，屏蔽库仑相互作用强度高达1.6 eV。

Conclusion: 多重证据表明，相互作用安德森行为是高熵材料的一种本征特性（native characteristic），可在高达500 K的温度下存续。

Abstract: The Anderson effect, arising from destructive quantum interference of electron wave packets in disordered potentials, is usually observed at cryogenic temperatures and typically arises from extrinsic defects in conventional materials such as phosphorus dopants in silicon or oxygen vacancies in transition metal oxides. Here we demonstrate that a multication lattice of transition metals in a high-entropy oxide (HEO) natively establishes an interacting Anderson insulator that extends up to $500\,\mathrm{K}$. By controlling the population of transition metal cations in $\mathrm{La}_{0.7}\mathrm{Sr}_{0.3}\mathrm{Mn}_{1-x}(\mathrm{Cr},\mathrm{Fe},\mathrm{Co},\mathrm{Ni})_{x/4}\mathrm{O}_3$ (LS5B), we tune the system from the prototypical correlated metal $\mathrm{La}_{0.7}\mathrm{Sr}_{0.3}\mathrm{Mn}\mathrm{O}_3$ at $x=0.00$ to a localized insulator at $x=0.80$, where the disorder length contracts to near the unit cell (3.85 $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$) and drives the electron mean free path to the Ioffe-Regel limit. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) reveals the systematic localization of itinerant $d$-quasiparticle states at the Fermi level, consistent with the emergence of a soft Coulomb gap, while the underlying band structure and symmetry are preserved. Transport reveals Efros-Shklovskii variable-range hopping governed by a colossal soft gap with characteristic temperature $T_{\mathrm{ES}}>50{,}000\,\mathrm{K}$ and screened Coulomb interaction strengths as high as 1.6 eV. Together, multiple lines of evidence indicate that interacting Anderson behavior is a native characteristic that survives to temperatures as high as $500\,\mathrm{K}$ in high-entropy materials.

</details>


### [126] [Fine-tuned atomistic foundation model for accurate sodium-carbon chemistry in Na-ion batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18490)
*Andres Felipe Usuga,Ihor Radchenko,Yuxin Tang,Oleksandr I. Malyi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于 MACE 架构的预训练原子基础模型（atomistic foundation models）在硬碳（hard carbon）储钠多种局部构型中的可靠性，发现其在金属钠团簇与石墨烯相互作用上存在明显偏差，并通过定向微调（fine-tuning）恢复 DFT 级能量描述，进而建立闭孔半径与平台电压的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子基础模型被视为在复杂化学环境下实现 DFT 级精度材料模拟的可行途径，但其在竞争性成键机制（competing bonding regimes）下的可靠性、以及复杂成键如何影响材料性质预测仍不明确；硬碳储钠因包含缺陷/弯曲石墨区、非晶区与闭孔/开孔等多种局部结构基元，是检验该问题的代表性体系。

Method: 针对不同 Na/C 局部结构基元（local motifs），对现有基于 MACE 架构的预训练基础模型进行系统评测，并采用定向微调（targeted fine-tuning）以恢复 DFT 级能量描述、检验对未见 Na/C 环境的迁移能力。

Result: 预训练模型无法准确刻画石墨烯上金属钠团簇的 Na–Na 与 Na–C 相互作用；定向微调后模型可描述局部基元的能量稳定性，并对未见 Na/C 环境具备足够迁移性（transferability）。

Conclusion: 基于微调后的基础模型建立了硬碳闭孔半径（closed-pore radius）与平台电压（plateau voltage）的关联，表明小孔在更高电压下贡献容量，展示了基础模型可将原子尺度信息与复杂成键环境下的材料设计相联系的潜力。

Abstract: Atomistic foundation models have emerged as a promising route to DFT-level accuracy for materials simulation with complex chemical environments. However, their reliability across competing bonding regimes and, more importantly, how complex bonding affects predictions of material properties remain unclear. Among materials with competing bonding regimes, sodium storage in hard carbon poses a substantial challenge. Hard carbon features diverse local structural motifs, including defective and curved graphitic regions, amorphous regions, and closed/open pores. Motivated by its importance for Na-ion batteries, we systematically evaluate existing pretrained foundation models based on the MACE architecture across different local Na/C motifs. We find that the pretrained models fail to accurately capture Na-Na and Na-C interactions for metallic Na clusters on graphene. To recover a DFT-level energetic description, we employ targeted fine-tuning, showing that it can describe the energetic stability of local motifs and achieve sufficient transferability to unseen Na/C environments. With the developed fine-tuned model, we establish a correlation between closed-pore radius in hard carbon and plateau voltage, showing that the small pores contribute at larger voltages than large ones, suggesting thus how the developed fine-tuned foundation model can link atomistic information with materials design for complex bonding environments.

</details>


### [127] [Pressure and Magnetic Field Control of Topology in Antiferromagnetic Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18525)
*Narsimha Rao Elaprolu,Marco Gibertini*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出以压力与磁场诱导的磁重取向协同调控A型反铁磁双层中带拓扑的策略，并以双层CrSBr的第一性原理计算验证：面外磁化得到Chern绝缘体，面内磁化得到二维Weyl半金属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性半导体中调控带拓扑需要将电子结构与实验可及的自由度耦合。A型反铁磁双层中反平行层磁化会抑制交换极化带边态之间的杂化，从而限制其对层间压缩的响应，因此需要新的协同调控方案。

Method: 提出基于压力与磁重取向的协同方法：外加磁场使层磁矩对齐，消除自旋失配，使压力增强的层间杂化（interlayer hybridization）驱动带反转（band inversion）；并用双层CrSBr的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），在压缩的正交结构模型中验证该机制。

Result: 磁化方向决定反转带的对称性及自旋轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）的后果：面外磁化产生具有手性边缘态的Chern绝缘体（Chern insulator），面内磁化支持带节点连接边缘态的二维Weyl半金属（two-dimensional Weyl semimetal）。连续自旋倾转（spin canting）可在完全磁饱和前驱动该转变并稳定Chern相。

Conclusion: 层间杂化被识别为连接磁序与带拓扑的纽带；尽管具体转变条件依赖电子关联（electronic correlations）与压缩下的结构稳定性，该策略为层状反铁磁半导体中外部可调拓扑相提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Controlling band topology in magnetic semiconductors requires strategies that couple electronic structure to experimentally accessible degrees of freedom. Here we propose a cooperative approach based on pressure and magnetic reorientation in A-type antiferromagnetic bilayers. Antiparallel layer magnetizations suppress hybridization between exchange-polarized band-edge states, limiting their response to interlayer compression. A magnetic field that aligns the layer moments removes this spin mismatch, enabling pressure-enhanced hybridization to drive a band inversion. The magnetization direction then controls the symmetries of the inverted bands and whether spin--orbit coupling produces a Chern-insulating gap or symmetry-protected nodal crossings. First-principles calculations for bilayer CrSBr illustrate this mechanism within a compressed orthorhombic structural model. Out-of-plane magnetization yields a Chern insulator with a chiral edge state, whereas in-plane magnetization supports a two-dimensional Weyl semimetal with node-connecting edge states. Continuous spin canting further demonstrates how magnetic reorientation can drive the transition and stabilize the Chern phase before full magnetic saturation. Although material-specific transition conditions depend on electronic correlations and structural stability under compression, the underlying strategy identifies interlayer hybridization as a link between magnetic order and band topology. These results provide a design principle for externally tunable topological phases in layered antiferromagnetic semiconductors.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Quantum Fundamental Groups of Spaces with Finite-Sheeted Universal Covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17485)
*Shuzhou Wang,Zhenhua Wang,Matthew Zediker*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文构造了量子基本群（quantum fundamental groups），其以经典基本群为子群，从而为离散量子群（discrete quantum groups）提供了一类源自拓扑覆盖空间对称性的新自然例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散量子群虽备受关注，但自 Voigt 在 arXiv:2208.01310 构造有限与无限集合的量子置换（quantum permutations）之前，一直缺乏自然的例子；本文旨在从拓扑中寻找新的自然实例。

Method: 通过覆盖空间（covering spaces）的对称性构造量子基本群，使其包含经典基本群作为子群，并对包括实射影平面 $\mathbb{RP}^2$ 在内的一大类例子进行刻画。

Result: 给出了包含经典基本群作为子群的量子基本群的构造，刻画了广泛一类例子的量子基本群（含 $\mathbb{RP}^2$），并证明当空间的基本群有限且非阿贝尔（non-abelian）或阶非素数（non prime order）时，这些量子基本群是真正量子的（genuinely quantum）。

Conclusion: 量子基本群构成了由拓扑覆盖空间对称性产生的离散量子群新自然例子类，并在基本群有限且非阿贝尔或阶非素数的情形下严格区别于经典群。

Abstract: Discrete quantum groups have attracted much interest, but there was a lack of natural examples until Voigt constructed quantum permutations of finite and infinite sets in arXiv:2208.01310. In this paper, we construct the quantum fundamental groups that contain the classical fundamental groups as subgroups. This is a new class of natural examples of discrete quantum groups arising from symmetries of covering spaces in topology. We characterize quantum fundamental groups for a wide class of examples, including $\mathbb{RP}^2$. We show that these quantum fundamental groups are genuinely quantum for spaces with finite fundamental groups that are either non-abelian or of non prime order.

</details>


### [129] [Rigidity for pivotal braided r-categories of moderate growth and applications to vertex operator algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17732)
*Robert McRae*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 论文给出一个由 ChatGPT 生成、经作者补充的证明：在 abelian pivotal braided r-category 中，若对象 X 具有 moderate growth（存在 n 使 dim End(X^{⊗n}) < n!），则 X 是 rigid 的，无需非平凡性（non-negligible）假设；并由此推出顶点算子代数模范畴的刚性、模张量范畴结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Etingof 与 Penneys 近期证明了非平凡（non-negligible）对象在满足维数增长条件时是刚性的，但该非平凡性假设限制了结论的适用范围。本文旨在去掉这一假设，并应用于非半单（non-semisimple）范畴及顶点算子代数模范畴的刚性判定。

Method: 采用作者此前为非半单范畴发展的刚性判据（rigidity criterion），并结合 Etingof–Penneys 证明中的思想，构造 ChatGPT 生成、由 Bin Gui 提示的证明；另给出一个不含 AI 辅助的变体结果，用 Grothendieck-Verdier 对偶函子（Grothendieck-Verdier duality functor）D 与 dim Hom((DX)^{⊗n}⊗X^{⊗n}, 1) < n! 的条件替换原增长条件。

Result: 证明了具有 moderate growth 的对象 X 在 abelian pivotal braided r-category 中必为 rigid，无需 non-negligible 假设；变体结果给出以 D-相关维数条件为判据的刚性结论。进而：若 V 为简单自反（self-contragredient）顶点算子代数且 C^1_V 对反变模封闭，则 C^1_V 是局部有限 C-线性 abelian rigid braided ribbon 范畴；若 V 为简单 Z_{≥0}-分次自反 C_2-cofinite 顶点算子代数，则其全部 grading-restricted generalized 模范畴是（未必半单的）模张量范畴（modular tensor category）。

Conclusion: 刚性可由纯维数增长条件推出，non-negligible 假设可被彻底移除；所得判据为顶点算子代数模范畴在非半单情形下的刚性、ribbon 与模张量结构提供了统一工具。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{C}$ be an abelian pivotal braided r-category with simple unit object $\mathbf{1}$ over any field. We present a ChatGPT-generated proof, prompted by Bin Gui, that any object $X$ in $\mathcal{C}$ of moderate growth, in the sense that $\dim\mathrm{End}(X^{\otimes n})<n!$ for some $n\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}$, is rigid. The proof crucially uses ideas from the recent proof of Etingof and Penneys that $X$ is rigid if it is also non-negligible, that is, $X\otimes Y$ contains $\mathbf{1}$ as a direct summand for some object $Y$. In the present paper, the non-negligible assumption is removed using a rigidity criterion developed previously by the author for use in non-semisimple categories. We also present a variant result (found without further AI assistance) that $X$ is also rigid if $\dim\mathrm{Hom}((DX)^{\otimes n}\otimes X^{\otimes n},\mathbf{1})<n!$ for some $n\in\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}$, where $D$ is the Grothendieck-Verdier duality functor of $\mathcal{C}$. Using this variant, we show that if $V$ is a simple self-contragredient vertex operator algebra such that the category $\mathcal{C}^1_V$ of $C_1$-cofinite grading-restricted generalized $V$-modules is closed under contragredient modules, then $\mathcal{C}^1_V$ is a locally finite $\mathbb{C}$-linear abelian rigid braided ribbon category. We also show that if $V$ is a simple $\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}$-graded self-contragredient $C_2$-cofinite vertex operator algebra, then the category of all grading-restricted generalized $V$-modules is a (not necessarily semisimple) modular tensor category.

</details>


### [130] [Towards invertibility of derived categories of sheaf-theoretic line operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18555)
*Claudia Scheimbauer,Jackson Van Dyke*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究三维拓扑量子场论（topological quantum field theory, TQFT）中层论线算子（sheaf-theoretic line operators）所构成的编织幺半 $\infty$-范畴（braided monoidal $\infty$-category）在更高 Morita 范畴中何时可逆，从而决定可逆的四维反常理论（anomaly theory）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是判定三维 TQFT 的线算子范畴是否给出可逆的四维反常理论，并考察在导出（derived）情形下这一可逆性判据与经典有限情形之间的差异。

Method: 方法包含三部分：其一，推广 Ben-Zvi–Francis–Nadler 的结论，考察拟凝聚层（quasi-coherent sheaves）$\mathrm{QC}(X)$ 与环路栈（loop stack）上的卷积范畴的可逆性/平凡性；其二，构造奇数向量空间 $V[1]$ 上二次移位 $\mathcal{D}$-模（2-shifted $\mathcal{D}$-modules）$\mathrm{DMod}^2(V[1])$ 的反例；其三，将群作用反常定义为 $\mathrm{Rep}(G)$ 上的 Witt 类（Witt class），并分析单位根处环面量子群（quantum group）表示范畴相对于量子 Frobenius 的可逆性。

Result: 得到三项结果：$\mathrm{QC}(X)$ 可逆仅当 $X$ 为单点；当栈沿其对角线满足下降（descent）时（如分类栈），环路栈上的卷积范畴为 Morita 平凡；$\mathrm{DMod}^2(V[1])$ 是 3-可对偶（3-dualizable）且非退化（non-degenerate），但既不可因子化（factorizable）也不可余因子化（cofactorizable），与线性 Rozansky–Witten 理论的线算子密切相关；此外，单位根处环面量子群的表示范畴相对于其量子 Frobenius 可逆。

Conclusion: 在导出情形下，非退化（non-degeneracy）不再蕴含可逆性（invertibility），这区别于有限编织张量范畴（finite braided tensor categories）的已知结论；同时，通过 Witt 类刻画群作用反常，可将可逆性判据推广至量子群表示等具体实例。

Abstract: We investigate when the braided monoidal $\infty$-categories of sheaf-theoretic line operators of $3$-dimensional topological quantum field theories are invertible in the higher Morita category, so that they determine invertible $4$-dimensional anomaly theories. We consider a zoo of different examples which are inherently derived. First, enhancing Ben-Zvi--Francis--Nadler, we show that a symmetric monoidal category of quasi-coherent sheaves $\mathrm{QC}(X)$ is invertible only if $X$ is a point, and convolution categories on loop stacks are Morita trivial whenever the stack satisfies descent along its diagonal, e.g. for classifying stacks. Secondly, we provide an example showing that, unlike for finite braided tensor categories, non-degeneracy does not imply invertibility in the derived setting: For an odd vector space $V[1]$, the $2$-shifted $\mathcal{D}$-modules $\mathrm{DMod}^2(V[1])$ are $3$-dualizable and non-degenerate but neither factorizable nor cofactorizable. This category is closely related to the line operators of linear Rozansky--Witten theory. Finally, we define an anomaly of a group action on an invertible braided monoidal $\infty$-category as a Witt class over $\mathrm{Rep}(G)$, and show that the category of representations of the quantum group of a torus at a root of unity is invertible relative to its quantum Frobenius.

</details>

### [131] [Equilibrium Elongation of Charged Nanoclusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17513)
*Efstratios M. Kritikos,William A. Goddard,Paul M. Bellan*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出带电椭球形纳米团簇（charged ellipsoidal nanoclusters）平衡结构的解析模型，通过自由能分析构建能量图谱，确定其平衡态与伸长形状，并与含溶剂化电子的冰纳米团簇分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当电子排斥产生的内部静电应力（electrostatic stresses）超过表面张力（surface tension）时，带电纳米团簇会发生伸长，但其平衡时所达到的具体形状此前尚不清楚，因此需要建立可预测平衡结构的理论模型。

Method: 基于自由能分析（free energy analysis）建立带电椭球形纳米团簇平衡结构的解析模型，并构建能量图谱（energy maps），以团簇尺寸、净电荷（net charge）、表面张力和局部曲率（local curvature）为变量确定平衡态；随后与含溶剂化电子（solvated electrons）的冰纳米团簇分子动力学模拟进行对比。

Result: 尽管模拟中的原子与电子具有离散性（discreteness），连续介质解析模型对伸长量及平衡伸长形状的预测与分子动力学模拟结果均表现出良好一致性。

Conclusion: 该模型可用于预测天体物理与实验室复杂等离子体（complex plasmas）以及大气气溶胶（atmospheric aerosols）中纳米团簇的形状演化。

Abstract: When internal electrostatic stresses from electron repulsions overcome surface tension, charged nanoclusters elongate. However, the exact shape they reach at equilibrium has been unknown. We present an analytical model for the equilibrium structure of charged ellipsoidal nanoclusters via a free energy analysis. Energy maps are constructed that determine the equilibrium states as a function of cluster size, net charge, surface tension, and local curvature. The model is compared with molecular dynamics simulations of ice nanoclusters having solvated electrons. Despite the discreteness of the simulated atoms and electrons, both the predictions of the continuum analytical model for elongation and the equilibrium elongated shape show good agreement. The model could therefore predict nanocluster shape evolution in astrophysical and laboratory complex plasmas and atmospheric aerosols.

</details>
