### [132] [Exact stochastic theory of post-transcriptional gene autoregulation mediated by RNA-binding proteins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18062)
*Chen Jia*

Main category: q-bio.MN

TL;DR: 本文建立转录后基因自调控（post-transcriptional gene autoregulation）的精确随机理论，给出蛋白爆发规模分布、稳态蛋白数分布（合流 Heun 函数形式）及亚泊松涨落判据，并揭示转录与转录后调控在噪声抑制与节律传递上的优势反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转录反馈回路（transcriptional feedback loops）的解析理论已较成熟，但转录后反馈回路（post-transcriptional feedback loops）的精确随机分析仍相对稀缺且极具挑战性，因而需要发展精确理论。

Method: 针对可逆的蛋白-mRNA 结合同时调控翻译与 mRNA 稳定性的转录后自调控机制，推导状态依赖的蛋白爆发规模分布（burst-size distribution）、以合流 Heun 函数（confluent Heun functions）表示的稳态蛋白数分布，以及亚泊松涨落（sub-Poissonian fluctuations）的精确判据。

Result: 通过比较转录网络与转录后网络，发现在噪声抑制与节律传递（rhythm transmission）两方面，二者的相对优势发生反转：负反馈（negative feedback）有利于转录后调控，而正反馈（positive feedback）有利于转录调控。

Conclusion: 调控模式（regulatory pattern）从根本上决定基因表达涨落与动态响应，即反馈类型与调控层级的组合共同塑造表达噪声与动力学行为。

Abstract: While analytical theories of transcriptional feedback loops have been well established, exact stochastic analyses of post-transcriptional feedback loops remain relatively scarce and are notoriously challenging. Here, we develop an exact theory of post-transcriptional gene autoregulation, in which reversible protein-mRNA binding modulates both translation and mRNA stability. We derive the state-dependent protein burst-size distribution, the steady-state protein number distribution in terms of confluent Heun functions, and an exact criterion for sub-Poissonian fluctuations. By comparing transcriptional and post-transcriptional networks, we reveal a reversal in their relative advantages in noise suppression and rhythm transmission: negative feedback favors post-transcriptional regulation, whereas positive feedback favors transcriptional regulation. Our results reveal how the regulatory pattern fundamentally shapes gene expression fluctuations and dynamic responses.

</details>

### [133] [In-plane diffusion, spurious drift and a physics-structured gap predictor for a free colloidal pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17508)
*Juan Pablo Staforelli Vivanco,Cristobal Augusto Valdés Cruces*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作利用两枚1.95 μm聚苯乙烯小球在光学镊子释放实验中的间隙依赖扩散系数构建Fokker-Planck预测器，虽能复现间隙分布的90%宽度，但无法区分随机约定α=0、1/2、1，因而给出平面测量方法基线并指出分辨α所需的测量条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当布朗粒子扩散系数随位置变化时，会产生由随机约定（stochastic convention）α决定的噪声诱导（伪）漂移，本文探讨该物理能在何种程度上从实验数据中被提取。

Method: 在2–40 °C的五个浴温度下，用闪烁全息光镊（blinking holographic optical tweezers）释放一对1.95 μm聚苯乙烯小球，测量单球扩散系数及间隙依赖的平行与垂直扩散系数D∥(h)、D⊥(h)，据此构建预测未来间隙的Fokker-Planck预测器，并在留出释放数据上评分。

Result: 球对内部单球扩散系数为0.093–0.098 μm² s⁻¹，远低于水的Stokes-Einstein值（0.126–0.361 μm² s⁻¹），孤立小球亦复现该亏损，说明并非成对效应；预测器可还原间隙分布90%宽度至800 ms，但因α依赖的平均位移比该宽度小一个数量级而无法辨别α；系综伪漂移在温度范围两端与零一致，α回归的R²不超过0.22。

Conclusion: 本文提供一个平面方法基线（planar methods baseline），并明确了分辨随机约定α所需的测量条件。

Abstract: When the diffusivity of a Brownian particle depends on position, its mean displacement acquires a noise-induced (spurious) drift whose magnitude is set by the stochastic convention $α$. We ask how much of this physics can be extracted from a pair of 1.95 $μ$m polystyrene spheres released from blinking holographic optical tweezers at five bath temperatures between 2 and 40 $^\circ$C. The single-sphere diffusivity inside the pair stays between 0.093 and 0.098 $μ$m$^2$ s$^{-1}$, whereas the Stokes-Einstein value for water rises from 0.126 to 0.361 $μ$m$^2$ s$^{-1}$; an isolated sphere reproduces the deficit, which is therefore not a pair effect. From the measured gap-dependent diffusivities $D_\parallel(h)$ and $D_\perp(h)$ we build a Fokker-Planck predictor of the future gap and score it on held-out releases. It recovers the 90% width of the gap distribution up to 800 ms but cannot discriminate between $α= 0$, $1/2$ and $1$, because the $α$-dependent mean shift is an order of magnitude smaller than that width. The ensemble spurious drift is consistent with zero at both ends of the temperature range, and the regression for $α$ never exceeds $R^2 = 0.22$. We therefore present a planar methods baseline and identify the measurements required to resolve $α$.

</details>


### [134] [Stress peaks and rupture in star-polymer networks with bidisperse arm lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17648)
*Yuichi Masubuchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用能量最小化模拟（energy-minimization simulations）研究双分散臂长（bidisperse arm lengths）幻影星形聚合物网络（phantom star-polymer networks）的拉伸响应，发现大臂长对比下可出现双应力峰，且峰应变遵循以环拓扑密度（cycle-rank density）为变量的主曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究短、长前驱体（precursors）在同一端基交联网络（end-linked network）中形成短-短、短-长、长-长链段时，链段对比、组成、官能度与反应转化率如何影响网络断裂行为，并检验单分散网络中的尺度规律是否适用于双分散体系。

Method: 以能量最小化模拟考察幻影星形聚合物网络的拉伸行为，系统改变臂长对比、组成、官能度（functionality）和反应转化率（reaction conversion）；采用 FENE 力律（FENE force law），分析峰应变与环拓扑密度的关系并与近期提出的力学模型（mechanical model）对照。

Result: 大臂长对比时，在部分组成区间出现两个应力峰：第一峰主要由短-短链段承载，应力下降后由短-长链段主导。峰应变（含双峰情形）在以环拓扑密度为横轴时遵循组成依赖的主曲线（master curves），与单分散网络一致；所提力学模型可描述峰应变对环拓扑序（cycle-rank）的依赖，但参数随组成和臂长对比变化，且两个峰序列的参数不同、在某些条件下难以分别确定。

Conclusion: 加入少量短链段可实现对网络力学性能的 Mark 型（Mark-type）改善；在 FENE 力律下，至全局应力峰所做的功更接近纯长链网络的值，但超出该参考值的改善在统计上未能分辨。

Abstract: The tensile response of phantom star-polymer networks with bidisperse arm lengths is examined by energy-minimization simulations. Short and long precursors form short-short, short-long, and long-long strands within one end-linked network. The contrast among these strands, composition, functionality, and reaction conversion are varied, and their effects on network rupture are examined. At a large length contrast, two stress peaks appear over part of the composition range. In such realizations, short-short strands carry most of the stress at the first peak, while short-long strands carry most of it after the intervening stress drop. Interesting findings are i) that the peak stretches, including those in double-peak cases, follow composition-dependent master curves when plotted against cycle-rank density, as in monodisperse networks, and ii) that a recently proposed mechanical model describes their cycle-rank dependence, with parameters that vary with composition and arm-length contrast. The parameters differ between the peak series, although their separate determination is limited in some conditions. In addition, the Mark-type improvement of the mechanical properties of the networks by adding a fraction of short strands is discussed. With the implemented FENE force law, the work up to the global stress peak is closer to the long-only value, but an improvement above that reference is not statistically resolved.

</details>


### [135] [Active turbulent flows of dense persistent particles driven at constant velocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17777)
*Yonglun Jiang,Juliane Klamser,Ludovic Berthier*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We study dense assemblies of self-propelled particles with infinitely persistent active forces, driven at a constant average velocity $V$ along their propulsion directions. Because the velocity $V$ introduces a finite timescale, it plays a role analogous to the shear rate in globally-deformed amorphous solids, and the system approaches a transition analogous to a yielding transition as $V \to 0$. We construct a force-velocity flow curve which is of the Herschel-Bulkley type, with a finite force threshold for flow. As $V$ decreases, the instantaneous velocity field becomes increasingly dominated by rare, localized plastic events, which produce fat tails in the velocity distribution and coexist with a slow, spatially correlated flow of streams and vortices. Many such events must accumulate throughout the system to fully relax its structure, so that the flow patterns reorganize after particles have been driven over a few particle diameters. The spatial extent of these correlated flows grows rapidly as $V$ decreases, and becomes only limited by the finite system size in our simulations. The kinetic energy spectrum displays a robust $k^{-1/2}$ regime also found in self-propelled particles at large but finite persistence times. Approaching the flow threshold thus leads to the same active turbulent state as increasing the persistence time in dense active matter, while displaying both analogies and differences with the yielding transition in sheared glassy materials.

</details>


### [136] [Shape of semiflexible polymer chains: Lévy walks vs. Persistent random walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17992)
*V. Blavatska,Yu. Honchar,Yu. Holovatch*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文通过数值模拟比较持久随机游走（persistent random walk, PRW）与莱维游走（Lévy walk, LW）两种框架下半柔性聚合物链（semiflexible polymer chain）的构象尺寸与形状特征，并提出以莱维分布平均跳跃长度作为有效持续长度（persistence length）的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在检验莱维游走统计能否有效刻画半柔性聚合物链的普适构象性质，并比较其与经典持久随机游走模型在描述柔性与刚性行为转变上的差异。

Method: 采用数值模拟分析两种模型的链构象；引入映射，将莱维分布（~ℓ^{-(1+σ)}）的平均跳跃长度⟨ℓ⟩视为由指数σ控制的有效持续长度，并定量估算非球形度（asphericity）⟨A₃⟩与扁长度（prolateness）⟨S⟩等普适形状参数。

Result: PRW模型在最大持续概率p→1附近表现出从柔性线团（flexible coil）到刚性棒状（rigid-rod-like）行为的急剧转变（sharp crossover）；而LW模型呈现更丰富的构象行为，形状参数随σ减小（即持续长度增大）而逐渐增大，与既往半柔性聚合物观测结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明莱维游走统计可有效捕捉半柔性聚合物结构的普适构象性质，为描述此类链的尺寸与形状特征提供了可行框架。

Abstract: The conformational size and shape characteristics of semiflexible polymer chains, modeled via two distinct frameworks: persistent random walks (PRW) and Lévy walks (LW), are analyzed through numerical simulations. We introduce a mapping that treats the mean jump length $\langle \ell \rangle$ of a Lévy distribution ($\sim \ell^{-(1+σ)}$) as an effective persistence length of the semiflexible polymer chain, controlled by the exponent $σ$. While the conformational characteristics of the PRW model statistically exhibit a sharp crossover from flexible coil to the rigid-rod-like behavior in the immediate vicinity of the maximum persistence probability $p \to 1$, the LW model shows a much richer conformational behavior. We obtain the quantitative estimates for universal shape parameters, such as asphericity \(\langle A_3 \rangle\) and prolateness \(\langle S \rangle\), which gradually increase with decreasing parameter $σ$ (and thus with an increase of the persistence length), thus supporting previously observed findings for semiflexible polymers. Our results demonstrate the effictiveness of applying Lévy-walk statistics to capture the universal conformational properties of semiflexible polymer structures.

</details>


### [137] [A Cantilever-Based Method for Probing Adhesion Force and Kinetic Detachment Energy in Cohesive Granular Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18021)
*Maxime Lajeunesse,Francisco M. Rocha,Denis Dumont,Vincent Bertin,Yoanh Moratille,Nathalie Sintes-Zydowicz,Eric Drockenmuller,Olivier Pouliquen*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于悬臂梁（cantilever）的方法，结合玻璃梁测力与新型"弹射模式"（catapult mode）测能，可同时测定颗粒间准静态分离力（quasi-static detachment force）与动态分离能（kinetic detachment energy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒间黏附（interparticle adhesion）对黏性颗粒材料（cohesive granular materials）的流动行为至关重要，但目前在颗粒尺度上的定量表征仍具挑战性。

Method: 采用玻璃梁悬臂测量力，并引入新型弹射模式（catapult mode）测量能量，从而在同一装置中同时获得准静态分离力与动态分离能；将该方法应用于聚合物包覆二氧化硅颗粒、湿颗粒介质（wet granular media）和交联聚（2-乙基己基丙烯酸酯）（PEHA）微粒三种黏性材料。

Result: 测量结果揭示了力与能量之间的基本解耦（decoupling）现象，表明二者在表征颗粒性质方面同等关键。

Conclusion: 力与能量应被视为相互独立且同等重要的颗粒黏附表征量，仅依靠单一指标不足以完整描述黏性颗粒材料的性质。

Abstract: Interparticle adhesion plays a critical role in the flow behavior of cohesive granular materials, yet quantitative characterization at the particle scale remains challenging. Here, we present a cantilever-based method that combines a glass-beam cantilever for force measurements with a novel catapult mode for energy measurements, enabling the determination of both quasi-static detachment force and kinetic detachment energy. Applied to three different cohesive materials: polymer-coated silica particles, wet granular media, and cross-linked poly(2-ethylhexyl acrylate) (PEHA) microparticles, this method reveals a fundamental decoupling between force and energy, suggesting that both quantities are equally critical for characterizing grain properties.

</details>


### [138] [Comment on "Kinematic and Rheological Equivalence of Steady Shearing and Planar Extensional Flows"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18048)
*Fabian Hillebrand*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该评论（Comment）反驳了原文关于简单剪切（simple shear）与平面拉伸（planar extension）在“运动学”与“流变学”上等价的普适性主张，指出该关系仅对特定单模式本构模型（constitutive model）成立，并非源于流场本身的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原文声称其等价性结论“对任何物质客观性本构方程（materially objective constitutive equation）均成立”，评论旨在指出这一普适性论断缺乏依据，需要界定该结果成立的模型条件与失效范围。

Method: 以模型层面的反例作为论证手段：指出该关系仅对“应力与单一张量共轴、且该张量演化方程线性依赖于速度梯度（velocity gradient）”的单模式模型精确成立，并考察稀溶液动力学理论（kinetic theory）与缠结体系积分型模型是否满足条件。

Result: 该关系对稀溶液的标准动力学理论（如 FENE 哑铃模型，FENE dumbbells）失败，对缠结溶液与熔体的积分型管模型（integral tube model）或 K-BKZ 模型同样失败；因此原文结论是所采用闭合近似（closure）的产物，而非流场间存在内在等价性。

Conclusion: 真实流体中该关系的精度无法由此类闭合模型推断，必须通过实测平面拉伸数据（planar extensional data）进行独立验证。

Abstract: In Reference [Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 144003 (2026)], the authors claim the 'kinematic' and 'rheological' equivalence between simple shear and planar extension. They state that their result holds "for any materially objective constitutive equation." The proposed relation is exact for single-mode constitutive models whose stress is coaxial with a single tensor obeying an evolution equation that depends linearly on the velocity gradient. However, it fails for standard kinetic theories of dilute solutions, e.g. FENE dumbbells, and for the integral tube or K-BKZ models of entangled solutions and melts. Their result is a consequence of the employed closures, not of an underlying equivalence between the flows. Its accuracy for real fluids cannot be inferred from such closed models and instead requires independent validation against measured planar extensional data.

</details>


### [139] [A Tissue Vertex Model grounded in Continuum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18295)
*Jairo M. Rojas,Mayisha Z. Nakib,Sascha Hilgenfeldt*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于连续介质弹性（continuum elasticity）的修正顶点模型（revised vertex model），用以替代二维标准顶点模型（SVM）对无序单层融合组织（confluent tissues）建模，其能量可由多边形细胞形状解析处理且力对形变的依赖显著弱于SVM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有无序单层融合组织建模高度依赖二维标准顶点模型（SVM），其以细胞面积与周长的二次依赖构成能量泛函来简化细胞弹性，但真实细胞内部即便是粗粒化描述也应由代表细胞质与外周皮层（cortex）的连续介质材料构成，弹性应取决于应力与应变场，因此需要更贴近材料性质的模型基础。

Method: 从连续介质弹性（continuum elasticity）出发构造弹性能量，证明该能量对多边形细胞形状可解析求解，并可将面积依赖消除，转而与细胞周长长度建立相关性分析，且该相关性的周长依赖并非二次形式，由此导出修正顶点模型。

Result: 所导出的修正顶点模型参数可由组织材料性质（material properties）预测，且其产生的力（resultant forces）对应变（strain）的依赖性远小于SVM；同时能量与周长相关性良好，但相关性的周长依赖不呈二次关系。

Conclusion: 以连续介质弹性为基础的顶点模型为无序单层融合组织提供了物理上更合理且参数可预测的建模框架，并指出SVM中面积—周长二次能量假设在应变依赖方面存在偏差。

Abstract: Practical modeling of disordered single-layer confluent tissues has relied heavily on the two-dimensional standard vertex model (SVM), which simplifies cell elasticity using an energy functional determined by quadratic dependence of cell area and perimeter values. Realistically, even a coarse-grained cell interior consists of effective continuum materials representing cytoplasm and peripheral cortex, with elastic energy depending on stress and strain fields. It is shown that this energy is analytically tractable for polygonal cell shapes, that its area dependence can be eliminated, that it correlates well with cell perimeter length, and that the perimeter dependence of the correlation is not quadratic. This results in a revised vertex model derived from continuum elasticity whose parameters are predicted from tissue material properties and whose resultant forces are far less strain-dependent than in the SVM.

</details>


### [140] [Delay-induced current reversal in traveling activity waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18341)
*Ueli Töpfer,Adrià Garcés,Miguel Chamorro-Burgos,Demian Levis,Alvaro Domínguez,Ignacio Pagonabarraga,Lucio Isa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文研究响应型自驱动粒子（self-propelled particles）在行波式活性场中的定向输运，揭示由速度适应延迟（speed adaptation delay）产生的第二记忆通道可重塑甚至逆转粒子流方向，且该现象依赖于响应的时间不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动粒子的速度可对局部活性源（local activity source）作出响应，但速度响应存在内在延迟，现有研究多关注取向记忆（orientational memory），对速度适应所携带的独立记忆及其对输运的影响缺乏系统认识。

Method: 以受紫外光（UV）调控速度的二氧化硅-二氧化钛Janus微游泳体（Janus microswimmers）为实验体系，其速度因二氧化钛光导性适应（photoconductivity adaptation）而延迟弛豫；结合实验、朗之万模拟（Langevin simulations）与Fokker-Planck方程的矩展开（moment expansion）。

Result: 延迟响应在行波紫外照明下重塑输运，并可使粒子流相对于仅由取向记忆设定的方向发生反转；超过临界延迟值后，粒子流（particle current）方向与波传播方向相反。该行为要求对速度适应呈不对称响应，即瞬时上升、延迟下降；理论表明该现象源于响应不对称而非延迟本身，反转不对称性即反转延迟对粒子流的影响。

Conclusion: 研究确立“推进-延迟不对称性（propulsion-delay asymmetry）”作为响应型活性粒子在行波活性景观中进行定向输运的控制参数。

Abstract: Self-propelled particles that regulate their speed based on a response to a local activity source can move with or against a traveling wave of that source depending on their response time. Silica-titania Janus microswimmers, whose UV-controlled speed relaxes with a delay intrinsic to the titania photoconductivity adaptation, carry two independent memory channels: the familiar directional memory dictated by persistent active Brownian motion, and a distinct memory from the speed adaptation. Combining experiments, Langevin simulations, and a moment expansion of the corresponding Fokker-Planck equation, we show that this delayed response to speed adaptation reshapes transport under traveling UV-illumination waves and can even reverse it relative to the direction set by orientational memory alone. In particular, we observe that delay promotes transport in the direction opposite to the traveling activity waves, where, beyond a critical value, the particle current reverses with respect to the wave propagation direction. Crucially, this behavior requires an asymmetric response to the speed adaptation, with an instantaneous rise but a delayed fall upon exposure to time-varying UV light. Theory further shows that this phenomenology is specific to the response asymmetry rather than to the mere presence of a delay, i.e. reversing the asymmetry reverses the effect of the delay on the particle current relative to the wave. We thus establish propulsion-delay asymmetry as a control parameter for directed transport of responsive active particles in traveling activity landscapes.

</details>


### [141] [Frustrated Interlayer Coupling Creates a Higher-Order Helical Superstructure in a Twist-Bend Smectic Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18347)
*Abigail Pearson,Damian Pociecha,Magdalena M. Majewska,Nataša Vaupotič,Rebecca Walker,Ewa Gorecka*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 弯曲液晶二聚体通过自发分级结构组织，解决螺旋倾斜与同向/反向倾斜堆积之间的竞争与阻挫，并在两者可比区间形成新的调制近晶相SmCTB-mod。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弯曲液晶二聚体（bent liquid-crystalline dimers）是研究非手性分子自发产生手性（chirality）的模型体系；不同端链长度下，螺旋倾斜（heliconical）、同向倾斜（synclinic）与反向倾斜（anticlinic）堆积倾向在倾斜近晶相（tilted smectic phases）中相互竞争，其协调机制尚待阐明。

Method: 以端链长度作为调控参量，系统考察短同系物与长端链体系中的相行为，并利用共振X射线衍射（resonant x-ray diffraction）的峰形特征解析结构周期性。

Result: 短同系物中扭曲-弯曲（twist-bend）倾向占优，形成螺旋周期为四个分子长度的SmCTB-SH相，以及低温下四层单元渐进旋转的双螺旋SmCTB-DH相；端链增长使该倾向逐渐受抑，转为连续的简单同向与反向倾斜排列。在两种倾向相当的区间出现SmCTB-mod相，其内部四层单元发生周期性调制，并使共振X射线衍射峰分裂为围绕四层周期性信号对称分布的四个信号。

Conclusion: 弯曲二聚体通过复杂的自发分级结构组织（hierarchical structural organization）化解局域堆积模式阻挫（local-packing-mode frustration），SmCTB-mod相提供了兼顾竞争性螺旋倾斜与同向/反向倾斜倾向的结构途径。

Abstract: Bent liquid-crystalline dimers provide a model system for studying the spontaneous emergence of chirality from achiral molecules. Depending on terminal-chain length, these materials exhibit a competition between heliconical, synclinic, and anticlinic packing tendencies in tilted smectic phases. In short homologues, the tendency to form twist-bend phases dominates, leading to two distinct helical phases: the SmCTB-SH phase, with a helical periodicity of four molecular lengths, and at lower temperatures, a double helical structure - the SmCTB-DH phase, with progressive rotation of the four-layer unit. With increasing terminal chain length, this twist-bend tendency is gradually suppressed, favoring phases with simple synclinic and anticlinic tilt arrangement in successive layers. Remarkably, in the regime where these tendencies become comparable, a new modulated smectic phase, SmCTB-mod develops; a double helical structure in which the internal four-layer unit undergoes a periodic modulation. This additional level of structural organization provides a means of accommodating competing heliconical and synclinic/anticlinic tendencies and produces a characteristic splitting of the resonant x-ray diffraction peaks into four signals symmetrically located around a signal of four-layer periodicity. These results reveal how bent dimers resolve local-packing-mode frustration through complex spontaneous hierarchical structural organization.

</details>


### [142] [Emergent intercellular contact-force networks shaped by deformability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18414)
*Koshi Yoshida,Nen Saito,Shuji Ishihara,Kaoru Sugimura*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用傅里叶轮廓细胞模型（Fourier Contour Cell model）对密集可形变活性细胞的接触力（contact force）组织进行数值研究，发现接触力呈双峰分布（bimodal），并由持续同调（persistent homology）揭示出弱尖端-尖端互作被强侧向-侧向互作环包围的网络基元（network motif）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在阐明细胞可形变性（cell deformability）与主动运动性（active motility）如何塑造细胞间力的空间组织，这一问题在密集活性细胞聚集体中尚不清晰。

Method: 采用 Fourier Contour Cell model，实现大尺度细胞形变的高效模拟；考察中等自推进强度（self-propulsion strength）下的软液态区（soft liquid regime），并结合持续同调分析力网络结构；另以细长但不可形变细胞作对照，并引入曲率约束（curvature constraint）检验局部曲率作用。

Result: 软液态区接触力分布呈双峰，出现弱互作的独立群体；持续同调显示弱尖端-尖端（tip-tip）互作被强侧向-侧向（side-side）互作构成的环包围。细长不可形变细胞对照表明该双峰分布与网络基元依赖可形变性而非细长形状本身；曲率约束显示局部细胞曲率调控弱尖端-尖端互作强度。

Conclusion: 细胞可形变性与局部几何约束共同组织密集活性细胞聚集体的力传递（force transmission）。

Abstract: We present a numerical study of contact-force organization in dense assemblies of active, deformable cells. Using the Fourier Contour Cell model, which enables efficient simulation of large cell deformations, we investigate how cell deformability and active motility shape spatial organization of intercellular forces. We find that, in the soft liquid regime at intermediate self-propulsion strength, the contact-force distribution becomes bimodal, reflecting the emergence of a distinct population of weak interactions. Persistent-homology analysis further reveals a characteristic force-network structure in which weak tip-tip interactions are enclosed by loops of strong side-side interactions. Simulations with elongated but non-deformable cells demonstrate that both the bimodal force distribution and the associated network motif require cell deformability rather than elongated cell shape per se. Furthermore, introducing a curvature constraint shows that local cell curvature regulates the strength of weak tip-tip interactions. Together, these results demonstrate that cell deformability and local geometric constraints can organize force transmission across dense active cellular assemblies.

</details>

### [143] [Painlevé quadrics and their confluence for the dispersionless Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18209)
*Benoit Huard Lewis Patterson,Anh-Asma Koheeallee*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 论文研究无色散Kadomtsev–Petviashvili方程（dispersionless KP, dKP）中一类特殊解，其几何由第一与第二Painlevé方程（P_I, P_II）控制：解的等值面（level surfaces）对应三类中心二次曲面，并刻画其退化为平面的条件，最后给出临界标度下二次曲面的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅表明旨在构造并系统研究dKP方程中与Painlevé方程几何相关联的一类特殊解，并刻画其等值面的退化结构。

Method: 将dKP方程的解按等值面对应的中心二次曲面（central quadrics）分为三族：抛物族由P_I参数化、双曲族由P_II的有理解参数化、齐次椭圆族涉及P_II的Hastings–McLeod解；通过Painlevé超越函数的驻点、相邻解交点及极点结构分析退化条件，并用临界标度（critical scaling）考察n阶有理解的极限。

Result: 抛物族的退化等值面出现在超越函数的驻点（stationary points）；双曲族的退化发生在相邻解的交点处，并给出退化点个数的精确计数；齐次椭圆族中，Hastings–McLeod解（至多相差符号）被刻画为具有Airy渐近、无实极点且其二次曲面无有限退化层的唯一非平凡解。

Conclusion: 在临界标度下，第n个有理Painlevé-II解生成的二次曲面在局部、避开极点处收敛到与Painlevé-I tritronquée解相关联的抛物二次曲面，误差为O(n^{-1/5})，从而建立了P_I与P_II有理解所刻画几何之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We construct and study a distinguished class of solutions of the dispersionless Kadomtsev--Petviashvili (dKP) equation whose geometry is governed by the first and second Painlevé equations. The level surfaces of these solutions correspond to three geometric families of central quadrics, and we determine when their level surfaces degenerate into planes. In the parabolic family parametrised by $\mathrm{P_I}$, degenerate levels occur at the stationary points of the transcendent. For the hyperbolic family parametrised by rational solutions of $\mathrm{P_{II}}$, we show that degenerate levels occur at points where consecutive solutions intersect and we give a precise count of such degeneracies. In the homogeneous elliptic family, the Hastings--McLeod solution of $\mathrm{P_{II}}$ is characterised, up to sign, as the unique non-trivial real pole-free solution with Airy asymptotics whose quadric has no finite degenerate level. Finally, under critical scaling, the quadrics generated by the $n$-th rational Painlevé-II solution converge locally, and away from poles, to the parabolic quadrics associated with the Painlevé-I tritronquée solution, with error $O(n^{-1/5})$.

</details>

### [144] [Localization for Lipschitz monotone quasi-periodic operators with power-law hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17799)
*Hongyi Cao,Yunfeng Shi,Peizhen Wang*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文对 Z^d 上具有 Lipschitz 单调势与幂律跳跃项的准周期算子，证明在小耦合条件下若跳跃衰减指数 r 大于 Diophantine 指数 τ，则在每个允许相位均出现谱局域化与动力学局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准周期算子（quasi-periodic operators）在长程幂律跳跃（power-law hopping）情形下的局域化（localization）问题，将已有局域化结论推广到跳跃衰减指数与 Diophantine 指数可比较的范围内。

Method: 采用指数增长尺度下的多尺度分析（multiscale analysis）；通过耦合常数上的同伦（homotopy）选取局部本征值分支，并利用 Schur 补估计（Schur complement estimates）控制坐标的逐次消去。

Result: 在小耦合且 r > τ 的条件下，对每个允许相位（allowed phase）均建立谱局域化（spectral localization）与动力学局域化（dynamical localization）。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Lipschitz 单调势与幂律跳跃类准周期算子在给定指数条件下具有完全的局域化性质，其证明框架依赖于多尺度分析、参数同伦与 Schur 补估计的组合。

Abstract: We prove spectral and dynamical localization for quasi-periodic operators on $\mathbb Z^d$ with Lipschitz monotone potentials and power-law hopping. For sufficiently small coupling, localization holds at every allowed phase whenever the hopping decay exponent $r$ exceeds the Diophantine exponent $τ$. The proof is based on multiscale analysis with exponentially growing scales. We use homotopy in the coupling constant to select the local eigenvalue branches and Schur complement estimates to control the successive elimination of coordinates.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Essential spectrum of singularly perturbed Dirac operators on bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17994)
*Jussi Behrndt,Badreddine Benhellal,Markus Holzmann,Konstantin Pankrashkin*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{R}^2$、$\mathbb{R}^3$ 有界区域上带椭圆边界条件的自伴 Dirac 算子，受支撑于紧曲线或曲面的奇异 $\delta$-壳势扰动；证明耦合常数的临界值可使原本纯离散谱出现本质谱，且其形态与维数、几何及耦合强度有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未扰动的自伴 Dirac 算子（self-adjoint Dirac operator）在椭圆边界条件下具有纯离散谱（purely discrete spectrum），而奇异 $\delta$-壳势（$\delta$-shell potential）扰动可能在临界耦合强度下改变谱的定性结构，这一现象值得刻画。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 与 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 的有界区域上，考虑带椭圆边界条件（elliptic boundary conditions）的自伴 Dirac 算子，并施加支撑于紧曲线或紧曲面的奇异 $\delta$-壳势扰动，分析其谱性质。

Result: 当耦合常数（coupling constants）取某些临界值时，本质谱（essential spectrum）出现：在二维中，相互作用支撑（interaction support）的每个连通分支产生一个本质谱点；在三维中则产生一个闭区间，其长度取决于几何与耦合常数。

Conclusion: 奇异 $\delta$-壳势的临界耦合强度能够使纯离散谱转变为含本质谱的谱结构，且本质谱的维数依赖性与几何依赖性明确。

Abstract: In this paper self-adjoint Dirac operators on bounded domains in $\mathbb{R}^2$ and $\mathbb{R}^3$ with elliptic boundary conditions perturbed by singular $δ$-shell potentials supported on compact curves or surfaces are considered. While the unperturbed operator has purely discrete spectrum, it is shown that certain critical values of the interaction strengths lead to the appearance of essential spectrum. More precisely, each connected component of the interaction support, where the coupling constants have such critical values, produces in dimension two a point and in dimension three even a closed interval in the essential spectrum, where the size of the latter depends on the geometry and the coupling constants.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Annihilation versus anchoring of a topologically charged pair on a perforated two-dimensional surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17570)
*Patrick Romanescu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究离散环面（discrete torus）上两个带相反拓扑荷（topological charge）的独立随机游走（random walk）：二者靠近到核心距离内即湮灭（annihilation），任一先触到“洞”即被锚定（anchoring）。作者证明湮灭先于锚定的概率收敛到由核心与洞尺寸对数决定的显式权重之比，并给出“结局定理”（fate theorem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象是含多个分离孔洞（holes）的二维离散环面上带相反电荷的两随机游走的竞争性结局问题，即湮灭与锚定两种终止方式之间的概率竞争，旨在给出该类稀有事件（rare events）竞争的定量刻画。

Method: 方法结合了可逆马尔可夫链（reversible Markov chains）中稀有集合的抽象竞争引理（abstract competition lemma）与尖锐的二维格林函数估计（Green's function estimates）；权重显式给出，仅对数依赖于核心与孔洞尺寸。

Result: 湮灭先于锚定的概率收敛到 w_0/(w_0+Σ_j w_j)；对点状核心与孔洞的极限为 1/(1+k)。结局定理表明：点状情形下，该对游走以概率 (k-1)/(k+1) 终结为锚定在两个不同孔洞上的两个相反电荷，且承载孔洞对 {j,l} 以正比于 w_j w_l 的概率被选中。

Conclusion: 环面上相反电荷随机游走的最终结局（湮灭或分置于两洞）可由显式权重完全刻画，权重仅对尺寸呈对数依赖；点状极限下概率仅由孔洞数 k 决定。

Abstract: Two independent random walks of opposite topological charge move on the discrete torus $(\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z})^2$, which contains $k$ well-separated holes. The walks annihilate when they come within a core distance of each other, and each walk is anchored when it first touches a hole. We prove that the probability of annihilation before anchoring converges to $w_0/(w_0+\sum_j w_j)$, where the weights are explicit and depend only logarithmically on the sizes of the core and of the holes; for point-like core and holes, the limit is $1/(1+k)$. We then prove a fate theorem: the pair either terminates or ends as two opposite charges anchored on two different holes, with probability $(k-1)/(k+1)$ in the point-like case, and the hosting pair $\{j,l\}$ is chosen with probability proportional to $w_jw_l$. The proof combines an abstract competition lemma for rare sets in reversible Markov chains with sharp two-dimensional Green's function estimates.

</details>


### [147] [Cubic local observability of Gaussian-blind sources in thermal $φ^4_2$ fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17716)
*Rubin Zhang,Ning Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究圆周上大质量高斯热场中局部观测不可见的外部源，证明对四阶模型局部 Wick 三次均值存在与截断无关、对源一致的 C^1 展开，并据此给出源空间上的单射性、奇异值上界及局部 Araki 相对熵下界与一致分离定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量热场中热质量移动的一阶信号会掩盖外部源的可探测性，故需通过二次参考标定消去该一阶项，以便研究剩余的局部可观测性极限与源反演的稳定性。

Method: 采用二次参考标定（quadratic-reference calibration）去除一阶热质量移动信号，在所得四阶模型上证明局部 Wick 三次均值的截断无关、源一致 C^1 展开；用正的双边 Laplace 表示（bilateral Laplace representation）证单射性，用解析性给出指数型奇异值上界，并由反射正性（reflection positivity）恒等式导出稳定性界。

Result: 所得展开的首项算子以热协方差（thermal covariance）时间积分的立方为核；单射性在整个外部源空间上成立，且具有指数奇异值上界；在任意固定有限维源空间上得到局部 Araki 相对熵（Araki relative entropy）下界，并在任意给定 Sobolev 分辨率下得到一致分离定理；在附加区间长度条件下给出显式但非最优的双对数条件稳定性界。

Conclusion: 结果建立了该热核下外部源可辨识性与反演条件稳定性的理论基础，连续概率估计与 Feynman–Kac–Nelson 构造的可积性假设在第 3 节中被验证。

Abstract: We study external sources that are invisible to a spatially local observation in a massive Gaussian thermal field on a circle. A quadratic-reference calibration removes the first-order thermal mass-shift signal. For the resulting quartic model we prove a cutoff-independent, source-uniform $C^1$ expansion of local Wick cubic means. Its leading operator has the time-integrated cube of the thermal covariance as its kernel. A positive bilateral Laplace representation proves injectivity on the entire external source space, while analyticity gives exponential singular-value upper bounds. The expansion yields local Araki relative-entropy lower bounds on every fixed finite-dimensional source space, and a uniform separation theorem at every prescribed Sobolev resolution. Under an additional interval-length condition, a reflection-positivity identity yields an explicit, nonoptimal double-logarithmic conditional stability bound for this specific thermal kernel. Continuum probability estimates and the integrability assumptions of the Feynman--Kac--Nelson construction are verified in Section~3.

</details>


### [148] [The speed of convergence for propagation of chaos in the mean-field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17911)
*Jonas Jalowy,Matthias Löwe,Sara Terveer*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文针对零外场 Curie–Weiss 模型（Curie–Weiss model），推导了混沌传播（propagation of chaos）增强情形下相对熵（relative entropy）的精确渐近展开，并区分亚临界与临界温度下的不同速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作对 Curie–Weiss 模型中 k-自旋边缘分布（k-spin marginal）与对称乘积测度（symmetric product measure）之间的相对熵缺乏精细的定量刻画，尤其在临界温度附近速率如何转变尚不清楚。

Method: 对固定逆温度（inverse temperature）0<β<1 与临界点 β=1 分别给出相对熵的渐近公式，并研究 β_N↑1 的插值序列；证明核心在于利用对关联（pair correlations）控制似然比（likelihood ratio）的首阶修正，从而同时精确控制卡方散度（chi-square divergence）与相对熵。

Result: 亚临界情形下相对熵为 β²k(k−1)/(4(1−β)²N²)+o(k²/N²)（k=o(N)）；临界点处速率变为 α_c²k(k−1)/(4N)+o(k²/N)（k=o(√N)），其中 α_c 为重整化磁化强度（rescaled magnetization）极限四次律（quartic law）的二阶矩；并给出连接两区间的插值渐近。

Conclusion: 相对熵的渐近行为由温度区间决定，临界点附近速率由 N⁻¹ 主导而非 N⁻²；证明表明对关联是决定似然比首阶修正的关键量，为相关散度的精细估计提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We derive sharp relative-entropy asymptotics for increasing propagation of chaos in the Curie--Weiss model at zero external field. For every fixed inverse temperature $0<β<1$, the relative entropy of a $k$-spin marginal with respect to the symmetric product measure is $$ \frac{β^2}{4(1-β)^2}\frac{k(k-1)}{N^2}+o\left(\frac{k^2}{N^2}\right),\qquad k=o(N). $$ At the critical inverse temperature $β=1$, the rate changes to $α_{\mathrm c}^2 k(k-1)/(4N)+o(k^2/N)$ in the regime $k=o(\sqrt N)$, where $α_{\mathrm c}$ is the second moment of the limiting quartic law of the rescaled magnetization. We also obtain asymptotics for sequences $β_N\uparrow1$ that interpolate between these two regimes.
  Crucially, the proof reveals that the pair correlations determine the leading correction to the likelihood ratio, providing precise control of both the chi-square divergence and relative entropy.

</details>


### [149] [Rigidity of the moving particle lemma for the interchange process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18483)
*Joe P. Chen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文确定了有限连通加权图（weighted graph）上互换过程（interchange process）在移动粒子引理（moving particle lemma）中取等号的精确条件，并给出各阶与各粒子扇区中的等号核（equality kernel）分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动粒子引理是分析互换过程与排他过程（exclusion process）谱隙（spectral gap）等定量性质的工具，但等号成立的情形此前未被完整刻画。论文旨在给出等号可达的精确图论与电压条件。

Method: 在两端点 $x\neq y$ 间施加单位电压降，借助表示论（representation theory）中的不可约表示（irreducible representation）按至多两行分拆（partition）分类；再将研究化归到两粒子排他过程（two-particle exclusion process），通过调和电压（harmonic voltage）在非端点顶点及非端点诱导子图（induced subgraph）连通分量上的取值分类情形。

Result: 等号只能在由至多两行分拆标号的不可约表示中出现，且常数扇区与线性扇区之外的等号现象在两粒子情形已可见。等号恰在两种情形下成立：其一，每个非端点顶点的调和电压为 $\tfrac12$ 且非端点诱导子图不连通；其二，调和电压在每个连通分量上为常数，且两种取值 $a$ 与 $1-a$（$0\le a<\tfrac12$）均出现。

Conclusion: 论文在每种情形下计算了各阶与各粒子扇区中的等号核，所得分解给出排他过程的完整等号核，并经由正则表示（regular representation）给出互换过程的完整等号核。

Abstract: Let $G=(V,c)$ be a finite connected weighted graph. We establish the exact conditions for attaining equality in the moving particle lemma for the interchange process on $G$ subject to a unit voltage drop across two terminal vertices $x\neq y \in V$. First, equality can occur only in irreducible representations indexed by partitions with at most two rows. We then show that the equality phenomenon beyond the constant and linear sectors is already visible in the two-particle exclusion process. Such equality occurs in exactly two cases: either the harmonic $x$--$y$ voltage is $\frac12$ on every nonterminal vertex, and the induced nonterminal graph $G[V\setminus\{x,y\}]$ is disconnected; or the harmonic voltage is constant on each connected component of $G[V\setminus \{x,y\}]$, and both voltage values $a$ and $1-a$, with $0\le a<\frac12$, occur. In each case we compute the equality kernel in every degree and particle sector. The resulting decomposition gives the complete equality kernel for exclusion and, through the regular representation, for the interchange process.

</details>


### [150] [Internal diameter bounds for DLA on random planar maps under invariance principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18540)
*Deven Mithal*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在随机平面映射（random planar maps）上建立了外部扩散限制聚集（External Diffusion Limited Aggregation, DLA）簇内部直径的次线性界（sublinear bounds），并给出两类条件性生长结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机平面映射上DLA簇的内部直径（internal diameter）尺度，需刻画其生长速度是否受次线性限制；为此需在确定性平面映射上建立可验证的生长条件。

Method: 发展针对确定性平面映射（deterministic planar maps）的两个条件性DLA生长结果：较弱条件给出DLA的次线性生长，较强条件给出多项式次线性（polynomially sublinear）生长；随后分别在γ-mated CRT映射上验证强假设几乎必然（almost surely, a.s.）成立，在遍历无标度随机环境（ergodic scale-free random environments）中验证弱条件a.s.成立。

Result: 得到对应的几乎必然次线性界：在γ-mated CRT映射上获得多项式次线性界，在遍历无标度随机环境中获得次线性界。

Conclusion: 摘要表明，通过“确定性映射上的条件性结果＋随机环境下的假设验证”这一框架，可在两类随机平面映射模型中确立DLA簇内部直径的a.s.次线性上界。

Abstract: We establish sublinear bounds for the internal diameter of External Diffusion Limited Aggregation (DLA) clusters on random planar maps. We develop two conditional DLA growth results for deterministic planar maps: the weaker condition yielding sublinear growth for DLA, and the stronger yielding polynomially sublinear growth for DLA. We then verify the strong assumption a.s. for the $γ$-mated CRT maps, and the weak condition a.s. for ergodic scale-free random environments, establishing the corresponding a.s bounds.

</details>

### [151] [Sasakian Hodge Numbers of Calabi-Yau Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17419)
*Edward Hirst,Henrique N. Sá Earp,Tomás S. R. Silva*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了两条此前由数据提出的猜想：同一权重多项式的Milnor代数相关分次维数相同（因偏导数构成正则序列），且链环的Sasakian Hodge数被底Calabi-Yau三维轨形的Hodge数所界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立加权齐次超曲面奇点（degree等于权重和）的链环带有接触Calabi-Yau结构，其Sasakian Hodge数可由Milnor代数的分次维数给出；此前对7555个加权Calabi-Yau三维链环中的7549个用Gröbner基计算，并由此提出两条猜想，本文旨在证明它们。

Method: 对第一条猜想，利用孤立奇点多项式的偏导数构成正则序列（regular sequence）证明Milnor代数相关分次维数仅依赖权重；并给出基于叶空间轨形（leaf-space orbifold）上sheaf cohomology的独立推导。对第二条，借助Calabi-Yau轨形的stringy Hodge数（stringy Hodge numbers）界定中间Sasakian Hodge数。

Result: 两条猜想均被证明：任一维数n≥2时stringy Hodge数界定中间Sasakian Hodge数；n≥3时h^{n-1,1}的差距是轨形点轨迹几何亏格之和；三维情形当且仅当所有轨形点曲线为有理曲线时取等号。

Conclusion: 所得闭式公式给出此前遗留的6个7维链环的Sasakian Hodge数，补全95个5维K3链环与1 100 055个9维Calabi-Yau四重链环的数据集；K3链环情形复现了已知的第二Betti数。

Abstract: The link of an isolated weighted homogeneous hypersurface singularity whose degree equals the sum of its weights is a Sasakian manifold that admits a contact Calabi-Yau structure, and its Sasakian Hodge numbers are dimensions of graded pieces of the Milnor algebra of the defining polynomial. In earlier work these numbers were computed with Gröbner bases for the $7$-dimensional links of $7549$ of the $7555$ weighted Calabi-Yau threefold links, and two conjectures were raised from the data. The first asserts that polynomials with the same weights have Milnor algebras whose relevant graded pieces have equal dimensions; the second asserts that the Sasakian Hodge number $h^{2,1}_S$ of the link is bounded above by the Hodge number $h^{2,1}$ of the base Calabi-Yau threefold. Both conjectures are proved here. The first holds for every polynomial with an isolated singularity, because its partial derivatives form a regular sequence; an independent derivation of the Sasakian Hodge numbers by sheaf cohomology on the leaf-space orbifold is also given. For the second, the stringy Hodge numbers of the Calabi-Yau orbifold are shown to bound the middle Sasakian Hodge numbers in every dimension $n\ge2$, and for $n\ge3$ the gap in $h^{n-1,1}$ is shown to be a sum of geometric genera of loci of orbifold points; for threefolds the bound is attained exactly when all curves of orbifold points are rational. The resulting closed formulae give the Sasakian Hodge numbers of the six $7$-dimensional links left open in the earlier work and complete datasets for all $95$ five-dimensional K3 links and all $1\,100\,055$ nine-dimensional Calabi-Yau fourfold links; for the K3 links they recover the previously known second Betti numbers.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

### [152] [Machine learning of strength surfaces enables high regularization length phase-field fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18473)
*S Chockalingam,Aditya Kumar,Adrian Buganza Tepole*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出一种直接从强度数据学习的神经网络（neural-network, NN）强度函数，构造性地满足物理约束，并将其嵌入相场断裂（phase-field fracture）框架，使相场强度面（phase-field strength surface, PFSS）在较大正则化长度（regularization length）下仍能准确重现材料强度面，从而大幅降低大结构仿真所需的网格分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典脆性断裂相场模型仅能刻画韧性控制的裂纹扩展，无法描述控制裂纹形核（crack nucleation）的强度面（strength surface）。虽有理论通过形核驱动力（nucleation driving force）引入强度面，但实际中强度面需以解析形式逐案选取与标定，常用的解析面（n=2）要达到准确强度重现需正则化长度与Irwin特征长度（Irwin characteristic length）相当，对大结构造成难以承受的网格要求。

Method: 引入由强度数据直接学习的神经网络强度函数，该函数在构造上受物理约束，且可提供远多于解析面的标定参数；标定过程被推广至任意数量的强度状态，并可在指定方向上施加开放性（openness）约束。单一网络架构用于拟合Mohr-Coulomb、Drucker-Prager、Mogi-Coulomb与3D Hoek-Brown等合成数据，以及石墨、天然橡胶和丁苯橡胶的实验数据。

Result: 所得PFSS在正则化长度达 ε = 10 l_ch 时仍保持准确，而精确解析面即便在 ε = 0.5 l_ch 时表现亦相对较差。有限元（finite-element）模拟在多种断裂区间验证了该理论。

Conclusion: 通过解耦强度保真度与正则化长度，该方法显著降低了大结构相场断裂仿真所需的网格分辨率。

Abstract: Classical phase-field models of brittle fracture capture toughness-controlled crack growth but not the material's strength surface, which governs crack nucleation. The theory of Kumar et al. (2020) incorporates the strength surface through a nucleation driving force, and Chockalingam (2025) derived this driving force for arbitrary strength surfaces described by $n$ material parameters, with which the phase-field strength surface (PFSS) converges to the material strength surface as the regularization length $\varepsilon \to 0$ and matches it exactly at $n$ chosen states for any $\varepsilon$. In practice, however, the strength surface must be supplied as an analytic form chosen and calibrated case by case, and for the widely used analytic surfaces ($n=2$) accurate strength reproduction requires $\varepsilon$ commensurate with the Irwin characteristic length $l_{ch}$, imposing prohibitive mesh requirements for large structures. We introduce a neural-network (NN) strength function that is learned directly from strength data, is physics-constrained by construction, and provides far more calibration parameters than analytic surfaces; the calibration is generalized to any number of strength states and to enforce openness in chosen directions. A single architecture captures synthetic data from the Mohr-Coulomb, Drucker-Prager, Mogi-Coulomb and 3D Hoek-Brown surfaces and experimental data for graphite, natural rubber and styrene-butadiene rubber. The resulting PFSS is accurate up to $\varepsilon = 10\,l_{ch}$, whereas that of the exact analytic surfaces performs comparatively poorly even at $\varepsilon = 0.5\,l_{ch}$. Finite-element simulations validate the theory across fracture regimes. By decoupling strength fidelity from the regularization length, the approach substantially reduces the mesh resolution required for phase-field fracture simulations of large structures.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Global saddle selection and exact absolute-instability boundaries for convection in porous media with throughflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17614)
*Oleg N. Kirillov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究Darcy-Prats问题中水平穿透流对对流起始的影响，证明在$Ra>\pi^2$、$Pe>0$时恒有同一离轴鞍点对（off-axis saddle pair）贡献增长，从而给出唯一解析的绝对阈值（absolute threshold），并将该选择通过重标度推广至多种扩展物理情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水平穿透流不改变对流的时间起始，却会提高局域扰动在固定点增长所需的加热量；已有工作留下鞍点选择（saddle selection）不明确的问题，即五个候选鞍点中哪些真正贡献增长尚无定论，因此需要严格确定贡献鞍点并给出绝对阈值。

Method: 由鞍点方程得到五个候选，借助Fourier围道（Fourier contour）的形变判定贡献鞍点；用代数消元（algebraic elimination）导出边界多项式，并通过频率恢复选取物理分支；在Darcy层面将所选鞍点刻画为椭圆曲线（elliptic curve）11a3的弧段，再在该弧上做级数反演（series inversion），并用有理证书（rational certificates）确定收敛半径。

Result: 对$Ra>\pi^2$、$Pe>0$证明同一离轴鞍点对在全域贡献增长，不存在可改变其贡献的Stokes跃迁（Stokes transition）；其增长率给出唯一解析绝对阈值，且由最低竖直模态首先达到。该结果通过重标度迁移至Forchheimer阻力、幂律流体横向滚胞、各向异性渗透率、开放上边界与竖直磁场；在给定穿透流下阈值随任一阻力系数严格增大，竖直磁场在固定Péclet数下提高绝对阈值，表格化阈值可校验该约化。

Conclusion: 所选鞍点在Darcy层面形成椭圆曲线11a3的一段弧，其为亏格二族（genus-two family）在非零移位水平下的退化；弧上级数反演给出整数弱穿透流级数与全阶强穿透流展开，并由有理证书精确确定两序列在所选叶（sheet）上的收敛半径。

Abstract: In the Darcy-Prats problem, horizontal throughflow leaves the temporal onset of convection unchanged but raises the heating required for a localized disturbance to grow at a fixed point. The saddle equations give five candidates, but only the saddles reached by deformation of the Fourier contour contribute to the growth. For $Ra>π^2$, $Pe>0$ we prove that the same off-axis pair contributes throughout the domain: no Stokes transition can change its contribution. Its growth gives a unique analytic absolute threshold, first reached by the lowest vertical mode. Rescaling transfers the selection to Forchheimer drag, transverse rolls of a power-law fluid, anisotropic permeability, an open upper boundary and a vertical magnetic field. A shift of the same growth function accounts for interphase heat exchange in a model of metallic foam. At prescribed throughflow the threshold increases strictly with either resistance coefficient. In particular, a vertical magnetic field raises the absolute threshold at fixed Péclet number. Tabulated thresholds check the reduction. Algebraic elimination gives the boundary polynomial, and frequency recovery selects its physical branch. At the Darcy level the selected saddles trace an arc of the elliptic curve 11a3, a degeneration of a genus-two family at nonzero shifted levels. Series inversion on this arc gives an integer weak-throughflow series and an all-order strong-throughflow expansion. Rational certificates establish the exact convergence radii of both series on the selected sheets.

</details>


### [154] [Potential Flow, Stress, and Straightness in Flow Matching: Euler Error on Gaussian-Mixture Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18343)
*Zijian Liu,Julian Olszewski,Jie Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该工作将线性流匹配（linear flow matching）中的耦合直线性（straightness）与条件协方差、动能应力、Jeans平衡及位力恒等式（virial identity）联系起来，并通过高斯混合目标实验表明：在直线性匹配条件下，主导的Euler误差系数比直线性本身更能解释Euler误差的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究常以耦合的“直线性”作为流匹配质量的代理指标，但其与Euler离散误差之间的因果与定量关系并不清楚。论文意在揭示直线性背后的物理/几何结构，并检验直线性是否真正决定Euler误差。

Method: 采用理论刻画与构造性实验相结合：把条件协方差（conditional covariance）解释为Jeans平衡中的动能应力（kinetic stress），用位力恒等式把协方差均值迹与加速度的径向矩相联系；在构造的高斯混合（Gaussian-mixture）目标上比较具有匹配直线性的闭式耦合，并比较由不同小批量分配得到的学习场，同时引入梯度约束（gradient constraint）的修正。

Result: 在匹配直线性的闭式耦合之间，Euler误差中位数不同；学习场与其匹配估计直线性的对应场之间，Euler误差亦有差异。实验中，领先的Euler系数对误差变异的解释程度高于直线性。梯度约束在独立各向同性高斯例上降低速度误差，但在两个具旋转精确场（rotational exact fields）的例子上反而增大误差。

Conclusion: 直线性并非Euler误差的充分决定因素，误差主要由流的加速度传播方式及相关的领先Euler系数支配。论文同时将各向同性高斯源的可加噪声刻画与势流（potential flow）相联系，并指出梯度约束的效果取决于精确场是否含旋转分量。

Abstract: In linear flow matching, coupling straightness measures conditional displacement variance. The conditional covariance gives a kinetic stress in the Jeans balance. The virial identity relates the mean trace of the covariance to a radial moment of acceleration. Euler error also depends on how the flow propagates acceleration. On constructed Gaussian-mixture targets, closed-form couplings with matched straightness yield different median Euler errors. Learned fields from fresh minibatch assignments also yield different Euler errors from their partners at matched estimated straightness. The leading Euler coefficient accounts for more of the error variation than straightness in these experiments. We relate the additive-noise characterization of isotropic Gaussian sources to potential flow. A gradient constraint reduces velocity error on the independent isotropic Gaussian example and increases it on two examples with rotational exact fields.

</details>


### [155] [Influence of transverse oscillatory shear on the viscous fingering instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17551)
*Zhaoning Liu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 振荡剪切（oscillatory shear）可延迟黏性指进（viscous fingering）的发生，单轴平移剪切（uniaxial translational shear）则破坏图案对称性，使界面演化依赖于传播方向与剪切轴的相对取向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究剪切流动如何影响黏性指进的发生时刻、指状生长速率及图案形态，并揭示横向指状相互作用与径向传播之间的关联。

Method: 在振荡剪切与单轴平移剪切条件下观测指进界面演化，定量比较不同方向上的起始延迟与指状生长速率，并借助浓度剖面（concentration profiles）分析图案结构。

Result: 随剪切速度或振幅增大，各方向的指进起始均延迟、后续生长速率均下降；剪切使垂直方向的指变宽、平行方向的指被拉长，且当振幅超过起始时过量线性指波长（excess linear finger wavelength）约五倍时，垂直方向相邻指在基部附近可重新连接。

Conclusion: 剪切通过涂抹（smear）单个指状结构并连接相邻指，将横向指状相互作用与径向传播联系起来。

Abstract: Oscillatory shear can delay the onset of viscous fingering. In addition, uniaxial translational shear also breaks the symmetry of the resulting pattern so that the interface evolves differently depending on its direction of propagation relative to the shear axis. We observe a delayed fingering onset and reduced subsequent finger growth rate for all directions as shear speed or amplitude increases. However, shear widens fingers growing in the direction perpendicular to shear and elongates those in the parallel directions. After onset, neighboring fingers in the perpendicular directions can reconnect near their base when the shear amplitude exceeds approximately five times the excess linear finger wavelength at the onset. Concentration profiles reveal smoother azimuthal edges and overlapping structures behind the tips. These observations show that shear smears individual fingers and connects neighboring ones. This correlates lateral fingering interactions with radial propagation.

</details>
