### [156] [Resurgence without the Borel Plane: Mellin-Barnes Summation and Analytic Stokes Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17352)
*Roberto Ricci*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: We develop a route to resurgence without the Borel plane: sums of divergent perturbative series and their encoded nonperturbative monodromy are constructed directly in complex perturbative order. In the Analytic Umbral Framework (AUF), an analytic coefficient clock produces the sum through a Mellin-Barnes integral and its monodromy through a bare inverse Mellin transform. Neither a Borel transform nor continuation of a Borel germ is required for this construction. For Watson-admissible Gevrey clocks, the completion agrees with the canonical Borel-Laplace sum in the guaranteed directions. When the admissible aperture exceeds $2π$, the clockwise difference of adjacent logarithmic determinations is given by $2πi$ times the inverse-Mellin carrier. This carrier is also the AUF completion of a null clock and is flat on the overlap. The sum and its aggregate monodromy are thus obtained ''in one shot'', before resolving individual actions and fluctuation series.
  Euler's equation, zero-dimensional quartic models, the scaled Gamma function and a solvable $O(4)$ toy model exemplify the procedure, and show how further analysis recovers directional Stokes variations. The lognormal benchmark extends the construction beyond every finite Gevrey order: no finite-order Borel transform gives a convergent germ, yet explicit clock-selection conditions yield an AUF completion and a nonzero ordinary flat carrier. Distributed relaxation supplies a physical realization of this growth class. AUF therefore both reconstructs familiar resurgent information without Borel-plane continuation and extends completion beyond finite-order Borel-Laplace summation.

</details>


### [157] [Algebra-Valued Yang-Mills-Type Theories: Polynomial Obstructions and Holonomy Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17370)
*Yuri Ximenes Lecca,Willian Goulart Gomes Velasco*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究 $\mathfrak{m}$-分级代数取值的 Yang-Mills 型理论（$A$-YMT），推导场方程、规范变换与广义 Bianchi 恒等式，并证明在弱 $(2,s)$-可解代数、约化 Cartan-Yang-Mills 与奇数维 $A$-Chern-Simons 三类情形中的存在性阻碍（obstruction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将规范场论从 Lie 代数推广到一般 $\mathfrak{m}$-分级代数（$A,*$）时，非结合性（non-associativity）会破坏规范协变性与 Bianchi 恒等式；本文旨在系统刻画这些结构缺陷（由 Jacobiator 与 associator 度量）所导致的阻碍。

Method: 在光滑 $n$ 维流形上构造取值于 $A$ 的联络及其关联丛 $E_A = P\times_\rho A$，推导场方程与广义 Bianchi 恒等式；利用 Jacobiator、结合子（associator）以及 $(Q,S)$-分裂扩张、二叉树减半与四元块括号化等代数工具，在三类设定中建立阻碍定理，并分类 $4\le n\le 7$ 的 Berger 和乐（holonomy）约化。

Result: 仿射 Ehresmann 相容性（affine Ehresmann compatibility）迫使 $\mathfrak{g}$ 的垂直像落入 Lie 核 $\mathcal{N}_{\mathrm{Lie}}(A)$，且规范协变性与 Bianchi 恒等式恰由 Jacobiator $\mathrm{Jac}_{[\cdot,\cdot]_*}$ 阻碍，主 $G$-规范对称性保持无反常；内积 Chern-Weil 与自对偶 $A$-YMT 泛函在弱 $(2,s)$-可解代数上消失；四元块括号化给出 $n\ge 8$ 的严格截断，二叉树减半给出精确的 Jacobsthal 数截断；4 维 $U(2)/SU(2)$ 联络与 7 维 $G_2$ 联络呈现 on-shell 消失（无挠）与 off-shell 存活（有挠）的二分性。

Conclusion: 代数取值规范理论的非结合结构导致可精确量化的阻碍现象；结果为 $A$-$BF$ 理论、八元数（octonionic）与约化 Higgs Proca 质量谱及约化对偶性提供应用框架。

Abstract: We study algebra-valued Yang-Mills-type ($A$-YMT) theories on a smooth $n$-manifold $M$, where connections take values in an $\mathfrak{m}$-graded algebra $(A, *)$ and descend to an associated bundle $E_A = P \times_ρA \to M$. We derive the field equations, gauge transformations, and generalized Bianchi identities, showing that affine Ehresmann compatibility forces the vertical image of $\mathfrak{g}$ into the Lie nucleus $\mathcal{N}_{\mathrm{Lie}}(A)$ of $[x,y]_* = x*y - y*x$, and that gauge covariance and the Bianchi identity are obstructed precisely by the Jacobiator $\mathrm{Jac}_{[\cdot,\cdot]_*}$ (leaving principal $G$-gauge symmetries anomaly-free). We then prove obstructions in three settings. First, internal-product Chern-Weil and self-dual $A$-YMT functionals vanish in weak $(2,s)$-solvable algebras, invariant under $(Q,S)$-splitting extensions. Second, in reductive Cartan-Yang-Mills theories on $A = \mathbb{R}^k \oplus \mathfrak{h}$, quartic-block parenthesization yields a strict cutoff at $n \ge 8$, while binary-tree halving yields an exact Jacobsthal-number cutoff ($n - 4 \notin \bigcup_{r \ge 3}\{2 J_r, J_{r+1}\}$, plus rank vanishing for $n \ge k + 5$). We catalog Berger holonomy reductions for $4 \le n \le 7$, proving an on-shell vanishing (torsion-free) vs. off-shell survival (torsionful) dichotomy in 4D for $U(2)$ and $SU(2)$ connections---via $e \curlywedge_\rtimes (e \curlywedge_\rtimes Ω_ω) = \frac{1}{2}(e \curlywedge_\rtimes e) \curlywedge_\rtimes Ω_ω$---and in 7D for $G_2$-connections. Third, we prove an associator-quotient collapse and Jacobiator vertex dichotomy for odd-dimensional internal $A$-Chern-Simons theories, with applications to $A$-$BF$ theories, octonionic and reductive Higgs Proca mass spectra, and reductive dualities.

</details>


### [158] [Toward a Holographic Representation Theory of the Bosonic de Sitter Discrete Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17380)
*Maryam Bajalan,Jean-Pierre Gazeau,Hamed Pejhan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个面向 de Sitter 群 SO₀(1,4) 非标量玻色离散系列表示的全息表示论研究纲领，通过有界域实现与时空实现的互补，提出并刻画三个显式的"相容性问题"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已在标量族 Π_{p,0}（具 S³ 球面实现）上建立原型，但非标量玻色离散系列 Π^±_{p,q} 缺乏统一的全息表示论框架；该文旨在用两种互补实现相互约束，填补这一空缺。

Method: 利用同一酉不可约表示（unitary irreducible representation, UIR）的两种实现：在开球 𝔹（模型双曲空间 G/SO(4)，∂𝔹≃S³）上的有界域实现，以及在 dS₄（共形边界 ℐ^±≃S³）上经规范约化得到的协变时空实现；由此引出两个体内-边界互缠（intertwiner）问题与一个边界间酉互缠子的构造与归一化问题。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定理或计算结论；仅将研究任务形式化为三个待解问题：两侧的外部全息（hol）互缠子及其不变范数、约束与物理商的识别，以及两个边界实现之间的酉互缠子的构造与归一化。

Conclusion: 该纲领以标量族 Π_{p,0} 为引导原型，主张有界域图像揭示表示的酉与调和结构、时空图像揭示其协变与规范结构，二者互为约束与阐发；具体结论部分因摘要截断而未给出。

Abstract: We propose a program toward a holographic representation theory of the non-scalar bosonic discrete series $Π^\pm_{p,q}$, $p,q\in\mathbb{N}$, $1\leqslant q\leqslant p$, of the de Sitter ($\mathrm{dS}_{4}$) group $G=\mathrm{SO}_0(1,4)$. The proposal exploits two complementary realizations of the same unitary irreducible representation (UIR): a bounded-domain realization on an open ball $\mathbb{B}$ modeling the hyperbolic space $G/\mathrm{SO}(4)$, with $\partial\mathbb{B}\simeq\mathbb{S}^{3}$, and a covariant spacetime realization on $\mathrm{dS}_{4}$, with conformal boundaries $\mathscr{I}^{\pm}\simeq\mathbb{S}^{3}$, in which the physical UIR is obtained through the corresponding gauge reduction. This leads to two boundary constructions and an explicit compatibility problem,
  \begin{align*}
  \mathbb{B}
  \;\overset{?\mathrm{hol}}{\longleftrightarrow}\;
  \partial\mathbb{B}\simeq\mathbb{S}^{3}
  \quad\overset{?}{\simeq}\quad
  \mathscr{I}^{\pm}\simeq\mathbb{S}^{3}
  \;\overset{?\mathrm{hol}}{\longleftrightarrow}\;
  \mathrm{dS}_{4} \,.
  \end{align*}
  The two outer problems seek explicit intertwiners between the bulk and boundary realizations, with their invariant norms, constraints, and physical quotients properly identified. The middle problem asks how to construct and normalize a unitary intertwiner between the two boundary realizations once both have been shown to carry the same UIR. The bounded picture exposes the unitary and harmonic structure of the representation, while the spacetime picture makes its covariant and gauge-theoretic structure manifest, allowing the two constructions to constrain and illuminate one another. The established scalar family $Π_{p,0}$, $p\in\mathbb{N}$, with its spherical realization on $\mathbb{S}^{3}$, provides the guiding prototype. Particular attention is given to ...

</details>


### [159] [Remarks on Souriau's Geometric Thermodynamics for Weak Two-Dimensional Euler Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17383)
*Jean-Pierre Magnot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究低正则性涡量空间中相对熵（relative entropy）的无穷小对称性，并论证其与二维弱欧拉轨迹平稳律的对称性并不一致：白噪声涡量的边缘熵对称性不必然保持时间相关性，从而区分热力学对称性与统计路径动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究常将相对熵的对称性与弱解路径律的对称性混同，本文旨在厘清低正则性（low-regularity）涡量下两者的关系并检验其可分离性。

Method: 在样本空间变换下，将相对熵不变性等价表述为参考律保持及参考散度（reference-divergence）恒等式；构造连续高斯反例及其无穷小形式，并针对平稳白噪声弱欧拉路径律显式构造非等距保面积剪切（area-preserving shears），另考察重整化能量（renormalized energy）条件化下保证性熵对称性的约化。

Result: 白噪声涡量具有由光滑保面积输运生成的无穷维对称族，但边际熵对称性不能保证路径律不变；显式非等距保面积剪切保持白噪声边际，却无法保持满足标准对称化弱涡量方程的欧拉路径律。

Conclusion: 研究区分了热力学对称（thermodynamic symmetry）、哈密顿协变（Hamiltonian covariance）与统计路径动力学三者，且不对非唯一弱解提出选择原则。

Abstract: We examine infinitesimal symmetries of relative entropy on low-regularity vorticity spaces and compare them with symmetries of stationary laws of weak two-dimensional Euler trajectories. Relative-entropy invariance under sample-space transformations is equivalent to preservation of the reference law; the corresponding weak infinitesimal condition is a vanishing reference-divergence identity. White-noise vorticity has an infinite-dimensional family of such symmetries, generated by smooth area-preserving transport. At the level of path laws, however, marginal entropy symmetries need not preserve temporal correlations. We first give a continuous Gaussian counterexample, including its infinitesimal form. We then establish a counterexample directly for stationary white-noise weak Euler path laws: explicit non-isometric area-preserving shears preserve the white-noise marginal but cannot preserve an Euler path law satisfying the standard symmetrized weak vorticity equation. We also examine the reduction of guaranteed entropy symmetries under conditioning by renormalized energy. These observations distinguish thermodynamic symmetry, Hamiltonian covariance, and statistical path dynamics without asserting a selection principle for nonunique weak solutions.

</details>


### [160] [Polynomial integrals and Poisson structures of the Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17457)
*Ilmar Gahramanov,Gulmammad Mammadov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对非退化四阶Pais-Uhlenbeck振子的自治多项式积分（autonomous polynomial integrals）进行完整分类：有理频率比m:n时由两个扇区能量（sector energies）与单一阶梯乘积（ladder product）的实部、虚部在一条定义关系下生成全部多项式积分，且不可由扇区能量表示的积分最小总次数恰为m+n并可达到；无理比时仅扇区能量的多项式守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四阶Pais-Uhlenbeck振子是高阶导数（higher-derivative）系统的代表性模型，其守恒量结构与可积性尚缺乏系统的分类结果，尤其有理与无理频率比下的差异需要刻画。

Method: 在有理频率比m:n（最简形式）下，以扇区能量及单一阶梯乘积的实部与虚部为生成元，在一条定义关系约束下构造全部多项式积分；并通过正规模式变量（normal-mode variables）将共度情形识别为负振子共振（negative oscillator resonance），把分类结果实现于原始高阶导数变量中。此外考察两个相容Poisson结构（compatible Poisson structures）及其对偶的生成元对角重标度（diagonal rescalings）。

Result: 不可由扇区能量表示的积分之最小总次数为m+n且该界可达；无理比时仅扇区能量的多项式守恒。两种Poisson结构对同一物理生成元诱导不同的括号，仅当m或n为偶数时存在识别两抽象Poisson代数的对角重标度，且无一保持非负扇区能量的物理定义域。经经典乘子恒等式（classical multiplier identity）给出生成元的变分特征（variational characteristics）闭式，并表明额外共振积分不能来自点变分对称性（point variational symmetries）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了非退化四阶Pais-Uhlenbeck振子多项式守恒量的完备分类，并在共振框架下厘清了其Poisson结构与变分对称性来源的局限。

Abstract: We classify the autonomous polynomial integrals of the nondegenerate fourth-order Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator. For a rational frequency ratio $m:n$ in lowest terms, the two sector energies together with the real and imaginary parts of a single ladder product generate all polynomial integrals, subject to one defining relation. The smallest total degree of an integral not expressible through the sector energies is $m+n$, and this bound is attained. For an irrational ratio only polynomials in the sector energies are conserved. In normal-mode variables the commensurate case is the negative oscillator resonance, and the classification realizes its invariant theory in the original higher-derivative variables. Two compatible Poisson structures generate the same dynamics with an indefinite and a positive-definite Hamiltonian. They induce different brackets on the same physical generators. We show that diagonal rescalings of the generators identifying the two abstract Poisson algebras exist only when $m$ or $n$ is even, and that none of them preserves the physical domain of nonnegative sector energies. Finally, we express the generators in terms of the original coordinate and its first three derivatives. The classical multiplier identity gives their variational characteristics in closed form and shows that the additional resonant integrals cannot arise from point variational symmetries.

</details>


### [161] [Relabeling symmetry and conservation laws in hydrostatic and nonhydrostatic upper-ocean models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17597)
*F. J. Beron-Vera*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在 Euler–Poincaré 框架下，将静水与非静水约化重力上层海洋模型（含垂向无限制型与柱状型）的守恒积分，明确等同于粒子重标记（particle relabeling）对应的 Noether 量，并进一步构造非静水柱状模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约化重力上层海洋模型存在若干守恒积分，其对称性来源与不同模型族之间的统一关系尚缺乏系统说明；本文旨在借助粒子重标记对称性给出统一的 Noether 解释。

Method: 证明柱状速度约化保持李代数（Lie-algebra）结构，从而为两类模型提供共同的 Euler–Poincaré 框架；据此引入具有多项式垂向浮力结构的非静水柱状模型，并利用时间端点配对构造输运重标记生成元，在空间边界项消失的假设下求其涡度相关守恒积分；对 Legendre 变换（Legendre transform）正则的情形给出相应括号及 Casimir 族，对退化的静水母模型则直接用 Euler–Poincaré 原理处理。

Result: 在分层柱状模型中，只要进入拉格朗日量的有效浮力（effective buoyancy）保持不变，即使各浮力系数单独变化对称性仍成立，所得守恒量一般不是完整 Lie–Poisson 括号的 Casimir；正则情形下给出括号与 Casimir 族，并区分由平流直接得到的积分与经 Noether 配对得到的积分。

Conclusion: 粒子重标记对称性为静水与非静水、垂向无限制与柱状等约化重力模型提供了统一的守恒积分图景，并把非静水柱状分层模型纳入同一 Euler–Poincaré 层级；守恒量的 Casimir 性质依赖于模型与 Legendre 变换的正则性。

Abstract: We explicitly identify conserved integrals of hydrostatic and nonhydrostatic reduced-gravity upper-ocean models, both vertically unrestricted and columnar, with Noether quantities associated with particle relabeling. The columnar velocity reduction preserves the Lie-algebra structure, providing a common Euler--Poincaré framework for both families. Within this framework, we introduce nonhydrostatic columnar models with polynomial vertical buoyancy structure, extending the hydrostatic stratified hierarchy. Retaining the temporal endpoint pairing, we construct transported relabeling generators and evaluate their vorticity-dependent conserved integrals, assuming that spatial boundary terms vanish. For stratified columnar models, preservation of the effective buoyancy entering the Lagrangian suffices for symmetry even when individual buoyancy coefficients change, yielding conserved quantities that generally are not Casimirs of the full Lie--Poisson bracket. Where the Legendre transform is regular, we state the corresponding brackets and identify their Casimir families. The hydrostatic parent is treated directly through the Euler--Poincaré principle because its Legendre transform is degenerate. Integrals following directly from advection are distinguished from those obtained through the Noether pairing.

</details>


### [162] [The non-stationary $\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_N$ affine Laumon function and its difference equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17719)
*Philippe Di Francesco,Rinat Kedem*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出 Shiraishi 所引入的 $\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_N$ 非平稳仿射相对论 Toda 函数（又称仿射 Laumon 函数）的一个显式 $q$-Givental 型公式，并证明其满足一个含算子 $\widehat{\mathcal T}$ 的非平稳差分方程，从而证实 Shiraishi (2019) 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shiraishi (2019) 提出了关于非平稳 Ruijsenaars 函数的猜想，即仿射 Laumon 函数应服从涉及算子 $\widehat{\mathcal T}$ 的非平稳差分方程，但该猜想此前缺乏证明，且缺少显式的 $q$-Givental 型表示。

Method: 构造 $\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_N$ 非平稳仿射相对论 Toda（仿射 Laumon）函数的显式 $q$-Givental 型公式，并以此为基础证明该函数满足相应的非平稳差分方程；其中算子 $\widehat{\mathcal T}$ 表示仿射 $A_{N-1}^{(1)}$ Q-系统（Q-system）的量子簇代数（quantum cluster algebra）的箭图自同构（quiver automorphism），即 Dehn 扭转（Dehn twist）。

Result: 所提出的显式 $q$-Givental 型公式成立，且该函数确实服从含 $\widehat{\mathcal T}$ 的非平稳差分方程，Shiraishi (2019) 的猜想得证。$\widehat{\mathcal T}$ 被识别为仿射 $A_{N-1}^{(1)}$ Q-系统量子簇代数中对应于 Dehn 扭转的箭图自同构。

Conclusion: 该工作以显式 $q$-Givental 型公式为工具，将非平稳仿射相对论 Toda（仿射 Laumon）函数纳入非平稳差分方程与量子簇代数框架，并把算子 $\widehat{\mathcal T}$ 与 Q-系统的箭图自同构（Dehn 扭转）对应起来，从而解决了 Shiraishi 提出的猜想。

Abstract: We present an explicit $q$-Givental type formula for the $\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_N$ non-stationary affine relativistic Toda or affine Laumon function introduced by Shiraishi. We prove the conjecture of [J. Shiraishi, Affine screening operators, affine Laumon spaces and conjectures concerning non-stationary Ruijsenaars functions. J. of Int. Systems, 4 (2019)], that this function obeys a non-stationary difference equation involving an operator $\widehat{\mathcal T}$, which represents a quiver automorphism (Dehn twist) of the quantum cluster algebra of the affine $A_{N-1}^{(1)}$ Q-system.

</details>


### [163] [Contractions of realizations and differential invariants of the algebra sl(2,C)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17829)
*M. Staryi,M. Nesterenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文形式化了一套基于代数收缩（contraction）构造李代数局部实现之间极限过渡（limit transition）的方法，并在复全纯范畴中显式给出 sl(2, C) 收缩到三维复 Poincaré 代数与 Weyl–Heisenberg 代数的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为李代数（Lie algebra）不同局部实现之间的极限过渡提供系统化、可形式化的构造框架，从而把代数层面的收缩与实现的收缩统一处理。

Method: 以相应代数的收缩为基础，提出构造局部实现极限过渡的形式化方法；在复全纯范畴（complex holomorphic category）中，对 sl(2, C) 的忠实实现（faithful realization）实施显式收缩，并对到 g_{3.4}^{-1} 的生成实现收缩构造微分不变量（differential invariants）。

Result: 显式构造了 sl(2, C) 的忠实实现向复三维 Poincaré 代数（complex three-dimensional Poincaré algebra）及 Weyl–Heisenberg 代数的收缩；在收缩到 g_{3.4}^{-1} 的情形下得到微分不变量，并发现一个牛顿型（Newtonian type）的极限系统。

Conclusion: 代数收缩可作为构造局部实现极限过渡的有效工具，且该框架能产生具有物理意义的极限系统（如牛顿型系统），说明该方法在代数实现与动力学极限之间建立了联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we formalize a method for constructing limit transitions between local realizations of Lie algebras based on contractions of the corresponding algebras. In the complex holomorphic category, contractions of faithful realizations of the special linear algebra sl(2, C) to the complex three-dimensional Poincaré algebra and the Weyl-Heisenberg algebra are explicitly constructed. For the contraction of a generating realization of the algebra sl(2,C) to the algebra g_{3.4}^{-1}, differential invariants are constructed and a limit system of Newtonian type is found.

</details>


### [164] [Projection of massive and interacting conformal theories to the future null infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17943)
*Beatrice Costeri,Claudio Dappiaggi,Alberto Ferrari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在四维全局双曲渐近平坦时空中，将质量项视为相互作用，用Møller映射把有质量标量场可观测量表为无质量共形耦合理论中关于质量平方的幂级数，并借助体-边界对应投影到未来零无穷；在大范围绝热极限平凡的情形下，利用时间切片公理局部去截断获得非平凡极限，并推广至自相互作用情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为有质量（及自相互作用）标量场建立体-边界对应（bulk-to-boundary correspondence），即将体时空可观测量投影到未来零无穷（future null infinity, $\mathscr{I}^+$），并处理大范围绝热极限（adiabatic limit）平凡性带来的障碍。

Method: 在微扰代数量子场论（perturbative algebraic quantum field theory）框架下，受微扰一致性（perturbative agreement）原理启发，把拉格朗日量中的质量项当作相互作用势，通过Møller映射将可观测量展开为质量平方的形式幂级数，系数取自无质量共形耦合标量场的可观测量代数；仿照[DMP06]用无质量理论的体-边界对应逐项投影。

Result: 在四维闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）中证明：带红外截断（infrared cut-off）时投影级数收敛，且全局绝热极限存在但平凡；借助时间切片公理（time-slice axiom）将注意力限制在含柯西超曲面（Cauchy hypersurface）的因果凸邻域内的局部可观测量后，局部移除截断得到非平凡极限，从而给出有质量理论的体-边界对应。

Conclusion: 该程序可推广到自相互作用实标量场：相互作用可观测量表为耦合常数的形式幂级数，系数位于无质量共形耦合理论的可观测量代数中，因而都能投影到未来零无穷；作为具体例子，计算了带四次自相互作用（quartic self-interaction）的无质量标量场应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor）在微扰论一阶的投影。

Abstract: On a four-dimensional, globally hyperbolic and asymptotically flat spacetime, we consider a real, linear, massive scalar field. Within the framework of perturbative algebraic quantum field theory and inspired by the principle of perturbative agreement, we regard the mass term in the underlying Lagrangian as an interaction potential. This allows us to construct the algebra of observables of the massive theory and, using a Møller map, to represent its elements as formal power series in the squared mass, with coefficients in the algebra of observables of a massless, conformally coupled scalar field. Following [DMP06], we use the bulk-to-boundary correspondence for the massless theory to project each coefficient to future null infinity $\mathscr{I}^+$.In four-dimensional Minkowski spacetime, we prove convergence of the projected series in the presence of an infrared cut-off and show that the global adiabatic limit exists but is trivial. We circumvent this obstruction by exploiting the time-slice axiom, which allows us to restrict our attention to observables localized in a causally convex neighbourhood containing a Cauchy hypersurface. Removing the cut-off locally yields a non-trivial limit and hence a bulk-to-boundary correspondence for the massive theory. We then extend our procedure to a self-interacting real scalar field. We represent interacting observables as formal power series in the coupling constant, with coefficients in the algebra of observables of a massless, conformally coupled scalar field. Each coefficient in the domain of the bulk-to-boundary map can thus be projected to future null infinity. As a concrete example, we apply this method to the stress-energy tensor of a massless scalar field with a quartic self-interaction, computing its projection up to first order in perturbation theory.

</details>


### [165] [Exponential Lower Bounds for the Spherically Averaged Density of Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17969)
*Yukimi Goto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 $N$ 电子原子哈密顿量 $H_{N,Z}$ 束缚态球平均密度 $\widetilde\rho$ 的渐近行为，证明若本征能量 $E$ 低于本质谱底部 $\Sigma$，则对数密度的渐近衰减率恰为 $-2\alpha_0$，并在 $H_{3,Z}$ 基态上给出 $\alpha_0^2=\Sigma-E$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子密度在无穷远处的指数衰减率（decay rate）是原子物理与数学物理中的基本量，与电离能及本质谱（essential spectrum）位置密切相关；已有结果多针对特定体系，缺乏对一般 $N$ 电子束缚态的统一刻画。

Method: 对满足 $H_{N,Z}\psi=E\psi$ 且 $E<\Sigma$ 的本征函数，分析其球平均密度（spherically averaged density）$\widetilde\rho$ 的对数在 $r\to\infty$ 时的极限与导数极限，并引入使 $e^{2\alpha r}\widetilde\rho$ 属于加权 $L^1(\mathbb{R}_+, r^2dr)$ 的最大指数 $\alpha_0$ 作为刻画参数。

Result: 证明 $\lim_{r\to\infty}\ln\widetilde\rho(r)/r=\lim_{r\to\infty}\frac{d}{dr}\ln\widetilde\rho(r)=-2\alpha_0$，且阈值情形（threshold）在若干条件下同样成立；此外，对核电荷 $Z$ 足够大的 $H_{3,Z}$ 基态（ground state）证明了 $\alpha_0^2=\Sigma-E$。

Conclusion: 球平均密度的指数衰减率被 $\alpha_0$ 完全确定，并与本质谱底部通过 $\alpha_0^2=\Sigma-E$ 相联系，至少在三电子大 $Z$ 基态情形成立；阈值处的类似行为表明该结论在临界能量附近具有一定稳健性。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of the spherically averaged density $\widetilde ρ$ for fermionic bound states of the $N$-electron atomic Hamiltonian $H_{N,Z}$ with nuclear charge $Z$. For the density associated with an eigenfunction satisfying $H_{N,Z}ψ=Eψ$, we show that if the energy $E$ is below the bottom of the essential spectrum $Σ:=\inf\mathop{\rm ess.spec} (H_{N,Z})$, then \[ \lim_{r\to\infty}\frac{\ln \widetilde ρ(r)}{r} =\lim_{r\to\infty}\frac{d}{dr}{\ln \widetildeρ(r)} =-2α_0, \] with $α_0=\sup\{α\ge 0\colon e^{2αr}\widetilde ρ\in L^1(\mathbb{R_+},r^2dr)\}$. The same is true at threshold in certain cases. Moreover, we prove $α_0^2=Σ-E$ for the ground states of $H_{3,Z}$ with sufficiently large $Z$.

</details>


### [166] [Two correlation scales in homogeneous density-matrix functionals: the Müller logarithm and the sharp Csányi--Arias asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18018)
*Heinz Siedentop*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文严格求解了三维平移不变、自旋对称的 Müller 与 Csányi–Arias 修正 Hartree–Fock（corrected Hartree–Fock, CHF）泛函在高密度极限下的精确关联尺度与系数：Müller 泛函的每粒子增益为对数增长，CHF 增益则为带指数小因子的 k²e^{-π^{3/2}√k} 形式，从而解释了 Müller 过关联与 CHF 关联近乎消失的长期数值现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决 Müller 关于"过关联（overcorrelation）"以及 CHF 关联"看似消失"的长期数值观察，并给出两种泛函高密度关联行为的严格解析刻画。同时检验 Müller 泛函是否具备猜想中的下界性质（lower-bound property）。

Method: Müller 情形保留占据数上限（occupation cap），并识别交换缺陷（exchange defect）与能量增益之间的二倍抵消；对周期 Müller jellium 利用凸性与平移–自旋平均选取不变全局极小元。CHF 上界由基于显式 Hartree–Fock 色散关系的确定性比较给出，结合完整 Birman–Schwinger 界、单侧范数与配方；匹配下界由保数（number-preserving）试探态给出，其余项导出占据缺陷的加权 L² 收敛，无需分析自洽能隙。

Result: 得到固定自旋多重度 q 与费米动量 k 下的精确渐近：D_M(k)=(3/4π²)log k+O(1)，D_CHF(k)=12e^{-1/2-π²/8}k²e^{-π^{3/2}√k}(1+o(1))。相对于无极化量子高密度基准，Müller 具有更大的对数系数，为 Müller 下界性质猜想提供定量证据；CHF 则完全缺失对数尺度。此外证明了两泛函解的存在性及 CHF 极小元的唯一性。

Conclusion: 研究严格确认了 Müller 泛函在高密度下呈对数型关联并满足更大的对数系数，定量支持其下界性质猜想，而 CHF 的关联按指数小速率衰减、缺失对数尺度。量子基准未用于两泛函的证明，表明结论独立于该基准。

Abstract: We resolve the exact high-density correlation scales and coefficients of the three-dimensional translation-invariant, spin-symmetric Müller and Csányi--Arias corrected Hartree--Fock (CHF) functionals, settling long-standing numerical observations of Müller overcorrelation and apparently vanishing CHF correlation. In Hartree units, for fixed spin multiplicity $q$ and Fermi momentum $k=(6π^2ρ/q)^{1/3}$, their gains per particle over the filled Fermi sea satisfy \[
  D_{\rm M}(k)=\frac{3}{4π^2}\log k+O(1),\qquad
  D_{\rm CHF}(k)=12e^{-1/2-π^2/8}
  k^2e^{-π^{3/2}\sqrt{k}}(1+o(1)). \] The Müller proof retains the occupation cap and identifies the factor-of-two cancellation between exchange defect and energy gain. For periodic Müller jellium, convexity and translation--spin averaging allow a global minimizer to be chosen invariant and identify the unrestricted thermodynamic energy with the homogeneous spin-symmetric value. The CHF upper bound follows from a deterministic comparison built from the explicit Hartree--Fock dispersion, combining a full Birman--Schwinger bound with one-sided norms and completion of the square; a number-preserving trial state provides the matching lower bound, and the retained remainder yields weighted $L^2$ convergence of the occupation defect without analyzing the self-consistent gap. We also prove existence for both functionals and uniqueness of the CHF minimizer. Relative to the unpolarized quantum high-density benchmark, Müller has a larger logarithmic coefficient, providing quantitative evidence for the conjectured Müller lower-bound property, whereas CHF misses the logarithmic scale altogether. The quantum benchmark is not used in either functional proof.

</details>


### [167] [The Thermodynamic Limit and the Landau degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18165)
*Stéphane Ouvry*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文讨论一种源于20世纪80年代末的替代性正则化方案：不借助硬壁或周期边界盒，而用光滑谐振子势约束系统，再逐步撤除该势以取得热力学极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学量计算依赖带硬壁或周期边界的约束盒先正则化、再取热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）；该文关注另一种历史上用于任意子气体热力学方程研究的正则化方案。

Method: 以光滑谐振子势（harmonic potential）替代壁面约束系统，在势场存在下计算各物理量，随后逐渐移除该势；为得到有意义的热力学结果，还需采用适当的极限处方（prescription）。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或公式结果，仅指出该方法需配合适当的取极限处方，方能获得有意义的热力学结果；原始方案出自20世纪80年代末针对任意子气体（anyon gas）的研究。

Conclusion: 谐振子势正则化构成盒约束之外的可行替代方案，其有效性取决于撤除势场时极限处方的恰当选择。

Abstract: Usually, thermodynamic quantities are calculated by first regularizing the system in a confining box, which may have hard walls or periodic boundaries. One then studies the finite system and takes the thermodynamic limit by letting the box grow indefinitely. Here, we discuss a regularization scheme devised in the late 1980s [1] to study the thermodynamic equations of an anyon gas [2]. Instead of confining the system with walls, the method adds a smooth harmonic potential. Quantities are evaluated in its presence, and the potential is then gradually removed. A suitable prescription for taking this limit is needed to obtain meaningful thermodynamic results.

</details>


### [168] [Group classification of a class of generalized Korteweg-de Vries equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18247)
*Oleksandra O. Vinnichenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文借助分叉分裂方法（furcate splitting）对一类广义Korteweg-de Vries方程完成了完整的群分类（group classification），并给出守恒律分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类方程涵盖KdV、mKdV、Harry Dym及Rosenau-Hyman紧子（compacton）方程等多个重要模型，需要统一的群分类与守恒律刻画以揭示其对称性质结构。

Method: 采用分叉分裂法进行群分类；以两种独立途径计算该方程类的等价群（equivalence group）：直接法的改进版本与基于巨理想（megaideal）的代数法，并由此构造等价代数的自同构群及其无限多个巨理想。

Result: 求出了分类情形之间的全部额外点等价（point equivalences）；该方程类中方程的最大李不变代数（maximal Lie invariance algebra）维数介于二至五之间，除与Harry Dym方程相似的情形为奇异扩展且是唯一达到最大维数五者外，其余李对称扩展均正则。

Conclusion: 最后完成了该类方程具有非正阶特征（characteristics of nonpositive order）的守恒律（conservation laws）的分类。

Abstract: Applying the method of furcate splitting, we carry out the complete group classification of a class of generalized Korteweg-de Vries equations. This class includes the Korteweg-de Vries, modified Korteweg-de Vries and Harry Dym equations, as well as the Rosenau-Hyman compacton equations. The equivalence group of the class is computed in two independent ways, by the advanced version of the direct method and by the megaideal-based version of the algebraic method. As a by-product, we construct the automorphism group of the equivalence algebra and all its megaideals, whose number is infinite. We also find all additional point equivalences between the classification cases. The maximal Lie invariance algebras of equations from the class are of dimensions from two to five, and all the corresponding Lie-symmetry extensions are regular except those for the equations similar to the Harry Dym equation, which are singular and are the only ones of the maximal dimension five. Finally, we classify the conservation laws of equations from the class with characteristics of nonpositive order.

</details>


### [169] [Correlation functions of the Lieb--Liniger Bose gas in equilibrium states: a microscopic approach to space-time asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18316)
*Frank Göhmann,Karol K. Kozlowski,Mikhail D. Minin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无近似（approximation-free）方法，用于分析热力学极限下量子可积模型（quantum integrable models）在平衡态及淬火后稳态（含广义吉布斯系综，generalized Gibbs ensembles）中的形状因子级数（form-factor series），并成功导出关联函数的长时大距离渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对关联函数长时大距离渐近行为的认知多依赖唯象或启发式方法（如广义流体力学，generalised hydrodynamics），缺乏严格且无近似的推导，尤其对某些关联函数其行为超出既有启发式方法的适用范围。

Method: 发展无近似方法直接分析形状因子级数（form-factor series），以 Lieb–Liniger 玻色气体（即非线性薛定谔模型，non-linear Schrödinger model）为范例，适用于所有局域算符（local operators）的关联函数，涵盖热平衡态与淬火后稳态。

Result: 对粒子密度或粒子流密度算符的两点函数，所得结果证实了基于广义流体力学的唯象预言；同时提取了场共轭场关联函数（field-conjugated field correlation function）的长时大距离渐近行为，其由依赖空间与时间的非线性积分方程（non-linear integral equation）支配，目前超出任何启发式方法的范围。

Conclusion: 该无近似框架为量子可积模型关联函数的渐近分析提供了严格工具，既验证了广义流体力学预言，又揭示了启发式方法无法触及的新普适结构（空间-时间依赖的非线性积分方程）。

Abstract: We develop an approximation-free approach to the analysis of form-factor series for the correlation functions of quantum integrable models in the thermodynamic limit and in equilibrium states described by a class of stationary density matrices. This allows us to obtain the long-time, large-distance asymptotics of the correlation functions in thermal equilibrium and in stationary states after a quench, including such states that can be described by generalized Gibbs ensembles. Our approach will be exemplified with the Lieb--Liniger Bose gas or non-linear Schrödinger model. It applies to the correlation functions of all local operators. For the two-point functions of particle-density or particle-current density operators our findings confirm the phenomenological predictions based on generalised hydrodynamics. We extract as well the long-time, large-distance asymptotics of a field- conjugated field correlation function. At present its behaviour is outside the reach of any heuristic method. It is governed by a novel non-linear integral equation that depends on space and time.

</details>


### [170] [All coupling Anderson localization in the quantum kicked rotor model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18320)
*Yunfeng Shi,Li Wen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一类由拉普拉斯算子与正切型准周期势（tanπ(θ+n²α)）构成的离散薛定谔算子，在对所有耦合常数λ>0及几乎处处的(α,θ)参数下均呈现安德森局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是验证 Fishman-Grempel-Prange 关于量子混沌抑制研究中提出的核心假设：当α为无理数时，正切序列{tanπ(θ+n²α)}的伪随机性应导致与一维安德森模型类似的全耦合安德森局域化，该假设此前仅在满测度意义下作为假设存在而未被严格证明。

Method: 方法基于非微扰格林函数估计（nonperturbative Green's function estimates）框架，具体使用凯莱变换（Cayley transform）、二次Weyl和估计（quadratic Weyl sum estimate）以及Rellich函数分析。

Result: 证明了算子mathscr{H}=λΔ+tanπ(θ+n²α)δ_{n,n'}对所有λ>0及勒贝格几乎处处的(α,θ)∈𝕋²均满足安德森局域化，从而在满测度意义下证实了上述假设。

Conclusion: 该工作严格确立了正切型准周期势诱导的安德森局域化现象，为量子混沌抑制研究中的假设提供了数学证明，并展示了格林函数非微扰方法在奇异性势（正切函数具有极点）情形下的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study $$\mathscr{H}=λΔ+\tanπ(θ+n^2α)δ_{n,n'},\ n\in\Bbb Z$$ and prove its Anderson localization for all $λ>0$ and Lebesgue almost every (a.e.) $(α, θ)\in\Bbb T^2$. This conforms in the full measure sense the main assumption of Fishman-Grempel-Prange [{\it Phys. Rev. Lett.} 49(8), 509--512 (1982)] in the study of the quantum suppression of chaos: The pseudo-randomness of the sequence $\{\tanπ(θ+n^2α)\}_{n\in\Bbb Z}$ (for $α\in\Bbb T\setminus \Bbb Q$) should result in all coupling Anderson localization for $H$ similar to that for the one-dimensional Anderson model. Our proof is based on nonperturbative Green's function estimates approach using Cayley transform, quadratic Weyl sum estimate and Rellich function analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Restoring Consistency with Poynting's Theorem in Numerical MHD and RMHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18508)
*Dinshaw S. Balsara,Deepak Bhoriya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，在采用面心磁场（face-centered magnetic fields）与全局无散度演化的 MHD/RMHD 代码中，坡印廷定理（Poynting's theorem）未被一致地离散实现，导致强磁场下出现负压；作者通过用边心变量（edge-centered variables）计算面心坡印廷通量来恢复一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坡印廷定理表明电磁能量密度的变化率等于坡印廷通量（Poynting flux）散度的负值，但该恒等式此前从未被离散格式"模仿式"地（mimetically）纳入代码。作者意在揭示并修正磁流体与相对论磁流体代码中能量通量评估的不一致。

Method: 不一致性源于：面心能量密度通量（含坡印廷矢量）由施加于面心的一维黎曼求解器（one-dimensional Riemann solvers）计算，而面心磁场的演化依据边心电场，后者来自施加于网格边上的二维黎曼求解器（two-dimensional Riemann solvers）；边心电场不影响面心坡印廷通量。修正方法是用边心变量来评估面心坡印廷通量，并对面心能量密度通量作保持精度的简单修正。

Result: 原格式在磁能远大于热能的情形下会在网格中心产生负压（negative pressure）；所提出的保精度修正可恢复一致性，并在极强磁场的模拟中带来显著的改进。

Conclusion: 将坡印廷定理一致地（mimetic）纳入离散格式，是处理强磁场 MHD/RMHD 模拟中负压问题的有效途径；边心变量与面心通量的一致性耦合对数值稳定性具有实质意义。

Abstract: For perfectly conducting plasmas, Poynting's theorem states that the time rate of change of electromagnetic energy density equals the negative of the divergence of the Poynting flux. To the best of our knowledge, this involutive theorem has never been mimetically incorporated in codes. We show that there is an inconsistency in MHD and RMHD codes that evolve face-centered magnetic fields in globally-divergence-free fashion. This is because the face-centered flux of energy density, which incorporates a Poynting vector, is evaluated using one-dimensional Riemann solvers that are applied at face-centers. However, the face-centered magnetic fields are evolved according to edge-centered electric fields that come from two-dimensional Riemann solvers that are applied to the edges of a zone. While edge-centered electric fields are used to evolve the magnetic field, they have no influence on the face-centered Poynting flux. For situations where the magnetic energy is much larger than the thermal energy, this causes a negative pressure to be evaluated at the zone-centers. This paper restores consistency by using edge-centered variables to evaluate a face-centered Poynting flux. A simple accuracy-preserving correction is made to the face-centered flux of energy density. We show that it helps considerably in simulations that have very strong magnetic fields.

</details>

### [172] [Methanol substitution suppresses deflagration-excited knock and increases the efficiency of a hydrogen-fuelled CFR engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17449)
*Riming Xu,Mebin Panithasan,Xinyang Wang,Giovanni Vorraro,James W. G. Turner*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究首次在恒定燃料能量输入条件下用甲醇（methanol）部分替代氢气，发现氢火花点火（spark-ignition）发动机近爆震（knock）边界处的极限振荡由爆燃（deflagration）本身激发，甲醇可在不降低性能的前提下消除该振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爆震（knock）限制氢火花点火发动机性能，通常被归因于末端气体自燃（end-gas autoignition）；研究旨在检验甲醇替代部分氢气能否缓解爆震而保持输出。

Method: 在可变压缩比（variable-compression）合作燃料研究（Cooperative Fuel Research, CFR）发动机上，以进气道喷射（port-injected）甲醇替代20–50%氢能量，转速600 rpm、压缩比8–12，并结合层流火焰速度（laminar flame speed）与末端气体自燃积分（end-gas autoignition integral）分析。

Result: 纯氢时4–15 kHz振荡在CA10前即出现（97–100%循环），MAPO与最大压升率高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.94）；50%甲醇下燃烧持续期延长2.7–3.0倍、振荡起始推迟4.5–8.3°CA、平均MAPO下降89–93%，同时IMEP与指示热效率（indicated thermal efficiency）提升2.4–7.8%；末端气体自燃积分始终低于0.07。

Conclusion: 甲醇主要通过降低层流火焰速度（燃烧持续期按S_L^{-0.58}缩放，R²=0.99）推迟振荡起始，而非引发末端气体自燃；代价是甲醛（formaldehyde）排放升高5–6倍，NO_x下降49–85%。

Abstract: Knock restricts the performance of hydrogen spark-ignition engines and is usually attributed to end-gas autoignition. To the authors' knowledge, this is the first study in which methanol is used to replace part of the hydrogen in a hydrogen engine at constant fuel energy addition, and it shows that near the knock boundary the limiting oscillation is excited by the deflagration itself, which methanol removes without derating the performance. Port-injected methanol replaced 20--50% of the hydrogen energy in a variable-compression Cooperative Fuel Research engine at 600 rpm and compression ratios of 8--12. With pure hydrogen, oscillation in the 4--15 kHz range began before 10% of the charge had burned in 97--100% of cycles, and its maximum amplitude (MAPO) followed the maximum pressure-rise rate across 2499 cycles (Spearman $ρ=0.94$). Methanol slowed the deflagration: it replaced high-flame-speed hydrogen, cooled the intake by 24--25 K and, at unchanged intake pressure, enleaned the mixture by 10--13%. At 50% methanol, the 10--90% combustion duration lengthened 2.7--3.0-fold, oscillation onset moved 4.5--8.3$^\circ$CA after CA10 and mean MAPO fell by 89--93% although the spark timing was advanced by 13--19$^\circ$CA, and IMEP and indicated thermal efficiency rose by 2.4--7.8%. Methanol was thus worth more than this advance, and at matched CA50 MAPO was still about 90% lower. The measured combustion duration of all 25 data sets scaled with the computed laminar flame speed as $S_L^{-0.58}$ ($R^2=0.99$); fuel replacement accounted for about three quarters of the reduction in $S_L$, and the end-gas autoignition integral stayed below 0.07, against 1 at autoignition. The chamber kept its cylindrical acoustic modes, whose frequencies fell by 8--9%. The trade-off is formaldehyde emissions, which rose five- to sixfold while NO$_x$ fell by 49--85%.

</details>


### [173] [Bond Anharmonicity in a Caged Molecule Probed by Vibronic Core Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18331)
*Connor J. B. Fields,Bang Huynh,Filipe Junqueira,Tyler James,Aleksandra Foerster,Ilya Popov,James Kerfoot,Graham Rance,Victor Porée,Alessandro Nicolaou,David Duncan,Tien-Lin Lee,Gabriela Hoffman,Mark C. Walkey,Elizabeth S. Marsden,Malcolm H. Levitt,Richard J. Whitby,Michael W. George,Elena Besley,Brian Kiraly,James O'Shea,Philip Moriarty*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: N2@C60 中 N≡N 键的非谐性（anharmonicity）显著增强，非谐常数约为自由 N2 的两倍，但高精度计算排除了富勒烯笼对势能的静态畸变，作者归因于单分子 Morse 描述的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验富勒烯内嵌（endohedral fullerene）环境如何改变被封装小分子的化学键性质，特别是 N2@C60 中 N≡N 键的非谐性是否与自由分子不同。

Method: 结合氮 K 边（N K-edge）的 X 射线吸收谱（X-ray absorption spectroscopy）与共振非弹性 X 射线散射（resonant inelastic X-ray scattering），测量三个振动电子势中的非谐常数；并以高精度耦合簇（coupled-cluster）方法计算 N2@C60 的势能面。

Result: 基态（X^1Σ_g^+）非谐常数 ħω_e x_e 为 3.8±0.3 meV，约为自由 N2（~1.8 meV）的两倍，而谐频基本不变；该增强在三个可及势中一致出现。耦合簇计算则未发现封装引起显著非谐性变化。

Conclusion: 排除笼壁静态畸变 N≡N 势能的解释，提出非谐常数的大幅升高反映单分子 Morse 描述的失效（类似 N2 团簇中的异常非谐性），并得到富勒烯宿主拉曼模（Raman modes）位移的佐证。

Abstract: X-ray absorption spectroscopy and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering at the nitrogen K-edge show that the N$\equiv$N bond in N$_2$@C$_{60}$ is substantially more anharmonic than in free N$_2$, with the ground-state ($X^1Σ_g^+$) anharmonic constant $\hbarω_e x_e$ a factor of two larger ($3.8\pm0.3$~meV versus $\sim 1.8$~meV) despite an essentially unchanged harmonic frequency. The same enhancement is found across all three vibronic potentials accessible to the experiment---the electronic ground state, the core-excited intermediate state, and the spectator-decay final state---pointing to a common origin rather than an effect specific to one electronic configuration. High-level coupled-cluster calculations of the N$_2$@C$_{60}$ potential energy surface, however, find no significant change in anharmonicity upon encapsulation, ruling out a simple static distortion of the N$\equiv$N bonding potential by the fullerene cage. We propose instead that the dramatic increase in $\hbarω_e x_e$ above the gas-phase value -- which, taken literally, would imply an unphysical halving of the N$_2$ dissociation energy - reflects the breakdown of the single-molecule Morse description, as previously invoked to explain anomalous anharmonicities in N$_2$ clusters, and supported by the observation of shifts in the fullerene host's Raman modes.

</details>

### [174] [Optimal heterogeneous diffusivity profiles for mean first-passage minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17470)
*Luiz Menon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究在固定体积平均扩散系数的约束下，求解球形壳层（内反射、外吸收边界）中逃逸平均首次通过时间（mean first-passage time）的最优空间变化扩散系数分布，并给出解析与数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间变化的扩散系数（diffusivity）如何影响并优化受限区域内的逃逸时间尚缺乏系统性认识，尤其在不同空间维度、随机积分规定（stochastic integration prescription）与初始位置条件下缺少统一刻画。

Method: 在体积平均扩散系数固定的约束下，推导适用于任意空间维度与随机积分规定的变分条件（variational condition）；对从内边界出发的粒子给出精确最优分布，并用数值优化作为补充验证。

Result: 一维情形可得精确最优分布，Stratonovich 规定下均匀扩散最优；高维中 Stratonovich 最优分布递减，Itô 最优分布在所有维度均递减，而 Hänggi–Klimontovich 最优分布在一、二维递增、二维以上可出现极大值。当出发位置位于内边界之外时，最优分布可在初始位置处出现不连续，且内部区域扩散系数为常数。

Conclusion: 最优扩散系数分布由几何结构、随机积分规定与初始位置共同决定，表明平均逃逸时间的最小化对模型解释约定与起点条件具有显著依赖性。

Abstract: We investigate how spatially varying diffusivity can be optimized to minimize the mean first-passage time for escape from a spherical shell with an inner reflecting boundary and an outer absorbing boundary. Under a fixed volume-averaged diffusivity, we derive a variational condition for arbitrary spatial dimension and stochastic integration prescription. For particles starting at the inner boundary, we obtain exact optimal profiles in one dimension, with homogeneous diffusion being optimal under the Stratonovich prescription. In higher dimensions, the optimal Stratonovich profile becomes decreasing. The optimal Itô profile is decreasing in all dimensions, whereas the optimal Hänggi--Klimontovich profile is increasing in one and two dimensions and can exhibit a maximum in dimensions greater than two. For particles starting beyond the inner boundary, optimal profiles can exhibit a discontinuity at the initial position, with constant diffusivity in the inner region. Numerical optimization complements these results, showing how geometry, stochastic prescription, and initial position jointly determine the diffusivity distribution that minimizes the mean escape time.

</details>


### [175] [Foundation Tensor Networks Generalize Across Quantum Lattice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17655)
*Ryan T. Grimm,Michael Lindsey*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出基础张量网络（foundation tensor network, FTN），利用神经网络从哈密顿量直接生成基态波函数的张量网络表示，并借助 GridBP 技术将能量期望计算降至两次张量收缩，从而高效求解无序量子多体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序系统（disordered systems）需对每个无序实现（disorder realization）单独求解基态，在神经量子态（neural quantum states）与张量网络（tensor networks）框架下均极具挑战；已有神经量子态基础模型仍需大量采样（sampling）来估计可观测量。

Method: 以神经网络从哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）生成基态波函数的张量网络表示；提出基于迭代一维信念传播（belief propagation）的 \texttt{GridBP} 技术加速训练，将能量期望值计算成本降至两次张量收缩（tensor contraction）。

Result: 在量子 Edwards-Anderson 模型中，通过有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling analysis）计算玻璃转变（glass transition）临界参数；利用矩生成函数（moment-generating function）评估序参量，将成本降至单次张量收缩，并对 500,000 个无序实现取平均获得高保真临界参数估计。

Conclusion: FTN 结合 GridBP 与矩生成函数，为无序量子多体系统的基态求解与临界参数估计提供了高效且高精度的途径，避免了传统采样瓶颈。

Abstract: Neural quantum states and tensor networks have emerged as leading techniques for simulating quantum many-body systems. However, disordered systems remain extremely challenging in both frameworks as they require computation of the ground state for every realization of the disorder. Recently, neural quantum states have been used to construct foundation models that generate the ground state directly from the Hamiltonian. Still, they are hindered by the need to perform extensive sampling to estimate observables. To address these difficulties, we introduce the foundation tensor network (FTN), which uses a neural network to generate the tensor network representation of the ground-state wavefunction from the Hamiltonian. To accelerate training, we develop a technique, called \texttt{GridBP}, based on iterated one-dimensional belief propagation that reduces the cost of computing the energy expectation value to just two tensor contractions. We use FTNs to calculate the critical parameters of the glass transition in the quantum Edwards-Anderson model via finite-size scaling analysis. To facilitate rapid averaging, we use a moment-generating function to evaluate the order parameter, lowering the cost to a single tensor contraction. We obtain high-fidelity estimates for the critical parameters by averaging over 500,000 disorder realizations.

</details>


### [176] [A Multiobjective Optimization Framework for Sensor Array Design in Magnetocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17976)
*Kesavaraja C,Sammita Kashyap,Korak Biswas,Pranab Dutta,Kushal Patel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究比较物理驱动、数据驱动与混合三种传感器优化框架，为心磁图（magnetocardiography, MCG）筛选最佳传感器布局；混合方法以14个传感器达到相关系数0.9945，较物理驱动方案减少12.5%传感器数量并满足极值误差阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低温心磁系统采用固定多通道阵列，而室温传感器支持灵活布置，因此需要确定最优传感器位置以减少阵列规模与测量冗余，并提升MCG作为常规低成本临床测量的可行性。

Method: 研究构建三类传感器优化框架：物理驱动（physics driven，基于D-最优性、空间相关性与最小二乘误差优化连续平面坐标）、实验数据驱动（data driven，利用实测时空场与极几何指标）以及两者结合的混合方法；评价指标包括训练目标、皮尔逊相关系数（Pearson correlation）、归一化均方根误差（normalized root-mean-square error, NRMSE）及极点间距（mm）、方向（°）与轨迹（mm）误差。

Result: 物理驱动优化选出16个传感器，相关系数0.9914、NRMSE 0.0139，极点间距、方向与轨迹误差分别为3.71 mm、1.38°、3.55 mm；数据驱动在超过17个传感器后收益递减，相关系数0.9840、NRMSE 0.0035，极点误差约为4.30 mm、1.70°、6.80 mm；混合方法以14个传感器实现相关系数0.9945与NRMSE 0.0201，传感器数量较物理驱动配置减少12.5%，其极点误差6.50 mm、2.31°、7.50 mm均满足研究设定阈值。

Conclusion: 优化后的传感器布局可缓解数据冗余带来的复杂性，兼顾阵列紧凑性与磁场形态保真度，从而提升MCG作为常规、经济可行的临床测量手段的性能。

Abstract: Magnetocardiography (MCG) noninvasively measures cardiac magnetic fields. Conventional cryogenic systems use fixed multichannel arrays, whereas room-temperature sensors enable flexible placement to reduce array size and measurement redundancy. The present study aims at developing a scheme to identify the optimal sensor placements by utilizing three methodological sensor optimization frameworks such as physics driven, experimental data driven and a hybrid combination of both. The physics-driven method optimizes continuous planar coordinates using D-optimality, spatial correlation, and leastsquares error. The data-driven method uses measured spatiotemporal fields and pole-geometry metrics, while the hybrid method integrates both approaches. The evaluation is performed using training objective, Pearson correlation, normalized root-meansquare error (NRMSE) and pole separation(mm), orientation(°) and trajectory(mm) errors, where physics-driven optimization identified 16 sensors with a correlation of 0.9914 and an NRMSE of 0.0139, with pole separation, orientation, and trajectory errors of 3.71 mm, 1.38°, and 3.55 mm, respectively. Data-driven optimization shows diminishing improvements beyond 17 sensors, achieving correlation 0.9840, NRMSE 0.0035, and pole errors of approximately 4.30 mm, 1.70°, and 6.80 mm. The hybrid method achieves correlation 0.9945 and NRMSE 0.0201 with 14 sensors, reducing sensor count by 12.5% relative to the physics-driven configuration. Its pole errors of 6.50 mm, 2.31°, and 7.50 mm satisfy the study-defined thresholds, balancing array compactness with magnetic-field morphology preservation. These results show that optimized placement helps alleviate the complexity of data redundancy and enhances the performance of MCG as a routine cost-effective clinical measurement.

</details>


### [177] [Real-Space-RG for the drawups and drawdowns of run-and-tumble trajectories: universality classes for models with space-dependent velocities and switching rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18270)
*Cecile Monthus*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维run-and-tumble过程在尺度Γ下的重整化轨迹统计，将扩散过程理论中单一尺度函数推广为一对尺度函数s±(x)，并据此给出重整化轨迹的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（arXiv:2611.04853）针对具任意扩散系数D(x)与Ito力F_I(x)的扩散过程建立了重整化轨迹的尺度函数框架，但该框架依赖单一比值F_I(x)/D(x)，无法直接适用于含两个速度v±(x)与两个切换率γ±(x)的run-and-tumble过程，故需推广。

Method: 将一条随机轨迹视为由切换事件对应的局部极小与局部极大交替构成；在尺度Γ下仅保留振幅大于Γ的drawup与drawdown所分隔的极值序列，得到重整化轨迹，并类比扩散过程的分析方法引入一对尺度函数s±(x)。

Result: 单一尺度函数s(x)（仅依赖F_I(x)/D(x)）被一对尺度函数s±(x)取代，后者分别只依赖比值γ+(x)/v+(x)与γ−(x)/v−(x)；该对函数决定区间离开概率，并刻画重整化轨迹的普适类——凡具有相同两个比值的run-and-tumble过程共享相同的重整化轨迹统计。

Conclusion: 该框架以全直线上的均匀对称与非对称过程、半直线上的几何过程、以及具可归一化稳态的telegraph过程指数泛函为例，与相应的扩散过程对照，验证了其适用性。

Abstract: For one-dimensional run-and-tumble processes with the two velocities $v_{\pm}(x)$ and the two switching rates $γ_{\pm}(x)$, a given stochastic trajectory is an alternating sequence of local minima and local maxima corresponding to the switching events between the ballistic motions with velocities $v_{\pm}(x) $ in the two directions. The renormalized trajectory at scale $Γ$ is obtained by keeping only the alternating sequence of local minima and local maxima that are separated by drawups and drawdowns of amplitude bigger than $Γ$. Its statistical properties can be analyzed via the generalization of the previous work arxiv:2611.04853 concerning diffusion processes involving arbitrary diffusion coefficients $D(x)$ and Ito forces $F_I(x)$. The main result is that the scale function $s(x)$ of diffusion processes that depends only on the ratio $\frac{F_I(x)}{D(x) } $ is replaced by a pair of scale functions $s_{\pm}(x)$ that depend only on the two ratios $\frac{γ_+( x ) }{v_+( x )}$ and $\frac{γ_-( x ) }{v_-( x )}$. This pair of scale functions $s_{\pm}(x)$ govern the exit probabilities from an interval and determine the universality classes of the renormalized trajectories: all run-and-tumble processes with the same two ratios $\frac{γ_+( x ) }{v_+( x )}$ and $\frac{γ_-( x ) }{v_-( x )}$ share the same statistics of renormalized trajectories. This general framework is illustrated with examples that are compared to their diffusive counterparts : uniform symmetric and asymmetric processes on the whole line $x \in]-\infty,+\infty[$, geometric process on the half-line $x \in]0,+\infty[$, and an exponential functional of the telegraph process with a normalizable steady state.

</details>


### [178] [Entanglement and information dynamics in charged dual-unitary circuits with impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18435)
*Shachar Fraenkel,Colin Rylands*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究破缺整体对称性的量子杂质（quantum impurity）对封闭非平衡量子多体系统纠缠弛豫的影响，在带电对偶幺正电路（dual-unitary circuit）中考察一个对称破缺、非对偶幺正的定域门，发现杂质可导致非单调纠缠熵演化、稳态改变及持续的子系统互信息与纠缠负度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭非平衡量子多体系统的局域部分可通过区域间纠缠的增长达到稳态，而实际系统中常存在破缺整体对称性的局域缺陷，其对这一弛豫过程的影晌尚不清晰，因而有必要定量刻画对称破缺杂质（symmetry-breaking impurity）如何改变量子弛豫行为。

Method: 构建带电对偶幺正量子电路，并在空间固定的一对格点上施加对称破缺的非对偶幺正门作为杂质；利用体系统的对偶幺正性（dual unitarity）精确计算早期纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）、互信息（mutual information）与对数负度（logarithmic negativity），并在晚期借助准粒子图像（quasiparticle picture）与纠缠膜图像（entanglement membrane picture）给出预测，同时通过体纠缠能力（entangling power）的相应极限获得精确结果加以佐证。

Result: 对称破缺杂质会使量子弛豫行为更丰富：纠缠熵出现非单调演化，长时间稳态被修正，且子系统互信息与纠缠负度持续存在；这些效应可由杂质散射产生的长程关联（long-range correlations）以及守恒电荷的弹道传播（ballistic propagation）加以解释。

Conclusion: 整体对称性的局域破缺显著改变封闭量子多体系统的纠缠动力学与稳态性质，准粒子与纠缠膜图像结合对偶幺正性可对该过程给出定量描述；杂质诱导的长程关联与电荷弹道输运是理解上述现象的物理机制。

Abstract: Local parts of a closed, non-equilibrium quantum many-body system can reach stationarity through the buildup of entanglement between different regions. In this paper we investigate how this process is affected by the presence of a quantum impurity that breaks a global symmetry of the bulk. In particular, we study a charged dual-unitary quantum circuit perturbed by a symmetry-breaking, non-dual-unitary gate acting on a spatially fixed pair of sites. In this setup we calculate the entanglement entropy for a subsystem that does not contain the impurity, as well as the mutual information and logarithmic negativity for a bipartite subsystem that straddles the impurity. We obtain exact results in the early time regime for these quantities from the dual unitarity of the bulk system. Beyond this regime, we compute the predictions of the quasiparticle and entanglement membrane pictures, supporting them with exact calculations obtained by taking the corresponding limits of the bulk entangling power. We find that the presence of a symmetry-breaking impurity can lead to a richer behaviour of quantum relaxation, including non-monotonic evolution of the entanglement entropy, a modification of the long-time steady state, and persistent subsystem mutual information and entanglement negativity. This behaviour is understood in terms of long-range correlations generated by scattering from the impurity, and facilitated by the ballistic propagation of the conserved charges.

</details>

# astro-ph.SR [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [The Gravitational Wave Foreground from Galactic Cataclysmic Variables in LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17421)
*Levi Schult,Alexander Criswell,Stephen R. Taylor,Katelyn Breivik*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 基于BLIP对含灾变变星（cataclysmic variable, CV）种群的模拟LISA数据进行分析，发现局部CV种群影响可忽略，而银河系CV种群信噪比约76，若忽略将偏置双白矮星（double white dwarf, DWD）光谱模型，亟需多成分光谱模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对CV系统的研究主要依赖电磁观测（electromagnetic observations），而LISA任务的发射将提供多信使（multi-messenger）视角。此前工作已初步探讨CV的引力波（gravitational wave, GW）频谱，本文旨在进一步考察局部与银河系CV种群对LISA数据及与DWD频谱联合建模的影响。

Method: 采用贝叶斯LISA推断软件包（Bayesian LISA Inference Package, BLIP），模拟包含CV种群的LISA数据，评估其影响以及联合建模CV与DWD引力波频谱的能力。

Result: 局部CV种群对LISA数据流影响可忽略；银河系CV种群信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）约为76（依赖所设DWD种群特征），若被忽略将使现有DWD光谱模型产生偏置。现有方法难以准确恢复两个种群各自的频谱。

Conclusion: 需要构建同时包含CV与DWD引力波贡献的多成分光谱模型（multi-component spectral models）；结果预示着利用LISA研究CV系统的良好前景。

Abstract: Electromagnetic observations have been the primary medium for studying cataclysmic variable (CV) systems, capitalizing on the invaluable opportunity they present to study binary stellar evolution and accretion. The launch of the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) mission, however, will enable a multi-messenger perspective of the population. We extend previous work to explore the gravitational wave (GW) spectra sourced by a local and Galactic population of CVs. Using the Bayesian LISA Inference Package (BLIP), we simulate LISA data containing these CV populations to understand their impact and our ability to jointly model CV and double white dwarf (DWD) GW spectra. While we find that a local CV population has negligible effect on the LISA datastream, a Galactic CV population will have a signal-to-noise ratio of approximately 76, depending on the characteristics of the DWD population present, and will bias existing DWD spectral models if ignored. Current approaches struggle to accurately recover both populations' respective spectra, calling for the creation of multi-component spectral models that include GW contributions from both CVs and DWDs. These results point toward a promising future for the study of CVs with LISA.

</details>


### [180] [Receiving Exoplanet Messages with Gravitational-wave Carriers: The Gravitational-wave Radial Velocity Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17571)
*Casey McGrath*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该文提出并量化基于引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）径向速度（radial velocity, RV）探测系外行星的方法，指出其灵敏度由“相位调制指数”（phase modulation index, PMI）决定，且PMI从中频段到高频段提升约4–6个数量级，从而为高质量、长周期系外行星探测提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系外行星探测的电磁径向速度法难以适用于白矮星、中子星与黑洞等超致密后主序天体，而这类天体可作为引力波源，因此有必要评估利用引力波径向速度探测其周围系外行星的可行性。

Method: 以类比电磁径向速度法的框架引入相位调制指数（PMI）作为核心参数，分别考察中频段（未来LISA任务）与高频段（当前LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA观测站）的引力波源；通过分析观测引力波信号的功率成分，解析推导行星导致的载波频率功率损失及源与行星的信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR），并讨论观测站运动带来的额外多普勒效应（Doppler effect）与未探测行星引起的源参数偏差（parameter bias）。

Result: PMI从中频段到高频段增加约O(10^4–10^6)倍，使行星存在导致的观测功率再分配更显著；由此获得载波功率损失与信噪比的解析表达式，并识别出观测站运动多普勒效应及未探测行星所致参数偏差的影响。

Conclusion: 引力波径向速度法在高频段更具优势，研究结果有助于界定该方法在系外行星质量—周期发现空间（mass-period discovery space）中的最佳适用区域。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) radial velocity (RV) may offer a new method for detecting exoplanets, one that would primarily target planets orbiting GW-emitting, ultracompact, post-main-sequence stellar objects such as white dwarfs, neutron stars, and black holes. In this work we show that while similar to electromagnetic-based RV, this GW-based method depends on the ''phase modulation index'' (PMI), a parameter that is more sensitive to high-mass, long-period exoplanets. We investigate the potential of GW RV for GW sources in both the mid-frequency spectrum (where the upcoming LISA mission will operate) and the high-frequency spectrum (where the current LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) observatories operate). Remarkably, we find that the PMI increases by a factor of $\sim \mathrm{O}\left(10^4 - 10^6\right)$ going from the mid-frequency to the high-frequency GW spectrum, which results in a greater redistribution of observed power due to the exoplanet's presence. By analyzing the power content of the observed GW signal, we derive analytic expressions to predict the GW carrier frequency power loss due to the exoplanet and the signal-to-noise ratio of the GW source and the exoplanet. We also explore the effects of the additional Doppler effect caused by the motion of the GW observatory and the potential for GW source parameter bias introduced by the presence of an undetected exoplanet. All of this helps us better understand where the GW RV method may work best in the exoplanet mass-period discovery space.

</details>


### [181] [Multimessenger Exoplanets: Combining Infrared Excess and Astrometry with Gravitational-wave Detections of Double White Dwarf Exoplanets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17572)
*Casey McGrath,Mary Anne Limbach,Scott C. Noble,Vincent Kofman*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该文提出以LISA引力波探测DWD双白矮星系统的环双星行星，并用JWST红外超与天体测量两种电磁手段进行独立确认，实现引力波与电磁联合验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往多信使研究多集中于超致密天体，而DWD环双星行星既无已知样本、又难以通过电磁巡天高效发现，因此需要引力波探测加定向电磁确认的路径来识别并刻画此类系统。

Method: 以已知DWD系统ZTF J2243+5242为代表性案例并缩放至150 pc基准距离，计算LISA对环双星系外行星的可探测性，并演示JWST红外超与Gaia或宜居世界天文台天体测量两种互补电磁技术进行独立确认。

Result: 两种电磁技术的可探测参数空间均与LISA对DWD系外行星的敏感区域重叠；同时首次探讨将红外超方法由单白矮星拓展至DWD系统用于系外行星探测。

Conclusion: 引力波探测结合定向电磁确认是识别和刻画DWD环双星行星的可行途径，多信使联合观测可为其提供稳健的确认与表征。

Abstract: Multimessenger astrophysics provides a powerful framework for characterizing astrophysical systems through multiple, independent signals rather than relying solely on electromagnetic (EM) observations. While most prior multimessenger studies have focused on the properties of ultracompact objects, recent work has shown that gravitational waves (GWs) detectable with the upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) observatory can also be used to detect exoplanets orbiting stellar remnants. In particular, this includes circumbinary planets around tight double white dwarf (DWD) systems. This population is unique: no such planets are currently known around DWDs, and only a handful of circumbinary planets around main-sequence stars and planets orbiting white dwarfs (WDs) have been identified. Independent EM confirmation will be essential, especially for the first GW-detected exoplanets, to validate this method. While EM techniques are unlikely to efficiently survey large populations of DWDs, GW detection followed by targeted EM confirmation provides one pathway for identifying and characterizing these systems. Using a representative case, the known DWD ZTF J2243+5242 scaled to a fiducial distance of 150 pc, we compute the detectability of circumbinary exoplanets via LISA GW observations. We then demonstrate two complementary EM techniques--JWST infrared (IR) excess and Gaia or Habitable Worlds Observatory astrometry--that could independently confirm a GW-detected planet in this fiducial system. We present the first exploration of IR excess around DWDs as a viable exoplanet detection method, extending its established use for solitary WDs. Both EM techniques overlap with regions of parameter space where LISA is sensitive to DWD exoplanets, enabling robust confirmation and characterization.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Gravitational-wave hierarchical inference from population samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17428)
*Federico De Santi,Davide Gerosa,Matthew Mould*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种仅依赖样本、无需对目标族群模型做密度评估（density evaluation）的引力波族群推断（gravitational-wave population inference）方法，从而实现与族群合成（population synthesis）数值模拟的直接比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准族群推断需计算族群模型的概率密度，这仅适用于解析函数形式的模型，而先进的天体物理族群合成代码（population-synthesis codes）只能提供模拟样本、密度未知，二者因此不兼容。

Method: 方法仅使用样本、从不评估目标族群模型的密度；关键技术包括单事件似然（single-event likelihood）与探测概率（detection probability）的高效代理模型（surrogate），以及高效的采样策略（sampling strategy）。

Result: 使用截至 GWTC-5.0 的探测事件，方法在参数化模型上与标准推断一致；在包含层级并合（hierarchical mergers）的模拟族群上展示了灵活性；并成功应用于含五个形成通道（formation channels）的族群合成模拟网格。

Conclusion: 该工作为天体物理模型与引力波数据的直接比较铺平了道路。

Abstract: Standard approaches to gravitational-wave population inference require evaluating the probability density of the population model. While appropriate for models composed of exact functional forms, this approach is not compatible with state-of-the-art astrophysical predictions from population-synthesis codes, which simulate samples from astrophysical models whose densities are unknown. We present an approach that solely requires samples and never density evaluations of the targeted population model. Key technical ingredients include efficient surrogates for the single-event likelihoods and detection probability, together with an efficient sampling strategy. Using detections up to GWTC-5.0, we validate the method against standard inference with a parametric model, showcase its flexibility on a simulated population including hierarchical mergers, and present a realistic application to a grid of astrophysical population-synthesis simulations with five formation channels. This work paves the way toward a direct comparison of astrophysical models and gravitational-wave data.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Emulating spatially extended systems using Fourier Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18007)
*Gabriele Poccianti,Duccio Fanelli*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文将算子学习（Operator Learning, OL）框架从孤立刚性离子模型推广到定义于多种图拓扑（graph topologies）上的空间延展系统，并在实证神经活动数据上验证了其建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作如傅里叶神经算子（Fourier Neural Operator, FNO）多基于孤立、刚性离子模型生成的数值数据训练，而真实生物与物理系统通常由复杂异质网络中的相互连接空间单元构成，存在推广需求。

Method: 将数据驱动的算子学习框架推广至定义于多样图拓扑（从 Erdos-Renyi 网络到 Barabasi-Albert 网络）的空间延展系统，并在实证数据上评估所提方法。

Result: 所提方法在实证数据上展现出准确且高效地建模任务导向神经活动（task-oriented neural activity）的能力。

Conclusion: 算子学习可作为传统数值积分方案的数据驱动替代，并适用于复杂异质网络上的空间延展系统建模。

Abstract: Operator Learning (OL) algorithms approximate underlying mathematical operators by mapping entire classes of input functions to their corresponding solutions. For instance, architectures such as the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) have been trained on numerical datasets generated by isolated, stiff ionic models to provide a data-driven alternative to traditional numerical integration schemes. However, real-world biological and physical systems typically consist of interconnected spatial units arranged in complex, heterogeneous networks. Motivated by this observation, we generalize this data-driven framework to spatially extended systems defined on diverse graph topologies, ranging from Erdos-Renyi to Barabasi-Albert networks. Furthermore, we evaluate the proposed approach on empirical data, demonstrating its ability to accurately and efficiently model task-oriented neural activity.

</details>


### [184] [Analytical treatment of reentrant localization via Lyapunov branch structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18009)
*Taylan Yildiz,B. Tanatar*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文在弱耦合二聚体链（weakly coupled dimers）中解析证明了重入局域化（reentrant localization）：随固定格点势（onsite potential）整体强度增大，下能带中央三分之一态在有限势强区间内Lyapunov指数为零且对相位扰动稳定，两侧则全为局域态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重入局域化——随准周期势（quasiperiodic potential）增强，态先局域、再部分扩展、又再次局域——此前仅有数值证据，缺乏严格证明。

Method: 构建弱耦合二聚体链，仅调节固定格点势的整体强度；利用二聚体下轨道（lower orbital）在两个格点间的权重转移，使准周期势在特定强度下被抵消而二聚体间跃迁（hopping）保持有限。在能量尺度良好分离条件下，证明下能带中央部分态的Lyapunov指数为零并对小相位变化稳定。

Result: 在有限势强区间内，下能带中央三分之一态为扩展态（extended states），区间两侧所有态均局域；数值计算确认扩展恢复覆盖整个下能带，而上能带始终局域。

Conclusion: 该工作将重入局域化从数值观察提升为可解析证明的现象，并给出其机制：轨道权重转移导致的准周期势有效抵消，同时指出扩展态恢复的范围与Lyapunov指数稳定性判据。

Abstract: In reentrant localization, states become localized under a growing quasiperiodic potential partly become extended again before localizing once more. So far this has been demonstrated only numerically. Here we prove it in a chain of weakly coupled dimers in which we vary only the overall strength of a fixed onsite potential. As the strength increases, the lower orbital of each dimer shifts its weight between the two sites. At a particular strength, the quasiperiodic potential it feels cancels, while the hopping between dimers stays finite. When the energy scales are well separated, we prove that, over a finite range of potential strengths, all states in a central part of the lower band, one third of its states, have a vanishing Lyapunov exponent that is stable against small changes of phase, the certification of extended states. On both sides of this range, all states are localized. Numerical calculations confirm that the recovery covers the whole lower band, while the upper band stays localized.

</details>


### [185] [Guerra Interpolation and Optimal Transport: Kantorovich Bounds, Brenier Maps and the Replicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18373)
*Elena Agliari,Adriano Barra,Andrea Ladiana,Andrea Lepre,Diego Pallara*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文证明 Guerra 的插值方法本质上是一次最优传输（optimal transport）计算：有限 $N$ 压强经线性变换后成为满足 Hamilton–Jacobi 不等式的作用量 $u$，其积分给出 Guerra 求和规则，去掉余项则得到对偶 Kantorovich 问题的可行对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自 Guerra 工作以来，平均场自旋玻璃（mean-field spin glasses）惯用 Hamilton–Jacobi 方程研究，但插值方法与最优传输之间的联系尚未被明确刻画，本文旨在揭示二者是同一计算。

Method: 以 Curie–Weiss 与 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick 模型为原型，通过线性变量替换把有限 $N$ 压强化为经典子解 $\partial_t u+\tfrac12(\partial_x u)^2\le 0$；沿直线段积分恢复求和规则，并用 Kantorovich 对偶、Brenier 势、$\mathcal{W}_2$ 测地线与 Hopf–Lax 公式分析相关结构。

Result: Curie–Weiss 模型中 $T_t(y)=y-t\tanh y$ 为最优传输映射当且仅当 $t\le 1$；复制对称 SK 模型中腔图在物理半直线上单射，其 Brenier 势曲率为纵向本征值且大于 replicon 本征值 $Λ_{\mathrm{AT}}$，故 Dirac 质量传输无法探测 AT 线；replicon 沿把 Dirac 质量分裂为两原子的 $\mathcal{W}_2$ 测地线出现。

Conclusion: Dirac 初值下 Guerra 的 $K$ 步界取 Hopf–Lax 形式，Parisi 公式成为原子测度上的 Hopf–Lax 公式；借助 Cole–Hopf 层比较得到的 $\mathcal{W}_1$-Lipschitz 界，令 $K\to\infty$ 可在 Dirac 数据处恢复 Mourrat 及 Mourrat–Panchenko 的 Hopf–Lax 形式，并给出等质量逼近的速率。

Abstract: Since Guerra's work, mean-field spin glasses have been studied through Hamilton--Jacobi equations. Here, using the Curie--Weiss and the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick models as prototypical examples, we show that his celebrated interpolation method is itself an optimal transport computation.
  After a linear change of variables the finite-$N$ pressure becomes an action $u$ with $\partial_t u = -A/2$ and $\partial_x u = -B$, where $B^2 \le A$, so $u$ is a classical subsolution of $\partial_t u+\frac12(\partial_x u)^2 \le 0$. Integrating along a straight segment gives Guerra's sum rule, and dropping its remainder makes $u(0,\cdot)$ and $u(t,\cdot)$ an admissible pair for the dual Kantorovich problem with quadratic cost. For the Curie--Weiss model and $t > 0$, $T_t(y) = y-t\tanh y$ is an optimal transport map from every source with finite first moment if and only if $t \le 1$, and the Kantorovich potentials are the limiting pressures. For the replica-symmetric Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model, the cavity map is injective on the physical half-line and the curvature of its Brenier potential is the longitudinal eigenvalue, which exceeds the replicon eigenvalue $Λ_{\mathrm{AT}}$: transport of Dirac masses does not detect the de Almeida--Thouless line. The replicon appears along the $\mathcal{W}_2$ geodesic that splits a Dirac mass into two atoms, as the second variation of a second-order surrogate of the two-atom Parisi functional. From a Dirac initial datum, Guerra's $K$-step bound takes Hopf--Lax form, and the Parisi formula becomes a Hopf--Lax formula over atomic measures. With a $\mathcal{W}_1$-Lipschitz bound for its initial datum, proved by comparing Cole--Hopf layers, we let $K\to\infty$ and recover at Dirac data the Hopf--Lax form of the Parisi formula due to Mourrat and to Mourrat and Panchenko, with a rate for equal-mass approximations.

</details>


<div id='cs.MS'></div>

# cs.MS [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Red-Blue Hamiltonian Tomography via the Operator Lightcone Wavemap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17555)
*Paolo D'Alberto*

Main category: cs.MS

TL;DR: 提出一种无模型（model-free）的哈密顿量层析（Hamiltonian tomography）方法，利用算符扩散波前（operator spreading wavefront）的符号特征定位受扰键；在7×7格点上单键识别达82倍裕度，并在耦合退化与多故障情形下实现单次扫描每键探测率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈密顿量层析通常依赖模型假设或对扰动类型与幅度的先验知识，本文旨在实现一种盲探测（blind detection）：无需模型假设、无需知道扰动类型或大小，即可定位量子晶格中的受扰键。

Method: 对同一量子晶格的两次张量网络模拟（nominal 与扰动版本）计算非恒等泡利权重场的有符号交叉熵（signed cross-entropy）L(v,t) = -ln(n_pert/n_base)，以端点处的红蓝符号翻转作为因果签名；再将p个观测中心的测量通过系综对数似然求和（ensemble log-likelihood sum）整合，并引入积分对数比 Λ(v) = ln(B(v)/N(v)) 及迭代重扫描（iterative re-scan）策略处理多键情形。

Result: 在7×7格点上，单键识别相对次优候选具有82倍裕度；在耦合退化（δJ = -0.20）且最多8个同时故障的条件下，仅用6次模拟、单次扫描即实现每键探测率（detection rate）超过90%；迭代重扫描可随每次重校准逐步厘清多键情形。

Conclusion: 该方法无需模型假设与扰动先验信息，属于刻意设计的盲探测方案；通过波前因果签名与系综似然整合，可在少量模拟下高效定位多个受扰键，并借助迭代重校准逐步提升多故障的可分辨性。

Abstract: We introduce a model-free method for Hamiltonian tomography based on the operator spreading wavefront. Given two tensor network simulations of the same quantum lattice (one with the nominal Hamiltonian and one with one or more perturbed bonds), the signed cross-entropy of the non-identity Pauli weight field $\mathcal{L}(v,t) = -\ln(n_{\rm pert}/n_{\rm base})$ identifies each perturbed bond by its causal signature: a red-blue sign flip at the two endpoints, visible from the first wavefront crossing. Combining measurements from $p$ strategically placed observation centers via an ensemble log-likelihood sum, a single broken bond is identified with an 82-fold margin over the next candidate on a $7\times7$ lattice. Extending to realistic coupling degradation ($δJ = -0.20$) and up to 8 simultaneous faults, the integrated log-ratio $Λ(v) = \ln(\mathcal{B}(v)/\mathcal{N}(v))$ achieves a per-bond detection rate above 90\% in a single seismic pass using only 6 simulations. An iterative re-scan strategy progressively resolves multi-bond cases as the signal clears with each recalibration pass. The method requires no model assumptions and no knowledge of the perturbation type or magnitude; the detection is deliberately blind.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [fieldsmith: Automated symbolic derivation in classical field theory and gauge theory from LaTeX input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17359)
*Nikola Mirkov*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文介绍开源 Python 库 fieldsmith，它基于 SymPy 符号计算，从 LaTeX 指标记法的拉格朗日量出发自动推导经典场论与非阿贝尔规范理论的分析结果，并以十八个自检算例（自 Klein-Gordon 场到 BPST 瞬子与 't Hooft-Polyakov 单极子）说明其用法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典场论与规范理论的解析推导（如 Euler-Lagrange 方程、Noether 恒等式、能量-动量张量、约束分析）繁琐且易错，作者希望以可验证的符号计算替代手工与模式匹配式工具，同时服务于研究与教学（含学生作业批改）。

Method: 用户以拉普拉斯（LaTeX）指标记法直接输入拉格朗日量；张量按分量存储于带标签、区分协变/逆变（variance-aware）的槽位中，所有结论均由精确符号计算（exact symbolic computation）而非模式匹配确立。该库为 SymPy 之上约 2,700 行的紧凑实现层。

Result: 库可产生并验证任意阶导数、平坦或弯曲背景上的 Euler-Lagrange 方程，Noether 流与 Noether 恒等式，正则与 Hilbert 能量-动量张量，正则动量与哈密顿量，局域 Poisson 括号，Dirac-Bergmann 约束分析与自由度计数，线性化波动方程与色散关系，酉群乘积的规范协变导数与场强，以及自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）后的质量谱；另有拟合例程将分量级输出转回协变指标记法，并有评分模块检查学生提交的场方程、恒等式与守恒流。

Conclusion: 该文为库的配套指南，阐明设计意图与算法，并借十八个自检算例兼作计算视角下的经典场论与规范理论导论；作者亦明确说明了方法的局限性。

Abstract: We present fieldsmith, an open-source Python library for the automated derivation of analytical results in classical field theory, and non-abelian gauge theories. A Lagrangian is entered the way it is written on a blackboard, in LaTeX index notation, and the library produces and verifies Euler-Lagrange equations (of arbitrary derivative order, on flat or curved backgrounds), Noether currents and identities, canonical and Hilbert energy-momentum tensors, canonical momenta and Hamiltonians, local Poisson brackets and a Dirac-Bergmann constraint analysis with degree-of-freedom counting, linearised wave equations and dispersion relations, gauge-covariant derivatives and field strengths for products of unitary groups, and mass spectra after spontaneous symmetry breaking. A fitting routine translates component-level output back into covariant index notation, and a grading module checks students' submitted field equations, identities and conserved currents. The implementation is a compact (about 2,700 lines) layer on top of SymPy: tensors are stored by components with labelled, variance-aware slots, and every statement is established by exact symbolic computation rather than pattern matching. This document is the companion guide to the library. It explains the intent and design, describes the algorithms, and then works through eighteen self-checking examples, ranging from the Klein-Gordon field to the BPST instanton and the 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole, each preceded by the background physics needed to follow it. The examples double as a compact introduction to classical field theory and gauge theory as seen through a computational lens. We are explicit about the limitations of the approach.

</details>
