### [188] [WDM_GW_Wavelets: A fast, JAX-based Python implementation of the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer wavelet transform for the time-frequency analysis of gravitational wave data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17396)
*Christopher J. Moore,Tomasz Kinowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文介绍一种基于 JAX 的 Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer（WDM）时频变换（time-frequency transformation）实现，面向引力波（gravitational-wave）数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要仅说明该实现的预期用途为引力波数据分析，未明确阐述具体研究动机或现有方法的不足。

Method: 采用 JAX 框架实现 Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer（WDM）时频变换。

Result: 摘要未给出具体的实验结果或性能指标。

Conclusion: 摘要仅指出该实现服务于引力波数据分析，未提供进一步结论。

Abstract: This paper describes a JAX-based implementation of the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) time-frequency transformation, intended for use in gravitational-wave data analysis.

</details>


### [189] [Axial gravitational-wave propagation and mass--gravity identifiability in conformal scalar--tensor lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17840)
*Z. F. Gao,W. Q. Ma,H. Wang,Z. J. Cao,N. Wang,G. Q. Jin,Luiz C. Garcia de Andrade*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在无量纲、无屏蔽的共形标量—张量理论中，利用静态 Einstein–标量外部解匹配弱自引力物质透镜，分析张量波传播：奇宇称主势在固定 Einstein 系质量下出现标量荷修正与高阶多极符号反转，并与后牛顿光偏折、几何光学极限及费希尔可辨识性相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验共形标量—张量理论中标量荷与多极结构如何改变张量波（引力波）传播，并厘清固定 Einstein 系质量与固定测量物质质量两种基准下可观测量的差异，避免将色散现象与作用量推导量混淆。

Method: 采用静态 Einstein–标量外部解匹配弱自引力物质透镜；展开奇宇称引力主势（odd-parity gravitational master potential），检验 R^{-4}、R^{-5} 系数解析展开与精确外部解；引入有限半径 WKB 展开、固定碰撞参数极限、零测地线偏折与数值求积进行交叉验证，并以双像费希尔（Fisher）计算评估差分相位可辨识性。

Result: 标量荷修正在 R^{-4} 阶出现；R^{-5} 系数依赖 ℓ(ℓ+1)−9，在四极与更高多极间变号。有限半径 WKB 展开显示固定多极下四极的主导频率依赖势矩相消，而固定碰撞参数极限恢复消色差几何光学作用量及次主导轴向贡献。点透镜情形下，消色差可观测量仅约束有效质量，而非透镜质量与标量耦合各自独立。

Conclusion: 共形标量—张量理论中的张量波传播在多极阶次与质量基准上表现不同，消色差几何光学极限与频率依赖项需分层处理；模型特定的色散观测须借助完整匹配的像传递函数（image transfer function）才能与作用量推导量对接。

Abstract: We investigate tensor-wave propagation in a specified massless, unscreened conformal scalar--tensor theory using a static Einstein--scalar exterior matched to a weakly self-gravitating matter lens. The odd-parity gravitational master potential has a scalar-charge correction at radial order $R^{-4}$ for fixed Einstein-frame mass. Its next $R^{-5}$ coefficient depends on $\ell(\ell+1)-9$, reversing sign between the quadrupole and higher multipoles; we test the expansion against the exact exterior potential. We connect this result to the locally normalized post-Newtonian light-deflection parameter and distinguish the Schwarzschild baseline at fixed Einstein-frame mass from that at fixed measured matter mass. A finite-radius WKB expansion reveals cancellation of the leading frequency-dependent potential moment for the quadrupole at fixed multipole, whereas a separate fixed-impact-parameter limit recovers an achromatic geometrical-optics action and a subleading axial contribution. Independent null-geodesic bending and numerical quadrature check these limits. For a point lens, achromatic observables constrain an effective mass rather than the lens mass and scalar coupling separately. An illustrative two-image Fisher calculation quantifies differential-phase identifiability after arrival-time and constant-phase nuisance parameters are released. The phenomenological phase experiment is separated from action-derived propagation quantities; model-specific dispersive observations require a complete matched image transfer function.

</details>


### [190] [Complexity and symmetry: The asymmetry factor approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17940)
*L. Herrera*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究自引力流体球（self-gravitating fluid spheres）演化过程中类时Killing矢量（time-like Killing vector, TKV）瞬时出现所产生的影响，并首次建立TKV与复杂性因子（complexity factor）之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察演化中的自引力流体球在某一时刻突然具备类时Killing矢量对称性时，物理变量与流体复杂性所留下的印记（imprints），并填补对称性与复杂性之间关系研究的空白。

Method: 引入非对称因子（asymmetry factor）概念，使特定类型对称性（如TKV）仅在某一特定类时坐标取值下成立；并对绝热流体（adiabatic fluids）与耗散流体（dissipative fluids）分别进行分析。

Result: 对过程相关物理变量的分析显示，TKV的出现会在演化过程中留下可辨识的印记；并揭示了TKV与描述流体复杂程度的复杂性因子（complexity factor）之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该研究首次揭示复杂性（complexity）与对称性（symmetry）之间的联系，表明TKV的出现与复杂性因子存在内在关联。

Abstract: We study the influence of the instantaneous appearance of a time-like Killing vector (TKV) in self-gravitating fluid spheres during their evolution. For doing so we resort to the concept of asymmetry factor which allows the existence of any specific type of symmetry (e.g. a TKV), exclusively, for a given value of the time-like coordinate. We consider adiabatic and dissipative fluids. The analysis of different relevant physical variables in this process shows the imprints of the emergence of TKV at some point of the evolution. Particular attention is paid to the relationship appearing between the TKV, and the complexity factor describing the degree of complexity of the fluid, bringing out for the first time a link between complexity and symmetry.

</details>


### [191] [Quasinormal modes of dyonic axion black holes with nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18117)
*Jialun Zhao,Antonio De Felice,Shinji Tsujikawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究含无质量轴子（axion）与非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics, NED）的静态带电黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs），分析轴子耦合导致的宇称等谱性（isospectrality）破缺及NED四阶修正对频率与阻尼的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于现有黑洞扰动分析多以Reissner–Nordström（RN）背景与线性电动力学为基准，缺乏对轴子耦合与非线性电动力学修正如何改变准正规模谱的系统认识。

Method: 采用谱方法（spectral method）计算五个动力学自由度对应的复QNM频率，并以积分—匹配（integration-and-matching）方法独立验证；同时引入由两个标准电磁不变量平方得到的两种四阶NED相互作用。

Result: 无NED修正时，五个振荡模式族由RN基模延续而来；轴子耦合一般破坏引力与电磁宇称等谱性，在代表性非零耦合下五个频率即便在纯电RN背景上也彼此不同。加入磁荷后轴子毛发使五种扰动耦合，改变振荡频率与阻尼率，强耦合基准中还出现纯虚增长模式。Q/M=√7/4、ℓ=2处已知的电磁—标量谱简并解释了小轴子耦合诱导的线性频率劈裂。等四阶系数且轴子退耦时，电磁对偶使单个分支的一阶NED频移在固定Q/M下与电荷构成无关；系数不等时对偶仅约束各RN宇称对内部的频移之和。NED使阻尼率变化约百分之一，四阶项在外部区域仍远小于局域Maxwell能量密度。

Conclusion: 轴子耦合与非线性电动力学共同显著改写带电黑洞的准正规模结构，且电磁对偶对NED频移给出了与电荷构成相关的约束；谱方法结果与独立数值方法一致，支持结论的可靠性。

Abstract: We study quasinormal modes (QNMs) of static charged black holes with a massless axion and nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). Without NED corrections, we compute the complex QNM frequencies of five oscillatory mode families in the system of five dynamical degrees of freedom. These families are continued from the fundamental Reissner--Nordström (RN) modes. Axion coupling generically breaks gravitational and electromagnetic parity isospectrality. At representative nonzero couplings, all five frequencies are distinct even on purely electric RN backgrounds without axion hair. Adding magnetic charge sources axion hair, couples all five perturbations, and modifies their oscillation frequencies and damping rates. Additional purely imaginary growing modes occur in some strong-coupling benchmarks. The known electromagnetic--scalar spectral degeneracy at $Q/M=\sqrt{7}/4$ for $\ell=2$ in the decoupled RN limit explains the linear frequency splitting induced by a small axion coupling. We include two quartic NED interactions obtained by squaring the two standard electromagnetic invariants. With the axion decoupled and equal quartic coefficients, electromagnetic duality makes the first-order NED frequency shift of each individual gravitational or electromagnetic branch independent of electric--magnetic charge composition at fixed $Q/M$. For unequal coefficients, duality instead constrains the sum of the first-order NED frequency shifts within each isolated RN parity pair. Depending on the coefficients, NED increases or decreases the damping rates by about one percent in representative backgrounds, while the quartic terms remain small compared with the local Maxwell energy density throughout the exterior. The QNM frequencies and NED shifts obtained with the spectral method agree with independent integration-and-matching results at representative parameter values.

</details>


### [192] [Separability of coupled gravitational perturbations in Plebański--Demiański spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18200)
*Finnian Gray,Hryhorii Ovcharenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将 Hintz、Yoshida、Zhao 近期关于 Kerr–Newman 及带宇宙学常数黑洞引力扰动可分离性的手征化（chiral）方法，推广至 Einstein–Maxwell 方程最一般的 D 型电真空解（含对齐物质与宇宙学常数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有可分离性方法需借助冻结近似（freezing approximation），忽略与电磁自由度的耦合；作者希望在不做该近似的前提下，把可分离性结果推广到更一般的黑洞背景。

Method: 将引力辐射自由度的耦合方程组转化为手征形式（chiral form），使其适用改进的矩阵 R 分离变量法（matrix R-separation of variables），并利用方程的共形性质（conformal properties），构造出支配手征系统解耦的对称算子（symmetry operator）。

Result: 构造成功推广到最一般的 D 型电真空解，涵盖对齐物质（aligned matter）与宇宙学常数；并识别出使手征系统解耦的底层对称算子。

Conclusion: 手征化与共形方法为一般 D 型电真空背景下引力扰动的可分离性提供了统一框架，无需冻结近似即可处理引力与电磁自由度的耦合。

Abstract: Recently, a major breakthrough in the separability of gravitational perturbations of the Kerr--Newman rotating charged black hole was achieved by Hintz [arxiv:2609.33661], and extended to non-zero cosmological constant Kerr--Newman-(A)dS black holes by Yoshida [arxiv:2610.20500] and Zhao [arxiv:2610.22274]. This method removes the need for the freezing approximation, wherein coupling to the electromagnetic degrees of freedom are ignored, at the price of a coupled set of equations in the gravitational radiative degrees of freedom. The key insight is to transform the coupled system of equations into a chiral form which is amenable to a modified matrix $R$-separation of variables. In this work we extend the construction to the most general type D electro-vacuum solution to the Einstein-Maxwell equations, with aligned matter and cosmological constant. The construction makes use of the conformal properties of the equations and we find a symmetry operator underlying the decoupling of the chiral system.

</details>


### [193] [Notes on the singularity theorems of general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18398)
*Georgios Papadopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该讲义从时空的局部几何出发，系统考察其整体因果结构，并据此给出霍金宇宙学奇点定理与彭罗斯黑洞奇点定理的教学式但技术性完整的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在以兼具教学性与技术严谨性的方式，呈现奇点定理（singularity theorems）的证明，并通过大量图示与实例帮助理解关键概念。

Method: 先探讨时空的局部几何（local geometry）与整体因果结构（global causal structure），再引入证明所需的数学工具，包括曲线及其空间的拓扑与分析性质、Arzelá–Ascoli 定理、半连续函数（semi-continuous functions）的性质，并部分演示 Hopf–Rinow 定理。

Result: 基于所获得的几何与因果结构结果，完成了霍金宇宙学奇点定理（Hawking cosmological singularity theorem）与彭罗斯黑洞奇点定理（Penrose black hole singularity theorem）的证明；同时部分演示了刻画黎曼流形（Riemannian manifolds）中测地完备性（geodesic completeness）条件的 Hopf–Rinow 定理。

Conclusion: 该讲义以图示与实例辅助，将局部几何、整体因果结构与相关数学工具贯通，构成奇点定理的技术性教学证明。

Abstract: The notes begin with an exploration of the local geometry of spacetime and proceed with an investigation of its global causal structure. The results obtained are used to present a proof of the Hawking cosmological and the Penrose black hole singularity theorems. The aim is to give a pedagogical, but still technical, proof of the statements. The notes include a large number of drawings to aid the understanding of the material and many examples to explain key concepts. Some of the mathematical tools utilised in the proof of the singularity theorems are also illustrated. These include an extensive description of the topological and analytic properties of curves and their spaces, of the Arzelá-Ascoli theorem and of the properties of semi-continuous functions. The Hopf-Rinow theorem, which describes the conditions for geodesic completeness in the context of Riemannian manifolds, is also partially demonstrated.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Chiral or unidirectional? Multiterminal current partition in the quantum Hall regime from self-consistent screening theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17441)
*Afif Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文用自洽屏蔽理论（self-consistent screening theory）重新审视六端霍尔条（Hall bar）多端电流测量所得“量子化霍尔平台（quantized Hall plateaus）上电流手性（chiral）流动”的结论，指出平台上的电流分配仅由纵向电导为零与霍尔电导量子化决定，与电流空间分布无关；电流密度的手性属于无净输运的平衡（磁化）电流部分，而输运电流在两侧边缘条带中同向流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期六端霍尔条多端电流测量被用于论证量子化霍尔平台上电流呈手性流动、平台间为非手性，且所施加电流仅沿一条边缘流动。作者意在检验这些结论是否成立，并厘清多端电流分配究竟测量了什么物理量。

Method: 采用量子霍尔效应（quantum Hall effect）的自洽屏蔽理论（self-consistent screening theory）进行理论分析；引入流函数（stream function）显式表示终端电流无法确定的环流部分，并对实际测量器件（含两个内部接触）做二维局域输运计算（local transport calculation）。

Result: 平台上的电流分配仅源自纵向电导（longitudinal conductivity）为零与霍尔电导（Hall conductivity）量子化为 νe²/h，任何满足该条件的三维导体（原文为二维导体）都具有“相干手性”（coherent and chiral）模型的电导矩阵；二维局域计算在无边缘通道（edge channels）的情况下复现了所有接触构型的平台电流分配及平台间抑制的排序。自洽电流密度可分解为无净电流的手性平衡（磁化）电流与在两侧边缘条带同向流动的输运电流；并预测两内部接触的浮动电位（floating potentials）在体区量子化时相等、在电流经边缘条带流动时相差最多半个霍尔电压。

Conclusion: 多端电流分配测量的是霍尔电导的符号与耗散的缺失，而非输运电流的位置或手性；边缘通道是解释的前提而非测量结果，“单边”施加电流只是参考化学势（chemical potential）的记账选择。

Abstract: Multiterminal current measurements on a six-terminal Hall bar were recently used to argue that the current flow is chiral on the quantized Hall plateaus and non-chiral between them, and, in a companion analysis, that the imposed current flows along one edge only. We examine these conclusions with the self-consistent screening theory of the quantum Hall effect. The plateau values of the current partition follow from a vanishing longitudinal conductivity and a quantized Hall conductivity alone: every two-dimensional conductor with $σ_\ell=0$ and $σ_{\rm H}=νe^2/h$ has the conductance matrix of the "coherent and chiral" model, whatever the spatial distribution of the current. Terminal currents fix the current density only up to circulating currents, made explicit here with a stream function, and the chirality of the current density is a property of exactly this part. A two-dimensional local transport calculation for the measured device, including its two interior contacts, reproduces the plateau partition for all contact configurations and the ordering of the suppression between the plateaus without edge channels. The self-consistent current density separates into a chiral equilibrium (magnetization) current, which carries no net current, and a transport current that flows in the same direction in both edge strips; the "one-edge" imposed current is a bookkeeping choice of the reference chemical potential. The floating potentials of the two interior contacts are predicted to be equal when the bulk around them is quantized and to differ by up to half the Hall voltage when the current flows in edge strips. Multiterminal current partition measures the sign of the Hall conductivity and the absence of dissipation, not the location or the chirality of the transport current; the edge channels are the premise of the interpretation, not a result of the measurement.

</details>


### [195] [Roton softening and crystallization in a mass-asymmetric electron-hole bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17474)
*Ambuj Jain,Mu-Yang Chen,Chao-Xing Liu,Binghai Yan,Wangqian Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在质量不对称电子-空穴双层体系中，结合自洽Hartree–Fock–Bogoliubov理论及其含时扩展，研究激子凝聚与相互作用驱动晶化之间的竞争，发现体系以一级相变进入晶态并先于roton不稳定性发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: roton软化（roton softening）是否能驱动向密度有序态或超固态（supersolid）的连续相变，是相互作用量子流体中的长期问题；本文旨在质量不对称电子-空穴双层（electron–hole bilayer）中从微观层面予以澄清。

Method: 采用自洽Hartree–Fock–Bogoliubov理论及其含时扩展（time-dependent extension），考察载流子质量比与层间距对均匀激子凝聚液体（exciton condensate liquid）中roton极小值的影响。

Result: 增大质量比或层间距会在均匀激子凝聚液体中诱导显著的roton极小；体系经一级相变（first-order transition）进入晶态，从而先占（preempt）roton不稳定性。在亚稳区，强相互作用空穴先驱动巡游电子晶化，层间距更大时体系演化为空穴晶体–电子金属相（hole-crystal–electron-metal phase）；在晶化边界附近，层间相干与电荷密度序共存的超固态解在能量上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明roton软化并非该体系中连续相变的驱动机制，晶化以一级相变方式先于其发生；作者进一步结合二维材料异质结构（two-dimensional material heterostructures）的近期实验方案讨论了上述发现。

Abstract: Whether roton softening drives a continuous transition into a density-ordered or supersolid state is a longstanding question in interacting quantum fluids. Here, we address this microscopically in a mass-asymmetric electron--hole bilayer in which exciton condensation competes with interaction-driven crystallization. Using self-consistent Hartree--Fock--Bogoliubov theory and its time-dependent extension, we show that increasing the carrier-mass ratio or interlayer distance induces a pronounced roton minimum in the homogeneous exciton condensate liquid. The system undergoes a first-order transition to a crystalline state, preempting the roton instability. In this metastable regime, strongly interacting holes first drive the crystallization of the itinerant electrons, and the system then evolves into a hole-crystal--electron-metal phase at larger interlayer distance. Supersolid solutions with coexisting interlayer coherence and charge density order are also found to be energetically competitive near the crystallization boundary. We further discuss our findings based on recent experimental setups in two-dimensional material heterostructures.

</details>


### [196] [Non-ergodic Area Scaling of Andreev Transport in Al/AlO$_x$/Al Tunnel Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17511)
*David J. Perello,Marco Coraiola,Minje Park,Cliff Chen,Hanho Lee,Abhishek Banerjee,Gil Refael,Yuki Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究测量 Al/AlOₓ/Al 约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction, JJ）中的二阶亚能隙安德烈夫电流（second-order subgap Andreev current），发现其由不足 300 个高透明通道主导，呈现非遍历性标度（non-ergodic scaling），且该效应对用于标定电感的常态电导基本不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特依赖 Al/AlOₓ/Al 结的理想正弦电流-相位关系，但这一理想化仅在非晶氧化层中众多导电通道弱且近乎等同透射时成立。作者因此检验实际结中是否存在偏离理想隧穿极限的高透明通道。

Method: 测量次能隙安德烈夫电流（其决定第二约瑟夫森谐波，second Josephson harmonic），并针对量子比特相关尺寸的结及小尺寸、高纵横比结，考察其随结面积与几何的标度行为，辅以数值模拟对照。

Result: 量子比特相关尺寸下，该电流由 ≲300 个有效通道主导，其透明度超出理想隧穿极限（≲10⁻⁶）若干数量级；增大结尺寸并不平均掉高透明通道，反而引入透明度更高的新通道，直至宽度与长度均超过临界长度尺度后趋势才反转，该非遍历标度由氧化层厚度变化导致的非自平均统计（non-self-averaging statistics）解释，并被模拟重现。

Conclusion: 高透明通道效应在常态电导中基本不可见，却揭示了面向大规模多结超导量子比特系统的几何与尺寸权衡，表明结尺寸扩展策略需重新审视。

Abstract: State-of-the-art superconducting qubits rely on Al/AlO$_x$/Al Josephson junctions (JJs) as a nonlinear element with an ideal sinusoidal current-phase dependence, with its inductance set by the junction area and oxidation conditions. This idealization holds only if the many conduction channels through the amorphous AlO$_x$ barrier transmit weakly and almost identically. Here, we measure the second-order subgap Andreev current, which sets the second Josephson harmonic, in Al/AlO$_x$/Al JJs. For junction sizes relevant to qubits, we find that this current is dominated by $\lesssim 300$ effective channels whose transparencies exceed the ideal tunneling limit ($\lesssim 10^{-6}$) by orders of magnitude. For small and high-aspect-ratio junctions, increasing the junction size does not average out the effect of these high transparency channels but instead adds additional channels of even greater transparency. The trend inverts once both the width and length of the junction exceed a critical length scale, above which the effective transparency decreases with area. This non-ergodic scaling of the Andreev current is reproduced by simulations and attributed to non-self-averaging statistics of the transparency distribution, which arise from variations in oxide thickness. The effect is largely invisible to the normal-state conductance used to target the inductance and reveals unexpected geometric and size-related trade-offs for scaling superconducting qubit systems to large numbers of junctions.

</details>


### [197] [Thermally Activated Magnetic Switching in Antiferromagnetic Van der Waals Trilayers Resolved by Single-Spin Diamond Magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17521)
*Fabian A. Gerritsma,Roland W. Mulder,Balázs Nagyfalusi,Pieter C. J. A. van Zweeden,Samer Kurdi,Francisco Machado,Jaime Ferrer,Samuel Mañas-Valero,Toeno van der Sar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用金刚石中扫描氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）色心磁测量技术，测得A型反铁磁体CrPS₄三层的零场双稳态磁化，并确定其磁化反转机制为热激活畴成核（thermally activated domain nucleation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德瓦尔斯磁体（van der Waals magnets）为研究低维体系中涌现的磁性现象（如层依赖磁有序）提供了理想平台，但需要对少层样品的磁化反转进行定量测量。

Method: 采用金刚石中扫描NV色心磁测量技术测量A型反铁磁体（A-type antiferromagnet）CrPS₄三层的磁化，分辨↑↓↑与↓↑↓两种层磁化构型间的零场双稳态；并结合密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）计算与空间磁成像分析。

Result: 矫顽场（coercive field）随温度近似线性下降，符合Néel–Arrhenius模型；DFT计算可再现磁化的温度依赖并给出相干旋转（coherent-rotation）情形下的矫顽场，但实测矫顽场更低，表明反转由热激活畴成核主导；空间磁成像显示存在独立翻转的区域，可能被结构不均匀性钉扎（pinned）。

Conclusion: 扫描NV磁测量是定量研究二维磁体（two-dimensional magnets）磁化反转的有力手段，有助于揭示低维体系中涌现的磁学现象。

Abstract: Van der Waals magnets provide a versatile platform for exploring magnetic phenomena that emerge from reduced dimensionality, including layer-dependent magnetic ordering. Here, we use scanning nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond magnetometry to measure the magnetization of a trilayer of the A-type antiferromagnet CrPS$_4$, resolving its zero-field bistability between the up-down-up ($\uparrow\downarrow\uparrow$) and down-up-down ($\downarrow\uparrow\downarrow$) layer magnetization configurations. The coercive field decreases approximately linearly with temperature, consistent with the Néel--Arrhenius model. Density Functional Theory calculations capture the temperature dependence of the magnetization and determine the coercive field in the coherent-rotation case. The measured coercive fields are lower, identifying thermally activated domain nucleation as the reversal mechanism. Spatial magnetic imaging supports this picture, revealing independently switching regions likely pinned by structural inhomogeneities. Our results highlight the power of scanning NV magnetometry for the quantitative investigation of magnetization reversal in two-dimensional magnets, unraveling the magnetic phenomenology that emerges in low-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [198] [Chiral topological phases of proximitized planar germanium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17579)
*Vittorio Coppini,Bernard van Heck*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究平面锗二维空穴气（two-dimensional hole gas）在自旋轨道耦合、近邻诱导s波超导与磁化共同作用下的拓扑相图，发现由于立方Rashba自旋轨道相互作用（Rashba spin-orbit interaction）可出现陈数（Chern number）高达四的多种拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空穴气具有动量三次方的Rashba自旋轨道耦合（可能同时存在线性项），与常见电子体系不同，可能产生更高陈数的拓扑相，因此有必要系统刻画其拓扑相图并评估实验可及性。

Method: 基于含自旋与不含自旋的有效单带模型（effective single-band models）绘制拓扑相图，模型参数取自平面锗空穴气的自洽薛定谔-泊松计算（self-consistent Schrödinger-Poisson calculation）；并以稀释锰杂质（manganese impurities）提供的交换相互作用作为磁化机制的具体模型。

Result: 相图中出现多种拓扑相，其陈数最高可达四；在无应变平面锗（unstrained planar germanium）中，C=3的拓扑相有望在实验上实现。

Conclusion: 立方Rashba自旋轨道耦合使平面锗空穴气成为实现高陈数拓扑相的候选平台，其中C=3相在无应变条件下具备实验可达性。

Abstract: We investigate the topological phase diagram of a two-dimensional hole gas in planar germanium resulting from the combination of spin-orbit coupling, proximity-induced $s$-wave superconductivity, and magnetization. The peculiarity of the hole gas is the presence of a Rashba spin-orbit interaction cubic in momentum, possibly coexisting with a linear term, which results in the presence of distinct topological phases with Chern numbers as high as four. We map out the topological phase diagram based on effective single-band models with and without spin, and use input parameters based on a self-consistent Schrödinger-Poisson calculation of a planar germanium hole gas. As a concrete model for inducing magnetization in the hole gas, we consider the exchange interaction coming from the presence of a dilute ensemble of manganese impurities. A topological phase with $C=3$ could be accessible experimentally in unstrained planar germanium.

</details>


### [199] [Selective coupling and spectral evolution of topological domain-wall states under local perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17607)
*Ricardo Y. Díaz-Bonifaz,Edna M. Hernández,Carlos Ramírez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究一维Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型中拓扑畴壁态（topological domain-wall state）的谱演化，发现局域势可诱导拓扑态与特定体态（bulk states）的选择性耦合，使畴壁态能量渐近进入体带并在带对侧产生新畴壁态；输运计算与RLC拓扑电路阻抗谱实验均验证了该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示局域势（local potential）对拓扑畴壁态谱结构的调控机制，并探寻可在电路中实验观测该类拓扑谱演化的可行平台。

Method: 基于一维SSH模型的紧束缚理论分析，结合输运（transport）电导计算；实验上构建RLC拓扑电路（topo-electrical circuit）并进行阻抗谱（impedance spectroscopy）测量。

Result: 施加于畴壁的局域势使拓扑态与特定体态发生选择性耦合；随门电势（gate potential）增大，畴壁态能量渐近趋近体带，同时带对侧出现新的畴壁态，谱由一系列与体连续谱保持分离的局域态构成；电导峰随能谱演化，实验阻抗与局域态密度（local density of states）相符，在与选择性耦合体能量对应的频率处出现共振。

Conclusion: 局域势可有效调控拓扑畴壁态谱并保持其与体连续谱的分离，RLC拓扑电路可作为该类拓扑谱演化现象的实验验证平台。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the spectral evolution of topological domain-wall states in a one-dimensional structure described by the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model. We show that a local potential applied to the domain wall induces a selective coupling between a topological state and specific bulk states. Moreover, by increasing the gate potential, we observe that the domain wall state energies asymptotically reach a bulk band, whereas new domain wall states appear on the opposite side of the band. As a consequence, the domain-wall-state spectrum evolves through a sequence of localized states that remain separated from the bulk continuum. These effects are also reflected in transport calculations, where the conductance peaks follow the evolution of the energy spectrum. To experimentally verify these phenomena, we realize an RLC topo-electrical circuit and perform impedance spectroscopy measurements. We find that the impedance matches the local density of states, exhibiting resonances at frequencies associated with the bulk energies that selectively couple to the domain wall topological state.

</details>


### [200] [A Unified Device-Level Physical Framework for DRAM Read Disturbance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17634)
*Zhaoqiang Bai,Haocong Luo,Xinhe Wang,Xujing Li,Yuelin Jiang,Xuelu Huang,Guilei Wang,Hao Meng,Ataberk Olgun,Ismail Emir Yuksel,F. Nisa Bostancı,Jikun Wang,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将RowHammer、RowPress与ColumnDisturb统一到器件级物理框架中，归因于陷阱辅助电子迁移、电容耦合与感放器介导扰动三种机制的不同空间作用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAM中的读扰动（read disturbance）既受局部器件相互作用影响，也受共享阵列电路所施加的电学条件支配，现有认识缺乏统一的器件级物理解释。

Method: 结合DIMM级表征、器件测量与TCAD仿真，将浮置存储节点的电荷演化关联到陷阱辅助电子迁移（trap-assisted electron migration, EM）、电容耦合（capacitive coupling, CC）和感放器介导扰动（sense-amplifier-mediated disturb, SAMD）三类机制。

Result: EM具有局域性与陷阱敏感性，CC随字线间距增大而减弱，SAMD通过共享列电路扩展扰动；高压存储态的关态漏电（OFF-state leakage）约为类保持条件的九倍，可解释局域性RowHammer/RowPress失效、列边界突变的远距ColumnDisturb，以及高锤击数下1-to-0失效占优但0-to-1失效更早出现的现象。

Conclusion: 该器件级框架解释了RowPress与ColumnDisturb的极性以及邻字线与通过字线有效性的时长依赖反转，为跨访问模式与阵列配置的紧凑DRAM可靠性建模提供物理基础。

Abstract: Read disturbance in dynamic random-access memory (DRAM) depends on both local device interactions and the electrical conditions imposed by shared array circuitry. Here, we combine DIMM-level characterization results, device measurements, and TCAD simulations to interpret RowHammer, RowPress, and ColumnDisturb within a unified device-level physical framework. We relate charge evolution at the floating storage node to three key mechanisms: trap-assisted electron migration (EM), capacitive coupling (CC), and sense-amplifier-mediated disturb (SAMD). EM is local and trap-sensitive, while CC weakens with wordline separation. SAMD extends disturbance through shared column circuitry by modifying unselected-cell leakage under rail-level bitline biases, a contribution supported by the approximately ninefold higher OFF-state leakage measured relative to retention-like conditions for a high-voltage storage state. These distinct spatial ranges account for localized RowHammer and RowPress failures and remote ColumnDisturb populations with abrupt changes at subarray boundaries. We further interpret prolonged RowPress activation as a transition from an initial RowHammer-like transient to sustained leakage driven mainly by SAMD under held wordline and bitline biases. Within this device-level framework, trap effectiveness and storage-state-dependent leakage provide an interpretation of previously puzzling failure signatures, including the earlier onset of 0-to-1 RowHammer failures despite the predominance of 1-to-0 failures at high hammer counts. The same framework explains the observed RowPress and ColumnDisturb polarities and the duration-dependent reversal of neighboring- and passing-wordline effectiveness. These results provide a physical basis for developing compact DRAM reliability models across access patterns and array configurations.

</details>


### [201] [Quantum spin-wave statistics and dimensionality in thermally activated magnetic transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17641)
*Jacob J. Mankenberg,Grzegorz J. Kwiatkowski,Pavel F. Bessarab*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在谐波过渡态理论（harmonic transition state theory）与Kramers-Langer理论框架内，引入量子自旋波统计，给出了磁性状态间热激活跃迁速率的量子修正：零点涨落重整化（renormalize）能垒，Bose-Einstein占据修正指前因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Arrhenius描述依赖能量均分（equipartition），对高频激发赋予过多权重，可能导致跃迁速率预测出现系统性误差；需考察量子统计修正的必要性及其随体系维度的变化。

Method: 对初始态与过渡态附近稳定自旋波激发采用量子统计，在谐波过渡态理论与Kramers-Langer理论中构造量子修正的跃迁速率表达式，并比较经典与量子统计的指前因子。

Result: 零点涨落使能垒重整化，Bose-Einstein占据改变指前因子；经典均分导致的高频激发过度加权误差在自旋波谱上累积，并随体系维度升高而加剧。在相干磁化反转（coherent magnetization reversal）、磁斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）坍缩与斯格明子管（skyrmion tube）断裂等非共线磁织构跃迁中，三维体系里经典与量子统计的速率预测可相差多个数量级。

Conclusion: 量子自旋波统计对热激活磁性跃迁速率有显著修正，尤其在高维体系中经典估计可能产生数量级偏差，因而在磁性织构稳定性与跃迁分析中需采用量子修正的速率公式。

Abstract: A quantum correction to the rate of thermally activated transition between magnetic states is formulated within harmonic transition state theory and the Kramers-Langer theory by employing quantum statistics for stable spin-wave excitations around the initial state and the transition state. Quantum spin-wave statistics modifies both parameters of the Arrhenius expression for the transition rate relative to their classical values: the zero-point fluctuations renormalize the energy barrier, while the Bose-Einstein occupation of the spin-wave modes modifies the pre-exponential factor. Comparison of the classical and quantum-statistical pre-exponential factors reveals a systematic error associated with the excessive weight assigned to high-frequency excitations by classical equipartition. This error accumulates over the spin-wave spectrum and becomes increasingly severe with increasing dimensionality of the system. The effect is demonstrated for coherent magnetization reversal, and transitions involving noncollinear magnetic textures, including collapse of a magnetic skyrmion and rupture of a skyrmion tube, where classical and quantum-statistical predictions for the transition rate can differ by many orders of magnitude in three-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [202] [Low-Temperature Heat Capacity of Single-Walled Carbon Nanotube Bundles with low dimensional gas systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18010)
*M. S. Barabashko,A. I. Krivchikov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在现象学框架下，以 C(T)=C3T³+C5T⁵ 拟合单壁碳纳米管束及吸附气体体系的热容数据，揭示吸附质对纳米管振动激发谱的非独立、耦合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清吸附气体对碳纳米管体系低温热容的贡献是否可视为与纳米管独立的叠加项，并刻画耦合激发谱的特征。

Method: 对原始单壁碳纳米管束（SWCNT bundles）及吸附 Xe、Ne、N₂、CH₄ 的体系进行低温热容数据分析，采用现象学形式（phenomenological form）C(T)=C3T³+C5T⁵ 拟合，将系数解释为耦合纳米管-吸附质激发谱（coupled SWCNT-adsorbate excitation spectrum）的有效参数。

Result: 原始纳米管束的 C5 为负，反映与低能类弯曲模式（flexural-like modes）相关的非德拜色散贡献；气体吸附系统性地改变 C3 与 C5。Xe 系列显示质量负载、吸附质-纳米管相互作用改变及管间耦合变化轻微重塑振动响应；Ne 的覆盖度依赖测量显示激发从纳米管主导逐步转向吸附质低能模式主导；N₂、CH₄ 引入平动、天平动、取向与转动自由度，产生更强扰动。

Conclusion: 吸附质热容贡献不能作为完全独立项处理；吸附气体的质量负载、相互作用强度及分子自由度共同重塑单壁碳纳米管体系的低温振动激发谱。

Abstract: Low-temperature heat-capacity data for pristine single-walled carbon nanotube (SWCNT) bundles and for SWCNT/gas systems containing adsorbed Xe, Ne, N2, and CH4 were analyzed within a phenomenological framework. The data were fitted within the phenomenological form C(T) = C3T3 + C5T5, where the coefficients are interpreted as effective parameters of the coupled SWCNT-adsorbate excitation spectrum. Pristine SWCNT bundles exhibit a negative C5 coefficient, reflecting a non-Debye dispersive contribution associated with low-energy flexural-like modes. Gas adsorption systematically modifies both C3 and C5, indicating that the adsorbate's heat-capacity contribution may not be treated as fully independent. The Xe series suggests that mass loading, modified adsorbate-nanotube interaction, and changes in intertube coupling slightly reshape the nanotube vibrational response. Coverage-dependent measurements for Ne reveal a progressive shift from nanotube-dominated excitations toward domination of additional low-energy adsorbate modes in the phonon spectrum of the SWCNT-Ne system, while molecular adsorbates (N2, CH4) introduce translational, librational, orientational, and rotational degrees of freedom that generate correspondingly stronger perturbations.

</details>


### [203] [Tunable Valley Filtering and Splitting via Gate Control in Wrinkled Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18042)
*C. I. Osuala,A. Belayadi,M. Moutan,S. Elatresh,A. Johanson,A. Abbout*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用石墨烯纳米带中的应变诱导褶皱产生的赝磁场实现可调谷过滤与谷分裂，并通过顶栅电压实现谷流的动态调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谷电子学器件（valleytronic devices）的发展亟需可控的谷过滤与谷分裂手段。研究旨在探索褶皱（wrinkle）这一应变工程途径在石墨烯纳米带中实现可调谷输运的可行性。

Method: 结合紧束缚模拟（tight-binding simulations）、应变张量分析（strain tensor analysis）与量子输运计算（quantum transport calculations），系统考察褶皱高度、应变扩展/形变及褶皱取向（倾斜角，tilt angle）等参数的作用，并施加顶栅电压（top-gate voltage）进行调控。

Result: 褶皱诱导的赝磁场（pseudomagnetic fields）促成电流分裂与谷选择性过滤；顶栅电压可实现谷分裂的动态控制，随电压增大呈现由分离谷流到谷合并的连续转变；在其他参数区间，谷过滤源于初始电流分裂机制，并表现出清晰的栅控输运行为转变。

Conclusion: 结果为应变工程谷输运（strain-engineered valley transport）提供了新认识，并指出面向实用石墨烯基谷电子器件的可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate the use of strain-induced wrinkles in graphene nanoribbons to achieve tunable valley filtering and splitting, crucial for advancing valleytronic devices. By combining tight-binding simulations, strain tensor analysis, and quantum transport calculations, we explicitly demonstrate that wrinkle-induced pseudomagnetic fields facilitate current splitting and valley-selective filtering in folded graphene nanoribbons based on wrinkle parameters like height, strain spreading/deformation and wrinkle orientation (tilt angle). Moreover, we show that the application of a top-gate voltage provides dynamic control over valley splitting, allowing a continuous transition from well-separated valley currents to valley merging as the gate voltage increases. In other parameter regimes, valley filtering arises from an initial current-splitting mechanism, exhibiting a clear gate-controlled transition between distinct transport behaviors. Our results offer new insights into strain-engineered valley transport and suggest a promising pathway toward practical graphene-based valleytronic devices.

</details>


### [204] [Charge-tunable acoustic surface plasmons in topological nodal-line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18158)
*Francesco Buccheri,Francesco M. D. Pellegrino,Jeroen van den Brink*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 拓扑节点线半金属（topological nodal-line semimetal）的鼓面表面态（drumhead surface state）可承载声学表面等离激元（acoustic surface plasmon），其速度因库仑相互作用（Coulomb interaction）显著增强；该模式位于表面粒子-空穴连续谱之上、混合表面-体吸收阈值之下，主要阻尼来自体粒子-空穴激发，并可用于传感应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节点线半金属的鼓面表面态具有很大的表面态密度，其集体激发（如表面等离激元）尚未被充分研究；本文旨在探究此类表面态是否支持等离激元模式及其色散与阻尼特性。

Method: 采用理论分析（解析推导）方法，在长波极限下考察鼓面表面态中等离激元模式的色散关系，并将其位置与表面粒子-空穴连续谱（surface particle-hole continuum）及混合表面-体吸收阈值（mixed surface-bulk absorption threshold）进行比较，以确定主导阻尼通道。

Result: 在足够小的波矢下，声学表面等离激元位于表面粒子-空穴连续谱之上、两个混合表面-体吸收阈值之下，因此体粒子-空穴产生（bulk particle-hole creation）成为主要阻尼通道；近鼓面耗尽（drumhead depletion）时色散对表面电荷高度敏感。

Conclusion: 节点线半金属支持速度被库仑相互作用增强的声学表面等离激元；其近鼓面耗尽的强色散敏感性提示等离激元谱学（plasmon spectroscopy）可用于传感技术。

Abstract: Topological nodal-line semimetals host drumhead surface states, carrying a large surface density of states. We show that these states support an acoustic surface plasmon, whose velocity is strongly enhanced by the Coulomb interaction. At sufficiently small wavevectors, the mode lies above the surface particle-hole continuum and below both mixed surface-bulk absorption thresholds, leaving bulk particle-hole creation as the leading damping channel. Near drumhead depletion, the dispersion is highly sensitive to the surface charge, which suggests applications of plasmon spectroscopy of nodal-line semimetals to sensing technologies.

</details>


### [205] [Digitally-alloyed magnetic topological insulator nanostructures grown via stencil mask molecular beam epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18280)
*Jan Karthein,Max Vaßen-Carl,Lars Maier,Abdur Rehman Jalil,Gion Toehgiono,Josua Thieme,Julian Feldewerth,Benjamin Bennemann,Joscha Domnick,Taizo Kawano,Sosuke Otsubo,René Heller,Jun Ishihara,Detlev Grützmacher,Makoto Kohda,Peter Schüffelgen,Thomas Schäpers*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）结合数字合金化（digital alloying）与模板掩模（stencil mask）原位图形化技术，制备Cr掺杂(Bi_ySb_{1-y})_2Te_3磁性拓扑绝缘体（magnetic topological insulator）微纳霍尔条，并在低温下研究其磁输运与磁性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性拓扑绝缘体纳米结构是实现容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computation）的潜在平台，但需将其嵌入超导架构（superconducting architectures），因而需要可控生长与图形化的纳米结构制备方法。

Method: 采用分子束外延生长磁性拓扑绝缘体纳米结构，通过数字合金化调控组分，并利用模板掩模技术原位定义形状；制备不同Cr掺杂量与不同尺寸的霍尔条（Hall bar），在低温下测量磁输运性质，并表征磁性质。

Result: 有迹象表明费米能（Fermi energy）位于体带隙（bulk band gap）内，同时无序（disorder）与电荷水坑（charge puddles）影响输运行为；研究还讨论了从铁磁（ferromagnetic）到顺磁（paramagnetic）行为的转变。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一条将原位图形化的磁性拓扑绝缘体微纳结构制备与超导架构集成的可行技术路线，并揭示了体带隙内费米能位置及无序、电荷水坑对输运的影响与磁性转变行为。

Abstract: Magnetic topological insulator nanostructures are a promising platform for the realization of fault-tolerant quantum computation. For this, such nanostructures need to be embedded into the necessary superconducting architectures. In this study, we demonstrate a method for the growth of magnetic topological insulator nanostructures via molecular beam epitaxy, utilising digital alloying and defining their shapes in-situ by stencil mask technology. Cr-doped (Bi$_y$Sb$_{1-y}$)$_2$Te$_3$ micro- and nanometer-sized Hall bars are fabricated and the magneto-transport properties for various Cr doping amounts and different Hall bar dimensions are investigated at low temperatures. We find indications that the Fermi energy is located inside the bulk band gap, while disorder and charge puddles affect the transport behaviour. Furthermore, we investigate the magnetic properties and discuss the transition from ferromagnetic to paramagnetic behaviour.

</details>


### [206] [Quantized resistance limitations for graphene p-n junction devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18318)
*Tamar M. Padwa,DaVonne Henry,Dominick S. Scaletta,Heather M. Hill,Antonio L. Levy,Dean G. Jarrett,David B. Newell,Albert F. Rigosi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估外延石墨烯 p-n 结（p-n junction）不同拓扑结构下有效电阻系数的可调参数空间，并开发自动化 Landauer-Büttiker Python 求解器，结合低温多端输运测量与 LTspice 仿真验证。通过香农熵（Shannon entropy）分析发现矩形棋盘结构拓扑复杂度与可调输出最高，Corbino 结构电阻可达性最为受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高阻值量子化霍尔电阻标准（quantized Hall resistance standards）需要获得超出外延石墨烯（epitaxial graphene）中稳健值 R_H = (1/2)(h/e²) 的电阻值，因此需探索器件拓扑对有效电阻系数的可调范围。

Method: 面向矩形、Corbino 与三角形三类外延石墨烯 p-n 结拓扑，开发了自动化 Landauer-Büttiker Python 求解器，以处理电阻到器件构型映射的数学简并性，并通过低温多端输运测量（cryogenic multi-terminal transport measurements）与 LTspice 仿真进行验证；同时采用香农熵（Shannon entropy）分析可用状态。

Result: 香农熵分析表明，矩形棋盘（rectangular checkerboard）构型可最大化拓扑复杂度与可调输出，而 Corbino 几何的电阻可达性高度受限。

Conclusion: 不同 p-n 结拓扑在有效电阻系数可调性上存在显著差异，矩形棋盘结构在提升电阻标准可及值范围方面最具潜力，Corbino 结构适用性有限。

Abstract: Developing high-resistance quantized Hall resistance standards requires accessing values beyond the robust value seen in epitaxial graphene (R_{\mathrm{H}}=\ \frac{1}{2}\frac{h}{e^{2}}). This study evaluates the available parameter space of the coefficient of effective resistance across various epitaxial graphene p-n junction topologies, including rectangular, Corbino, and triangular layouts. Because mapping desired resistances to device configurations is mathematically degenerate, an automated Landauer-Büttiker Python solver was developed for all three topologies and was validated by cryogenic multi-terminal transport measurements and LTspice simulations. By analyzing the Shannon entropy of available states, it is found that rectangular checkerboards maximize topological complexity and tunable outputs, whereas Corbino geometries exhibit highly constrained resistance availabilities.

</details>


### [207] [Symmetry induced spin splitting of topological surface states at equal carrier density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18342)
*Wouter Beugeling,Shyam Sundar Yadav,Maximilian Hofer,Valentin L. Müller,Moritz Siebert,Tobias Kießling,Ewelina M. Hankiewicz,Hartmut Buhmann,Laurens W. Molenkamp*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在基于 HgTe 的双栅三维拓扑绝缘体器件中，对称栅压条件下磁输运观察到奇、偶整数填充因子的霍尔平台，其源于结构反演对称性诱导的自旋劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维拓扑绝缘体的表面态输运通常以量子化霍尔电导和整数填充因子序列为特征；若将上下表面视为两个独立的狄拉克载流子，理论上仅预期奇数填充因子的霍尔平台。该工作旨在检验这一独立狄拉克载流子图像在载流子密度对称时是否成立。

Method: 采用基于 HgTe 的双栅三维拓扑绝缘体器件开展磁输运实验，通过对称栅压使上下表面载流子密度相等，并利用微扰模型与等宇称本征态（isoparity eigenstates，结构反演对应的守恒量子数）解释实验结果。

Result: 在拓扑表面态区间内，对称栅压下同时观察到奇数与偶数整数填充因子的霍尔平台；该现象源于结构反演对称性诱导的自旋劈裂，使本征态成为上下表面态的对称与反对称线性组合。

Conclusion: 结构反演对称性可诱导自旋劈裂，与二维系统中因反演对称性缺失导致自旋劈裂的情形相反；结果表明，当载流子密度对称时，将上下表面态视为独立狄拉克载流子的图像不再成立。

Abstract: Surface states are among the most striking consequences of band inversion in topological materials. In magnetotransport of three-dimensional topological insulators, the typical signature of surface state transport is a quantized Hall conductance with nontrivial sequences of integer filling factors, varying with the total carrier density. For two independent Dirac carriers, one expects Hall plateaus only at odd-integer filling factors. In this work, we report the observation of symmetry induced spin splitting in magnetotransport experiments on a dual-gate three-dimensional topological insulator device based on HgTe. In the topological surface state regime, we observe Hall plateaus at both odd and even integers when we gate the device symmetrically, i.e., with equal density at the top and bottom. This phenomenon originates in structural inversion symmetry, which remarkably induces a spin splitting and forces the eigenstates to be symmetric and antisymmetric linear combinations of the top and bottom surface states. This is in contrast with two-dimensional systems, where spin splitting is caused by absence of inversion symmetry. We interpret the experimental observations with a perturbative model using isoparity eigenstates, the conserved quantum number associated with structural inversion. The result demonstrates that the picture where the top and bottom states are treated as independent Dirac carriers is not valid when the carrier density is symmetric.

</details>


### [208] [Topological Hybridization Gaps from Molecular Orbitals Coupled to Dirac Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18400)
*Julián Castro,Jorge I. Facio,Verónica Vildosola*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 磁性带隙拓扑绝缘体表面的半量子化霍尔响应，会因表面狄拉克费米子与手性轨道平带杂化而增加一个整数；对总角动量为 j 的轨道，旋转对称性将表面霍尔电导固定为 je²/h。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半量子化霍尔响应（half-quantized Hall response）通常被认为由磁性质量项唯一确定。本文试图说明轨道自由度（分子轨道角动量）可作为额外调控手段改变表面拓扑响应。

Method: 通过将表面狄拉克费米子（surface Dirac fermions）与手性轨道平带（flat band of chiral orbitals）杂化，利用自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）导致杂化绕能带交叉环（band-crossing ring）缠绕并打开拓扑带隙；并对 Bi₂Se₃ 上单层 NO 吸附体系进行第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）。

Result: 杂化使表面霍尔电导增加整数：对总角动量 j 的轨道，旋转对称性给出 σ_xy = je²/h，符号由所穿越的狄拉克分支决定，且与狄拉克质量符号无关；该结果在晶体场淬灭轨道矩（crystal-field quenching）后仍成立，此时缠绕由杂化路径间的干涉决定。计算预测约 20 meV 的杂化带隙与本征表面霍尔电导 3/2 e²/h。

Conclusion: 分子轨道角动量可作为调控表面拓扑（surface topology）的有效参量，拓展了拓扑绝缘体表面量子化输运的设计空间。

Abstract: A magnetically gapped topological-insulator surface carries a half-quantized Hall response. We show that hybridizing the surface Dirac fermions with a flat band of chiral orbitals adds an integer to it: spin-momentum locking forces the hybridization to wind around the band-crossing ring, opening a topological gap. For orbitals with total angular momentum $j$, rotational symmetry fixes the surface Hall conductivity to $je^2/h$, with the sign set by the Dirac branch crossed and independent of the sign of the Dirac mass. The result survives crystal-field quenching of the orbital moment, with the winding then set by interference between hybridization pathways. First-principles calculations for a monolayer of nitric oxide adsorbed on Bi$_2$Se$_3$ predict a $\sim 20$ meV hybridization gap and an intrinsic surface Hall conductivity of $\frac{3}{2}e^2/h$, identifying molecular orbital angular momentum as a handle on surface topology.

</details>


### [209] [Topological phase diagram of an extended Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model with negative coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18456)
*P. A. Grizzi,P. Roura-Bas,A. A. Aligia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文解析地确定了一个扩展的 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型的相图，该模型在钙钛矿激子-极化激元微柱中已实现。相图以在位能劈裂（on-site energy splitting）与胞间/胞内跃迁振幅比（intercell to intracell hopping amplitude ratio）为参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该三原子/单胞扩展 SSH 模型已在钙钛矿激子-极化激元微柱（perovskite exciton-polariton micropillars）中实验实现，其第二、三能带通过 k=0 处能带交叉（band crossing）发生拓扑转变且伴随负耦合；需要理论相图来理解现有实验。

Method: 采用解析方法，以在位能劈裂与胞间/胞内跃迁振幅比为参数，计算三个能带的 Zak 相位（Zak phase）以刻画拓扑相。

Result: 在参数平面内识别出七个区域，包含四种不同的拓扑相，由三个能带各自不同的 Zak 相位加以区分。

Conclusion: 结果为解释现有实验提供理论框架，并为该体系的后续实验研究提供指导。

Abstract: Recently, an extended Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model with three atoms per unit cell has been experimentally realized in perovskite exciton-polariton micropillars. A distinctive feature of this system is that the second and third bands undergo a topological transition through a band crossing at the wave vector $k=0$, associated with a negative coupling. In this work, we analytically determine the phase diagram of the model as a function of the on-site energy splitting and the ratio of intercell to intracell hopping amplitudes. We identify seven regions in this lane, with four distinct topological phases, characterized by different Zak phases of the three bands. Our results provide a useful theoretical framework for interpreting existing experiments and serve as a guide for future experimental investigations of this system.

</details>


### [210] [Quantum melting and Pomeranchuk effect of zero-field Wigner solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18532)
*Boliang Liu,Simon Munyan,Sina Ahadi,Erez Berg,Susanne Stemmer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在砷化镉量子阱中研究零场电子与空穴维格纳固体（Wigner solids）与量子霍尔液体（quantum Hall liquids）的竞争关系，发现低温极限符合理论预期，而在更高温度下观察到由自旋熵（spin entropy）驱动的重入固体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维格纳固体与整数量子霍尔、分数量子霍尔液体如何竞争或协同，是长期未解的问题；本文针对零场下的电子与空穴维格纳固体，考察这一竞争机制。

Method: 在砷化镉（cadmium arsenide）量子阱（quantum wells）中制备零场电子与空穴维格纳固体，并在低温极限及其之外的温度区间测量相行为，将结果与已有理论模型对照，并结合自旋熵的贡献进行分析。

Result: 低温极限下结果与理论模型一致，即低整数填充因子（integer filling factors）时量子霍尔液体可在能量上优于固体；超出低温极限后，观察到从量子霍尔液体中涌现的重入固体（re-entrant solid）。

Conclusion: 该重入现象可用自旋熵解释，其机制类比于零场庞麦朗丘克效应（Pomeranchuk effect）但出现于有限磁场；量子霍尔液体与维格纳固体的竞争由能量与熵共同决定，从而产生由拓扑（topology）与关联（correlations）相互作用导致的丰富相稳定性图景。

Abstract: Wigner solids and crystals are many-body phases of matter in which repulsive interactions between the charge carriers cause them to freeze into a solid. How Wigner solids compete or cooperate with integer and fractional quantum Hall liquids has been a long-standing open question. Here, we investigate this question for zero-field, electron and hole Wigner solids in cadmium arsenide quantum wells. In the low temperature limit, our results are consistent with a recent theoretical model [A. P. Reddy and L. Fu, Phys. Rev. B 113, L161403 (2026)] that proposes that the quantum Hall liquid can become energetically favored over the solid at low integer filling factors. Going beyond the low-temperature limit, we observe a re-entrant solid that emerges from the quantum Hall liquid. We explain this observation by considering the role of spin entropy, in close analogy with the zero-field Pomeranchuk effect, but appearing here at finite fields. The competition between quantum Hall liquids and Wigner solids is thus governed by both energy and entropy terms, giving rise to rich phase stability diagrams that result from the interplay between topology and correlations.

</details>

### [211] [BeST: Beta splatted tomography for direct reconstruction from coherent imaging measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17982)
*Johannes Gruen,Philipp Paetzold,Andreas Schropp,Aknur Karabay,Shahin Sepanlou,Sebastian Eberle,Martin Seyrich,Felix Wittwer,Imke Greving,Silja Flenner,Ken Vidar Falch,Jan Garrevoet,Manuel Guizar-Sicairos,Christian G. Schroer,Johannes Hagemann*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出Beta Splatted Tomography（BeST），用紧支撑beta基元及其闭式解析投影表示公共物体体积，直接从相干散射图像联合重建相位与断层，降低计算需求并提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相干X射线断层（coherent X-ray tomography）需先高保真重建每个投影的相位，单个投影可能因定位误差等而有缺陷，导致断层重建不一致；而直接耦合相位恢复与断层虽通过公共体积保证一致性，却需要大量计算资源。

Method: 将公共物体体积用紧支撑（compactly supported）beta基元表示，该表示具有闭式解析投影（closed-form analytical projections），从而可直接由相干散射图像高效重建，并显著减少相对体素网格（voxel grid）的可优化参数数量。

Result: 在硬X射线近场全息（hard X-ray near-field holography）与远场叠层成像（far-field ptychography）的实验数据集上验证，联合方法相比两步法（two-step methods）提高了重建质量。

Conclusion: BeST以紧凑基元表示实现相位恢复与断层的直接耦合，在降低计算需求的同时改善重建一致性，并在两类相干成像实验中优于两步流程。

Abstract: Conventional coherent X-ray tomography requires the phase of each projection to be reconstructed with high fidelity. However, individual reconstructed projections might be flawed, e.g. due to positioning errors, yielding inconsistent tomographic reconstructions. By directly coupling phase retrieval and tomography, consistency is enforced through a common object volume. But reconstructing a full volume demands substantial computational resources. To reduce this demand, we introduce Beta Splatted Tomography (BeST), which represents the common object volume using compactly supported beta primitives with closed-form analytical projections, enabling efficient reconstruction directly from coherent scattering images. This representation substantially reduces the number of optimizable parameters compared to a voxel grid. We demonstrate BeST on experimental datasets from hard X-ray near-field holography and far-field ptychography measurements. In both cases, the joint technique improves reconstruction quality compared to two-step methods.

</details>

Minamino,Y. Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文利用Super-Kamiokande I-IV期太阳中微子数据，通过$^8$B中微子的绝热味转换（adiabatic flavor conversion）对太阳核心尺寸给出外边界，并测量核心密度与温度。高金属丰度B16+GS98模型下外边界为$0.499R_\odot$，结合SNO数据后收紧至$0.268R_\odot$；核心温度测量为$T_\text{C}=(15.599\pm0.0016)\times10^6$ K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳中微子（solar neutrino）探测可提供太阳核心聚变的直接证据并作为核心探针。由于$^8$B产生率对核心温度敏感、绝热味转换依赖局部电子密度，而电子密度随日心距离近似指数下降，因而可反推核心的尺寸、密度与温度。

Method: 基于Super-Kamiokande I-IV期太阳中微子数据（Super-Kamiokande phase I-IV solar neutrino data），推导太阳核心尺寸的外边界（outer bound），并对核心密度与温度进行测量；进一步与SNO多项式系数（SNO polynomial coefficients）联合拟合，并在不同金属丰度（metallicity）模型下比较结果。

Result: 在90%置信水平下，核心尺寸外边界为高金属丰度B16+GS98标准太阳模型（Standard Solar Model）的$0.499R_\odot$、含原太阳吸积（protosolar accretion）的K2-A09-MZvar-12的$0.381R_\odot$；联合拟合后分别收紧至$0.268R_\odot$与$0.215R_\odot$。在高金属丰度情景、取标称核心密度与尺寸时，日心温度为$T_\text{C}=(15.599\pm0.0016)\times10^6$ K，$^8$B产生剖面峰值半径处为$T_{\text{npz}}=(15.492\pm0.0017)\times10^6$ K。

Conclusion: 太阳中微子数据可用于约束太阳核心尺寸并测量核心密度与温度，且与SNO数据联合可显著改善核心尺寸的上限；结果随所采用金属丰度与太阳模型不同而异，其他金属丰度情形亦在文中报告。

Abstract: The detection of solar neutrino provides direct evidence for on-going fusion in the core of the Sun and serve as a probe for that solar core. Production rate of $^8$B is sensitive to the solar core temperature. Adiabatic flavor conversion of $^8$B solar neutrinos depends on the local solar electron density. Combined with the roughly exponential decrease in solar electron density with increasing distance from the center of the solar sphere, the solar core size, density, and temperature are inferred. This article presents an outer bound on solar core size and measurements of the density and temperature in the solar core based on Super-Kamiokande phase I-IV solar neutrino data. An outer bound is set for the solar core at $90\%$ confidence level as $0.499R_\odot$ for the high metallicity B16+GS98 Standard Solar Model and $0.381R_\odot$ for K2-A09-MZvar-12 which includes protosolar accretion. A combined fit with the SNO polynomial coefficients improves the limit to $0.268R_\odot$ for B16+GS98 and $0.215R_\odot$ for K2-A09-MZvar-12. Results for other metallicity cases are also reported. In the high metallicity scenario assuming nominal solar core density and solar core size, temperature at the solar center is reported at $T_\text{C}=(15.599\pm0.0016)\times10^6$ K and $T_{\text{npz}}=(15.492\pm0.0017)\times10^6$ K at the peak radius of the $^8$B production profile.

</details>


### [213] [LHC Based Antimuon-Nucleus Collider LH$μ$C: Nucleus Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17849)
*Ali Can Canbay,Burak Dagli,Arda Goroglu,Yigitali Karaca,Umit Kaya,Bora Ketenoglu,Saleh Sultansoy,Frank Zimmermann*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出基于大型强子对撞机（LHC）的反μ子-强子对撞机 LHμC 的原子核选项方案，评估其束流参数、亮度与物理潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究将超高能反μ子束与 LHC 原子核束对撞的可行性，以拓展运动学覆盖、触及部分子饱和（parton saturation）等物理前沿问题。

Method: 将基于 J-PARC μ子 g-2/EDM 实验技术（如 μTRISTAN 方案）的超冷 μ⁺ 束加速至 1 TeV，经 μTRISTAN 型增强器或置于 LHeC 能量回收直线加速器隧道内的增强器加速，并在与 LHC 相切的 3 km 主环中与 Pb、Xe、O、Ne 核束对撞；用 AloHEP 软件评估亮度（luminosity）与束束调谐移动（beam-beam tune shift）。

Result: 质心能量（centre-of-mass energy）达 13.1–47.9 TeV，约每核子 3.3 TeV；μ-Pb 对撞亮度达 2.7×10³⁰（2.2×10³⁰）cm⁻²s⁻¹，折合每核子 5.6×10³²（4.5×10³²）cm⁻²s⁻¹，Brinkmann-Dohlus 动态聚焦（dynamic focusing）方案可再提升 2–3 倍。

Conclusion: 该方案相较 LHеC 可将运动学可达范围扩展约 20 倍，并可研究微扰尺度下的部分子饱和、核部分子分布（nuclear parton distributions）、光子诱发过程及带电流味分离（charged-current flavour separation）。

Abstract: Nucleus options of the LH$μ$C, an antimuon-hadron collider based on the LHC, are presented. Ultra-cold $μ^+$ beams, based on the technology of the J-PARC muon $g-2$/EDM experiment as proposed for $μ$TRISTAN, are accelerated to 1 TeV in either a $μ$TRISTAN-type booster or a booster housed in the LH$e$C energy-recovery-linac tunnel, and collide with LHC nucleus beams in a 3 km main ring tangential to the LHC. Collisions with Pb, Xe, O and Ne beams provide centre-of-mass energies from 13.1 to 47.9 TeV, i.e. about 3.3 TeV per nucleon. Luminosities and beam-beam tune shifts are evaluated with the AloHEP software. For $μ$-Pb collisions, the luminosity reaches $2.7\times10^{30}$ ($2.2\times10^{30}$) cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$ with the $μ$TRISTAN-type (ERL-based) booster, i.e. $5.6\times10^{32}$ ($4.5\times10^{32}$) cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$ per nucleon, which a Brinkmann-Dohlus dynamic focusing scheme could increase by a factor of 2-3. The physics potential is discussed, including an extension of the kinematic reach by a factor of about 20 with respect to the LH$e$C, access to parton saturation at perturbative scales, nuclear parton distributions, photon-induced processes and charged-current flavour separation.

</details>


### [214] [Sensitivity to $Θ^+$ production in the $π^- p \to K^- X$ reaction at HIAF-HIRIBL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17904)
*Yu Xu,Ju-Jun Xie,Xueheng Zhang,Liangwen Chen,Yuhong Yu,Li Deng,Jiajia Zhai,Pei Yu,He Zhao,Lina Sheng,Guodong Shen,Zhiyu Sun*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文给出在HIAF及高刚度放射性束流线（HIHRBL）上以π⁻p→K⁻X缺失质量法搜寻窄Θ⁺(1.540 GeV/c²)的灵敏度预期，覆盖至N*(2400)形成能区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的E19能区不足以探测所提议的N*(2400)形成区域，需评估更高π介子动量下pion诱导Θ⁺搜寻的可行性与灵敏度。

Method: 基于E19的分辨率、效率与连续本底，对2.01与2.586 GeV/c两个π介子动量下各10天曝光做缺失质量（missing-mass）搜寻的灵敏度投影，并用法向非共振核子交换（nucleon-exchange）模型估计宽度标度。

Result: 仅本底假设下，90% CL微分截面上限（2°–15°平均）中位数分别为0.0293与0.0356 μb/sr；局部5σ发现阈值为0.0894与0.109 μb/sr。非共振模型给出宽度标度：J^P=1/2⁺为0.0039–0.079 MeV，J^P=1/2⁻为0.045–1.47 MeV。

Conclusion: 所有正宇称变体均可检验DIANA宽度标度0.34±0.10 MeV，负宇称灵敏度则依赖具体模型变体。

Abstract: Pion-induced searches for the $Θ^+$ can probe the proposed $N^*(2400)$ formation region beyond the E19 energies. We present sensitivity projections for a missing-mass search for a narrow $Θ^+$ at 1.540 GeV/$c^2$ in $π^- p \to K^- X$ at the High Intensity heavy-ion Accelerator Facility and its HIgh-rigidity Radioactive Ion Beam Line. With the E19 resolutions, efficiency, and continuum, separate 10-day exposures at pion momenta of 2.01 and 2.586 GeV/$c$ give background-only median expected 90% CL upper limits of 0.0293 and 0.0356 $μ$b/sr, respectively, on the differential cross section averaged over $2^\circ$ to $15^\circ$ on the selected high-momentum branch; the higher momentum corresponds to the proposed $N^*(2400)$ formation energy. Median local $5σ$ discovery thresholds are 0.0894 and 0.109 $μ$b/sr. Nonresonant nucleon-exchange models give width scales of 0.0039 to 0.079 MeV for $J^P=1/2^+$ and 0.045 to 1.47 MeV for $J^P=1/2^-$. All positive-parity variants would test the DIANA width scale of $0.34 \pm 0.10$ MeV; negative-parity sensitivity depends on the variant.

</details>
